========== index.js ==========
// index.js
import { registerRootComponent } from 'expo';
import TrackPlayer, { PlayerCommand } from '@rntp/player';
import App from './App';

TrackPlayer.registerPlaybackSession(async () => {
  // ---------- 1. Setup player ----------
  try {
    await TrackPlayer.setupPlayer({
      // â­ This is THE critical flag for capsule / lock-screen / notification.
      // Without 'music' the OS treats the notification as a generic one
      // and it will NOT show in ColorOS Aqua Dynamics or any media surface.
      contentType: 'music',

      // Pause when headphones unplugged / Bluetooth disconnects
      handleAudioBecomingNoisy: true,

      // Auto-pause on calls, auto-resume afterwards
      autoHandleInterruptions: true,

      android: {
        // â­ Keeps the foreground service + notification alive when the
        // user swipes the app away from recents. Without this the capsule
        // disappears the moment the app is removed from the recents list.
        taskRemovedBehavior: 'continue',
      },
    });
    console.log('[RNTP] Player ready');
  } catch (err) {
    // Expected on hot reload / resume â€” safe to ignore.
    console.log('[RNTP] setupPlayer skipped:', err?.message);
  }

  // ---------- 2. Remote commands (capsule / lock screen buttons) ----------
  try {
    await TrackPlayer.setCommands({
      capabilities: [
        PlayerCommand.PlayPause,   // play / pause toggle
        PlayerCommand.Next,        // skip forward
        PlayerCommand.Previous,    // skip back
        PlayerCommand.Seek,        // drag progress bar
        PlayerCommand.Stop,        // stop button
      ],
    });
    console.log('[RNTP] Commands registered');
  } catch (err) {
    console.log('[RNTP] setCommands failed:', err?.message);
  }
});

registerRootComponent(App);

========== App.tsx ==========
import React, { useEffect } from 'react';
import { Platform, View } from 'react-native';
import { StatusBar } from 'expo-status-bar';
import * as Notifications from 'expo-notifications';
import { GestureHandlerRootView } from 'react-native-gesture-handler';
import { SafeAreaProvider } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';

import RootNavigator from './src/navigation/RootNavigator';
import { useTheme } from './src/hooks/useTheme';
import { useLiveActivitySync } from './src/hooks/useLiveActivity';
import { useMediaSessionSync } from './src/hooks/useMediaSessionSync';
import { applyGlobalFont } from './src/utils/globalFont';
import OtaUpdater from './src/components/OtaUpdater';

// âœ… Delete live-notification setup
import {
  setupDeleteNotificationChannel,
  registerDeleteNotificationEvents,
} from './src/services/notifications/deleteNotificationService';

applyGlobalFont();

export default function App() {
  return (
    <GestureHandlerRootView style={{ flex: 1 }}>
      <SafeAreaProvider>
        <Inner />
      </SafeAreaProvider>
    </GestureHandlerRootView>
  );
}

function Inner() {
  const theme = useTheme();

  useLiveActivitySync();
  useMediaSessionSync(); // keeps the system media card / status-bar pill metadata fresh

  // Android 13+: without POST_NOTIFICATIONS the media notification never shows.
  // Also sets up the delete-progress notification channel + Cancel action.
  useEffect(() => {
    // âœ… Delete-progress channel + action category (safe to call multiple times)
    setupDeleteNotificationChannel().catch(() => {});
    registerDeleteNotificationEvents();

    if (Platform.OS !== 'android') return;
    (async () => {
      try {
        const cur = await Notifications.getPermissionsAsync();
        if (!cur.granted && cur.canAskAgain) {
          await Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync();
        }
      } catch {}
    })();
  }, []);

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.background }}>
      <StatusBar style={theme.mode === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark'} />

      {/* Check and download Production OTA updates */}
      <OtaUpdater />

      <View style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <RootNavigator />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Shared assets Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
const STAR_ICON = require("../../assets/icons/star-icon.png");

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ ProgressBar Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
const ProgressBar = ({
  position = 0,
  duration = 0,
  onSeek,
  trackColor,
  fillColor,
}: {
  position?: number;
  duration?: number;
  onSeek?: (seconds: number) => void;
  trackColor: string;
  fillColor: string;
}) => {
  const [isDragging, setIsDragging] = useState(false);
  const barWidthRef = useRef(0);
  const durationRef = useRef(duration);
  const onSeekRef = useRef(onSeek);
  const progressAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    durationRef.current = duration;
  }, [duration]);

  useEffect(() => {
    onSeekRef.current = onSeek;
  }, [onSeek]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isDragging) return;
    const ratio =
      duration > 0 ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, position / duration)) : 0;
    Animated.timing(progressAnim, {
      toValue: ratio,
      duration: 250,
      useNativeDriver: false,
    }).start();
  }, [position, duration, isDragging, progressAnim]);

  const ratioFromX = (x: number) => {
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(barWidthRef.current, x));
    return barWidthRef.current > 0 ? clamped / barWidthRef.current : 0;
  };

  const barPan = useRef(
    PanResponder.create({
      onStartShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: () => true,
      onPanResponderTerminationRequest: () => false,
      onPanResponderGrant: (evt) => {
        setIsDragging(true);
        progressAnim.stopAnimation();
        progressAnim.setValue(ratioFromX(evt.nativeEvent.locationX));
      },
      onPanResponderMove: (evt) => {
        progressAnim.setValue(ratioFromX(evt.nativeEvent.locationX));
      },
      onPanResponderRelease: (evt) => {
        const ratio = ratioFromX(evt.nativeEvent.locationX);
        onSeekRef.current?.(ratio * durationRef.current);
        setIsDragging(false);
      },
      onPanResponderTerminate: () => setIsDragging(false),
    }),
  ).current;

  const progressWidth = progressAnim.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: ["0%", "100%"],
  });

  return (
    <View style={barStyles.container}>
      <View
        style={[barStyles.track, { backgroundColor: trackColor }]}
        onLayout={(e) => {
          barWidthRef.current = e.nativeEvent.layout.width;
        }}
        {...barPan.panHandlers}
      >
        <Animated.View
          pointerEvents="none"
          style={[
            barStyles.progress,
            { width: progressWidth, backgroundColor: fillColor },
          ]}
        />
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const barStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { width: "100%", paddingVertical: 12 },
  track: {
    height: 7,
    borderRadius: 8,
    justifyContent: "center",
    position: "relative",
  },
  progress: {
    height: 7,
    borderRadius: 8,
    position: "absolute",
    left: 0,
  },
});

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ BoldStar Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
const BoldStar = ({
  size = 20,
  thickness = 0.7,
  color = "#FFFFFF",
}: {
  size?: number;
  thickness?: number;
  color?: string;
}) => {
  const pad = Math.ceil(thickness);
  const offsets = [
    [0, 0],
    [thickness, 0],
    [-thickness, 0],
    [0, thickness],
    [0, -thickness],
    [thickness, thickness],
    [-thickness, -thickness],
    [thickness, -thickness],
    [-thickness, thickness],
  ];
  return (
    <View style={{ width: size + pad * 2, height: size + pad * 2 }}>
      {offsets.map(([dx, dy], i) => (
        <Image
          key={i}
          source={STAR_ICON}
          resizeMode="contain"
          style={{
            position: "absolute",
            left: pad + dx,
            top: pad + dy,
            width: size,
            height: size,
            tintColor: color,
          }}
        />
      ))}
    </View>
  );
};

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Marquee Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
type MarqueeLine = {
  text: string;
  style: any;
  scroll?: boolean;
  onPress?: () => void;
};

const wordCount = (t: string): number =>
  t.trim().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).length;

const MarqueeTexts = ({
  lines,
  gap = 40,
  speed = 35,
  pause = MARQUEE_PAUSE_MS,
  startDelay = MARQUEE_START_DELAY_MS,
}: {
  lines: MarqueeLine[];
  gap?: number;
  speed?: number;
  pause?: number;
  startDelay?: number;
}) => {
  const [containerW, setContainerW] = useState(0);
  const [textWs, setTextWs] = useState<number[]>(() => lines.map(() => 0));

  const progress = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  const measured =
    containerW > 0 && lines.every((l, i) => !l.scroll || textWs[i] > 0);
  const moves = lines.map((l, i) => !!l.scroll && textWs[i] > containerW);
  const anyMove = measured && moves.some(Boolean);

  const effGaps = textWs.map((w) => Math.max(gap, containerW - w + 20));
  const dists = textWs.map((w, i) => w + effGaps[i]);
  const durations = dists.map((d) => d / speed);
  const maxDur = Math.max(...durations.filter((_, i) => moves[i]), 0.001);
  const sig = anyMove ? dists.map((d, i) => (moves[i] ? d : 0)).join(",") : "";

  useEffect(() => {
    progress.setValue(0);
    if (!anyMove) return;
    const anim = Animated.sequence([
      Animated.delay(startDelay),
      Animated.loop(
        Animated.sequence([
          Animated.timing(progress, {
            toValue: 1,
            duration: maxDur * 1000,
            easing: Easing.linear,
            useNativeDriver: true,
          }),
          Animated.delay(pause),
        ]),
      ),
    ]);
    anim.start();
    return () => anim.stop();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [sig]);

  return (
    <View>
      {lines.map((line, i) => {
        const wrapperProps = {
          activeOpacity: line.onPress ? 0.6 : 1,
          disabled: !line.onPress,
          onPress: line.onPress,
          style: { overflow: "hidden" as const, position: "relative" as const },
          onLayout:
            i === 0
              ? (e: LayoutChangeEvent) =>
                  setContainerW(e.nativeEvent.layout.width)
              : undefined,
        };

        if (!line.scroll) {
          return (
            <TouchableOpacity key={i} {...wrapperProps}>
              <Text style={line.style} numberOfLines={1} ellipsizeMode="tail">
                {line.text}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          );
        }

        const f = durations[i] / maxDur;
        const translateX: any = moves[i]
          ? progress.interpolate(
              f < 1
                ? {
                    inputRange: [0, f, 1],
                    outputRange: [0, -dists[i], -dists[i]],
                  }
                : { inputRange: [0, 1], outputRange: [0, -dists[i]] },
            )
          : 0;

        return (
          <TouchableOpacity key={i} {...wrapperProps}>
            <Animated.View
              style={{
                flexDirection: "row",
                width: 10000,
                transform: [{ translateX }],
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={line.style}
                numberOfLines={1}
                onLayout={(e) => {
                  const w = Math.ceil(e.nativeEvent.layout.width) + 1;
                  setTextWs((prev) =>
                    prev[i] === w
                      ? prev
                      : prev.map((v, k) => (k === i ? w : v)),
                  );
                }}
              >
                {line.text}
              </Text>
              {moves[i] && (
                <Text
                  style={[line.style, { marginLeft: effGaps[i] }]}
                  numberOfLines={1}
                >
                  {line.text}
                </Text>
              )}
            </Animated.View>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        );
      })}
    </View>
  );
};

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ RippleButton Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
const RippleButton = ({
  onPress,
  children,
  style,
  rippleSize = 90,
  activeOpacity = 0.7,
  hitSlop,
  rippleColor = "#000000",
}: {
  onPress?: () => void;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  style?: any;
  rippleSize?: number;
  activeOpacity?: number;
  hitSlop?: any;
  rippleColor?: string;
}) => {
  const ripple = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  const handlePress = useCallback(() => {
    ripple.setValue(0);
    Animated.timing(ripple, {
      toValue: 1,
      duration: 560,
      easing: Easing.out(Easing.quad),
      useNativeDriver: true,
    }).start();
    onPress?.();
  }, [onPress, ripple]);

  const scale = ripple.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: [0.15, 1],
  });

  const opacity = ripple.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 0.15, 0.6, 1],
    outputRange: [0, 0.85, 0.45, 0],
  });

  return (
    <TouchableOpacity
      onPress={handlePress}
      activeOpacity={activeOpacity}
      hitSlop={hitSlop}
      style={[style, { overflow: "hidden", borderRadius: rippleSize / 2 }]}
    >
      <Animated.View
        pointerEvents="none"
        style={[
          StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
          { alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
        ]}
      >
        <Animated.View
          style={{
            width: rippleSize,
            height: rippleSize,
            borderRadius: rippleSize / 2,
            backgroundColor: rippleColor,
            opacity,
            transform: [{ scale }],
          }}
        />
      </Animated.View>
      {children}
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );
};

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ PlayerControlsBlock Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
type PlayerControlsBlockProps = {
  songId: string | number;
  title: string;
  artist: string;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  isFav: boolean;
  position: number;
  duration: number;
  onToggleFav?: () => void;
  onOpenOptions?: () => void;
  onPlayPause?: () => void;
  onPrev?: () => void;
  onNext?: () => void;
  onSeek?: (seconds: number) => void;
  onOpenAlbum?: () => void;
  onDark: boolean;
};

const PlayerControlsBlock = ({
  songId,
  title,
  artist,
  isPlaying,
  isFav,
  position,
  duration,
  onToggleFav,
  onOpenOptions,
  onPlayPause,
  onPrev,
  onNext,
  onSeek,
  onOpenAlbum,
  onDark,
}: PlayerControlsBlockProps) => {
  const theme = useTheme();
  const remaining = Math.max(0, (duration || 0) - (position || 0));

  const fg = onDark ? "#FFFFFF" : theme.text;
  const fgMuted = onDark ? "rgba(232, 226, 226, 0.48)" : theme.textSecondary;
  const fgFaint = onDark ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.6)" : theme.textSecondary;

  const trackBg = onDark ? "rgba(174, 176, 177, 0.13)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.08)";
  const progressBg = onDark ? "rgba(240, 244, 245, 0.48)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.4)";
  const rippleColor = onDark ? "#000000" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.15)";
  const bottomIconColor = onDark
    ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.6)"
    : theme.textSecondary;

  return (
    <View>
      <View style={blockStyles.infoContainer}>
        <View style={blockStyles.titleRow}>
          <View style={blockStyles.titleTextContainer}>
            <MarqueeTexts
              key={`${songId}|${title}|${artist}`}
              lines={[
                {
                  text: title,
                  style: [blockStyles.title, { color: fg }],
                  scroll: wordCount(title) >= TITLE_SCROLL_MIN_WORDS,
                  onPress: onOpenAlbum,
                },
                {
                  text: artist,
                  style: [blockStyles.artist, { color: fgMuted }],
                  scroll: wordCount(artist) >= ARTIST_SCROLL_MIN_WORDS,
                  onPress: onOpenAlbum,
                },
              ]}
              speed={35}
              pause={MARQUEE_PAUSE_MS}
              startDelay={MARQUEE_START_DELAY_MS}
            />
          </View>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={onToggleFav}
            style={[
              blockStyles.iconButton,
              {
                backgroundColor: isFav
                  ? "transparent"
                  : onDark
                    ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.15)"
                    : "rgba(0,0,0,0.08)",
              },
            ]}
            hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            {isFav ? (
              <Image
                source={FAVOURITED_ICON}
                style={{ width: 27, height: 27 }}
                resizeMode="contain"
                fadeDuration={0}
              />
            ) : (
              <BoldStar size={14} thickness={0.1} color={fg} />
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={onOpenOptions}
            style={[
              blockStyles.iconButton,
              blockStyles.dotsButton,
              {
                backgroundColor: onDark
                  ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.15)"
                  : "rgba(0,0,0,0.08)",
              },
            ]}
            hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <EllipsisIcon size={16} color={fg} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={blockStyles.progressContainer}>
        <ProgressBar
          position={position}
          duration={duration}
          onSeek={onSeek}
          trackColor={trackBg}
          fillColor={progressBg}
        />
        <View style={blockStyles.timeRow}>
          <Text style={[blockStyles.timeText, { color: fgFaint }]}>
            {formatDuration(position)}
          </Text>
          <Text style={[blockStyles.timeText, { color: fgFaint }]}>
            -{formatDuration(remaining)}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </View>

      <View style={blockStyles.controlsContainer}>
        <RippleButton
          onPress={onPrev}
          style={blockStyles.controlBtn}
          rippleSize={72}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
          rippleColor={rippleColor}
        >
          <BackwardIcon size={47} color={fg} />
        </RippleButton>

        <RippleButton
          onPress={onPlayPause}
          style={blockStyles.playButton}
          rippleSize={108}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          rippleColor={rippleColor}
        >
          {isPlaying ? (
            <PauseIcon size={60} color={fg} />
          ) : (
            <PlayIcon size={56} color={fg} />
          )}
        </RippleButton>

        <RippleButton
          onPress={onNext}
          style={blockStyles.controlBtn}
          rippleSize={72}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
          rippleColor={rippleColor}
        >
          <ForwardIcon size={47} color={fg} />
        </RippleButton>
      </View>

      <View style={blockStyles.bottomRow}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={blockStyles.bottomBtn}
          onPress={() => showToast("Lyrics feature soon...")}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
          hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
        >
          <LyricsIcon size={27} color={bottomIconColor} />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={blockStyles.bottomBtn}
          onPress={() => showToast("Cast feature soon...")}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
          hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
        >
          <CastIcon size={23} color={bottomIconColor} />
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={blockStyles.bottomBtn}
          onPress={() => showToast("Queue feature soon...")}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
          hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
        >
          <QueueIcon size={23} color={bottomIconColor} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

const blockStyles = StyleSheet.create({
  infoContainer: { paddingLeft: 30, marginTop: 33 },
  titleRow: { flexDirection: "row", alignItems: "center" },
  titleTextContainer: {
    width: "65%",

  },
  title: {
    fontSize: 20,
    fontFamily: SF.semibold,

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ ExpandedPreview Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
export const ExpandedPreview = ({
  itemKey,
  isVideo,
  title,
  artist,
  isPlaying,
  isFav,
  onToggleFav,
  onOpenAlbum,
  onOpenOptions,
  playPause,
  next,
  hasNext,
  prev,
  seek,
  onDark,
}: {
  itemKey: string | number;
  isVideo: boolean;
  title: string;
  artist: string;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  isFav: boolean;
  onToggleFav: () => void;
  onOpenAlbum?: () => void;
  onOpenOptions: () => void;
  playPause: () => void;
  next: () => void;
  hasNext?: boolean;
  prev: () => void;
  seek: (seconds: number) => void;
  onDark: boolean;
}) => {
  const musicPos = usePlayerStore((s) => s.position);
  const musicDur = usePlayerStore((s) => s.duration);
  const videoPos = useVideoBridge((s) => s.position);
  const videoDur = useVideoBridge((s) => s.duration);

  return (
    <PlayerControlsBlock
      songId={itemKey}
      title={title}
      artist={artist}
      isPlaying={isPlaying}
      isFav={isFav}
      position={isVideo ? videoPos : musicPos}
      duration={isVideo ? videoDur : musicDur}
      onToggleFav={onToggleFav}
      onOpenOptions={onOpenOptions}
      onPlayPause={playPause}
      onPrev={prev}
      onNext={next}
      onSeek={seek}
      onOpenAlbum={onOpenAlbum}
      onDark={onDark}
    />
  );
};

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ SpinSkipButton Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
const SpinSkipButton = ({
  direction,
  onPress,
  size = 54,
}: {
  direction: "forward" | "backward";
  onPress: () => void;
  size?: number;
}) => {
  const spin = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  const handlePress = () => {
    spin.stopAnimation();
    spin.setValue(0);
    Animated.timing(spin, {
      toValue: 1,
      duration: 650,
      easing: Easing.inOut(Easing.quad),
      useNativeDriver: true,
    }).start();
    onPress();
  };

  const rotate = spin.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: ["0deg", direction === "forward" ? "360deg" : "-360deg"],
  });

  const circle = size + 22;
  const pivot = (2.4961 / 56) * size;
  const Ring = direction === "forward" ? Forward10Ring : Replay10Ring;
  const Digits = direction === "forward" ? Forward10Digits : Replay10Digits;

  return (
    <TouchableOpacity
      onPress={handlePress}
      activeOpacity={0.7}
      hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 8, right: 8 }}
      style={{
        width: circle,
        height: circle,
        borderRadius: circle / 2,
        backgroundColor: SKIP_BG,
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
      }}
    >
      <View style={{ width: size, height: size }}>
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
            {
              transform: [
                { translateY: pivot },
                { rotate },
                { translateY: -pivot },
              ],
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Ring size={size} color="#fff" />
        </Animated.View>
        <View style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} pointerEvents="none">
          <Digits size={size} color="#fff" />
        </View>
      </View>
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );
};

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ ProgressSection Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
// Bottom block layout (top Ã¢â€ â€™ bottom):
//   title Ã¢â€ â€™ artist Ã¢â€ â€™ progress bar Ã¢â€ â€™ time row
// Ã¢Å“â€¦ When chrome hides, this whole block slides DOWN and fades to opacity 0
const ProgressSection = React.memo(function ProgressSection({
  isLandscape,
  insets,
  title,
  artist,
  onSeek,
  bump,
  chromeAnim,
}: {
  isLandscape: boolean;
  insets: any;
  title: string;
  artist: string;
  onSeek: (s: number) => void;
  bump: () => void;
  chromeAnim: Animated.Value;
}) {
  const position = useVideoBridge((s) => s.position);
  const duration = useVideoBridge((s) => s.duration);
  const remaining = Math.max(0, (duration || 0) - (position || 0));

  const translateY = chromeAnim.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: [CHROME_SLIDE_DISTANCE, 0], // hidden Ã¢â€ â€™ pushed down
  });

  return (
    <Animated.View
      style={[
        fs.bottom,
        {
          bottom: insets.bottom + (isLandscape ? 26 : 44),
          left: insets.left + (isLandscape ? 28 : 28),   // Ã¢â€ Â controls left edge
      right: insets.right + (isLandscape ? 28 : 28), // Ã¢â€ Â controls right edge
          opacity: chromeAnim,
          transform: [{ translateY }],
        },
      ]}
      pointerEvents="box-none"
    >
      <Text numberOfLines={1} style={isLandscape ? fs.lsTitle : fs.title}>
        {title}
      </Text>
      <Text numberOfLines={1} style={isLandscape ? fs.lsArtist : fs.artist}>
        {artist}
      </Text>

      <ProgressBar
        position={position}
        duration={duration}
        onSeek={(s) => {
          bump();
          onSeek(s);
        }}
        trackColor="rgba(255,255,255,0.25)"
        fillColor="rgba(255,255,255,0.9)"
      />

      <View style={fs.timeRow}>
        <Text style={fs.time}>{formatDuration(position)}</Text>
        <Text style={fs.time}>-{formatDuration(remaining)}</Text>
      </View>
    </Animated.View>
  );
});

// Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ FullscreenPlayer Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
export const FullscreenPlayer = React.memo(function FullscreenPlayer({
  title,
  artist,
  onClose,
  onCollapse,
}: {
  title: string;
  artist: string;
  onClose: () => void;
  onCollapse: () => void;
}) {
  const win = useWindowDimensions();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const player = useVideoBridge((s) => s.player);
  const bridgeIsPlaying = useVideoBridge((s) => s.isPlaying);
  const muted = useVideoBridge((s) => s.muted);
  const volume = useVideoBridge((s) => s.volume);

  const onToggleMute = useVideoBridge((s) => s.toggleMute);
  const onTogglePlay = useVideoBridge((s) => s.togglePlay);
  const onSkip = useVideoBridge((s) => s.skip);
  const onSeek = useVideoBridge((s) => s.seekTo);

  // Ã¢Å“â€¦ Local echo of play/pause so the icon flips even if the bridge is
// Use actual playback state from the video bridge
const isPlaying = bridgeIsPlaying;

const handlePlayPause = useCallback(() => {
  try {
    onTogglePlay();
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("[Fullscreen] Play/pause failed:", error);
  }
}, [onTogglePlay]);
  const collapseRef = useRef(onCollapse);
  collapseRef.current = onCollapse;
  const heightRef = useRef(win.height);
  heightRef.current = win.height;
  const dragY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  const dragScale = dragY.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 300],
    outputRange: [1, 0.86],
    extrapolate: "clamp",
  });
  const dragOpacity = dragY.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 300],
    outputRange: [1, 0.5],
    extrapolate: "clamp",
  });

  const swipePan = useRef(
    PanResponder.create({
      onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: (_, g) =>
        g.dy > 14 && Math.abs(g.dy) > Math.abs(g.dx) * 1.5,
      onPanResponderTerminationRequest: () => false,
      onPanResponderMove: (_, g) => {
        if (g.dy > 0) dragY.setValue(g.dy);
      },
      onPanResponderRelease: (_, g) => {
        if (g.dy > 90 || g.vy > 0.7) {
          Animated.timing(dragY, {
            toValue: heightRef.current,
            duration: 180,
            easing: Easing.in(Easing.quad),
            useNativeDriver: true,
          }).start(() => collapseRef.current());
        } else {
          Animated.spring(dragY, {
            toValue: 0,
            friction: 9,
            useNativeDriver: true,
          }).start();
        }
      },
      onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
        Animated.spring(dragY, {
          toValue: 0,
          friction: 9,
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }).start();
      },
    }),
  ).current;

  const isLandscape = win.width > win.height;

  // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ Chrome show / hide Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
  const [show, setShow] = useState(true);
  const autoHide = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  // 1 = fully visible, 0 = hidden (top moved up, bottom moved down, opacity 0)
  const chromeAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(1)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    Animated.timing(chromeAnim, {
      toValue: show ? 1 : 0,
      duration: CHROME_ANIM_MS,
      easing: show ? Easing.out(Easing.cubic) : Easing.in(Easing.cubic),
      useNativeDriver: true,
    }).start();
  }, [show, chromeAnim]);

  const bump = useCallback(() => {
    setShow(true);
    if (autoHide.current) clearTimeout(autoHide.current);
    autoHide.current = setTimeout(() => setShow(false), CHROME_VISIBLE_MS);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isPlaying) {
      setShow(true);
      if (autoHide.current) clearTimeout(autoHide.current);
      return;
    }
    bump();
    return () => {
      if (autoHide.current) clearTimeout(autoHide.current);
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [isPlaying]);

  const toggleChrome = useCallback(() => {
    if (show) {
      if (autoHide.current) clearTimeout(autoHide.current);
      setShow(false);
    } else if (isPlaying) {
      bump(); // show again and restart the auto-hide timer
    } else {
      setShow(true);
    }
  }, [show, isPlaying, bump]);

  // Interpolations
  const topTranslateY = chromeAnim.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: [-CHROME_SLIDE_DISTANCE, 0], // hidden Ã¢â€ â€™ pushed up
  });

  const playSize = isLandscape ? 96 : 92;
  const skipSize = isLandscape ? 46 : 40;
  const centerGap = isLandscape ? 56 : 48;

  return (
    <Animated.View
      style={{
        flex: 1,
        backgroundColor: "#000",
        opacity: dragOpacity,
        transform: [{ translateY: dragY }, { scale: dragScale }],
      }}
      {...swipePan.panHandlers}
    >
      <Pressable style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill} onPress={toggleChrome}>
        {player ? (
          <VideoView
            player={player}
            style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
            contentFit="contain"
            nativeControls={false}
            allowsPictureInPicture={false}
            pointerEvents="none"
          />
        ) : null}
      </Pressable>

      {/* Ã¢Å“â€¦ Always mounted. When hidden, touches pass through to the video */}
      <View
        style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
        pointerEvents={show ? "box-none" : "none"}
      >
        {/* dim overlay */}
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
            { backgroundColor: "rgba(0,0,0,0.42)", opacity: chromeAnim },
          ]}
          pointerEvents="none"
        />

        {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ CENTRE PLAY / SKIP (fade only) Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
        <Animated.View
          style={[fs.centerRow, { opacity: chromeAnim }]}
          pointerEvents="box-none"
        >
          <View
            style={[fs.centerInner, { gap: centerGap }]}
            pointerEvents="box-none"
          >
            <SpinSkipButton
              direction="backward"
              size={skipSize}
              onPress={() => {
                bump();
                onSkip(-10);
              }}
            />

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => {
                bump();
                handlePlayPause();
              }}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
              style={[
                fs.playBtn,
                {
                  width: playSize,
                  height: playSize,
                  borderRadius: playSize / 2,
                  backgroundColor: SKIP_BG,
                },
              ]}
            >
              {isPlaying ? (
                <PauseIcon size={isLandscape ? 68 : 66} color="#fff" />
              ) : (
                <PlayIcon size={isLandscape ? 62 : 60} color="#fff" />
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <SpinSkipButton
              direction="forward"
              size={skipSize}
              onPress={() => {
                bump();
                onSkip(10);
              }}
            />
          </View>
        </Animated.View>

        {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ TOP BAR: cross (left) + speaker (right) Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
        {/* Ã¢Å“â€¦ hides by moving UP + opacity 0 */}
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            fs.topRow,
            {
              top: insets.top + (isLandscape ? 8 : 12),
              left: insets.left + (isLandscape ? 18 : 28),
              right: insets.right + (isLandscape ? 18 : 28),
              opacity: chromeAnim,
              transform: [{ translateY: topTranslateY }],
            },
          ]}
          pointerEvents="box-none"
        >
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={onClose}
            style={fs.topBtn}
            hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <CloseIcon size={22} color="#fff" />
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => {
              bump();
              onToggleMute();
            }}
            style={fs.topBtn}
            hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            {muted ? (
              <Image
                source={MUTE_SPEAKER}
                style={{ width: 23, height: 23 }}
                resizeMode="contain"
                fadeDuration={0}
              />
            ) : (
              <VolumeIcon size={20} color="#fff" level={volume} />
            )}
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </Animated.View>

        {/* Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ BOTTOM: title, artist, progress, time Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ */}
        {/* Ã¢Å“â€¦ hides by moving DOWN + opacity 0 */}
        <ProgressSection
          isLandscape={isLandscape}
          insets={insets}
          title={title}
          artist={artist}
          onSeek={onSeek}
          bump={bump}
          chromeAnim={chromeAnim}
        />
      </View>
    </Animated.View>
  );
});

const fs = StyleSheet.create({
  // Ã¢Å“â€¦ bumped to 44Ãƒâ€”44 for easier tapping, matches the larger 28px icons
  topBtn: {
    width: 33,
    height: 33,
    borderRadius: 22,
    backgroundColor: SKIP_BG,
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
  },
  centerRow: {
    position: "absolute",

    left: 0,
    right: 0,

    justifyContent: "center",

  title: {

========== src\components\Header.tsx ==========
// src/components/Header.tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  Animated,
  Easing,
  Image,
  ScrollView,
} from "react-native";
import { useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import Svg, { Path } from "react-native-svg";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";
// ðŸ‘‡ Import global font family
import { APP_FONT_FAMILY } from "../utils/globalFont";

const DEFAULT_FONT = APP_FONT_FAMILY;
const PLUS_ICON = require("../../assets/icons/createplaylisticona.png");

const DEFAULT_COLORS = {
  background: "#000000",
  text: "#ffffff",
  textSecondary: "#98989e",
  primary: "#f92c47",
  surface: "#121216",
  border: "#22222a",
  popupColour: "#1c1c1e",
  isDark: true,
};

const MoreIcon = ({
  size = 22,
  color = "#000",
}: {
  size?: number;
  color?: string;
}) => (
  <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">
    <Path
      fill={color}
      d="M12 3C10.9 3 10 3.9 10 5C10 6.1 10.9 7 12 7C13.1 7 14 6.1 14 5C14 3.9 13.1 3 12 3ZM12 17C10.9 17 10 17.9 10 19C10 20.1 10.9 21 12 21C13.1 21 14 20.1 14 19C14 17.9 13.1 17 12 17ZM12 10C10.9 10 10 10.9 10 12C10 13.1 10.9 14 12 14C13.1 14 14 13.1 14 12C14 10.9 13.1 10 12 10Z"
    />
  </Svg>
);

type HeaderProps = {
  title?: string;
  scrollY: Animated.Value;
  onPlusPress?: () => void;
  fontFamily?: string;
  onEditPins?: () => void;
  onEditCategories?: () => void;
};

export default function Header({
  title = "Home",
  scrollY,
  onPlusPress,
  fontFamily = DEFAULT_FONT,
  onEditPins,
  onEditCategories,
}: HeaderProps) {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const themeRaw = useTheme() as any;
  const base =
    themeRaw && typeof themeRaw === "object" && themeRaw.background
      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : themeRaw?.theme && typeof themeRaw.theme === "object"
          ? themeRaw.theme
          : {};
  const COLORS = { ...DEFAULT_COLORS, ...base };

  const headerTitleOpacity = scrollY.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 30],
    outputRange: [1, 0],
    extrapolate: "clamp",
  });

  const headerBgOpacity = scrollY.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 40],
    outputRange: [1, 0],
    extrapolate: "clamp",
  });

  const headerTitleTranslateY = scrollY.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 30],
    outputRange: [0, -10],
    extrapolate: "clamp",
  });

  const [showMenu, setShowMenu] = useState(false);
  const dropdownScale = useRef(new Animated.Value(0.9)).current;
  const dropdownOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const menuClosing = useRef(false);

  const hasEditPins = typeof onEditPins === "function";
  const hasEditCategories = typeof onEditCategories === "function";
  const hasOptionsSection = hasEditPins || hasEditCategories;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!showMenu) return;
    menuClosing.current = false;
    dropdownScale.setValue(0.9);
    dropdownOpacity.setValue(0);
    Animated.parallel([
      Animated.timing(dropdownScale, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 200,
        easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
      Animated.timing(dropdownOpacity, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 180,
        easing: Easing.out(Easing.quad),
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
    ]).start();
  }, [showMenu, dropdownScale, dropdownOpacity]);

  const closeMenu = useCallback(
    (after?: () => void) => {
      if (menuClosing.current) return;
      menuClosing.current = true;
      Animated.parallel([
        Animated.timing(dropdownScale, {
          toValue: 0.94,
          duration: 140,
          easing: Easing.in(Easing.quad),
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
        Animated.timing(dropdownOpacity, {
          toValue: 0,
          duration: 140,
          easing: Easing.in(Easing.quad),
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
      ]).start(() => {
        menuClosing.current = false;
        setShowMenu(false);
        after?.();
      });
    },
    [dropdownScale, dropdownOpacity],
  );

  const navigateTo = useCallback(
    (name: string): boolean => {
      let n: any = nav;
      while (n) {
        const names: string[] | undefined = n.getState?.()?.routeNames;
        if (names && names.includes(name)) {
          n.navigate(name);
          return true;
        }
        n = n.getParent?.();
      }
      showToast(`"${name}" screen is not registered in the navigator`);
      return false;
    },
    [nav],
  );

  const runOrNavigate = useCallback(
    (handler: (() => void) | undefined, screenName: string) => {
      closeMenu(() => {
        if (handler) {
          handler();
          return;
        }
        navigateTo(screenName);
      });
    },
    [closeMenu, navigateTo],
  );

  const isDark = !!COLORS.isDark;

  // âœ… Glass background inside the buttons
  const glassBg = isDark ? "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.15)" : "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08)";
  const glassBorder = isDark
    ? "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.3)"
    : "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)";

  const dropdownBg = COLORS.popupColour ?? COLORS.surface;
  const dropdownBorder = COLORS.border;
  const dropdownShadow = isDark
    ? { color: "#000", opacity: 0.35, radius: 16, elevation: 18 }
    : { color: "#000", opacity: 0.12, radius: 12, elevation: 6 };

  return (
    <>
      <View style={styles.headerWrapper}>
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
            {
              backgroundColor: COLORS.background,
              opacity: headerBgOpacity,
              borderBottomWidth: 0,
              elevation: 0,
            },
          ]}
        />

        {/* ðŸ‘‡ BUG FIX: Replaced marginTop: 40 with dynamic insets.top */}
        <View style={[styles.header, { marginTop: insets.top + 10 }]}>
          <Animated.Text
            style={[
              styles.headerTitle,
              {
                color: COLORS.text,
                fontFamily,
                opacity: headerTitleOpacity,
                transform: [{ translateY: headerTitleTranslateY }],
              },
            ]}
          >
            {title}
          </Animated.Text>

          <View style={styles.headerRight}>
            {onPlusPress ? (
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={onPlusPress}
                style={[
                  styles.topIconBtn,
                  { backgroundColor: glassBg, borderColor: glassBorder },
                ]}
                hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
              >
                <Image
                  source={PLUS_ICON}
                  style={[styles.plusIconImage, { tintColor: COLORS.primary }]} 
                  resizeMode="contain"
                  fadeDuration={0}
                />
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ) : null}

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={() => setShowMenu(true)}
              style={[
                styles.headerBtn,
                { backgroundColor: glassBg, borderColor: glassBorder },
              ]}
              hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <MoreIcon size={22} color={COLORS.text} /> 
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      <Modal
        visible={showMenu}
        transparent
        animationType="none"
        statusBarTranslucent
        onRequestClose={() => closeMenu()}
      >
        <Pressable style={styles.dropdownBackdrop} onPress={() => closeMenu()}>
          <Animated.View
            style={[
              styles.dropdownCard,
              {
                top: insets.top + 50,
                right: 16,
                backgroundColor: dropdownBg,
                borderColor: dropdownBorder,
                opacity: dropdownOpacity,
                transform: [{ scale: dropdownScale }],
                transformOrigin: "top right",
                shadowColor: dropdownShadow.color,
                shadowOpacity: dropdownShadow.opacity,
                shadowRadius: dropdownShadow.radius,
                elevation: dropdownShadow.elevation,
              } as any,
            ]}
          >
            <ScrollView
              style={{ maxHeight: 400 }}
              showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
              bounces={false}
            >
              <Pressable onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                {hasOptionsSection && (
                  <>
                    {hasEditPins && (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        style={styles.dropdownRow}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                        onPress={() => runOrNavigate(onEditPins, "EditPins")}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={[styles.dropdownLabel, { color: COLORS.text }]}
                        >
                          Edit Pins
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}

                    {hasEditCategories && (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        style={styles.dropdownRow}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                        onPress={() =>
                          runOrNavigate(onEditCategories, "EditCategories")
                        }
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={[styles.dropdownLabel, { color: COLORS.text }]}
                        >
                          Edit Categories
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}

                    <View
                      style={[
                        styles.divider,
                        { backgroundColor: COLORS.border },
                      ]}
                    />
                  </>
                )}

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.dropdownRow}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={() => closeMenu(() => navigateTo("Settings"))}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.dropdownLabel, { color: COLORS.text }]}>
                    Settings
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.dropdownRow}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={() => closeMenu(() => navigateTo("Account"))}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.dropdownLabel, { color: COLORS.text }]}>
                    Account
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </Pressable>
            </ScrollView>
          </Animated.View>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>
    </>
  );
}

// src/components/LibraryPageHeaders.tsx
import React, { useMemo } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Image,
  TextInput,
} from "react-native";

const BACK_ICON = require("../../assets/icons/backicon.png");
const FONT = "sans-serif";

const DEFAULT_COLORS = {
  background: "#1c1c1e",
  surface: "#434446",
  border: "#3f4042",
  text: "#ffffff",
  textSecondary: "#a1a1aa",
  textMuted: "#71717a",
  primary: "#f92c47",
};

// â”€â”€ Helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const isLightHex = (raw: string | null | undefined): boolean => {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== "string") return false;
  let h = raw.trim().replace(/^#/, "");
  if (h.length === 3) h = h.split("").map((c) => c + c).join("");
  if (h.length === 8) h = h.slice(0, 6);
  if (h.length !== 6) return false;
  const r = parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16);
  const b = parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16);
  if (Number.isNaN(r) || Number.isNaN(g) || Number.isNaN(b)) return false;
  const lum = (0.299 * r + 0.587 * g + 0.114 * b) / 255;
  return lum > 0.55;
};

const deriveIsDark = (C: any): boolean => {
  if (typeof C?.isDark === "boolean") return C.isDark;
  if (C?.mode === "light") return false;
  if (C?.mode === "dark") return true;
  return !isLightHex(C?.background ?? C?.surface);
};

// â”€â”€ Types â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export type HeaderAction = {
  key?: string;
  icon?: any;
  isText?: boolean;
  text?: string;
  iconStyle?: any;
  onPress: () => void;
};

type Props = {
  colors?: any;
  topInset?: number;
  showBack?: boolean;
  onBack?: () => void;
  actions?: HeaderAction[];
  title: string;
  showSearch?: boolean;
  searchValue?: string;
  onSearchChange?: (text: string) => void;
  searchPlaceholder?: string;
  transparent?: boolean;
};

// â”€â”€ Glass Icon Button â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export const GlassIconBtn = ({
  icon,
  onPress,
  isText,
  text,
  iconStyle,
  colors,
}: any) => {
  const C = colors || DEFAULT_COLORS;
  const isDark = useMemo(() => deriveIsDark(C), [C]);
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(C, isDark), [C, isDark]);

  // ðŸ‘‡ 1. Strip out any tintColor from iconStyle to ensure it doesn't override our theme
  const safeIconStyle = { ...(iconStyle || {}) };
  delete safeIconStyle.tintColor;

  // ðŸ‘‡ 2. Force the theme color (White for Dark Mode, Black for Light Mode)
  const iconColor = isDark ? "#ffffff" : "#000000";
  const iconSize = safeIconStyle.width || 18;

  // ðŸ‘‡ 3. Safely render Text, SVG Components, React Elements, or PNG Images
  const renderContent = () => {
    if (isText) {
      return <Text style={[styles.glassIconText, { color: iconColor }]}>{text}</Text>;
    }
    
    // Handle custom SVG components passed as functions (e.g., ShareSvg)
    if (typeof icon === "function") {
      const IconComponent = icon;
      return <IconComponent color={iconColor} size={iconSize} {...safeIconStyle} />;
    }
    
    // Handle JSX passed directly
    if (React.isValidElement(icon)) {
      return React.cloneElement(icon as React.ReactElement<any>, { 
        color: iconColor, 
        size: iconSize, 
        ...safeIconStyle 
      });
    }

    // Handle regular PNG Images (require)
    return (
      <Image
        source={icon}
        style={[styles.glassIconImg, safeIconStyle, { tintColor: iconColor }]}
        resizeMode="contain"
      />
    );
  };

  return (
    <TouchableOpacity
      onPress={onPress}
      style={styles.glassIconBtn}
      activeOpacity={0.7}
    >
      {renderContent()}
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );
};

// â”€â”€ Library Page Header â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export default function LibraryPageHeaders({
  colors,
  topInset = 0,
  showBack = true,
  onBack,
  actions = [],
  title,
  showSearch = true,
  searchValue = "",
  onSearchChange,
  searchPlaceholder = "Search",
  transparent = false,
}: Props) {
  const C = useMemo(() => ({ ...DEFAULT_COLORS, ...(colors || {}) }), [colors]);
  const isDark = useMemo(() => deriveIsDark(C), [C]);
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(C, isDark), [C, isDark]);

  const hasTitle = !!(title && title.trim());

  if (transparent) {
    return (
      <View
        pointerEvents="box-none"
        style={[styles.floatingBar, { paddingTop: topInset + 8 }]}
      >
        {showBack ? (
          <GlassIconBtn
            colors={C}
            icon={BACK_ICON}
            iconStyle={{ width: 16, height: 16 }}
            onPress={onBack || (() => {})}
          />
        ) : (
          <View style={{ width: 38 }} />
        )}

        <View style={styles.topRight}>
          {actions.map((a, i) => (
            <GlassIconBtn
              key={a.key || `action_${i}`}
              colors={C}
              icon={a.icon}
              isText={a.isText}
              text={a.text}
              iconStyle={a.iconStyle}
              onPress={a.onPress}
            />
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  }

  return (
    <>
      <View style={[styles.topBar, { paddingTop: topInset + 8 }]}>
        {showBack ? (
          <GlassIconBtn
            colors={C}
            icon={BACK_ICON}
            iconStyle={{ width: 16, height: 16 }}
            onPress={onBack || (() => {})}
          />
        ) : (
          <View style={{ width: 38 }} />
        )}

        <View style={styles.topRight}>
          {actions.map((a, i) => (
            <GlassIconBtn
              key={a.key || `action_${i}`}
              colors={C}
              icon={a.icon}
              isText={a.isText}
              text={a.text}
              iconStyle={a.iconStyle}
              onPress={a.onPress}
            />
          ))}
        </View>
      </View>

      {(hasTitle || showSearch) && (
        <View style={styles.stickyHeader}>
          {hasTitle ? <Text style={styles.title}>{title}</Text> : null}

          {showSearch && (
            <View style={styles.searchWrap}>
              <TextInput
                value={searchValue}
                onChangeText={onSearchChange}
                placeholder={searchPlaceholder}
                placeholderTextColor={C.textSecondary}
                style={styles.searchInput}
                returnKeyType="search"
                autoCapitalize="none"
                autoCorrect={false}
                selectionColor={C.primary}
              />
              {searchValue.length > 0 && (
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => onSearchChange?.("")}>
                  <Text style={styles.clearIcon}>âœ•</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
            </View>
          )}
        </View>
      )}
    </>
  );
}

========== src\components\Loader.tsx ==========
// src/components/Loader.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import {
  Animated,
  Easing,
  StyleSheet,
  View,
  Text,
  ViewStyle,
  StyleProp,
} from "react-native";
import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";

// â”€â”€ Circular Rotating Loader â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export const CircularLoader = ({
  color,
  size = 36,
  thickness = 3,
}: {
  color?: string;
  size?: number;
  thickness?: number;
}) => {
  const theme = useTheme();
  const rotate = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;

  useEffect(() => {
    const loop = Animated.loop(
      Animated.timing(rotate, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 800,
        easing: Easing.linear,
        useNativeDriver: true,
      })
    );
    loop.start();
    return () => loop.stop();
  }, [rotate]);

  const spin = rotate.interpolate({
    inputRange: [0, 1],
    outputRange: ["0deg", "360deg"],
  });

  const loaderColor = color || theme.primary;

  return (
    <Animated.View
      style={{
        width: size,
        height: size,
        borderRadius: size / 2,
        borderWidth: thickness,
        borderColor: loaderColor,
        borderTopColor: "transparent",
        transform: [{ rotate: spin }],
      }}
    />
  );
};

// â”€â”€ Inline Loader (just the spinner) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export function InlineLoader({
  style,
  color,
  size,
}: {
  style?: StyleProp<ViewStyle>;
  color?: string;
  size?: number;
}) {
  return (
    <View style={[{ alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" }, style]}>
      <CircularLoader color={color} size={size} />
    </View>
  );
}

// â”€â”€ Screen Loader â€” centered overlay, transparent background â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export function ScreenLoader({
  visible,
  label,
  color,
  size = 36,
  blocker = false,
}: {
  visible: boolean;
  label?: string;
  color?: string;
  size?: number;
  blocker?: boolean;
}) {
  const theme = useTheme();

  if (!visible) return null;

  return (
    <View
      pointerEvents={blocker ? "auto" : "none"}
      style={styles.screenOverlay}
    >
      <View style={styles.loaderContainer}>
        <CircularLoader color={color || theme.primary} size={size} />
        {label ? (
          <Text style={[styles.label, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            {label}
          </Text>
        ) : null}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

// â”€â”€ Full-Screen Loader â€” dims entire screen, transparent loader bg â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export function FullScreenLoader({
  visible,
  label,
  color,
  size = 36,
  backdrop = "rgba(0,0,0,0.4)",
}: {
  visible: boolean;
  label?: string;
  color?: string;
  size?: number;
  backdrop?: string;
}) {
  const theme = useTheme();

  if (!visible) return null;

  return (
    <View style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, styles.fullOverlay]}>
      <View style={[StyleSheet.absoluteFill, { backgroundColor: backdrop }]} />
      <View style={styles.loaderContainer}>
        <CircularLoader color={color || theme.primary} size={size} />
        {label ? (
          <Text style={[styles.label, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            {label}
          </Text>
        ) : null}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

// â”€â”€ Styles â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  screenOverlay: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    zIndex: 9999,
    elevation: 9999,
  },
  fullOverlay: {
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    zIndex: 9999,
    elevation: 9999,
  },
  // âœ… No background color, no border
  loaderContainer: {
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
  },
  label: {
    fontSize: 13,
    marginTop: 12,
    textAlign: "center",
    fontWeight: "500",
  },
});

export default ScreenLoader;

========== src\components\MiniPlayer.tsx ==========
// src/components/MiniPlayer.tsx
//
// UNIFIED mini player for BOTH songs and videos.
//
// Entrance: the mini player slides up from BEHIND the tab bar the first
// time content appears, and again after content was cleared. While the
// entrance animation is running the overlay is clipped at the tab bar's
// top edge (overflow hidden), so nothing draws over the tab bar.
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  TouchableOpacity,
  StyleSheet,
  Image,
  Animated,
  PanResponder,
  Dimensions,
  ImageStyle,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  Share,
  BackHandler,
  LayoutChangeEvent,
  StatusBar,
  Easing,
  Platform,
} from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { openAlbumDetails } from "../navigation/navigationService";
import { getColors } from "react-native-image-colors";
import { VideoView } from "expo-video";
import * as ScreenOrientation from "expo-screen-orientation";

// NOTE: the glass settings (blur, tint, shadow, ...) now live in the theme
// file (src/hooks/useTheme.ts â†’ theme.glass), shared with the root navbar.
// Change the glass look there.
function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

const MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG = require("../../assets/images/musicdefault.png");

const MUSIC_DEFAULT_SRC: string | null = (() => {
  try {
    const src = Image.resolveAssetSource(MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG);
    return typeof src?.uri === "string" ? src.uri : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
})();

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Default song artwork
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const DefaultSongArt = React.memo(function DefaultSongArt({
  size,
}: {
  size: number;
}) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = React.useState(false);
  const theme = useTheme() as any;

  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";
  const bgColor = isDark ? "#1c1c1e" : "#bcbcc4";
  const textColor = isDark ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.7)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)";

  return (
    <View
      style={{
        width: size,
        height: size,
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
        overflow: "hidden",
      }}
    >
      {!failed ? (
        <Image
          source={MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG}
          style={{ width: size, height: size }}
          resizeMode="cover"
          fadeDuration={0}
          onError={(e) => {
            console.log("[DefaultSongArt] PNG failed to load:", e.nativeEvent);
            setFailed(true);
          }}
        />
      ) : (
        <Text
          style={{ color: textColor, fontSize: size * 0.5, fontWeight: "700" }}
        >
          â™ª
        </Text>
      )}
    </View>
  );
});

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Artwork helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const normalizeArtwork = (raw: any): string | null => {
  if (!raw || typeof raw !== "string") return null;
  const t = raw.trim();
  if (!t) return null;
  const lower = t.toLowerCase();
  if (
    lower === "null" ||
    lower === "undefined" ||
    lower.endsWith("/null") ||
    lower.endsWith("/undefined") ||
    lower === "file://" ||
    lower === "file:///"
  ) {
    return null;
  }
  if (
    t.startsWith("http") ||
    t.startsWith("file://") ||
    t.startsWith("content://") ||
    t.startsWith("ph://") ||
    t.startsWith("data:")
  ) {
    return t;
  }
  return `file://${t}`;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SmoothArt
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const SmoothArt = React.memo(function SmoothArt({
  uri,
  size,
}: {
  uri: string | null;
  size: number;
}) {
  const [shown, setShown] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [bad, setBad] = useState<Record<string, true>>({});
  const [slow, setSlow] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!uri) setShown(null);
  }, [uri]);

  const markBad = useCallback((u: string) => {
    console.log("[SmoothArt] artwork failed, using default:", u);
    setBad((p) => (p[u] ? p : { ...p, [u]: true }));
  }, []);

  const uriOk = !!uri && !bad[uri];
  const baseUri = uriOk && shown && !bad[shown] ? shown : null;
  const loadingUri = uriOk && uri !== shown ? uri : null;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loadingUri) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => setSlow(loadingUri), 1200);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [loadingUri]);

  const showDefault = !baseUri && (!uriOk || slow === uri);

  const fill = {
    position: "absolute",
    left: 0,
    top: 0,
    width: size,
    height: size,
  } as const;

  return (
    <View style={{ width: size, height: size, overflow: "hidden" }}>
      {baseUri ? (
        <Image
          source={{ uri: baseUri }}
          style={fill}
          resizeMode="cover"
          fadeDuration={0}
          onError={() => markBad(baseUri)}
        />
      ) : showDefault ? (
        <DefaultSongArt size={size} />
      ) : (
        <View style={[fill, { backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.08)" }]} />
      )}

      {loadingUri ? (
        <Image
          key={loadingUri}
          source={{ uri: loadingUri }}
          style={fill}
          resizeMode="cover"
          fadeDuration={0}
          onLoad={() => setShown(loadingUri)}
          onError={() => {
            markBad(loadingUri);
            setShown(null);
          }}
        />
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
});
