} from "react-native";

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// useSleepTimer â€” counts down and calls onExpire (e.g. pause the song)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export const useSleepTimer = (onExpire: () => void) => {
  const [endAt, setEndAt] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [remainingSec, setRemainingSec] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const onExpireRef = useRef(onExpire);
  onExpireRef.current = onExpire;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (endAt == null) {
      setRemainingSec(null);
      return;
    }
    const tick = () => {
      const left = Math.round((endAt - Date.now()) / 1000);
      if (left <= 0) {
        setEndAt(null);
        setRemainingSec(null);
        onExpireRef.current();
      } else {
        setRemainingSec(left);
      }
    };
    tick();
    const id = setInterval(tick, 1000);
    return () => clearInterval(id);
  }, [endAt]);

  const start = useCallback((minutes: number) => {
    setEndAt(Date.now() + minutes * 60 * 1000);
  }, []);
  const cancel = useCallback(() => setEndAt(null), []);

  return { remainingSec, isActive: endAt != null, start, cancel };
};

export const formatTimer = (sec: number) => {
  const h = Math.floor(sec / 3600);
  const m = Math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60);
  const s = sec % 60;
  const mm = String(m).padStart(2, "0");
  const ss = String(s).padStart(2, "0");
  return h > 0 ? `${h}:${mm}:${ss}` : `${mm}:${ss}`;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Wheel â€” position is an Animated.Value (in "rows"), driven by ONE pan
// responder that covers the whole screen. Rendering is 100% native-driven, so
// dragging up / down anywhere is smooth.
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const ITEM_H = 48;
const VISIBLE = 5; // odd number, centre row is the selected one
const WHEEL_H = ITEM_H * VISIBLE;
const PAD = ITEM_H * Math.floor(VISIBLE / 2);

const DRAG_SENSITIVITY = 1.1; // rows moved per row-height of finger travel
const MOMENTUM_MS = 380; // how far a flick glides
const RUBBER = 0.35; // resistance past the first / last row

type WheelColors = {
  surface: string;
  text: string;
  textSecondary: string;
  border: string;
};

type Ctrl = {
  pos: Animated.Value;
  posRef: { current: number };
  indexRef: { current: number };
  count: number;
  def: number;
  reset: () => void;
};

const useWheelCtrl = (count: number, def: number): Ctrl => {
  const ref = useRef<Ctrl | null>(null);
  if (!ref.current) {
    const pos = new Animated.Value(def);
    const c: Ctrl = {
      pos,
      posRef: { current: def },
      indexRef: { current: def },
      count,
      def,
      reset: () => {
        pos.stopAnimation();
        pos.setValue(def);
        c.posRef.current = def;
        c.indexRef.current = def;
      },
    };
    ref.current = c;
  }
  return ref.current;
};

const clampIdx = (n: number, count: number) =>
  Math.max(0, Math.min(count - 1, Math.round(n)));

const rubber = (v: number, max: number) => {
  if (v < 0) return v * RUBBER;
  if (v > max) return max + (v - max) * RUBBER;
  return v;
};

const Wheel = ({
  ctrl,
  data,
  format,
  colors,
}: {
  ctrl: Ctrl;
  data: number[];
  format: (n: number) => string;
  colors: WheelColors;
}) => {
  const translateY = Animated.multiply(ctrl.pos, -ITEM_H);

  return (
    <View style={s.wheelWrap}>
      <View
        pointerEvents="none"
        style={[s.selection, { top: PAD, borderColor: colors.border }]}
      />
      <View style={{ height: WHEEL_H, overflow: "hidden" }} pointerEvents="none">
        <Animated.View
          style={{ paddingTop: PAD, transform: [{ translateY }] }}
        >
          {data.map((v, i) => {
            const range = [i - 2, i - 1, i, i + 1, i + 2];
            const opacity = ctrl.pos.interpolate({
              inputRange: range,
              outputRange: [0.15, 0.4, 1, 0.4, 0.15],
              extrapolate: "clamp",
            });
            const scale = ctrl.pos.interpolate({
              inputRange: range,
              outputRange: [0.7, 0.85, 1.2, 0.85, 0.7],
              extrapolate: "clamp",
            });
            return (
              <View key={v} style={s.item}>
                <Animated.Text
                  style={[
                    s.itemText,
                    { color: colors.text, opacity, transform: [{ scale }] },
                  ]}
                >
                  {format(v)}
                </Animated.Text>
              </View>
            );
          })}
        </Animated.View>
      </View>
    </View>
  );
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// SleepTimerSheet
//   â€¢ Preset view : wheel 5 min â†’ 1 hr, "Start Timer", "Custom" button
//   â€¢ Custom view : hours + minutes wheels, "Continue" sets the timer
//   â€¢ Drag up / down ANYWHERE on the screen to change the value
//     (custom view: left half = hours, right half = minutes)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const PRESET_MINUTES: number[] = Array.from({ length: 12 }, (_, i) => (i + 1) * 5); // 5..60
const PRESET_DEFAULT = PRESET_MINUTES.indexOf(30);

const HOURS: number[] = Array.from({ length: 13 }, (_, i) => i); // 0..12
const MINS: number[] = Array.from({ length: 60 }, (_, i) => i); // 0..59

const presetLabel = (min: number) => (min === 60 ? "1 hr" : `${min} min`);

type Props = {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  onStart: (minutes: number) => void;
  onCancelTimer: () => void;
  remainingSec: number | null;
  colors: WheelColors;
};

export const SleepTimerSheet = ({
  visible,
  onClose,
  onStart,
  onCancelTimer,
  remainingSec,
  colors,
}: Props) => {
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<"preset" | "custom">("preset");
  const [zeroError, setZeroError] = useState(false);
  const modeRef = useRef<"preset" | "custom">("preset");
  modeRef.current = mode;

  const presetCtrl = useWheelCtrl(PRESET_MINUTES.length, PRESET_DEFAULT);
  const hoursCtrl = useWheelCtrl(HOURS.length, 0);
  const minsCtrl = useWheelCtrl(MINS.length, 30);

  // always reopen on the preset view with default values
  useEffect(() => {
    if (visible) {
      setMode("preset");
      setZeroError(false);
      presetCtrl.reset();
      hoursCtrl.reset();
      minsCtrl.reset();
    }
  }, [visible, presetCtrl, hoursCtrl, minsCtrl]);

  // ---- one pan responder for the whole screen -----------------------------
  const activeRef = useRef<Ctrl>(presetCtrl);
  const startPosRef = useRef(0);
  const baseDyRef = useRef(0);

  const settle = (c: Ctrl, vy: number) => {
    const cur = c.posRef.current;
    const extra = (-vy * MOMENTUM_MS) / ITEM_H;
    const target = clampIdx(cur + extra, c.count);
    c.indexRef.current = target;
    c.posRef.current = target;
    const dist = Math.abs(target - cur);
    Animated.timing(c.pos, {
      toValue: target,
      duration: Math.min(900, 220 + dist * 40),
      easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
      useNativeDriver: true,
    }).start();
  };

  const pan = useRef(
    PanResponder.create({
      onStartShouldSetPanResponder: () => false,
      onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: (_, g) =>
        Math.abs(g.dy) > 4 && Math.abs(g.dy) > Math.abs(g.dx),
      onMoveShouldSetPanResponder: (_, g) =>
        Math.abs(g.dy) > 4 && Math.abs(g.dy) > Math.abs(g.dx),
      onPanResponderTerminationRequest: () => false,

      onPanResponderGrant: (_, g) => {
        let c = presetCtrl;
        if (modeRef.current === "custom") {
          const half = Dimensions.get("window").width / 2;
          c = g.x0 < half ? hoursCtrl : minsCtrl;
        }
        activeRef.current = c;
        baseDyRef.current = g.dy;
        startPosRef.current = c.posRef.current;
        // catch the wheel mid-glide
        c.pos.stopAnimation((v) => {
          c.posRef.current = v;
          startPosRef.current = v;
        });
      },

      onPanResponderMove: (_, g) => {
        const c = activeRef.current;
        const raw =
          startPosRef.current -
          ((g.dy - baseDyRef.current) / ITEM_H) * DRAG_SENSITIVITY;
        const v = rubber(raw, c.count - 1);
        c.pos.setValue(v);
        c.posRef.current = v;
        c.indexRef.current = clampIdx(v, c.count);
      },

      onPanResponderRelease: (_, g) => settle(activeRef.current, g.vy),
      onPanResponderTerminate: () => settle(activeRef.current, 0),
    }),
  ).current;

  const active = remainingSec != null;

  const handleContinue = () => {
    const total =
      HOURS[hoursCtrl.indexRef.current] * 60 + MINS[minsCtrl.indexRef.current];
    if (total <= 0) {
      setZeroError(true);
      return;
    }
    onStart(total);
    onClose();
  };

  return (
    <Modal
      visible={visible}
      transparent
      animationType="slide"
      onRequestClose={onClose}
    >
      {/* the pan handlers cover the whole screen: drag anywhere */}
      <View style={s.fill} {...pan.panHandlers}>
        <Pressable style={s.backdrop} onPress={onClose}>
          <Pressable
            style={[s.sheet, { backgroundColor: colors.surface }]}
            onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <View
              style={[s.handle, { backgroundColor: colors.textSecondary }]}
            />

            {mode === "preset" ? (
              <>
                <Text style={[s.title, { color: colors.text }]}>
                  Sleep Timer
                </Text>

                {active && (
                  <View style={s.activeBox}>
                    <Text
                      style={[s.activeLabel, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}
                    >
                      Music stops in
                    </Text>
                    <Text style={[s.activeTime, { color: colors.text }]}>
                      {formatTimer(remainingSec as number)}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                )}

                <Text style={[s.hint, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                  Drag up or down anywhere to set the time
                </Text>

                <Wheel
                  ctrl={presetCtrl}
                  data={PRESET_MINUTES}
                  format={presetLabel}
                  colors={colors}
                />

                <TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.8}
                  style={[s.primaryBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.text }]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    onStart(PRESET_MINUTES[presetCtrl.indexRef.current]);
                    onClose();
                  }}
                >
                  <Text style={[s.primaryText, { color: colors.surface }]}>
                    {active ? "Update Timer" : "Start Timer"}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.8}
                  style={[s.outlineBtn, { borderColor: colors.border }]}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setZeroError(false);
                    setMode("custom");
                  }}
                >
                  <Text style={[s.outlineText, { color: colors.text }]}>
                    Custom
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                {active && (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    activeOpacity={0.7}
                    style={s.secondaryBtn}
                    onPress={() => {
                      onCancelTimer();
                      onClose();
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text style={s.cancelText}>Turn Off Timer</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <View style={s.customHeader}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => setMode("preset")}
                    hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
                    style={s.backBtn}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[s.backText, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}
                    >
                      â€¹ Back
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                  <Text style={[s.title, { color: colors.text }]}>
                    Custom Timer
                  </Text>
                  <View style={s.backBtn} />
                </View>

                <Text style={[s.hint, { color: colors.textSecondary }]}>
                  Drag the left side for hours, the right side for minutes
                </Text>

                <View style={s.customRow}>
                  <View style={s.customCol}>
                    <Wheel
                      ctrl={hoursCtrl}
                      data={HOURS}
                      format={(n) => `${n} hr`}
                      colors={colors}
                    />
                  </View>
                  <View style={s.customCol}>
                    <Wheel
                      ctrl={minsCtrl}
                      data={MINS}
                      format={(n) => `${n} min`}
                      colors={colors}
                    />
                  </View>
                </View>

                {zeroError && (
                  <Text style={s.errorText}>Choose at least 1 minute</Text>
                )}

                <TouchableOpacity
                  activeOpacity={0.8}
                  style={[s.primaryBtn, { backgroundColor: colors.text }]}
                  onPress={handleContinue}
                >
                  <Text style={[s.primaryText, { color: colors.surface }]}>
                    Continue
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </>
            )}
          </Pressable>
        </Pressable>
      </View>
    </Modal>
  );
};

const s = StyleSheet.create({
  fill: { flex: 1 },
  backdrop: { flex: 1, justifyContent: "flex-end" },
  sheet: {
    borderTopLeftRadius: 24,
    borderTopRightRadius: 24,
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    paddingTop: 12,
    paddingBottom: 32,
  },
  handle: {
    width: 40,
    height: 4,
    borderRadius: 2,
    alignSelf: "center",
    marginBottom: 16,
    opacity: 0.5,
  },
  title: { fontSize: 18, fontWeight: "700", textAlign: "center" },
  hint: { fontSize: 13, textAlign: "center", marginTop: 6, marginBottom: 8 },
  activeBox: { alignItems: "center", marginTop: 12 },
  activeLabel: { fontSize: 12 },
  activeTime: { fontSize: 28, fontWeight: "700", marginTop: 2 },

  wheelWrap: { height: WHEEL_H, justifyContent: "center" },
  selection: {
    position: "absolute",
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    height: ITEM_H,
    borderTopWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
    borderBottomWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
  },
  item: { height: ITEM_H, alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },
  itemText: { textAlign: "center", fontSize: 22, fontWeight: "600" },

  customHeader: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "space-between",
  },
  backBtn: { width: 64 },
  backText: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: "500" },
  customRow: { flexDirection: "row", gap: 12 },
  customCol: { flex: 1 },
  errorText: {
    color: "#f92c47",
    fontSize: 13,
    textAlign: "center",
    marginTop: 10,
  },

  primaryBtn: {
    marginTop: 16,
    borderRadius: 14,
    paddingVertical: 14,
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  primaryText: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: "700" },
  outlineBtn: {
    marginTop: 10,
    borderRadius: 14,
    paddingVertical: 13,
    alignItems: "center",
    borderWidth: 1,
  },
  outlineText: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: "600" },
  secondaryBtn: { marginTop: 8, paddingVertical: 12, alignItems: "center" },
  cancelText: { color: "#f92c47", fontSize: 15, fontWeight: "600" },
});

========== src\components\TopStatusGlass.tsx ==========
// src/components/TopStatusGlass.tsx
import React from "react";
import { StyleSheet, View, useWindowDimensions } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import Svg, {
  Defs,
  LinearGradient as SvgLinearGradient,
  Stop,
  Rect,
} from "react-native-svg";
import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";

const DEBUG_SHOW_RED = false;

// ===========================================================
// âœ… CHANGE THE LOOK HERE
// ===========================================================
const LOOK = {
  light: {
    color: "#ffffff",
    topOpacity: 0.85,
    midOpacity: 0.45,
    fadeExtra: 65, // ðŸ‘ˆ INCREASED from 26 to 65 (makes it spread further down)
    shadowHeight: 0, 
    shadowOpacity: 0.14,
  },
  dark: {
    color: "#000000",
    topOpacity: 0.92,
    midOpacity: 0.55,
    fadeExtra: 65, // ðŸ‘ˆ INCREASED from 18 to 65 (makes it spread further down)
    shadowHeight: 0, 
    shadowOpacity: 0.55,
  },
};

export default function TopStatusGlass() {
  const theme = useTheme();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { width } = useWindowDimensions(); 
  const look = theme.isDark ? LOOK.dark : LOOK.light;

  const topInset = Math.max(insets.top, 24);
  const color = DEBUG_SHOW_RED ? "#ff0000" : look.color;
  const topOpacity = DEBUG_SHOW_RED ? 1 : look.topOpacity;
  const midOpacity = DEBUG_SHOW_RED ? 1 : look.midOpacity;

  const fadeHeight = topInset + look.fadeExtra;
  const midStop = topInset / fadeHeight; 
  const totalHeight = fadeHeight + look.shadowHeight;

  return (
    <View
      pointerEvents="none"
      style={[styles.wrap, { height: totalHeight, width }]}
    >
      {/* wash / fade behind the status bar */}
      <Svg width={width} height={fadeHeight} style={styles.layer}>
        <Defs>
          <SvgLinearGradient id="topFade" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
            <Stop offset="0" stopColor={color} stopOpacity={topOpacity} />
            <Stop offset={midStop} stopColor={color} stopOpacity={midOpacity} />
            <Stop offset="1" stopColor={color} stopOpacity={0} />
          </SvgLinearGradient>
        </Defs>
        <Rect
          x="0"
          y="0"
          width={width}
          height={fadeHeight}
          fill="url(#topFade)"
        />
      </Svg>

      {/* extra soft shadow just under the status bar */}
      {look.shadowHeight > 0 ? (
        <Svg
          width={width}
          height={look.shadowHeight}
          style={[styles.layer, { top: topInset }]}
        >
          <Defs>
            <SvgLinearGradient id="topShadow" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1">
              <Stop
                offset="0"
                stopColor="#000000"
                stopOpacity={look.shadowOpacity}
              />
              <Stop
                offset="0.45"
                stopColor="#000000"
                stopOpacity={look.shadowOpacity * 0.35}
              />
              <Stop offset="1" stopColor="#000000" stopOpacity={0} />
            </SvgLinearGradient>
          </Defs>
          <Rect
            x="0"
            y="0"
            width={width}
            height={look.shadowHeight}
            fill="url(#topShadow)"
          />
        </Svg>
      ) : null}
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  wrap: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    zIndex: 1100, 
    elevation: 1100, 
  },
  layer: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
  },
});

========== src\components\VideoPlayerHost.tsx ==========
// src/components/VideoPlayerHost.tsx
//
// HEADLESS video engine. It no longer draws any UI (the mini player, the
// expanded sheet, the options sheet and the fullscreen player now live in
// MiniPlayer.tsx). This component only:
//   - owns the expo-video player
//   - loads / plays the current video from useVideoStore
//   - keeps music and video from playing at the same time
//   - publishes live state + controls to useVideoBridge for MiniPlayer
//
// Keep it mounted exactly where it was mounted before.
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { useVideoPlayer } from "expo-video";
import { useEvent, useEventListener } from "expo";

import { useAudioPlayer } from "../hooks/useAudioPlayer";
import { usePlayerStore } from "../store/playerStore";
import { useVideoStore } from "../store/videoStore";
import { useVideoBridge } from "../store/videoPlayerBridge";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";

// Position / duration go straight to the bridge so this component does not
// re-render on every time update.
const pushPosition = (n: number) => {
  if (useVideoBridge.getState().position !== n) {
    useVideoBridge.setState({ position: n });
  }
};
const pushDuration = (n: number) => {
  if (useVideoBridge.getState().duration !== n) {
    useVideoBridge.setState({ duration: n });
  }
};

export default function VideoPlayerHost() {
  const current = useVideoStore((s) => s.current);
  const playToken = useVideoStore((s) => s.playToken);
  const nextVideo = useVideoStore((s) => s.next);
  const closeVideo = useVideoStore((s) => s.close);
  const loadFavs = useVideoStore((s) => s.loadFavs);

  const { playPause: musicPlayPause } = useAudioPlayer() as any;
  const musicPlaying = usePlayerStore((s) => s.isPlaying);
  const muted = useVideoBridge((s) => s.muted);

  const musicToggleRef = useRef(musicPlayPause);
  musicToggleRef.current = musicPlayPause;

  const player = useVideoPlayer(null as any, (p: any) => {
    p.loop = false;
    p.timeUpdateEventInterval = 0.5;
    p.staysActiveInBackground = true;
    try {
      p.bufferOptions = { minBufferForPlayback: 0.5 };
    } catch {}
  });

  const { isPlaying } = useEvent(player, "playingChange", {
    isPlaying: player.playing,
  }) as { isPlaying: boolean };

  const [starting, setStarting] = useState(false);
  const uiPlaying = isPlaying || starting;

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isPlaying) setStarting(false);
  }, [isPlaying]);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadFavs();
  }, [loadFavs]);

  // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ publish state to the bridge Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
  useEffect(() => {
    useVideoBridge.setState({ player });
    return () => useVideoBridge.setState({ player: null });
  }, [player]);

  useEffect(() => {
    useVideoBridge.setState({ isPlaying: uiPlaying });
  }, [uiPlaying]);

  // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ player events Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
  useEventListener(player, "timeUpdate", (e: any) => {
    pushPosition(e?.currentTime ?? 0);
    const d = player.duration;
    if (d > 0) pushDuration(d);
  });

  useEventListener(player, "statusChange", (e: any) => {
    if (e?.status === "readyToPlay") {
      pushDuration(player.duration || 0);
    } else if (e?.status === "error") {
      showToast("Video failed to load");
    }
  });

  useEventListener(player, "playToEnd", () => {
    const { queue: q } = useVideoStore.getState();
    if (q.length > 1) {
      nextVideo();
    } else {
      try {
        player.currentTime = 0;
        player.pause();
      } catch {}
      pushPosition(0);
    }
  });

  // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ music / video never play together Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!current) return;
    if (usePlayerStore.getState().isPlaying) {
      void musicToggleRef.current?.();
    }
  }, [current?.url, playToken]);


  useEffect(() => {
    if (musicPlaying && useVideoStore.getState().current) {
      closeVideo();
    }
  }, [musicPlaying]);

  // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ load the current video Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!current) {
      try {
        player.pause();
      } catch {}
      pushPosition(0);
      pushDuration(0);
      setStarting(false);
      return;
    }
    pushPosition(0);
    pushDuration(0);
    setStarting(true);
    const startingTimer = setTimeout(() => setStarting(false), 8000);
    let cancelled = false;

    try {
      const p: any = player;
      const replace = p.replaceAsync ?? p.replace;

      const loading = replace.call(p, {
        uri: current.url,
        metadata: {
          title: current.title,
          artist: current.artist,
          artwork: current.thumbnail,
        },
      });

      Promise.resolve(loading)
        .then(() => {
          if (cancelled) return;
          // Keep playback start in one place, after the source is ready.
          // Do not toggle music here: the current-video effect handles it once.
          try {
            p.muted = useVideoBridge.getState().muted;
            p.play();
          } catch (error) {
            console.warn("[VideoPlayerHost] play failed:", error);
          }
        })
        .catch((e: any) => {
          if (cancelled) return;
          console.log("[VideoPlayerHost] load error:", e);
          setStarting(false);
          showToast("Video failed to load");
        });
    } catch (e) {
      console.log("[VideoPlayerHost] load error:", e);
      setStarting(false);
      showToast("Video failed to load");
    }

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      clearTimeout(startingTimer);
    };
  }, [current?.url, playToken]);

  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      player.muted = muted;
    } catch {}
  }, [muted, player, current?.url]);

  // Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬ controls (registered in the bridge) Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬Ã¢â€â‚¬
  const seekTo = useCallback(
    (sec: number) => {
      const d = player.duration > 0 ? player.duration : Infinity;
      const t = Math.max(0, Math.min(d, sec));
      try {
        player.currentTime = t;
      } catch {}
      pushPosition(t);
    },
    [player],
  );

  const skip = useCallback(
    (delta: number) => seekTo((player.currentTime ?? 0) + delta),
    [player, seekTo],
  );

  const togglePlay = useCallback(() => {
    try {
      if (player.playing || starting) {
        player.pause();
        setStarting(false);
      } else {
        if (
          player.duration > 0 &&
          player.currentTime >= player.duration - 0.3
        ) {
          player.currentTime = 0;
        }
        player.play();
      }
    } catch {}
  }, [player, starting]);

  const toggleMute = useCallback(() => {
    const nextMuted = !useVideoBridge.getState().muted;

    try {
      player.muted = nextMuted;
      useVideoBridge.setState({ muted: nextMuted });
    } catch (error) {
      console.warn("[VideoPlayerHost] toggleMute failed:", error);
    }
  }, [player]);

  useEffect(() => {
    useVideoBridge.setState({
      togglePlay,
      seekTo,
      skip,
      toggleMute,
    });
  }, [togglePlay, seekTo, skip, toggleMute]);

  // No UI here Ã¢â‚¬â€œ MiniPlayer draws everything.
  return null;
}
