========== src\hooks\useArtworkCache.ts ==========
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import * as FileSystem from 'expo-file-system/legacy';
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';
import * as MediaLibrary from 'expo-media-library/legacy';

// NOTE: `expo-music-info-2` was removed. Internally it imports the old
// "expo-file-system" API, which throws on Expo SDK 54 (that is why every
// song logged "[artwork] extract failed"). Cover art is now read directly
// from the ID3v2 "APIC" frame using the legacy file-system API.

const CACHE_KEY = '@artwork_map_v2'; // bumped: forces a fresh extraction
const ART_DIR = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}artwork_cache/`;
const MAX_TAG_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024; // never read more than 8 MB of tag

type ArtworkMap = Record<string, string>;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// base64 helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const B64_CHARS =
  'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/';
const B64_LOOKUP = (() => {
  const t = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < B64_CHARS.length; i++) t[B64_CHARS.charCodeAt(i)] = i;
  return t;
})();

const base64ToBytes = (b64: string): Uint8Array => {
  const clean = b64.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9+/]/g, '');
  const outLen = Math.floor((clean.length * 3) / 4);
  const out = new Uint8Array(outLen);
  let p = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i += 4) {
    const a = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i)] || 0;
    const b = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 1)] || 0;
    const c = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 2)] || 0;
    const d = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 3)] || 0;
    if (p < outLen) out[p++] = (a << 2) | (b >> 4);
    if (p < outLen) out[p++] = ((b & 15) << 4) | (c >> 2);
    if (p < outLen) out[p++] = ((c & 3) << 6) | d;
  }
  return out;
};

const bytesToBase64 = (bytes: Uint8Array): string => {
  let out = '';
  const len = bytes.length;
  for (let i = 0; i < len; i += 3) {
    const b0 = bytes[i];
    const b1 = i + 1 < len ? bytes[i + 1] : 0;
    const b2 = i + 2 < len ? bytes[i + 2] : 0;
    out += B64_CHARS[b0 >> 2];
    out += B64_CHARS[((b0 & 3) << 4) | (b1 >> 4)];
    out += i + 1 < len ? B64_CHARS[((b1 & 15) << 2) | (b2 >> 6)] : '=';
    out += i + 2 < len ? B64_CHARS[b2 & 63] : '=';
  }
  return out;
};

const readBytes = async (
  uri: string,
  position: number,
  length: number
): Promise<Uint8Array> => {
  const b64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(uri, {
    encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
    position,
    length,
  });
  return base64ToBytes(b64);
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// ID3v2 cover-art (APIC / PIC) reader
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const syncSafe = (b: Uint8Array, o: number) =>
  ((b[o] & 0x7f) << 21) |
  ((b[o + 1] & 0x7f) << 14) |
  ((b[o + 2] & 0x7f) << 7) |
  (b[o + 3] & 0x7f);

const be32 = (b: Uint8Array, o: number) =>
  ((b[o] << 24) | (b[o + 1] << 16) | (b[o + 2] << 8) | b[o + 3]) >>> 0;

// Undo "unsynchronisation": 0xFF 0x00 -> 0xFF
const removeUnsync = (b: Uint8Array): Uint8Array => {
  const out = new Uint8Array(b.length);
  let p = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
    out[p++] = b[i];
    if (b[i] === 0xff && i + 1 < b.length && b[i + 1] === 0x00) i++;
  }
  return out.subarray(0, p);
};

const imageExt = (img: Uint8Array, mime: string): string => {
  if (img[0] === 0xff && img[1] === 0xd8) return 'jpg';
  if (img[0] === 0x89 && img[1] === 0x50 && img[2] === 0x4e) return 'png';
  if (img[0] === 0x47 && img[1] === 0x49 && img[2] === 0x46) return 'gif';
  if (img[0] === 0x52 && img[1] === 0x49 && img[2] === 0x46) return 'webp';
  const m = mime.toLowerCase();
  if (m.includes('png')) return 'png';
  if (m.includes('gif')) return 'gif';
  if (m.includes('webp')) return 'webp';
  return 'jpg';
};

type Picture = { bytes: Uint8Array; ext: string };

const parseApic = (
  data: Uint8Array,
  isV22: boolean
): Picture | null => {
  if (data.length < 8) return null;
  const enc = data[0];
  let i = 1;
  let mime = '';

  if (isV22) {
    // 3-char image format, e.g. "JPG" / "PNG"
    mime = String.fromCharCode(data[1], data[2], data[3]);
    i = 4;
  } else {
    while (i < data.length && data[i] !== 0) {
      mime += String.fromCharCode(data[i]);
      i++;
    }
    i++; // null terminator
  }

  i++; // picture type byte

  // description (encoding-dependent terminator)
  if (enc === 1 || enc === 2) {
    while (i + 1 < data.length && !(data[i] === 0 && data[i + 1] === 0)) i += 2;
    i += 2;
  } else {
    while (i < data.length && data[i] !== 0) i++;
    i++;
  }

  if (i >= data.length) return null;
  const img = data.subarray(i);
  if (img.length < 32) return null;
  return { bytes: img, ext: imageExt(img, mime) };
};

async function readId3Picture(uri: string): Promise<Picture | null> {
  const header = await readBytes(uri, 0, 10);
  if (
    header.length < 10 ||
    header[0] !== 0x49 || // I
    header[1] !== 0x44 || // D
    header[2] !== 0x33 //    3
  ) {
    return null; // not an ID3v2 file (m4a / flac / etc.)
  }

  const major = header[3];
  const flags = header[5];
  const tagSize = syncSafe(header, 6);
  if (tagSize <= 0) return null;

  let body = await readBytes(uri, 10, Math.min(tagSize, MAX_TAG_BYTES));
  if (flags & 0x80 && major < 4) body = removeUnsync(body);

  let pos = 0;
  if (major >= 3 && flags & 0x40) {
    // extended header
    pos += major === 4 ? syncSafe(body, 0) : be32(body, 0) + 4;
  }

  const isV22 = major === 2;
  const idLen = isV22 ? 3 : 4;
  const hdrLen = isV22 ? 6 : 10;

  while (pos + hdrLen <= body.length) {
    let id = '';
    for (let k = 0; k < idLen; k++) id += String.fromCharCode(body[pos + k]);
    if (!/^[A-Z0-9]+$/.test(id)) break; // padding reached

    let size: number;
    if (isV22) size = (body[pos + 3] << 16) | (body[pos + 4] << 8) | body[pos + 5];
    else if (major === 4) size = syncSafe(body, pos + 4);
    else size = be32(body, pos + 4);

    const start = pos + hdrLen;
    if (size <= 0 || start + size > body.length) break;

    if (id === 'APIC' || id === 'PIC') {
      let data = body.subarray(start, start + size);
      // v2.4 per-frame unsync / data-length-indicator
      if (major === 4) {
        const fFlags = body[pos + 9];
        if (fFlags & 0x01) data = data.subarray(4); // data length indicator
        if (fFlags & 0x02) data = removeUnsync(data);
      }
      const pic = parseApic(data, isV22);
      if (pic) return pic;
    }

    pos = start + size;
  }
  return null;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Cache helpers
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
async function ensureDir() {
  const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(ART_DIR);
  if (!info.exists) {
    await FileSystem.makeDirectoryAsync(ART_DIR, { intermediates: true });
  }
}

async function loadPersistedMap(): Promise<ArtworkMap> {
  try {
    const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(CACHE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as ArtworkMap;

    const verified: ArtworkMap = {};
    for (const [id, uri] of Object.entries(parsed)) {
      if (!uri.startsWith('file://')) {
        verified[id] = uri;
        continue;
      }
      const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(uri);
      if (info.exists) verified[id] = uri;
    }
    return verified;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

function persistMap(map: ArtworkMap) {
  AsyncStorage.setItem(CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(map)).catch(() => {});
}

async function writeArtworkFile(
  songId: string,
  pic: Picture
): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    const path = `${ART_DIR}${songId}.${pic.ext}`;
    await FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(path, bytesToBase64(pic.bytes), {
      encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
    });
    return path;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

async function extractArtworkForSong(song: any): Promise<string | null> {
  try {
    let fileUri: string | undefined = song.uri;

    if (!fileUri || !fileUri.startsWith('file://')) {
      try {
        const asset: any = await MediaLibrary.getAssetInfoAsync(song.id);
        fileUri = asset?.localUri ?? asset?.uri ?? fileUri;
      } catch {}
    }

    if (!fileUri) return null;

    const pic = await readId3Picture(fileUri);
    if (!pic) return null;

    return await writeArtworkFile(String(song.id), pic);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('[artwork] extract failed for', song?.id, err);
    return null;
  }
}

export function useArtworkCache(songs: { id: string }[]) {
  const [map, setMap] = useState<ArtworkMap>({});
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  const runningRef = useRef(false);
  // songs we already tried (and found no embedded art) - don't retry forever
  const triedRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // 1) Load the persisted map on mount (fast â€” usually <50ms).
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true;
    (async () => {
      await ensureDir();
      const persisted = await loadPersistedMap();
      if (!alive) return;
      setMap(persisted);
      setReady(true);
    })();
    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, []);

  // 2) Background extraction in parallel batches.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!ready || runningRef.current || songs.length === 0) return;

    const missing = songs.filter(
      (s) => !map[s.id] && !triedRef.current.has(String(s.id))
    );
    if (missing.length === 0) return;

    runningRef.current = true;
    let cancelled = false;

    (async () => {
      const BATCH = 4;

      for (let i = 0; i < missing.length; i += BATCH) {
        if (cancelled) break;

        const batch = missing.slice(i, i + BATCH);
        const results = await Promise.all(
          batch.map((s) => extractArtworkForSong(s))
        );

        const found: ArtworkMap = {};
        batch.forEach((s, idx) => {
          triedRef.current.add(String(s.id));
          const uri = results[idx];
          if (uri) found[s.id] = uri;
        });

        if (Object.keys(found).length > 0 && !cancelled) {
          setMap((prev) => {
            const next = { ...prev, ...found };
            persistMap(next);
            return next;
          });
        }
      }

      runningRef.current = false;
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      runningRef.current = false;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [ready, songs]);

  const remove = useCallback((songId: string) => {
    triedRef.current.delete(String(songId));
    setMap((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      const uri = next[songId];
      delete next[songId];
      if (uri && uri.startsWith('file://')) {
        FileSystem.deleteAsync(uri, { idempotent: true }).catch(() => {});
      }
      persistMap(next);
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const clear = useCallback(async () => {
    triedRef.current.clear();
    setMap({});
    await AsyncStorage.removeItem(CACHE_KEY);
    try {
      await FileSystem.deleteAsync(ART_DIR, { idempotent: true });
      await ensureDir();
    } catch {}
  }, []);

  return { artworkMap: map, ready, remove, clear };
}

========== src\hooks\useAudioPlayer.ts ==========
import { useCallback, useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { RepeatMode } from "react-native-track-player";
import { usePlayerStore } from "../store/playerStore";
import { Song } from "../types";
import {
  getTrackPlayer,
  getTrackPlayerEnums,
  isTrackPlayerAvailable,
} from "../services/audio/trackPlayerSafe";
import { songToMediaItem } from "../services/audio/mediaItem";

type PlayingResult =
  | boolean
  | { playing: boolean; bufferingDuringPlay?: boolean };

interface SafeHooks {
  useIsPlaying: () => PlayingResult;
  useProgress: (interval?: number) => {
    position: number;
    duration: number;
    buffered: number;
  };
  useActiveMediaItem: () => any;
}

function getSafeHooks(): SafeHooks {
  const fallback: SafeHooks = {
    useIsPlaying: () => false,
    useProgress: () => ({ position: 0, duration: 0, buffered: 0 }),
    useActiveMediaItem: () => null,
  };

  if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return fallback;

  try {
    // ðŸ”¥ FIX: Changed from "@rntp/player" to "react-native-track-player"
    const mod = require("react-native-track-player");
    return {
      useIsPlaying: mod.useIsPlaying ?? fallback.useIsPlaying,
      useProgress: mod.useProgress ?? fallback.useProgress,
      // ðŸ”¥ FIX: v4 uses useActiveTrack instead of useActiveMediaItem
      useActiveMediaItem: mod.useActiveTrack ?? fallback.useActiveMediaItem,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("[useAudioPlayer] Hook loading failed:", error);
    return fallback;
  }
}

const toNativeRepeat = (mode: "off" | "queue" | "track") =>
  mode === "track"
    ? RepeatMode.Track
    : mode === "queue"
      ? RepeatMode.Queue
      : RepeatMode.Off;

// v5: getProgress() is synchronous. Falls back to the old calls / store.
function readPosition(TP: any): number {
  try {
    if (typeof TP.getProgress === "function") {
      const p = TP.getProgress();
      if (p && typeof p.position === "number") return p.position;
    }
    if (typeof TP.getPosition === "function") {
      const v = Number(TP.getPosition());
      if (Number.isFinite(v)) return v;
    }
  } catch {}
  return usePlayerStore.getState().position;
}

function readDuration(TP: any): number {
  try {
    if (typeof TP.getProgress === "function") {
      const p = TP.getProgress();
      if (p && typeof p.duration === "number") return p.duration;
    }
    if (typeof TP.getDuration === "function") {
      const v = Number(TP.getDuration());
      if (Number.isFinite(v)) return v;
    }
  } catch {}
  return usePlayerStore.getState().duration;
}

// ===========================================================================
// Module-level shared state (shared by EVERY useAudioPlayer() call)
// ===========================================================================

const USE_SKIP = true;
const PENDING_MS = 3000;

let pendingId: string | null = null;
let pendingUntil = 0;

const setPending = (id: string | number | undefined) => {
  pendingId = id != null ? String(id) : null;
  pendingUntil = Date.now() + PENDING_MS;
};

const isPending = (): boolean => {
  if (!pendingId) return false;
  if (Date.now() > pendingUntil) {
    pendingId = null;
    return false;
  }
  return true;
};

/** true => this report is stale and must be ignored */
const shouldIgnoreDetected = (id: string | undefined): boolean => {
  if (!isPending()) return false;
  if (id != null && id === pendingId) {
    pendingId = null; // native caught up
    return false;
  }
  return true;
};

// Every click gets a token; older async work is dropped.
let playToken = 0;

// Which queue (by song ids) the native player currently holds.
let nativeQueueSig = "";
const queueSig = (songs: Song[]) => songs.map((s) => String(s.id)).join("|");

// Let React draw the new song / icons BEFORE the heavy native work starts.
const nextFrame = () =>
  new Promise<void>((resolve) => requestAnimationFrame(() => resolve()));

// Converting a whole queue on every click is slow; convert each song once.
const mediaItemCache = new WeakMap<object, any>();
const toMediaItems = (songs: Song[]) =>
  songs.map((song) => {
    let item = mediaItemCache.get(song);
    if (!item) {
      item = songToMediaItem(song);
      mediaItemCache.set(song, item);
    }
    return item;
  });

// Only ONE hook instance runs the sync effects (detection, progress, etc).
let instanceCounter = 0;
let ownerId: number | null = null;
const waiters = new Map<number, (isOwner: boolean) => void>();

function registerInstance(id: number, cb: (isOwner: boolean) => void) {
  waiters.set(id, cb);
  if (ownerId === null) {
    ownerId = id;
    cb(true);
  } else {
    cb(false);
  }
  return () => {
    waiters.delete(id);
    if (ownerId === id) {
      ownerId = null;
      const first = waiters.entries().next().value as
        | [number, (o: boolean) => void]
        | undefined;
      if (first) {
        ownerId = first[0];
        first[1](true);
      }
    }
  };
}

export function useAudioPlayer() {
  const { useIsPlaying, useProgress, useActiveMediaItem } = getSafeHooks();

  const playingResult = useIsPlaying();
  const playing =
    typeof playingResult === "boolean" ? playingResult : playingResult.playing;

  // Progress â€” updates every 500ms
  const { position, duration } = useProgress(0.5);
  const activeTrack = useActiveMediaItem();

  const setPlaying = usePlayerStore((s) => s.setPlaying);
  const setProgress = usePlayerStore((s) => s.setProgress);
  const setQueue = usePlayerStore((s) => s.setQueue);
  const setCurrentIndex = usePlayerStore((s) => s.setCurrentIndex);

  const queue = usePlayerStore((s) => s.queue);
  const currentIndex = usePlayerStore((s) => s.currentIndex);

  // ---- owner election: only one instance runs the sync effects ----
  const idRef = useRef(0);
  if (!idRef.current) idRef.current = ++instanceCounter;
  const [isOwner, setIsOwner] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => registerInstance(idRef.current, setIsOwner), []);

  // ---- 1. playing state sync ----
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOwner) return;
    if (!playing && isPending()) return;
    setPlaying(playing);
  }, [isOwner, playing, setPlaying]);

  // ---- 2. progress sync ----
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOwner) return;
    if (isPending()) return;
    setProgress(position, duration);
  }, [isOwner, position, duration, setProgress]);

  // ---- 3. TRACK CHANGE DETECTION (one instance only) ----
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOwner) return;
    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;

    const TP = getTrackPlayer();
    if (!TP) return;

    let alive = true;
    let running = false;

    const syncTrack = async () => {
      if (!alive || running) return;
      running = true;

      try {
        const store = usePlayerStore.getState();
        const q = store.queue;
        const ci = store.currentIndex;

        if (q.length === 0) return;

        let activeId: string | undefined;
        let activeIndex: number | undefined;

        if (typeof TP.getActiveMediaItem === "function") {
          try {
            const item = await TP.getActiveMediaItem();
            if (item) {
              const mid = item.mediaId ?? item.id;
              if (mid != null) activeId = String(mid);
              if (typeof item.index === "number") activeIndex = item.index;
            }
          } catch {}
        }

        if (!activeId && typeof TP.getActiveTrack === "function") {
          try {
            const t = await TP.getActiveTrack();
            const mid = t?.mediaId ?? t?.id;
            if (mid != null) activeId = String(mid);
          } catch {}
        }

        if (
          activeIndex == null &&
          typeof TP.getActiveMediaItemIndex === "function"
        ) {
          try {
            const r = await TP.getActiveMediaItemIndex();
            if (typeof r === "number") activeIndex = r;
          } catch {}
        }

        if (activeIndex == null && typeof TP.getCurrentIndex === "function") {
          try {
            const r = await TP.getCurrentIndex();
            if (typeof r === "number") activeIndex = r;
          } catch {}
        }

        if (!alive) return;

        let reportedIndex = -1;
        if (activeIndex != null && activeIndex >= 0 && activeIndex < q.length) {
          reportedIndex = activeIndex;
        } else if (activeId) {
          reportedIndex = q.findIndex((s) => String(s.id) === activeId);
        }
        if (reportedIndex < 0) return;

        const reportedSongId = String(q[reportedIndex]?.id);

        if (shouldIgnoreDetected(reportedSongId)) return;

        const latest = usePlayerStore.getState();
        if (reportedIndex !== latest.currentIndex) {
          console.log(
            `[TrackDetect] index ${latest.currentIndex} â†’ ${reportedIndex} :`,
            latest.queue[reportedIndex]?.title,
          );
          latest.setCurrentIndex(reportedIndex);
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore
      } finally {
        running = false;
      }
    };

    const interval = setInterval(syncTrack, 1000);
    const warmup = setTimeout(syncTrack, 300);

    const E = getTrackPlayerEnums();
    let sub: any = null;
    if (typeof TP.addEventListener === "function" && E?.Event) {
      try {
        const evName = E.Event.PlaybackActiveTrackChanged;
        if (evName) {
          sub = TP.addEventListener(evName, () => {
            syncTrack();
          });
        }
      } catch {}
    }

    return () => {
      alive = false;
      clearTimeout(warmup);
      clearInterval(interval);
      try {
        if (sub && typeof sub.remove === "function") sub.remove();
      } catch {}
    };
  }, [isOwner]);

  // ---- 4. activeTrack hook fallback ----
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOwner) return;
    const aid = activeTrack?.mediaId ?? activeTrack?.id;
    if (!aid) return;

    if (shouldIgnoreDetected(String(aid))) return;

    const idx = queue.findIndex((s) => String(s.id) === String(aid));
    if (idx >= 0 && idx !== currentIndex) {
      console.log("[TrackDetect:hook] â†’", idx, queue[idx]?.title);
      setCurrentIndex(idx);
    }
  }, [
    isOwner,
    activeTrack?.mediaId,
    activeTrack?.id,
    queue,
    currentIndex,
    setCurrentIndex,
  ]);

  // ---- 5. Safety net: auto-play next song when the current one ends ----
  const nextRef = useRef<() => Promise<void>>(async () => {});
  const endHandledRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  const endTimerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!isOwner) return;
    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;
    if (!duration || duration <= 1) return;
    if (isPending()) return;

    const store = usePlayerStore.getState();
    const track = store.queue[store.currentIndex];
    if (!track) return;

    const key = `${store.currentIndex}:${track.id}`;

    if (position < duration - 3) {
      if (endHandledRef.current === key) endHandledRef.current = null;
      return;
    }

    if (position < duration - 0.6) return;
    if (endHandledRef.current === key) return;
    if (store.repeat === "track") return;
    endHandledRef.current = key;

    if (endTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(endTimerRef.current);
    endTimerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      const s = usePlayerStore.getState();
      const cur = s.queue[s.currentIndex];
      if (!cur || `${s.currentIndex}:${cur.id}` !== key) return;
      void nextRef.current();
    }, 1500);
  }, [isOwner, position, duration]);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (endTimerRef.current) clearTimeout(endTimerRef.current);
    };
  }, []);

  // ---- loadAndPlay ----
  const loadAndPlay = useCallback(
    async (songs: Song[], startIndex: number): Promise<void> => {
      if (!songs.length) throw new Error("Playback queue is empty.");
      if (startIndex < 0 || startIndex >= songs.length) {
        throw new Error(`Invalid song index: ${startIndex}`);
      }
      if (!songs[startIndex]?.uri) {
        throw new Error("Selected song has no audio URI.");
      }
      if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) {
        throw new Error("TrackPlayer native module is unavailable.");
      }

      const TP = getTrackPlayer();
      const token = ++playToken;

      try {
        if (typeof TP.setMediaItems !== "function") {
          throw new Error("RNTP V5 setMediaItems() is unavailable.");
        }

        setPending(songs[startIndex].id);
        setQueue(songs, startIndex);
        setProgress(0, songs[startIndex].duration || 0);
        setPlaying(true);
        await nextFrame();

        if (token !== playToken) return;
        await TP.setMediaItems(toMediaItems(songs), startIndex);
        nativeQueueSig = queueSig(songs);

        if (token !== playToken) return;

        await TP.play();
        if (token !== playToken) return;
        setPlaying(true);

        console.log("[loadAndPlay] Playing:", songs[startIndex].title);
      } catch (error) {
        if (token === playToken) {
          pendingId = null;
          setPlaying(false);
        }
        console.error("[loadAndPlay] Failed:", error);
        throw error;
      }
    },
    [setQueue, setProgress, setPlaying],
  );

  const playPause = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;
    const TP = getTrackPlayer();

    try {
      const actualPlaying =
        typeof TP.isPlaying === "function"
          ? TP.isPlaying()
          : usePlayerStore.getState().isPlaying;

      if (actualPlaying) {
        await TP.pause();
        setPlaying(false);
      } else {
        await TP.play();
        setPlaying(true);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[playPause] Failed:", error);
    }
  }, [setPlaying]);

  const playIndex = useCallback(
    async (targetIndex: number) => {
      if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;

      const state = usePlayerStore.getState();
      const songs = state.queue;

      if (
        targetIndex < 0 ||
        targetIndex >= songs.length ||
        !songs[targetIndex]?.uri
      ) {
        return;
      }

      const TP = getTrackPlayer();
      const token = ++playToken;

      try {
        setPending(songs[targetIndex].id);
        setCurrentIndex(targetIndex);
        setProgress(0, songs[targetIndex].duration || 0);
        setPlaying(true);
        await nextFrame();

        if (token !== playToken) return;
        const sig = queueSig(songs);
        let skipped = false;

        if (USE_SKIP && sig === nativeQueueSig) {
          for (const fn of ["skipToIndex", "skipTo"]) {
            if (typeof TP[fn] === "function") {
              try {
                await TP[fn](targetIndex);
                skipped = true;
                break;
              } catch {}
            }
          }
        }

        if (!skipped) {
          if (typeof TP.setMediaItems !== "function") {
            throw new Error("RNTP V5 setMediaItems() is unavailable.");
          }
          await TP.setMediaItems(toMediaItems(songs), targetIndex);
          nativeQueueSig = sig;
        }

        if (token !== playToken) return;

        await TP.play();
        if (token !== playToken) return;
        setPlaying(true);
      } catch (error) {
        if (token === playToken) {
          pendingId = null;
          setPlaying(false);
        }
        console.error("[playIndex] Failed:", error);
      }
    },
    [setCurrentIndex, setProgress, setPlaying],
  );

  const next = useCallback(async () => {
    const state = usePlayerStore.getState();
    if (state.queue.length === 0) return;
    if (state.currentIndex < state.queue.length - 1) {
      await playIndex(state.currentIndex + 1);
    } else if (state.repeat === "queue") {
      await playIndex(0);
    }
  }, [playIndex]);

  const prev = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;
    const TP = getTrackPlayer();

    try {
      if (readPosition(TP) > 3) {
        await TP.seekTo(0);
        setProgress(0, usePlayerStore.getState().duration);
        return;
      }

      const state = usePlayerStore.getState();
      if (state.currentIndex > 0) {
        await playIndex(state.currentIndex - 1);
      } else {
        await TP.seekTo(0);
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[prev] Failed:", error);
    }
  }, [playIndex, setProgress]);

  nextRef.current = next;

  const seek = useCallback(async (value: number) => {
    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;
    try {
      await getTrackPlayer().seekTo(value);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[seek] Failed:", error);
    }
  }, []);

  const seekBy = useCallback(async (delta: number) => {
    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;
    const TP = getTrackPlayer();

    try {
      const pos = readPosition(TP);
      const dur = readDuration(TP);
      await TP.seekTo(Math.max(0, Math.min(dur, pos + delta)));
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[seekBy] Failed:", error);
    }
  }, []);

  const setVolume = useCallback(async (value: number) => {
    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;
    try {
      const TP = getTrackPlayer();
      if (typeof TP.setVolume === "function") {
        await TP.setVolume(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, value)));
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[setVolume] Failed:", error);
    }
  }, []);

  const toggleShuffle = useCallback(async () => {
    const state = usePlayerStore.getState();
    const nextShuffle = !state.shuffle;
    state.setShuffle(nextShuffle);

    const songs = [...state.queue];
    const currentSong = songs[state.currentIndex];

    if (nextShuffle) {
      for (let i = songs.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
        const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
        [songs[i], songs[j]] = [songs[j], songs[i]];
      }
    }

    const nextIndex = Math.max(
      0,
      currentSong ? songs.findIndex((song) => song.id === currentSong.id) : 0,
    );

    setQueue(songs, nextIndex);

    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;
    const TP = getTrackPlayer();
    try {
      if (typeof TP.setMediaItems !== "function" || songs.length === 0) return;

      const wasPlaying = usePlayerStore.getState().isPlaying;
      const pos = readPosition(TP);

      await TP.setMediaItems(toMediaItems(songs), nextIndex);
      nativeQueueSig = queueSig(songs);
      if (pos > 0 && typeof TP.seekTo === "function") await TP.seekTo(pos);
      if (wasPlaying) await TP.play();
      else if (typeof TP.pause === "function") await TP.pause();
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[toggleShuffle] Failed to sync native queue:", error);
    }
  }, [setQueue]);

  const cycleRepeat = useCallback(async () => {
    const state = usePlayerStore.getState();
    const modes: Array<"off" | "queue" | "track"> = ["off", "queue", "track"];
    const nextMode = modes[(modes.indexOf(state.repeat) + 1) % modes.length];
    state.setRepeat(nextMode);

    if (!isTrackPlayerAvailable()) return;
    const TP = getTrackPlayer();
    try {
      if (typeof TP.setRepeatMode === "function") {
        await TP.setRepeatMode(toNativeRepeat(nextMode));
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[cycleRepeat] Failed:", error);
    }
  }, []);

  return {
    isPlaying: playing,
    position,
    duration,
    loadAndPlay,
    playPause,
    next,
    prev,
    seek,
    seekBy,
    setVolume,
    toggleShuffle,
    cycleRepeat,
  };
}

========== src\hooks\useId3Tags.ts ==========
// src/hooks/useId3Tags.ts
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";

// â”€â”€ Base64 decode â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const B64_CHARS =
  "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
const B64_LOOKUP = (() => {
  const t = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < B64_CHARS.length; i++) t[B64_CHARS.charCodeAt(i)] = i;
  return t;
})();

const base64ToBytes = (b64: string): Uint8Array => {
  const clean = b64.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9+/]/g, "");
  const out = new Uint8Array(Math.floor((clean.length * 3) / 4));
  let p = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i += 4) {
    const a = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i)] || 0;
    const b = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 1)] || 0;
    const c = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 2)] || 0;
    const d = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 3)] || 0;
    out[p++] = (a << 2) | (b >> 4);
    out[p++] = ((b & 15) << 4) | (c >> 2);
    out[p++] = ((c & 3) << 6) | d;
  }
  return out;
};

// â”€â”€ ID3 helpers â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const syncSafe = (b: Uint8Array, o: number) =>
  ((b[o] & 0x7f) << 21) |
  ((b[o + 1] & 0x7f) << 14) |
  ((b[o + 2] & 0x7f) << 7) |
  (b[o + 3] & 0x7f);

const decodeUtf16 = (b: Uint8Array, bigEndian: boolean): string => {
  let s = "";
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < b.length; i += 2) {
    const code = bigEndian ? (b[i] << 8) | b[i + 1] : (b[i + 1] << 8) | b[i];
    s += String.fromCharCode(code);
  }
  return s;
};

const decodeUtf8 = (b: Uint8Array): string => {
  try {
    let pct = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
      pct += "%" + (b[i] < 16 ? "0" : "") + b[i].toString(16);
    }
    return decodeURIComponent(pct);
  } catch {
    let s = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
    return s;
  }
};

const decodeId3Text = (b: Uint8Array, enc: number): string => {
  let s = "";
  if (enc === 1) {
    if (b[0] === 0xfe && b[1] === 0xff) s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), true);
    else if (b[0] === 0xff && b[1] === 0xfe)
      s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), false);
    else s = decodeUtf16(b, false);
  } else if (enc === 2) {
    s = decodeUtf16(b, true);
  } else if (enc === 3) {
    s = decodeUtf8(b);
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
  }
  return s
    .split("\u0000")
    .map((x) => x.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
};

const readId3Frame = (
  b: Uint8Array,
  frameIds: string[],
): string | undefined => {
  if (b.length < 10 || b[0] !== 0x49 || b[1] !== 0x44 || b[2] !== 0x33)
    return undefined;
  const major = b[3];
  const flags = b[5];
  const tagSize = syncSafe(b, 6);
  let pos = 10;

  if (major >= 3 && (flags & 0x40) !== 0) {
    const ext =
      major === 4
        ? syncSafe(b, pos)
        : (((b[pos] << 24) |
            (b[pos + 1] << 16) |
            (b[pos + 2] << 8) |
            b[pos + 3]) >>>
            0) +
          4;
    pos += ext;
  }

  const end = Math.min(b.length, 10 + tagSize);
  const idLen = major === 2 ? 3 : 4;
  const hdrLen = major === 2 ? 6 : 10;

  while (pos + hdrLen <= end) {
    let id = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < idLen; i++) id += String.fromCharCode(b[pos + i]);
    if (!/^[A-Z0-9]+$/.test(id)) break;

    let size: number;
    if (major === 2) {
      size = (b[pos + 3] << 16) | (b[pos + 4] << 8) | b[pos + 5];
    } else if (major === 4) {
      size = syncSafe(b, pos + 4);
    } else {
      size =
        ((b[pos + 4] << 24) |
          (b[pos + 5] << 16) |
          (b[pos + 6] << 8) |
          b[pos + 7]) >>>
        0;
    }

    const dataStart = pos + hdrLen;
    if (size <= 0 || dataStart + size > b.length) break;

    if (frameIds.includes(id)) {
      const text = decodeId3Text(
        b.subarray(dataStart + 1, dataStart + size),
        b[dataStart],
      );
      if (text && text.trim()) return text.trim();
    }

    pos = dataStart + size;
  }
  return undefined;
};

const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  const t = a.trim();
  if (!t) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(t);
};

const parseId3Artist = (b: Uint8Array): string | undefined => {
  const primary = readId3Frame(b, ["TPE1", "TP1"]);
  if (primary && !isUnknownArtist(primary)) return primary;
  const albumArtist = readId3Frame(b, ["TPE2", "TP2"]);
  return !isUnknownArtist(albumArtist) ? albumArtist : undefined;
};

const parseId3Album = (b: Uint8Array): string | undefined =>
  readId3Frame(b, ["TALB", "TAL"]);

const parseId3Title = (b: Uint8Array): string | undefined =>
  readId3Frame(b, ["TIT2", "TT2"]);

export type SongTags = {
  title?: string;
  artist?: string;
  album?: string;
};

const readTagsFromFile = async (uri: string): Promise<SongTags> => {
  try {
    const b64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(uri, {
      encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
      position: 0,
      length: 131072,
    });
    const bytes = base64ToBytes(b64);
    const title = parseId3Title(bytes);
    const artist = parseId3Artist(bytes);
    const album = parseId3Album(bytes);
    return {
      title: title && title.trim() ? title.trim() : undefined,
      artist: artist && !isUnknownArtist(artist) ? artist : undefined,
      album: album && album.trim() ? album.trim() : undefined,
    };
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
};

// â”€â”€ Module-level caches â€” shared across all hook instances â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const tagCache: Record<string, SongTags> = {};
const inFlight: Record<string, Promise<SongTags>> = {};
const EMPTY_TAGS: SongTags = {};

const STORAGE_KEY = "@id3_tags_v1";
const CONCURRENCY = 6;

let diskLoaded = false;
let diskLoading: Promise<void> | null = null;
let persistTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

const loadDiskCache = async (): Promise<void> => {
  if (diskLoaded) return;
  if (diskLoading) return diskLoading;
  diskLoading = (async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
          Object.assign(tagCache, parsed);
        }
      }
    } catch {}
    diskLoaded = true;
  })();
  return diskLoading;
};

const schedulePersist = () => {
  if (persistTimer) return;
  persistTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    persistTimer = null;
    AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(tagCache)).catch(() => {});
  }, 1500);
};

const readWithConcurrency = async (
  items: { id: string; uri: string }[],
  onResult: (id: string, tags: SongTags) => void,
): Promise<void> => {
  if (items.length === 0) return;
  let idx = 0;

  const run = async () => {
    while (idx < items.length) {
      const i = idx++;
      const { id, uri } = items[i];

      let tags: SongTags;
      if (inFlight[id]) {
        tags = await inFlight[id];
      } else {
        const p = readTagsFromFile(uri);
        inFlight[id] = p;
        try {
          tags = await p;
        } finally {
          delete inFlight[id];
        }
      }

      tagCache[id] = tags;
      onResult(id, tags);
    }
  };

  const n = Math.min(CONCURRENCY, items.length);
  await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: n }, run));
  schedulePersist();
};

// â”€â”€ Public hook â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export function useId3Tags(songs: any[]) {
  // Seed initial state from the in-memory cache so previously-read songs
  // appear immediately on mount (no blank frame).
  const [tagData, setTagData] = useState<Record<string, SongTags>>(() => ({
    ...tagCache,
  }));
  const [metaReady, setMetaReady] = useState(false);
  const mounted = useRef(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length === 0) {
      setMetaReady(true);
      return;
    }

    let cancelled = false;

    (async () => {
      // 1. Hydrate from AsyncStorage (fast â€” ~10 ms on first launch).
      await loadDiskCache();

      // 2. Push any cached values into state immediately.
      if (!cancelled && mounted.current) {
        setTagData({ ...tagCache });
      }

      // 3. Determine which songs still need reading.
      const missing = songs.filter((s) => {
        if (tagCache[s.id] || inFlight[s.id]) return false;
        const uri = (s as any).uri ?? (s as any).path;
        return !!uri;
      });

      if (missing.length === 0) {
        if (mounted.current && !cancelled) setMetaReady(true);
        return;
      }

      const items = missing.map((s) => ({
        id: s.id,
        uri: (s as any).uri ?? (s as any).path,
      }));

      // 4. Batch state updates every 60 ms so partial results render
      //    continuously instead of waiting for the whole batch to finish.
      let batch: Record<string, SongTags> = {};
      let flushTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

      const flush = () => {
        flushTimer = null;
        if (Object.keys(batch).length === 0) return;
        const toApply = batch;
        batch = {};
        if (!cancelled && mounted.current) {
          setTagData((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...toApply }));
        }
      };

      await readWithConcurrency(items, (id, tags) => {
        batch[id] = tags;
        if (!flushTimer) flushTimer = setTimeout(flush, 60);
      });

      if (flushTimer) clearTimeout(flushTimer);
      flush();

      if (!cancelled && mounted.current) setMetaReady(true);
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [songs]);

  return { tagData, metaReady };
}

export { EMPTY_TAGS };

========== src\hooks\useLiveActivity.tsx ==========
// src/hooks/useLiveActivity.tsx
// iOS  â†’ system Live Activity / Dynamic Island (expo-widgets)
// Android â†’ nothing custom: the SYSTEM media notification from @rntp/player is used.
import { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { Platform } from "react-native";
import type { LiveActivity } from "expo-widgets"; // type only â€“ erased at build time
import { usePlayerStore } from "../store/playerStore";

export type NowPlayingData = {
  title: string;
  artist: string;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  progress: number; // 0 to 1
};

// iOS Live Activity factory is loaded ONLY on iOS
function getIosActivity(): any {
  if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return null;
  try {
    return require("./musicLiveActivityIos").MusicLiveActivity;
  } catch (e) {
    console.log("[LiveActivity] iOS module load failed:", e);
    return null;
  }
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Low-level start / update / stop (iOS) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export function useLiveActivity() {
  const iosInstance = useRef<LiveActivity<NowPlayingData> | null>(null);
  const iosStarting = useRef<Promise<void> | null>(null);

  const update = useCallback(async (data: NowPlayingData) => {
    if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return;
    try {
      if (iosStarting.current) await iosStarting.current;
      await iosInstance.current?.update(data);
    } catch (e) {
      console.log("[LiveActivity] iOS update failed:", e);
    }
  }, []);

  const start = useCallback(
    async (data: NowPlayingData) => {
      if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return;
      // Never start a second activity â€“ reuse the running one
      if (iosInstance.current || iosStarting.current) {
        await update(data);
        return;
      }
      const factory = getIosActivity();
      if (!factory) return;
      iosStarting.current = (async () => {
        try {
          iosInstance.current = await factory.start(data);
        } catch (e) {
          console.log("[LiveActivity] iOS start failed:", e);
        } finally {
          iosStarting.current = null;
        }
      })();
      await iosStarting.current;
    },
    [update],
  );

  const stop = useCallback(async () => {
    if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return;
    try {
      if (iosStarting.current) await iosStarting.current;
      if (iosInstance.current) {
        await iosInstance.current.end("default");
        iosInstance.current = null;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.log("[LiveActivity] iOS stop failed:", e);
    }
  }, []);

  return useMemo(() => ({ start, update, stop }), [start, update, stop]);
}

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ Mount ONCE in App.tsx â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export function useLiveActivitySync() {
  const api = useLiveActivity();
  const apiRef = useRef(api);
  apiRef.current = api;

  const trackId = usePlayerStore((s) => s.currentTrack?.id);
  const isPlaying = usePlayerStore((s) => s.isPlaying);
  const active = useRef(false);

  const snapshot = useCallback((): NowPlayingData | null => {
    const { currentTrack, isPlaying, position, duration } =
      usePlayerStore.getState();
    if (!currentTrack) return null;
    const progress =
      duration > 0 ? Math.min(1, Math.max(0, position / duration)) : 0;
    return {
      title: currentTrack.title || "Unknown Title",
      artist: currentTrack.artist || "Unknown Artist",
      isPlaying,
      progress,
    };
  }, []);

  // Track change or play/pause change â†’ start / update / stop
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Platform.OS !== "ios") return;
    const data = snapshot();
    if (!data) {
      if (active.current) {
        active.current = false;
        apiRef.current.stop();
      }
      return;
    }
    if (!active.current) {
      active.current = true;
      apiRef.current.start(data);
    } else {
      apiRef.current.update(data);
    }
  }, [trackId, isPlaying, snapshot]);

  // Progress refresh every 15 s (iOS throttles frequent Live Activity updates)
  useEffect(() => {
    if (Platform.OS !== "ios" || !isPlaying) return;
    const timer = setInterval(() => {
      const data = snapshot();
      if (data && active.current) apiRef.current.update(data);
    }, 15000);
    return () => clearInterval(timer);
  }, [isPlaying, snapshot]);

  // End the activity when the app root unmounts
  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      active.current = false;
      apiRef.current.stop();
    };
  }, []);
}


========== src\hooks\useMediaScanner.ts ==========
import { useCallback } from "react";
import { useMediaStore } from "../store/mediaStore";
import { scanAudio } from "../services/mediaScanner/audioScanner";
import { mediaDatabase } from "../services/mediaScanner/mediaDatabase";

export function useMediaScanner() {
  const setSongs = useMediaStore((s) => s.setSongs);

  const scanAll = useCallback(async () => {
    console.log("[DEBUG] scanAll() CALLED");

    const songs = await scanAudio();

    console.log("[DEBUG] Songs returned:", songs.length);

    const favorites = useMediaStore.getState().favoritesSong;
    const withFav = songs.map((s) => ({
      ...s,
      isFavorite: !!favorites[s.id],
    }));

    setSongs(withFav);
    await mediaDatabase.saveSongIds(songs.map((s) => s.id));

    return songs.length;
  }, [setSongs]);

  const scanEverything = useCallback(async () => {
    await scanAll();
  }, [scanAll]);

  return { scanAll, scanEverything };
}

========== src\hooks\useMediaSessionSync.ts ==========
import { useEffect } from 'react';
import TrackPlayer from 'react-native-track-player';
import { usePlayerStore } from '../store/playerStore';
import { toArtworkUri } from '../services/audio/mediaItem';

export function useMediaSessionSync() {
  useEffect(() => {
    let lastKey = '';

    const push = async () => {
      try {
        const { currentTrack } = usePlayerStore.getState();
        if (!currentTrack) return;

        const art = toArtworkUri(
          (currentTrack as any).artwork ?? currentTrack.albumArt,
        );
        
        const key = [
          currentTrack.id,
          art ?? '',
          currentTrack.title,
          currentTrack.artist,
          currentTrack.album,
        ].join('|');
        
        if (key === lastKey) return;

        const id = String(currentTrack.id);
        
        // ðŸ”¥ FIX 1: getQueue is async in v4
        const queue = await TrackPlayer.getQueue();
        const idx = queue.findIndex((m: any) => m.mediaId === id || m.id === id);
        
        if (idx < 0) return; // Native queue not ready yet; the interval will retry

        // ðŸ”¥ FIX 2: Correct method and metadata keys for v4
        await TrackPlayer.updateMetadataForTrack(idx, {
          title: currentTrack.title || 'Unknown Title',
          artist: currentTrack.artist || 'Unknown Artist',
          album: currentTrack.album || 'Unknown Album', // v4 uses `album`, not `albumTitle`
          artwork: art || undefined, // v4 uses `artwork`, not `artworkUrl`
          duration: currentTrack.duration || 0, // Ensure lock screen seekbar works
        });
        
        lastKey = key;
      } catch (error) {
        // Silently ignore errors during sync (e.g., if player is resetting)
      }
    };

    // Initial push
    push();
    
    // Subscribe to Zustand store changes
    const unsub = usePlayerStore.subscribe(push);
    
    // Artwork may land after the song starts, so we retry periodically
    const timer = setInterval(push, 1500); 
    
    return () => {
      unsub();
      clearInterval(timer);
    };
  }, []);
}

========== src\hooks\useTheme.ts ==========
// src/hooks/useTheme.ts
import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Appearance, useColorScheme } from "react-native";
import { useSettingsStore } from "../store/settingsStore";

// ===========================================================
// âœ… NAVBAR GLASS SETTINGS â€” change the glass look only here
// ===========================================================
export interface GlassSettings {
  distortionStrength: number;
  frostBlur: number;
  tintColor: number;
  tintOpacity: number;
  shadowSpread: number;
  shadowBlur: number;
  blurTint: "light" | "dark";
}

export interface Theme {
  mode: "light" | "dark";
  background: string;
  surface: string;
  surfaceAlt: string;
  text: string;
  textSecondary: string;
  primary: string;
  border: string;
  inactive: string;
  overlay: string;

  isDark: boolean;
  accent: string;
  success: string;
  warning: string;
  error: string;
  glassTint: "light" | "dark" | "default";

  glass: GlassSettings;
  welcomeBg: string;
  popupColour: string;
  miniArtBorder: string;
  miniDivider: string;
}

const DARK_GLASS: GlassSettings = {
  distortionStrength: 40,
  frostBlur: 16,
  tintColor: 28,
  tintOpacity: 0.6,
  shadowSpread: 0,
  shadowBlur: 20,
  blurTint: "dark",
};

const LIGHT_GLASS: GlassSettings = {
  distortionStrength: 40,
  frostBlur: 16,
  tintColor: 255,
  tintOpacity: 0.7,
  shadowSpread: 0,
  shadowBlur: 20,
  blurTint: "light",
};

type AppThemeMode = "light" | "dark";

export function useTheme(): Theme {
  const themeMode = useSettingsStore((s) => s.theme);

  // âœ… Hook called directly at the top level
  const colorScheme = useColorScheme();

  // âœ… Initial value does NOT call a hook
  const [systemMode, setSystemMode] = useState<AppThemeMode>(
    colorScheme === "dark" ? "dark" : "light"
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    // Sync when useColorScheme changes
    setSystemMode(colorScheme === "dark" ? "dark" : "light");
  }, [colorScheme]);

  useEffect(() => {
    // Also listen directly to Android/iOS appearance changes
    const subscription = Appearance.addChangeListener(({ colorScheme }) => {
      setSystemMode(colorScheme === "dark" ? "dark" : "light");
    });

    return () => subscription.remove();
  }, []);

  const mode: AppThemeMode =
    themeMode === "system" ? systemMode : (themeMode as AppThemeMode);

  if (mode === "light") {
    return {
      mode,
      background: "#ffffff",
      surface: "#f4f4f6",
      surfaceAlt: "#e8e8ec",
      text: "#0a0a0a",
      textSecondary: "#070708",
      primary: "#f92c47",
      border: "#e0e0e4",
      inactive: "#98989e",
      overlay: "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",

      isDark: false,
      accent: "#f92c47",
      success: "#34C759",
      warning: "#FF9500",
      error: "#FF3B30",
      glassTint: "light",

      glass: LIGHT_GLASS,
      welcomeBg: "#D60017",
      popupColour: "#FFFFFF",

      miniArtBorder: "rgba(0,0,0,0.15)",
      miniDivider: "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.27)",
    };
  }

  return {
    mode,
    background: "#000000da",
    surface: "#1c1c1e",
    surfaceAlt: "#1c1c22",
    text: "#ffffff",
    textSecondary: "#98989e",
    primary: "#f92d48",

    border: "#22222a",
    inactive: "#98989e",
    overlay: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)",

    isDark: true,
    accent: "#f92c47",
    success: "#32D74B",
    warning: "#FFD60A",
    error: "#FF453A",
    glassTint: "dark",

    glass: DARK_GLASS,
    welcomeBg: "#D60017",
    popupColour: "#1c1c1e",

    miniArtBorder: "rgba(255,255,255,0.2)",
    miniDivider: "rgba(255,255,255,0.15)",
  };
}

========== src\hooks\useUserName.ts ==========
// src/hooks/useUserName.ts
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useUserStore } from "../store/userStore";

export function useUserName(): string {
  const name = useUserStore((s) => s.name);
  const loaded = useUserStore((s) => s.loaded);
  const load = useUserStore((s) => s.load);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!loaded) load();
  }, [loaded, load]);

  return (name ?? "").trim();
}

// âœ… VolumeIcon â€” ALWAYS renders the speaker + 3 arcs SVG.
//   Arc opacity is derived from `level` (0..1):
//     level = 0            â†’ speaker body only, all arcs hidden (opacity 0)
//     0 < level â‰¤ 0.45     â†’ arc 1 full, arcs 2 & 3 dimmed
//     0.45 < level â‰¤ 0.75  â†’ arcs 1 & 2 full, arc 3 dimmed
//     level > 0.75         â†’ all 3 arcs full
//
//   âš ï¸ The icon NEVER swaps to the PNG â€” that PNG is only rendered by
//      VolumeMuteIcon, which callers import explicitly if they want it.
// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
export const VolumeIcon = ({
  size = 24,
  color = "#fff",
  level = 1,
}: {
  size?: number;
  color?: string;
  level?: number;
}) => {
  const v = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, level));

  // 72Ã—48 viewBox scaled to `size` width (keeps aspect ratio).
  const w = size;
  const h = Math.round((size * 48) / 72);

  // Arc opacity by volume level.
  // At v = 0 â†’ all arcs are invisible (only the speaker body shows).
  const arc1Opacity = v > 0 ? 1 : 0;
  const arc2Opacity = v > 0.45 ? 1 : v > 0 ? 0.28 : 0;
  const arc3Opacity = v > 0.75 ? 1 : v > 0 ? 0.28 : 0;

return (
  <Svg width={w} height={h} viewBox="0 0 72 48" fill="none">
    {/* Narrower speaker body */}
    <Path
      d="M3 17H10L24 3C26 1 29 3 29 6V42C29 45 26 47 24 45L10 31H3C1 31 0 29 0 27V21C0 19 1 17 3 17Z"
      fill={color}
    />

    {/* Arc 1 â€” reduced gap */}
    <Path
      d="M34 15C39 20 39 28 34 33"
      stroke={color}
      strokeWidth={5}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      opacity={arc1Opacity}
    />

    {/* Arc 2 */}
    <Path
      d="M43 9C52 17 52 31 43 39"
      stroke={color}
      strokeWidth={5}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      opacity={arc2Opacity}
    />

    {/* Arc 3 */}
    <Path
      d="M54 3C67 15 67 33 54 45"
      stroke={color}
      strokeWidth={5}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      opacity={arc3Opacity}
    />
  </Svg>
);

};

// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
// VolumeMuteIcon â€” explicit mute PNG. Only rendered when a caller
// imports and uses this component directly. VolumeIcon never does.
// â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  <Image

    style={{
      width: size,

========== src\navigation\navigationService.ts ==========
// src/navigation/navigationService.ts
//
// One shared navigation ref, so components that live OUTSIDE the tab stacks
// (like the MiniPlayer, which is mounted in <Tabs/>) can still open screens
// that live INSIDE the Home / Library stacks.
import {
  createNavigationContainerRef,
  CommonActions,
} from "@react-navigation/native";

export const navigationRef = createNavigationContainerRef<any>();

/** Name of the tab that is currently visible ("Home" | "Library"). */
function getActiveTabName(): "Home" | "Library" {
  try {
    const root: any = navigationRef.getRootState();
    const tabsRoute = root?.routes?.find((r: any) => r.name === "Tabs");
    const tabState: any = tabsRoute?.state;
    const name = tabState?.routes?.[tabState?.index ?? 0]?.name;
    if (name === "Library") return "Library";
  } catch {
    // fall through
  }
  return "Home";
}

/**
 * Open the album details page inside the active tab's stack.
 * Returns false when navigation is not ready yet.
 */
export function openAlbumDetails(params: {
  albumName: string;
  songIds: any[];
}): boolean {
  if (!navigationRef.isReady()) return false;

  const tab = getActiveTabName();

  navigationRef.dispatch(
    CommonActions.navigate({
      name: "Tabs",
      params: {
        screen: tab,
        params: { screen: "AlbumDetails", params },
      },
    }),
  );
  return true;
}

// expo-blur: BlurView works everywhere. On Android, expo-blur SDK 54+ also
// needs BlurTargetView (the content that gets blurred). It is read safely
// here, so older SDKs (where it does not exist) still work.
const BlurView = ExpoBlur.BlurView;
const BlurTargetView: React.ComponentType<any> | undefined = (ExpoBlur as any)
  .BlurTargetView;

// NOTE: the navbar glass settings now live in the theme file
// (src/hooks/useTheme.ts â†’ theme.glass). Change the glass look there.

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

// Navbar height is written ONLY in styles.barRow (bottom of this file).
// This helper reads it from there so there is one place to change it.
function getNavbarHeight(): number {
  return (StyleSheet.flatten(styles.barRow).height as number) ?? 56;
}

// Bottom margin is also written ONLY in styles.barRow (`bottom`).
function getNavbarBottomMargin(): number {
  return (StyleSheet.flatten(styles.barRow).bottom as number) ?? 0;
}

export function useGlassTabBarSpace() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  return getNavbarHeight() + getNavbarBottomMargin() + insets.bottom + 12;
}

  size: number;
  gap: number;
  marginTop: number;
  iconMarginBottom?: number; // new: space under the icon
  labelMarginBottom?: number; // new: space under the label
  paddingTop: number;
  paddingBottom: number;
  labelSize: number;
};
const HOME_STYLE: NavItemStyle = {
  size: 26.5,
  gap: 0,
  marginTop: 0,
  paddingTop: 0,
  paddingBottom: 0,
  labelSize: 11,
};

const NEW_STYLE: NavItemStyle = {
  size: 21,
  gap: -1,
  marginTop: 4,
  paddingTop: 0,
  paddingBottom: 0,
  labelSize: 11,
};

const RADIO_STYLE: NavItemStyle = {
  size: 35,
  gap: 0,
  marginTop: -3,
  iconMarginBottom: 0,
  labelMarginBottom: 8,
  paddingTop: 0,
  paddingBottom: 0,
  labelSize: 11,
};

const LIBRARY_STYLE: NavItemStyle = {
  size: 25,
  gap: 0,
  marginTop: 0,
  paddingTop: 0,
  paddingBottom: 0,
  labelSize: 11,
};

const SEARCH_STYLE: NavItemStyle = {
  size: 23,
  gap: 1,
  marginTop: 0,
  paddingTop: 0,
  paddingBottom: 0,
  labelSize: 11,
};

// âœ… Nav items: Home â†’ New â†’ Radio â†’ Library â†’ Search
type NavItemName = "Home" | "New" | "Radio" | "Library" | "Search";

const TAB_ITEMS: {
  name: NavItemName;
  label: string;
  tabIndex: number;
  style: NavItemStyle;
  icon: (color: string, size: number) => React.ReactNode;
}[] = [
  {
    name: "Home",
    label: "Home",
    tabIndex: 0,
    style: HOME_STYLE,
    icon: (c, s) => (
      <Image
        source={HOME_ICON_PNG}
        style={{ width: s, height: s, tintColor: c }}
        resizeMode="contain"
        fadeDuration={0} // âœ… Instant show
      />
    ),
  },
  {
    name: "New",
    label: "New",
    tabIndex: 1,
    style: NEW_STYLE,
    icon: (c, s) => (
      <Image
        source={GRID_ICON_PNG}
        style={{ width: s, height: s, tintColor: c }}
        resizeMode="contain"
        fadeDuration={0} // âœ… Instant show
      />
    ),
  },
  {
    name: "Radio",
    label: "Radio",
    tabIndex: 2,
    style: RADIO_STYLE,
    icon: (c, s) => <RadioIcon size={s} color={c} />,
  },
  {
    name: "Library",
    label: "Library",
    tabIndex: 3,
    style: LIBRARY_STYLE,
    icon: (c, s) => (
      <Image
        source={LIBRARY_ICON_PNG}
        style={{ width: s, height: s, tintColor: c }}
        resizeMode="contain"
        fadeDuration={0} // âœ… Instant show
      />
    ),

  {
    name: "Search",
    label: "Search",
    tabIndex: 4,

    icon: (c, s) => <SearchIcon size={s} color={c} />,
  },
];

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Tab bar colours
// (tab bar background colour is now written in styles.barRow)
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
function useTabBarColors() {
  const theme = useTheme();
  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";

  return {
    theme,
    isDark,
    PILL_BG: isDark ? "rgba(131, 17, 17, 0.1)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.06)",
  };
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Ripple + dots + tab button
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const FIRST_RIPPLE_COLOR = "#7C797A";
const NORMAL_RIPPLE_COLOR = "#FF2D55";
const RIPPLE_DURATION = 550;
const NAV_DELAY = 140; // ms to wait before switching tab

// Animated Pressable keeps the existing tab layout and touch behaviour.
const AnimatedPressable = Animated.createAnimatedComponent(Pressable);

type TabButtonProps = {
  item: (typeof TAB_ITEMS)[number];
  isActive: boolean;
  color: string;
  rippleColor: string;
  onSelect: () => void; // runs instantly on tap (colour change)
  onPress: () => void; // runs after NAV_DELAY (screen switch)
  entryProgress: { value: number };
};

function TabButton({
  item,
  isActive,
  color,
  rippleColor,
  onSelect,
  onPress,
  entryProgress,
}: TabButtonProps) {
  const navTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    return () => {
      if (navTimer.current) clearTimeout(navTimer.current);
    };
  }, []);

  // 1 = finished / hidden, 0 = just tapped
  const progress = useSharedValue(1);
  const animatedRippleColor = useSharedValue<string>(rippleColor);
  const s = item.style;

  // All five tabs share this progress, so they slide left together
  // while keeping their original parent positions and gaps.
  const entryStyle = useAnimatedStyle(() => ({
    opacity: interpolate(entryProgress.value, [0, 1], [0, 1]),
    transform: [
      {
        translateX: interpolate(
          entryProgress.value,
          [0, 1],
          [360, 0],
          Extrapolation.CLAMP,
        ),
      },
    ],
  }));

  const rippleStyle = useAnimatedStyle(
    (): ViewStyle => ({
      backgroundColor: animatedRippleColor.value,
      opacity: interpolate(progress.value, [0, 1], [0.4, 0]),
      transform: [{ scale: interpolate(progress.value, [0, 1], [0.6, 1]) }],
    }),
  );

  const handlePress = () => {
    // Capture this tap's colour before the parent updates for later taps.
    animatedRippleColor.value = rippleColor;
    onSelect();

    progress.value = 0;
    progress.value = withTiming(1, {
      duration: RIPPLE_DURATION,
      easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
    });

    if (navTimer.current) clearTimeout(navTimer.current);
    navTimer.current = setTimeout(onPress, NAV_DELAY);
  };

  return (
    <AnimatedPressable
      accessibilityRole="button"
      accessibilityState={{ selected: isActive }}
      accessibilityLabel={item.label}
      onPress={handlePress}
      style={[
        styles.item,
        entryStyle,
        {
          gap: s.gap,
          paddingTop: s.paddingTop,
          paddingBottom: s.paddingBottom,
        },
      ]}
    >
      {/* big ripple, centred on the whole item (icon + label) */}
      <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.effectLayer}>
        <Animated.View
          pointerEvents="none"
          style={[styles.ripple, { backgroundColor: rippleColor }, rippleStyle]}
        />
      </View>

      {/* icon: optional bottom margin (only tabs that set it are affected) */}
      <View
        style={[styles.iconWrap, { marginBottom: s.iconMarginBottom ?? 0 }]}
      >
        {item.icon(color, s.size)}
      </View>

      {/* label: optional bottom margin (only tabs that set it are affected) */}
      <Text
        style={[
          styles.label,
          {
            color,
            fontSize: s.labelSize,
            marginTop: s.marginTop,
            marginBottom: s.labelMarginBottom ?? 0,
          },
        ]}
      >
        {item.label}
      </Text>
    </AnimatedPressable>
  );
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Tab bar
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
type GlassTabBarProps = {
  activeIndex: number;
  hidden: boolean;
  onPress: (name: NavItemName, tabIndex: number) => void;
  blurTarget?: React.RefObject<any>; // content that BlurView blurs (Android)
};

function GlassTabBar({
  activeIndex,
  hidden,
  onPress,
  blurTarget,
}: GlassTabBarProps) {
  const { theme, isDark } = useTabBarColors();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  // bottom margin (styles.barRow.bottom) + safe area (home indicator)
  const bottomOffset = insets.bottom + getNavbarBottomMargin();
  const navbarHeight = getNavbarHeight();

  // One-time app-launch entrance: all navbar items move together
  // from right to left into their original positions.
  const navEntryProgress = useSharedValue(0);
  useEffect(() => {
    navEntryProgress.value = withTiming(1, {
      duration: 900,
      easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
    });
  }, [navEntryProgress]);

  // â”€â”€ glass values from the theme file (clamped to their allowed ranges) â”€â”€
  const glass = theme.glass;
  const distortion = clamp(glass.distortionStrength, 0, 100);
  const frostBlur = clamp(glass.frostBlur, 0, 30);
  const gray = Math.round(clamp(glass.tintColor, 0, 255));
  const tintOpacity = clamp(glass.tintOpacity, 0, 1);
  const shadowSpread = clamp(glass.shadowSpread, -55, 55);
  const shadowBlur = clamp(glass.shadowBlur, 0, 50);

  // Edge highlight: light edge on dark glass, soft dark edge on light glass.
  const borderColor = isDark
    ? `rgba(255,255,255,${0.08 + (distortion / 100) * 0.3})`
    : `rgba(0,0,0,${0.06 + (distortion / 100) * 0.12})`;

  // Shadow is softer in light mode.
  const shadowColor = isDark ? "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.18)";

  // Instant highlight: show the tapped tab as active right away,
  // without waiting for the screen switch.
  const [pendingIndex, setPendingIndex] = useState<number | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    setPendingIndex(null);
  }, [activeIndex]);
  const shownIndex = pendingIndex ?? activeIndex;

  const wrapStyle = useAnimatedStyle(() => {
    const playerProgress = playerExpansionShared.value;

    const playerShift = interpolate(
      playerProgress,
      [0, 0.2],
      [0, navbarHeight + bottomOffset + 8],
      Extrapolation.CLAMP,
    );

    return {
      transform: [{ translateY: playerShift }],
    };
  });

  if (hidden) return null;

  return (
    <Animated.View
      pointerEvents="box-none"
      style={[
        styles.barRow,
        wrapStyle,
        {
          // floating bar: lifted above the bottom edge + home indicator
          bottom: bottomOffset,
          // âœ… Distortion Strength: lens edge highlight = border brightness,
          // same on all 4 sides
          borderColor,
          // âœ… Shadow Spread + Shadow Blur (works with a transparent bar,
          // needs React Native 0.76+ / New Architecture)
          boxShadow: [
            {
              offsetX: 0,
              offsetY: -3,
              blurRadius: shadowBlur,
              spreadDistance: shadowSpread,
              color: shadowColor,
            },
          ],
        },
      ]}
    >
      {/* âœ… Glass layer: Frost Blur + Tint Color + Distortion edge */}
      <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.glassLayer}>
        {frostBlur > 0 && (
          <BlurView
            // Frost Blur 0â€“30 â†’ expo-blur intensity 0â€“100
            // (blur is applied on top of the bg colour / content behind it)
            intensity={(frostBlur / 30) * 100}
            tint={glass.blurTint}
            // Android blur (prop names differ between expo-blur versions)
            {...({
              blurMethod: "dimezisBlurView",
              experimentalBlurMethod: "dimezisBlurView",
              blurTarget,
            } as any)}
            style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
          />
        )}
        {/* Tint Color (grayscale 0â€“255) */}
        <View
          style={[
            StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
            { backgroundColor: `rgba(${gray},${gray},${gray},${tintOpacity})` },
          ]}
        />
      </View>

      {/* âœ… Removed animated opacity wrapper for 0 waiting time */}
      <View style={styles.tabsLayer}>
        {TAB_ITEMS.map((item) => {
          const isActive = item.tabIndex === shownIndex;
          const contentColor = isActive ? theme.primary : theme.textSecondary;

          return (
            <TabButton
              key={item.name}
              item={item}
              entryProgress={navEntryProgress}
              isActive={isActive}
              color={contentColor}
              // Clicking the already-active tab = pink.
              // Switching from another active tab = grey.
              rippleColor={isActive ? NORMAL_RIPPLE_COLOR : FIRST_RIPPLE_COLOR}
              onSelect={() => {
                setPendingIndex(item.tabIndex);
              }}
              onPress={() => onPress(item.name, item.tabIndex)}
            />
          );
        })}
      </View>
    </Animated.View>
  );
}

function Tabs() {
  const theme = useTheme();

  const tabNavigationRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const [activeIndex, setActiveIndex] = useState(0);

  // content behind the navbar = what the glass blurs
  const blurTargetRef = useRef<any>(null);
  const TargetView: React.ComponentType<any> = BlurTargetView ?? View;

  const currentRoute = useNavigationState((state) => {
    const route = state.routes[state.index];
    return route?.name;
  });

  const handlePress = (name: NavItemName, tabIndex: number) => {
    const navigation = tabNavigationRef.current;
    if (!navigation) return;

    const initialScreens: Record<string, string> = {
      Home: "HomeMain",
      New: "NewMain",
      Radio: "RadioMain",
      Library: "LibraryMain",
      Search: "SearchMain",
    };

    if (tabIndex === activeIndex) {
      const initial = initialScreens[name];
      if (initial) {
        navigation.navigate(name, { screen: initial });
      }
      return;
    }

    navigation.navigate(name);
  };

  return (
    <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.background }}>
      <TargetView ref={blurTargetRef} style={{ flex: 1 }}>
        <Tab.Navigator
          id="MainTabs"
          tabBar={() => null}
          screenListeners={({ navigation }) => {
            tabNavigationRef.current = navigation;
            return {
              state: (e: any) => {
                const index = e?.data?.state?.index;
                if (typeof index === "number") setActiveIndex(index);
              },
            };
          }}
          screenOptions={{ headerShown: false, animation: "none", lazy: false }}
        >
          <Tab.Screen name="Home" component={HomeStackNavigator} />
          <Tab.Screen name="New" component={NewStackNavigator} />
          <Tab.Screen name="Radio" component={RadioStackNavigator} />
          <Tab.Screen name="Library" component={LibraryStackNavigator} />
          <Tab.Screen name="Search" component={SearchStackNavigator} />
        </Tab.Navigator>
      </TargetView>

      {/* soft fade behind the status bar â€” content scrolls under it */}
      <TopStatusGlass />

      <MiniPlayer blurTargetRef={blurTargetRef} />
      <VideoPlayerHost />

      <GlassTabBar
        activeIndex={activeIndex}
        hidden={currentRoute === "MusicPlayer"}
        onPress={handlePress}
        blurTarget={blurTargetRef}
      />
    </View>
  );
}

export default function RootNavigator() {
  const theme = useTheme();
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    ScreenOrientation.lockAsync(
      ScreenOrientation.OrientationLock.PORTRAIT_UP,
    ).catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (ready) {
      SplashScreen.hideAsync().catch(console.warn);
    }
  }, [ready]);

  const hydrateSettings = useSettingsStore((s) => s.hydrate);
  const hydrateFavorites = useMediaStore((s) => s.hydrateFavorites);

  useEffect(() => {
    let mounted = true;

    async function initialize() {
      try {
        await hydrateSettings();
        await hydrateFavorites();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("[RootNavigator] Initialization failed:", error);
      } finally {
        if (mounted) {
          setReady(true);
        }
      }
    }

    void initialize();

    return () => {
      mounted = false;
    };
  }, [hydrateSettings, hydrateFavorites]);

  useEffect(() => {
    const openSharedVideo = (url: string | null) => {
      if (!url) return;

      const match = url.match(/(?:localmedia:\/\/video\/|\/video\/)([^/?#]+)/i);
      if (!match?.[1]) return;

      let videoId = match[1];
      try {
        videoId = decodeURIComponent(videoId);
      } catch {}

      const index = MUSIC_VIDEOS.findIndex((video) => video.id === videoId);
      if (index < 0) return;

      const videoStore = useVideoStore.getState();
      videoStore.playQueue(MUSIC_VIDEOS, index);
      videoStore.setExpanded(true);
    };

    let mounted = true;
    Linking.getInitialURL()
      .then((url) => {
        if (mounted) openSharedVideo(url);
      })
      .catch(() => {});

    const subscription = Linking.addEventListener("url", ({ url }) => {
      openSharedVideo(url);
    });

    return () => {
      mounted = false;
      subscription.remove();
    };
  }, []);

  const navTheme =
    theme.mode === "dark"
      ? {
          ...DarkTheme,
          colors: {
            ...DarkTheme.colors,
            background: theme.background,
            card: theme.surface,
            text: theme.text,
            primary: theme.primary,
            border: theme.border,
            notification: theme.primary,
          },
        }
      : {
          ...DefaultTheme,
          colors: {
            ...DefaultTheme.colors,
            background: theme.background,
            card: theme.surface,
            text: theme.text,
            primary: theme.primary,
            border: theme.border,
            notification: theme.primary,
          },
        };

  return (
    <NavigationContainer ref={navigationRef} theme={navTheme}>
      <View style={{ flex: 1, backgroundColor: theme.background }}>
        <Stack.Navigator id="RootStack" screenOptions={{ headerShown: false }}>
          <Stack.Screen name="Tabs" component={Tabs} />
          <Stack.Screen name="Queue" component={QueueScreen} />
          <Stack.Screen
            name="Settings"
            component={SettingsScreen}
            options={{ animation: "slide_from_right" }}
          />
          <Stack.Screen
            name="Account"
            component={AccountScreen}
            options={{ animation: "slide_from_right" }}
          />
        </Stack.Navigator>
      </View>
    </NavigationContainer>
  );
}

// ===========================================================
// âœ… NAVBAR STYLESHEET â€” all navbar css is written here manually
// ===========================================================
const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  // â”€â”€ navbar container: height, width (left/right), bg colour, shadow â”€â”€
  barRow: {
    position: "absolute",
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    zIndex: 1001,

    // size
    left: 16, // side margin
    right: 16, // side margin
    bottom: 12, // bottom margin (safe-area is added at runtime)
    height: 64, // navbar height
    borderRadius: 50, // 50% of height = full pill

    // border on all sides (colour is set at runtime from the theme)
    borderWidth: 1,
    borderColor: "rgba(234, 226, 226, 0.9)",

    // background colour: transparent (glass layer draws blur + tint)
    backgroundColor: "transparent",

    // shadow comes from boxShadow (spread + blur) set in GlassTabBar
  },

  // blur + tint live here, clipped to the navbar area
  glassLayer: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    borderRadius: 31, // barRow radius (32) minus the 1px border
    overflow: "hidden",
  },

  // overflow hidden is here (not on barRow) so the ripple never shows
  // above the nav bar, but the container shadow is not clipped.
  tabsLayer: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    width: "100%",
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    borderRadius: 31, // barRow radius (32) minus the 1px border, ripple clipped round
    overflow: "hidden",
  },

  item: {
    flex: 1,
    height: "100%",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
  },

  iconWrap: {
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
  },

  // full-size centred layer used by both the ripple and the dots
  effectLayer: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
  },

  ripple: {
    position: "absolute",
    width: 100, // ripple circle size
    height: 100,
    borderRadius: 50, // half of width/height
    opacity: 0,
  },

  label: {
    fontWeight: "600",
    textAlign: "center",
  },
});

========== src\navigation\RootNavigator.tsx ==========
// src/navigation/RootNavigator.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  NavigationContainer,
  DefaultTheme,
  DarkTheme,
  useNavigationState,
} from "@react-navigation/native";
import { createNativeStackNavigator } from "@react-navigation/native-stack";
import { createBottomTabNavigator } from "@react-navigation/bottom-tabs";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Image,
  Pressable,
  Linking,
  type ViewStyle,
} from "react-native";
import Animated, {
  Easing,
  Extrapolation,
  interpolate,
  useAnimatedStyle,
  useSharedValue,
  withTiming,
} from "react-native-reanimated";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import * as SplashScreen from "expo-splash-screen";
import * as ScreenOrientation from "expo-screen-orientation";
import * as ExpoBlur from "expo-blur";

const BlurView = ExpoBlur.BlurView;
const BlurTargetView: React.ComponentType<any> | undefined = (ExpoBlur as any)
  .BlurTargetView;

function clamp(value: number, min: number, max: number) {
  return Math.min(max, Math.max(min, value));
}

function getNavbarHeight(): number {
  return (StyleSheet.flatten(styles.barRow).height as number) ?? 56;
}

function getNavbarBottomMargin(): number {
  return (StyleSheet.flatten(styles.barRow).bottom as number) ?? 0;
}

export function useGlassTabBarSpace() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  return getNavbarHeight() + getNavbarBottomMargin() + insets.bottom + 12;
}

};
