  | { kind: "song"; key: string; ts: number; song: any }
  | { kind: "playlist"; key: string; ts: number; playlist: UserPlaylist }
  | { kind: "fav"; key: string; ts: number };

export default function LibraryScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const audio = useAudioPlayer() as any;
  const { loadAndPlay, currentSong } = audio;

  const themeRaw = useTheme() as any;
  const base =
    themeRaw && typeof themeRaw === "object" && themeRaw.background
      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : themeRaw?.theme && typeof themeRaw.theme === "object"
          ? themeRaw.theme
          : {};
  const COLORS = { ...DEFAULT_COLORS, ...base };
  const styles = createStyles(COLORS);

  const songs = useMediaStore((s: any) => s.songs ?? []);
  const favorites: Record<string, boolean> = useMediaStore(
    (s: any) => s.favoritesSong ?? {},
  );
  const { scanAll } = useMediaScanner() as any;
  const { artworkMap } = useArtworkCache(songs);
  const { tagData } = useId3Tags(songs);

  const [showCreatePlaylist, setShowCreatePlaylist] = useState(false);

  const [playlistTitle, setPlaylistTitle] = useState("");
  const [coverImage, setCoverImage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);

  const [imageMenuOpen, setImageMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const [isEditing, setIsEditing] = useState(false);
  const [items, setItems] = useState<any[]>(DEFAULT_ITEMS);
  const [visibleMap, setVisibleMap] =
    useState<Record<string, boolean>>(INITIAL_VISIBLE);

  const [pinnedSongIds, setPinnedSongIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>(
    {},
  );
  const [isEditingPins, setIsEditingPins] = useState(false);
  const [editPinnedSongIds, setEditPinnedSongIds] = useState<
    Record<string, boolean>
  >({});
  const [editPinnedPlaylistIds, setEditPinnedPlaylistIds] = useState<
    Record<string, boolean>
  >({});

  const [contextSong, setContextSong] = useState<any | null>(null);
  const [sheetVisible, setSheetVisible] = useState(false);
  const [deleteConfirmVisible, setDeleteConfirmVisible] = useState(false);
  const [deleting, setDeleting] = useState(false);

  const mounted = useRef(true);

  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(songs.length === 0);
  const scanStarted = useRef(false);

  const [tagsReady, setTagsReady] = useState(
    () => songs.length > 0 && Object.keys(tagData || {}).length > 0,
  );
  const [artReady, setArtReady] = useState(
    () => songs.length > 0 && Object.keys(artworkMap || {}).length > 0,
  );

  const [userPlaylists, setUserPlaylists] = useState<UserPlaylist[]>([]);

  const scrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const onScroll = useMemo(
    () =>
      Animated.event([{ nativeEvent: { contentOffset: { y: scrollY } } }], {
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
    [scrollY],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadSavedLibraryConfig();
    loadPinnedSongs();
  }, []);

  const loadPinnedSongs = async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PINNED_SONGS);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
          setPinnedSongIds(parsed);
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] load pinned songs failed:", e);
    }
  };

  const loadUserPlaylists = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      const list: UserPlaylist[] = Array.isArray(parsed) ? parsed : [];
      list.sort(
        (a, b) =>
          (b.updatedAt ?? b.createdAt ?? 0) - (a.updatedAt ?? a.createdAt ?? 0),
      );
      if (mounted.current) setUserPlaylists(list);
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] load playlists failed:", e);
      if (mounted.current) setUserPlaylists([]);
    }
  }, []);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      loadUserPlaylists();
      loadPinnedSongs();
    }, [loadUserPlaylists]),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length > 0) {
      setScanning(false);
      return;
    }
    if (scanStarted.current) return;
    scanStarted.current = true;
    setScanning(true);
    Promise.resolve(scanAll?.())
      .catch((e: any) => console.error("[Library] scan failed:", e))
      .finally(() => {
        if (mounted.current) setScanning(false);
      });
  }, [songs.length]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length === 0) {
      setTagsReady(false);
      setArtReady(false);
      return;
    }

    const tagCount = Object.keys(tagData || {}).length;
    const artCount = Object.keys(artworkMap || {}).length;

    if (!tagsReady && tagCount > 0) setTagsReady(true);
    if (!artReady && artCount > 0) setArtReady(true);

    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      setTagsReady(true);
      setArtReady(true);
    }, TAG_WAIT_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [songs, tagData, artworkMap, tagsReady, artReady]);

  const [bootMinDone, setBootMinDone] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (MIN_LOADER_VISIBLE_MS <= 0) {
      setBootMinDone(true);
      return;
    }
    const t = setTimeout(() => setBootMinDone(true), MIN_LOADER_VISIBLE_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  const navigateTo = useCallback(
    (name: string): boolean => {
      let n: any = nav;
      while (n) {
        const names: string[] | undefined = n.getState?.()?.routeNames;
        if (names && names.includes(name)) {
          n.navigate(name);
          return true;
        }
        n = n.getParent?.();
      }
      console.log(`[Library] screen "${name}" is not registered`);
      showToast(`"${name}" screen is not registered in the navigator`);
      return false;
    },
    [nav],
  );

  const loadSavedLibraryConfig = async () => {
    try {
      const savedOrderRaw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_LIBRARY_ORDER);
      const savedVisibleRaw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(
        STORAGE_LIBRARY_VISIBLE,
      );

      if (savedVisibleRaw) {
        setVisibleMap({ ...INITIAL_VISIBLE, ...JSON.parse(savedVisibleRaw) });
      }

      if (!savedOrderRaw) return;

      const savedOrder: string[] = JSON.parse(savedOrderRaw);

      const reordered: any[] = [];
      savedOrder.forEach((id) => {
        const found = DEFAULT_ITEMS.find((it) => it.id === id);
        if (found && !reordered.some((r) => r.id === id)) {
          reordered.push(found);
        }
      });

      DEFAULT_ITEMS.forEach((it, defaultIdx) => {
        if (reordered.some((r) => r.id === it.id)) return;

        let insertAfterId: string | null = null;
        for (let i = defaultIdx - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
          const prevId = DEFAULT_ITEMS[i].id;
          if (reordered.some((r) => r.id === prevId)) {
            insertAfterId = prevId;
            break;
          }
        }

        if (insertAfterId) {
          const pos = reordered.findIndex((r) => r.id === insertAfterId);
          reordered.splice(pos + 1, 0, it);
          return;
        }

        let insertBeforeId: string | null = null;
        for (let i = defaultIdx + 1; i < DEFAULT_ITEMS.length; i++) {
          const nextId = DEFAULT_ITEMS[i].id;
          if (reordered.some((r) => r.id === nextId)) {
            insertBeforeId = nextId;
            break;
          }
        }

        if (insertBeforeId) {
          const pos = reordered.findIndex((r) => r.id === insertBeforeId);
          reordered.splice(pos, 0, it);
          return;
        }

        reordered.push(it);
      });

      setItems(reordered);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.log("Load library config error:", e.message);
    }
  };

  const saveLibraryConfig = async () => {
    try {
      const order = items.map((it) => it.id);
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_LIBRARY_ORDER, JSON.stringify(order));
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(
        STORAGE_LIBRARY_VISIBLE,
        JSON.stringify(visibleMap),
      );
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.log("Save library config error:", e.message);
    }
  };

  const handleEditPress = () => setIsEditing(true);

  const handleDonePress = async () => {
    await saveLibraryConfig();
    setIsEditing(false);
  };

  const toggleEditMode = useCallback(() => {
    if (isEditingPins) setIsEditingPins(false);
    if (isEditing) {
      handleDonePress();
    } else {
      handleEditPress();
    }
  }, [isEditing, isEditingPins, handleDonePress]);

  const enterEditPins = useCallback(() => {
    if (isEditing) setIsEditing(false);
    setEditPinnedSongIds({ ...pinnedSongIds });
    const plMap: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    userPlaylists
      .filter((p) => p.pinned)
      .forEach((p) => {
        plMap[p.id] = true;
      });
    setEditPinnedPlaylistIds(plMap);
    setIsEditingPins(true);
  }, [isEditing, pinnedSongIds, userPlaylists]);

  const removePinnedSongInEdit = useCallback((song: any) => {
    const id = song.id || song._id;
    if (!id) return;
    setEditPinnedSongIds((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      delete next[id];
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const removePinnedPlaylistInEdit = useCallback((pl: any) => {
    if (!pl?.id) return;
    setEditPinnedPlaylistIds((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      delete next[pl.id];
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const savePins = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const validSongIds: Record<string, boolean> = {};
      Object.keys(editPinnedSongIds).forEach((id) => {
        if (songs.some((s: any) => (s.id || s._id) === id)) {
          validSongIds[id] = true;
        }
      });

      setPinnedSongIds(validSongIds);
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(
        STORAGE_PINNED_SONGS,
        JSON.stringify(validSongIds),
      );

      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      if (raw) {
        const pls = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (Array.isArray(pls)) {
          const updated = pls.map((pl: any) => ({
            ...pl,
            pinned: !!editPinnedPlaylistIds[pl.id],
          }));
          await AsyncStorage.setItem(
            STORAGE_PLAYLISTS,
            JSON.stringify(updated),
          );
          await loadUserPlaylists();
        }
      }

      showToast("Pins updated");
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] save pins failed:", e);
      showToast("Could not save pins");
    } finally {
      setIsEditingPins(false);
    }
  }, [
    editPinnedSongIds,
    editPinnedPlaylistIds,
    songs,
    loadUserPlaylists,
  ]);

  const toggleVisible = (id: string) => {
    setVisibleMap((prev) => ({ ...prev, [id]: !prev[id] }));
  };

  const onDragEnd = ({ data }: any) => setItems(data);

  const visibleItems = items.filter((it) => visibleMap[it.id] !== false);

  const handlePickImage = () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(true);
  };

  const handleTakePhoto = async () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    try {
      const perm = await ImagePicker.requestCameraPermissionsAsync();
      if (!perm.granted) {
        Alert.alert(
          "Permission needed",
          "Camera access is required to take a photo.",
        );
        return;
      }
      const res = await ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 0.85,
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: [1, 1],
      });
      if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]?.uri) {
        setCoverImage(res.assets[0].uri);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] camera error:", e);
      showToast("Could not open camera");
    }
  };

  const handleChooseFromLibrary = async () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    try {
      const perm = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
      if (!perm.granted) {
        Alert.alert(
          "Permission needed",
          "Photo library access is required to pick an image.",
        );
        return;
      }
      const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 0.85,
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: [1, 1],
      });
      if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]?.uri) {
        setCoverImage(res.assets[0].uri);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] library error:", e);
      showToast("Could not open photos");
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveCover = () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    setCoverImage(null);
  };

  const handleCreatePlaylist = async () => {
    if (!playlistTitle.trim()) {
      Alert.alert("Error", "Please enter playlist title");
      return;
    }

    setCreating(true);

    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const existing = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];

      const savedArtwork = coverImage ? await persistArtwork(coverImage) : null;

      const newPlaylist = {
        id: `pl_${Date.now()}`,
        name: playlistTitle.trim(),
        producer: "You",
        description: "",
        isFeatured: false,
        artwork: savedArtwork,
        songs: [],
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        updatedAt: Date.now(),
      };

      const updated = [newPlaylist, ...existing];
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS, JSON.stringify(updated));

      await loadUserPlaylists();

      setPlaylistTitle("");
      setCoverImage(null);
      setShowCreatePlaylist(false);

      showToast("Playlist created successfully");
      navigateTo(ROUTE_MAP.playlists);
    } catch (err: any) {
      Alert.alert("Error", err.message || "Failed to create playlist");
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  };

  const renderIcon = (item: any) => {
    if (item.type === "emoji") {
      return <Text style={{ fontSize: 20 }}>{item.emoji}</Text>;
    }
    if (item.type === "star") {
      return (
        <StarFillIcon
          color={item.tint ?? COLORS.primary}
          size={item.size ?? 18}
        />
      );
    }
    if (item.type === "albumsvg") {
      return (
        <AlbumsSvgIcon
          color={item.tint ?? COLORS.primary}
          size={item.size ?? 24}
        />
      );
    }
    if (item.type === "artists") {
      const size = item.size ?? 26;
      return (
        <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="3.5 4 17 16" fill="none">
          <Path
            fill={COLORS.primary}
            d="M18.39 9.42c1.289-1.282 1.34-2.908.102-4.139-1.23-1.216-2.85-1.186-4.138.103zm-6.08 9.858a.66.66 0 0 0 .667-.667v-4.328l-.051-1.048 2.234-2.072c.842.11 1.728-.235 2.49-1.004l-4.03-4.035c-.776.761-1.098 1.633-.988 2.475l-6.805 7.332c-.278.3-.322.717.022 1.062L4.913 18.2a.31.31 0 0 0 .037.418l.212.22a.31.31 0 0 0 .425.029l1.208-.945c.33.344.755.3 1.048.022l3.8-3.516v4.182a.66.66 0 0 0 .667.667zm-5.053-2.073-.674-.674 6.453-6.84q.186.307.461.593.274.276.571.476z"
          />
        </Svg>
      );
    }
    if (item.type === "png" || item.type === "song") {
      const tint = item.tint ?? COLORS.primary;
      const size = item.size ?? 22;
      return (
        <Image
          source={item.source}
          style={{ width: size, height: size, tintColor: tint }}
          resizeMode="contain"
          fadeDuration={0}
        />
      );
    }
    return <Text style={{ fontSize: 18 }}>ðŸ“</Text>;
  };

  const renderEditRow = ({ item, drag, isActive }: any) => {
    const checked = visibleMap[item.id] !== false;

    return (
      <ScaleDecorator>
        <TouchableOpacity
          activeOpacity={0.9}
          onLongPress={drag}
          disabled={isActive}
          style={[styles.editRow, isActive && styles.editRowActive]}
        >
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => toggleVisible(item.id)}
            style={styles.checkboxTouch}
            hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
          >
            <View style={[styles.checkbox, checked && styles.checkboxChecked]}>
              {checked && <Text style={styles.checkmark}>âœ“</Text>}
            </View>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <View style={styles.iconWrapper}>{renderIcon(item)}</View>

          <Text style={styles.rowLabel}>{item.label}</Text>

          <View style={styles.dragHandle}>
            <View style={styles.dragLine} />
            <View style={styles.dragLine} />
            <View style={styles.dragLine} />
          </View>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </ScaleDecorator>
    );
  };

  const renderNormalRow = (item: any, index: number) => (
    <View key={item.id}>
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={styles.row}
        activeOpacity={0.7}
        onPress={() => {
          const target = ROUTE_MAP[item.id];
          if (target) navigateTo(target);
        }}
      >
        <View style={styles.iconWrapper}>{renderIcon(item)}</View>
        <Text style={styles.rowLabel}>{item.label}</Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>

      {index < visibleItems.length - 1 && <View style={styles.divider} />}
    </View>
  );

  const pinnedPlaylists = useMemo(
    () => userPlaylists.filter((p) => p.pinned),
    [userPlaylists],
  );

  const pinnedSongs = useMemo(() => {
    return songs.filter((s: any) => pinnedSongIds[s.id || s._id]);
  }, [songs, pinnedSongIds]);

  const displayPinnedSongs = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isEditingPins) return pinnedSongs;
    return songs.filter((s: any) => editPinnedSongIds[s.id || s._id]);
  }, [isEditingPins, pinnedSongs, songs, editPinnedSongIds]);

  const displayPinnedPlaylists = useMemo(() => {
    if (!isEditingPins) return pinnedPlaylists;
    return userPlaylists.filter((p) => editPinnedPlaylistIds[p.id]);
  }, [isEditingPins, pinnedPlaylists, userPlaylists, editPinnedPlaylistIds]);

  const navigateToAlbum = useCallback(
    (song: any) => {
      const albumName = String(song?.album ?? "").trim();
      if (!albumName) {
        showToast("Album info unavailable");
        return;
      }

      const sameAlbum = songs.filter(
        (s: any) =>
          String(s?.album || "")
            .trim()
            .toLowerCase() === albumName.toLowerCase(),
      );
      const songIds = sameAlbum.map((s: any) => s.id).filter(Boolean);

      nav.navigate("AlbumDetails", {
        albumName,
        songIds,
        artwork:
          (song as any).artwork ??
          (song as any).artworkUri ??
          artworkMap[song.id],
      });
    },
    [songs, artworkMap, nav],
  );

  const renderPinnedSection = () => {
    const songList = displayPinnedSongs;
    const playlistList = displayPinnedPlaylists;
    const isEmpty = songList.length === 0 && playlistList.length === 0;

    if (isEmpty && !isEditingPins) return null;

    return (
      <View style={styles.pinnedSection}>
        {isEditingPins && (
          <View style={styles.editPinsBar}>
            <Text style={styles.editModeLabel}>Edit Pins</Text>
            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={savePins}
              hitSlop={{ top: 12, bottom: 12, left: 12, right: 12 }}
              activeOpacity={0.7}
            >
              <Text style={styles.editPinsSave}>âœ“</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        )}

        {isEmpty ? (
          <Text style={styles.noPinsText}>No pinned items yet.</Text>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.pinnedGrid}>
            {songList.map((s: any) => {
              const sid = s.id || s._id;
              const art =
                (s as any).artwork ??
                (s as any).artworkUri ??
                artworkMap[sid];

              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={`pinned_song_${sid}`}
                  activeOpacity={isEditingPins ? 1 : 0.75}
                  style={styles.pinnedCard}
                  onPress={() => {
                    if (isEditingPins) return;
                    navigateToAlbum(s);
                  }}
                  onLongPress={
                    isEditingPins
                      ? undefined
                      : () => {
                          setContextSong({ ...s, artwork: art });
                          setSheetVisible(true);
                        }
                  }
                  delayLongPress={350}
                >
                  <View style={styles.pinnedArt}>
                    {art ? (
                      <Image
                        source={{ uri: art }}
                        style={styles.pinnedArtImg}
                        resizeMode="cover"
                        fadeDuration={0}
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <View style={styles.pinnedFallbackArt}>
                        <DefaultSongArt style={styles.pinnedArtImg} />
                      </View>
                    )}

                    {isEditingPins && (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        style={styles.minusBadge}
                        onPress={() => removePinnedSongInEdit(s)}
                        hitSlop={10}
                        activeOpacity={0.8}
                      >
                        <Text style={styles.minusBadgeText}>âˆ’</Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    )}
                  </View>

                  <Text style={styles.pinnedName} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {s.title || "Unknown"}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}

            {playlistList.map((pl) => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={`pinned_${pl.id}`}
                activeOpacity={isEditingPins ? 1 : 0.75}
                style={styles.pinnedCard}
                onPress={() => {
                  if (isEditingPins) return;
                  nav.navigate("PlaylistDetails", { playlistId: pl.id });
                }}
              >
                <View style={styles.pinnedArt}>
                  {pl.artwork ? (
                    <Image
                      source={{ uri: pl.artwork }}
                      style={styles.pinnedArtImg}
                      resizeMode="cover"
                      fadeDuration={0}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <View style={styles.pinnedFallbackArt}>
                      <UserPlaylistIcon size={70} color={COLORS.inactive} />
                    </View>
                  )}

                  {isEditingPins && (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={styles.minusBadge}
                      onPress={() => removePinnedPlaylistInEdit(pl)}
                      hitSlop={10}
                      activeOpacity={0.8}
                    >
                      <Text style={styles.minusBadgeText}>âˆ’</Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  )}
                </View>

                <Text style={styles.pinnedName} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {pl.name}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>
        )}
      </View>
    );
  };

  const playlistPreview = useMemo(
    () => userPlaylists.slice(0, PLAYLIST_PREVIEW_LIMIT),
    [userPlaylists],
  );

  const mergedGrid = useMemo<MergedItem[]>(() => {
    const items: MergedItem[] = [];

    songs.forEach((song: any) => {
      const id = song.id || song._id;
      items.push({
        kind: "song",
        key: `song_${id}`,
        ts: getSongTimestamp(song),
        song,
      });
    });

    playlistPreview.forEach((pl) => {
      items.push({
        kind: "playlist",
        key: `pl_${pl.id}`,
        ts: pl.updatedAt ?? pl.createdAt ?? 0,
        playlist: pl,
      });
    });

    items.sort((a, b) => {
      if (b.ts !== a.ts) return b.ts - a.ts;
      return a.key.localeCompare(b.key);
    });

    const pinned = items.filter(
      (it) => it.kind === "playlist" && (it as any).playlist.pinned,
    );
    const rest = items.filter(
      (it) => !(it.kind === "playlist" && (it as any).playlist.pinned),
    );
    const sorted = [...pinned, ...rest];

    sorted.push({ kind: "fav", key: "fav_songs", ts: -1 });

    return sorted;
  }, [songs, playlistPreview]);

  const handlePinToggle = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!contextSong) return;
    const id = contextSong.id || contextSong._id;
    if (!id) return;
    const next = { ...pinnedSongIds };
    if (next[id]) delete next[id];
    else next[id] = true;
    setPinnedSongIds(next);
    try {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PINNED_SONGS, JSON.stringify(next));
      showToast(next[id] ? "Pinned to Library" : "Unpinned");
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] save pinned failed:", e);
    }
    setSheetVisible(false);
    setContextSong(null);
  }, [contextSong, pinnedSongIds]);

  const handleFavouriteToggle = useCallback(() => {
    if (!contextSong) return;
    const id = contextSong.id || contextSong._id;
    if (!id) return;
    const store = useMediaStore.getState() as any;
    const fav: Record<string, boolean> = { ...(store.favoritesSong || {}) };
    const wasFav = !!fav[id];
    if (wasFav) delete fav[id];
    else fav[id] = true;
    useMediaStore.setState({ favoritesSong: fav });
    showToast(wasFav ? "Removed from Favourites" : "Added to Favourites");
    setSheetVisible(false);
    setContextSong(null);
  }, [contextSong]);

  const handlePlayNext = useCallback(() => {
    if (!contextSong) return;
    try {
      const st: any = usePlayerStore.getState();
      if (typeof st?.playNext === "function") {
        st.playNext(contextSong);
        showToast("Will play next");
      } else if (typeof (audio as any).playNext === "function") {
        (audio as any).playNext(contextSong);
        showToast("Will play next");
      } else {
        showToast("Queue not available");
      }
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not queue song");
    }
    setSheetVisible(false);
    setContextSong(null);
  }, [contextSong, audio]);

  const handleDeleteSong = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!contextSong || deleting) return;
    const id = contextSong.id || contextSong._id;
    const uri = contextSong.uri || contextSong.path;
    if (!id) {
      setDeleteConfirmVisible(false);
      setSheetVisible(false);
      setContextSong(null);
      return;
    }

    setDeleting(true);
    try {
      const playingId =
        currentSong?.id || currentSong?._id || (audio as any).currentId;
      if (playingId === id) {
        try {
          audio.stop?.();
          audio.pause?.();
        } catch {}
      }

          // ðŸ”¥ FIX: Instantly update PlayerStore and stop audio
         // ðŸ”¥ FIX: Instantly update PlayerStore and stop native background audio
           // ðŸ”¥ FIX: Instantly update PlayerStore and stop native background audio
      try {
        const ps: any = usePlayerStore.getState();
        if (ps?.currentTrack && String(ps.currentTrack.id) === String(id)) {
          
          // Force native TrackPlayer to stop and clear the queue
          try {
            await TrackPlayer.reset(); 
          } catch (e) {
            console.log("TrackPlayer reset error:", e);
          }

          // Fallback to hook methods
          try { await audio?.stop?.(); } catch (e) {}
          
          // Clear React Store
          if (typeof ps.setCurrentTrack === "function") ps.setCurrentTrack(null);
          if (typeof ps.setIsPlaying === "function") ps.setIsPlaying(false);
          if (typeof ps.clearQueue === "function") ps.clearQueue();
        } else if (Array.isArray(ps.queue)) {
          const newQueue = ps.queue.filter((s: any) => String(s.id) !== String(id));
          if (typeof ps.setQueue === "function") ps.setQueue(newQueue);
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[LibraryScreen] Failed to reset player:", e);
      }

      const store = useMediaStore.getState() as any;
      const nextSongs = (store.songs || []).filter(
        (s: any) => (s.id || s._id) !== id,
      );
      const nextFav = { ...(store.favoritesSong || {}) };
      delete nextFav[id];
      useMediaStore.setState({
        songs: nextSongs,
        favoritesSong: nextFav,
      });

      try {
        const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
        if (raw) {
          const pls = JSON.parse(raw);
          if (Array.isArray(pls)) {
            const updated = pls.map((pl: any) => ({
              ...pl,
              songs: Array.isArray(pl.songs)
                ? pl.songs.filter((s: any) => (s.id || s._id) !== id)
                : pl.songs,
            }));
            await AsyncStorage.setItem(
              STORAGE_PLAYLISTS,
              JSON.stringify(updated),
            );
            await loadUserPlaylists();
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[Library] playlist cleanup failed:", e);
      }

      if (pinnedSongIds[id]) {
        const next = { ...pinnedSongIds };
        delete next[id];
        setPinnedSongIds(next);
        try {
          await AsyncStorage.setItem(
            STORAGE_PINNED_SONGS,
            JSON.stringify(next),
          );
        } catch {}
      }

      showToast("Song deleted");
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] delete error:", e);
      showToast("Could not delete song");
    } finally {
      setDeleting(false);
      setDeleteConfirmVisible(false);
      setSheetVisible(false);
      setContextSong(null);
    }
  }, [
    contextSong,
    deleting,
    currentSong,
    audio,
    loadUserPlaylists,
    pinnedSongIds,
  ]);

  const renderGridItem = (entry: MergedItem, cardWidth: number) => {
    if (entry.kind === "fav") {
      return (
        <TouchableOpacity
          key={entry.key}
          activeOpacity={0.85}
          style={[styles.gridCard, { width: cardWidth }]}
          onPress={() => navigateTo("FavouriteSongs")}
        >
          <View
            style={[
              styles.gridArt,
              {
                width: cardWidth,
                height: cardWidth,
                backgroundColor: COLORS.surface,
                borderColor: "transparent",
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Image
              source={FAVOURITE_HERO}
              style={styles.gridArtImg}
              resizeMode="cover"
              fadeDuration={0}
            />
          </View>

          <View style={styles.favBottomRow}>
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={[
                styles.gridTitle,
                { color: COLORS.text, flex: 1, marginTop: 0 },
              ]}
            >
              Favourite Songs
            </Text>
          </View>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      );
    }

    if (entry.kind === "playlist") {
      const pl = entry.playlist;
      return (
        <TouchableOpacity
          key={entry.key}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
          style={[styles.gridCard, { width: cardWidth }]}
          onPress={() => nav.navigate("PlaylistDetails", { playlistId: pl.id })}
        >
          <View
            style={[
              styles.gridArt,
              {
                width: cardWidth,
                height: cardWidth,
                backgroundColor: COLORS.surface,
                borderColor: "transparent",
              },
            ]}
          >
            {pl.artwork ? (
              <Image
                source={{ uri: pl.artwork }}
                style={styles.gridArtImg}
                resizeMode="cover"
                fadeDuration={0}
              />
            ) : (
              <View style={styles.playlistFallbackArt}>
                <UserPlaylistIcon
                  size={Math.round(cardWidth * 0.7)}
                  color={COLORS.inactive}
                />
              </View>
            )}
          </View>

          <Text
            numberOfLines={1}
            style={[styles.gridTitle, { color: COLORS.text }]}
          >
            {pl.name}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      );
    }

    const song = entry.song;
    const songId = song.id || song._id;
    const isCurrent = currentSong?.id === songId;
    const artwork =
      (song as any).artwork ??
      (song as any).artworkUri ??
      artworkMap[songId];

    const taggedArtist = tagData[songId]?.artist;
    const artist =
      !isUnknownArtist(song.artist) && song.artist
        ? song.artist
        : taggedArtist || UNKNOWN_ARTIST;

    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        key={entry.key}
        activeOpacity={0.75}
        style={[styles.gridCard, { width: cardWidth }]}
        onPress={() => navigateToAlbum(song)}
        onLongPress={() => {
          setContextSong({ ...song, artwork });
          setSheetVisible(true);
        }}
        delayLongPress={350}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.gridArt,
            {
              width: cardWidth,
              height: cardWidth,
              backgroundColor: COLORS.surface,
              borderColor: isCurrent ? COLORS.primary : "transparent",
            },
          ]}
        >
          {artwork ? (
            <Image
              source={{ uri: artwork }}
              style={styles.gridArtImg}
              resizeMode="cover"
              fadeDuration={0}
            />
          ) : (
            <DefaultSongArt style={styles.gridArtImg} />
          )}

          {isCurrent && (
            <View
              style={{
                position: "absolute",
                bottom: 8,
                right: 8,
                width: 26,
                height: 26,
                borderRadius: 13,
                backgroundColor: COLORS.primary,
                alignItems: "center",
                justifyContent: "center",
              }}
            >
              <Text
                style={{
                  color: "#fff",
                  fontSize: 12,
                  fontWeight: "800",
                }}
              >
                â–¶
              </Text>
            </View>
          )}
        </View>

        <Text
          numberOfLines={1}
          style={[
            styles.gridTitle,
            { color: isCurrent ? COLORS.primary : COLORS.text },
          ]}
        >
          {song.title || "Unknown"}
        </Text>

        <Text
          numberOfLines={1}
          style={[styles.gridSub, { color: COLORS.textSecondary }]}
        >
          {artist}
        </Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  };

  const renderRecentlyAdded = () => {
    if (mergedGrid.length === 0) return null;

    const cols = 2;
    const gap = 12;
    const cardWidth =
      (SCREEN_W - CONTENT_H_PADDING * 2 - 8 - gap * (cols - 1)) / cols;

    return (
      <View style={styles.recentSection}>
        <View style={styles.sectionHeader}>
          <Text style={styles.sectionTitle}>Recently Added</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.gridWrap}>
          {mergedGrid.map((entry) => renderGridItem(entry, cardWidth))}
        </View>
      </View>
    );
  };

  const showLoading = !bootMinDone || (songs.length === 0 && scanning);

  const contextSongId = contextSong?.id || contextSong?._id;
  const isPinned = !!(contextSongId && pinnedSongIds[contextSongId]);
  const isFav = !!(contextSongId && favorites[contextSongId]);
  const contextArtwork =
    (contextSong as any)?.artwork ??
    (contextSong as any)?.artworkUri ??
    (contextSongId ? artworkMap[contextSongId] : undefined);

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      <Header
        title="Library"
        scrollY={scrollY}
        onPlusPress={() => setShowCreatePlaylist(true)}
        fontFamily="sans-serif"
        onEditCategories={toggleEditMode}
        onEditPins={enterEditPins}
      />

      <Animated.ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={[
          styles.content,
          { paddingTop: HEADER_SPACER + insets.top },
        ]}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        scrollEnabled={!isEditing && !showLoading && !isEditingPins}
        onScroll={onScroll}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        style={{ opacity: showLoading ? 0 : 1 }}
        pointerEvents={showLoading ? "none" : "auto"}
      >
        {!isEditing && renderPinnedSection()}

        {isEditing ? (
          <View style={styles.editListWrapper}>
            <View style={styles.editDoneRow}>
              <Text style={styles.editModeLabel}>Edit Categories</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={handleDonePress}
                hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
              >
                <Text style={styles.editBtn}>Done</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <DraggableFlatList
              data={items}
              keyExtractor={(item: any) => item.id}
              renderItem={renderEditRow}
              onDragEnd={onDragEnd}
              scrollEnabled={false}
              activationDistance={5}
            />
          </View>
        ) : isEditingPins ? (
          null
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.list}>
            {visibleItems.map((item, index) => renderNormalRow(item, index))}
            <View style={styles.listEndDivider} />
          </View>
        )}

        {!isEditing && !isEditingPins && renderRecentlyAdded()}

        <View style={{ height: 180 }} />
      </Animated.ScrollView>

      {showLoading && (
        <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.loaderOverlay}>
          <View style={styles.loaderBox}>
            <HorizontalLoader />
          </View>
        </View>
      )}

      <Modal
        visible={sheetVisible}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => {
          setSheetVisible(false);
          setContextSong(null);
        }}
      >
        <View style={styles.sheetRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.sheetBackdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => {
              setSheetVisible(false);
              setContextSong(null);
            }}
          />
          <View style={styles.songSheet}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />

            {contextSong && (
              <>
                <View style={styles.songHeader}>
                  <Image
                    source={
                      contextArtwork
                        ? { uri: contextArtwork }
                        : MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG
                    }
                    style={styles.songHeaderArt}
                    resizeMode="cover"
                    fadeDuration={0}
                  />
                  <View style={styles.songHeaderText}>
                    <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.songHeaderTitle}>
                      {contextSong.title || "Unknown"}
                    </Text>
                    <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.songHeaderArtist}>
                      {!isUnknownArtist(contextSong.artist)
                        ? contextSong.artist
                        : UNKNOWN_ARTIST}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.sheetDivider} />

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.sheetRow}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={handlePlayNext}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.sheetRowText}>Play next</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.sheetRow}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={handleFavouriteToggle}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.sheetRowText}>
                    {isFav ? "Undo favourite" : "Favourite"}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.sheetRow}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={handlePinToggle}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.sheetRowText}>
                    {isPinned ? "Unpin Album" : "Pin Album"}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.sheetRow}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setSheetVisible(false);
                    setDeleteConfirmVisible(true);
                  }}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.sheetRowText, { color: "#E53935" }]}>
                    Delete Song
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </>
            )}
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        visible={deleteConfirmVisible}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setDeleteConfirmVisible(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.confirmBackdrop}>
          <View style={styles.confirmCard}>
            <Text style={styles.confirmTitle}>Delete Song</Text>
            <Text style={styles.confirmBody}>
              Are you sure you want to delete this album from your library?
              This also removes songs from your favourites and from any
              playlist you made.
            </Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.confirmDeleteBtn}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={handleDeleteSong}
              disabled={deleting}
            >
              {deleting ? (
                <ActivityIndicator color="#fff" size="small" />
              ) : (
                <Text style={styles.confirmDeleteText}>
                  Delete FROM Library
                </Text>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.confirmCancelBtn}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
              onPress={() => {
                setDeleteConfirmVisible(false);
                setContextSong(null);
              }}
              disabled={deleting}
            >
              <Text style={styles.confirmCancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        visible={showCreatePlaylist}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setShowCreatePlaylist(false)}
      >
        <Pressable
          style={styles.createBackdrop}
          onPress={() => setShowCreatePlaylist(false)}
        >
          <Pressable
            style={styles.createSheet}
            onPress={(e: any) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <View style={styles.createTopBar}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setShowCreatePlaylist(false)}
                hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
              >
                <Text style={styles.crossIcon}>âœ•</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={handleCreatePlaylist}
                disabled={creating}
                hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
              >
                {creating ? (
                  <ActivityIndicator color={COLORS.primary} size="small" />
                ) : (
                  <Text style={styles.checkIcon}>âœ“</Text>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <TouchableOpacity
              onPress={handlePickImage}
              style={styles.coverUpload}
              activeOpacity={0.8}
            >
              {coverImage ? (
                <Image source={{ uri: coverImage }} style={styles.coverImage} />
              ) : (
                <View style={styles.coverPlaceholder}>
                  <Text style={styles.coverPlus}>+</Text>
                  <Text style={styles.coverHint}>Choose Image</Text>
                </View>
              )}
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TextInput
              style={styles.input}
              value={playlistTitle}
              onChangeText={setPlaylistTitle}
              placeholder="Playlist Title"
              placeholderTextColor={COLORS.textMuted}
              autoFocus
              maxLength={50}
            />
          </Pressable>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>

      <Modal
        visible={imageMenuOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.imageModalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.imageBackdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.imageSheet}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />
            <Text style={styles.imageSheetTitle}>Playlist cover</Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.imageOption}
              onPress={handleTakePhoto}
            >
              <Text style={styles.imageOptionText}>Take Photo</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.imageOption}
              onPress={handleChooseFromLibrary}
            >
              <Text style={styles.imageOptionText}>Your Photos</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {coverImage ? (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.imageOption}
                onPress={handleRemoveCover}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.imageOptionText, { color: "#E53935" }]}>
                  âŒ« Remove image
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ) : null}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.imageCancel}
              onPress={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.imageCancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* ðŸ‘‡ Added TopStatusGlass overlay for the edge-to-edge fade */}
      <TopStatusGlass />
    </View>
  );
}

  StyleSheet.create({

      width: 24,

      borderRadius: 12,
      backgroundColor: "#E53935",
      alignItems: "center",
      justifyContent: "center",

      shadowColor: "#000",
      shadowOpacity: 0.35,
      shadowRadius: 3,
      shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 1 },
      elevation: 3,
    },
    minusBadgeText: {
      color: "#ffffff",
      fontSize: 20,
      fontWeight: "900",
      lineHeight: 22,
      marginTop: -2,
      fontFamily: FONT,
    },

    list: { marginTop: 8 },
    row: {
      flexDirection: "row",
      alignItems: "center",
      paddingVertical: 2,
      paddingHorizontal: 0,
      minHeight: 35,
    },
    iconWrapper: {
      width: 40,
      height: 40,
      justifyContent: "center",
      alignItems: "center",
      marginRight: 16,
      backgroundColor: "transparent",
      borderWidth: 0,
    },

    listEndDivider: {
      height: DIVIDER_HEIGHT,
      backgroundColor: COLORS.divider ?? COLORS.border,
      opacity: 1,
      marginLeft: 62,
      marginBottom: 8,
    },

    rowLabel: {
      flex: 1,
      color: COLORS.text,
      fontSize: 21.5,
      fontWeight: "400",
      fontFamily: FONT,
    },

    divider: {
      height: DIVIDER_HEIGHT,
      backgroundColor: COLORS.divider ?? COLORS.border,
      marginLeft: 62,
      opacity: 1,
    },

    editListWrapper: { marginTop: 8 },
    editDoneRow: {
      flexDirection: "row",
      alignItems: "center",
      justifyContent: "space-between",
      paddingVertical: 6,
      marginBottom: 6,
    },
    editModeLabel: {
      color: COLORS.textSecondary,
      fontSize: 15,
      fontWeight: "600",
      fontFamily: FONT,
    },

    editRow: {
      flexDirection: "row",
      alignItems: "center",
      paddingVertical: 10,
      paddingHorizontal: 0,
      minHeight: 56,
      backgroundColor: COLORS.background,
    },
    editRowActive: { backgroundColor: COLORS.surfaceLight, borderRadius: 8 },
    checkboxTouch: { padding: 4, marginRight: 6 },
    checkbox: {
      width: 22,
      height: 22,
      borderRadius: 6,
      borderWidth: 2,
      borderColor: COLORS.border,
      justifyContent: "center",
      alignItems: "center",
      backgroundColor: "transparent",
    },
    checkboxChecked: {
      backgroundColor: COLORS.primary,
      borderColor: COLORS.primary,
    },
    checkmark: {
      color: "#fff",
      fontSize: 14,
      fontWeight: "900",
      lineHeight: 16,
    },
    dragHandle: {
      width: 30,
      height: 22,
      justifyContent: "center",
      alignItems: "center",
      gap: 3,
    },
    dragLine: {
      width: 18,
      height: 2,
      borderRadius: 1,
      backgroundColor: COLORS.textMuted,
    },

    recentSection: { marginTop: 24, marginBottom: 24 },
    sectionHeader: {
      flexDirection: "row",
      justifyContent: "space-between",
      alignItems: "center",
      paddingHorizontal: 0,
      marginBottom: 12,
    },

========== src\screens\Navigation\Newscreen.tsx ==========
// src/screens/Navigation/Newscreen.tsx
import React, { useMemo, useRef } from "react";
import { View, Text, StyleSheet, Animated } from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import Header from "../../components/Header";
import { useTheme } from "../../hooks/useTheme";
// ðŸ‘‡ Import TopStatusGlass
import TopStatusGlass from "../../components/TopStatusGlass";

const FONT = "serif";

const DEFAULT_COLORS = {
  background: "#000000",
  border: "#3a3a3c",
  text: "#ffffff",
  textSecondary: "#a1a1aa",
};

export default function NewScreen() {
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const themeRaw = useTheme() as any;
  const base =
    themeRaw && typeof themeRaw === "object" && themeRaw.background
      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : themeRaw?.theme && typeof themeRaw.theme === "object"
          ? themeRaw.theme
          : {};
  const COLORS = { ...DEFAULT_COLORS, ...base };
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(COLORS), [themeRaw]);

  const scrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const onScroll = useMemo(
    () =>
      Animated.event([{ nativeEvent: { contentOffset: { y: scrollY } } }], {
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
    [scrollY],
  );

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* âœ… Floating header, transparent over the status bar */}
      <Header title="New" scrollY={scrollY} />

      <Animated.ScrollView
        // âœ… Added insets.top so content sits below the notch/status bar
        contentContainerStyle={[styles.content, { paddingTop: 75 + insets.top }]}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        onScroll={onScroll}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
      >
        <Text
          style={{
            color: COLORS.textSecondary,
            paddingHorizontal: 20,
            paddingTop: 16,
          }}
        >
          Coming soon... {"\n"}New section is being updated by Rushikesh.  
        </Text>

        <View style={{ height: 500 }} />
      </Animated.ScrollView>

      {/* ðŸ‘‡ Added TopStatusGlass overlay for the edge-to-edge fade */}
      <TopStatusGlass />
    </View>
  );
}

const createStyles = (COLORS: any) =>
  StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: COLORS.background },
    content: { paddingTop: 105 },
  });

      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : themeRaw?.theme && typeof themeRaw.theme === "object"
          ? themeRaw.theme
          : {};

========== src\screens\AccountScreen.tsx ==========
// src/screens/AccountScreen.tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  TouchableOpacity,
  TextInput,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  ActivityIndicator,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
} from "react-native";
import { useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";
import * as Notifications from "expo-notifications";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { useUserStore } from "../store/userStore";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";
import { BackArrowIcon } from "../icons/VideoIcons";

const FONT = "sans-serif";

// Legacy storage keys â€” only used for one-time migration & backup write.
const LEGACY_NAME_KEY = "@account/username";
const LEGACY_NAME_SET_KEY = "@account/name_set";

const ACCOUNT_CHANNEL_ID = "account-updates";

// NOTE: The global Notifications.setNotificationHandler(...) was removed from
// this file. The single handler now lives in
// src/services/notifications/deleteNotificationService.ts (no deprecated
// `shouldShowAlert`, and it keeps delete-progress silent).

async function ensureAndroidChannel() {
  if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;
  try {
    await Notifications.setNotificationChannelAsync(ACCOUNT_CHANNEL_ID, {
      name: "Account updates",
      importance: Notifications.AndroidImportance.HIGH,
      vibrationPattern: [0, 250, 250, 250],
      lightColor: "#f92c47",
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[notif] channel setup failed:", e);
  }
}

async function ensureNotificationPermission(): Promise<boolean> {
  try {
    await ensureAndroidChannel();
    const current = await Notifications.getPermissionsAsync();
    const granted =
      current.granted ||
      current.ios?.status ===
        Notifications.IosAuthorizationStatus.PROVISIONAL ||
      current.ios?.status === Notifications.IosAuthorizationStatus.AUTHORIZED;
    if (granted) return true;

    const req = await Notifications.requestPermissionsAsync();
    return (
      req.granted ||
      req.ios?.status === Notifications.IosAuthorizationStatus.PROVISIONAL ||
      req.ios?.status === Notifications.IosAuthorizationStatus.AUTHORIZED
    );
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[notif] permission error:", e);
    return false;
  }
}

async function pushSystemNotification(title: string, body: string) {
  try {
    await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
      content: {
        title,
        body,
        sound: "default",
        ...(Platform.OS === "android" ? { channelId: ACCOUNT_CHANNEL_ID } : {}),
      },
      trigger: null,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[notif] failed to schedule:", e);
  }
}

const LIGHT_THEME = {
  mode: "light" as const,
  background: "#edeef0",
  surface: "#ffffff",
  surfaceLight: "#e2e3e5",
  border: "#c8c9cc",
  divider: "#b8b9bc",
  text: "#000000",
  textSecondary: "#3c3c43",
  textMuted: "#8e8e93",
  primary: "#f92c47",
};

const DARK_THEME = {
  mode: "dark" as const,
  background: "#2d2e30",
  surface: "#3a3b3d",
  surfaceLight: "#45464a",
  border: "#525356",
  divider: "#5a5b5e",
  text: "#ffffff",
  textSecondary: "#a1a1aa",
  textMuted: "#71717a",
  primary: "#f92c47",
};

const DISCLAIMER =
  "Your name and photo will be shown to others in playlists or while listing together.";

export default function AccountScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const themeRaw = useTheme() as any;
  const base =
    themeRaw && typeof themeRaw === "object" && themeRaw.background
      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : LIGHT_THEME;

  const COLORS = useMemo(() => {
    const isDark = (base.mode ?? "dark") === "dark";
    const fallback = isDark ? DARK_THEME : LIGHT_THEME;
    return { ...fallback, ...base };
  }, [base]);

  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(COLORS), [COLORS]);

  // â”€â”€ Single source of truth: useUserStore persists to @user_profile â”€â”€
  const name = useUserStore((s) => s.name);
  const loaded = useUserStore((s) => s.loaded);
  const setName = useUserStore((s) => s.setName);
  const load = useUserStore((s) => s.load);

  // â”€â”€ One-time load + legacy migration â”€â”€
  useEffect(() => {
    if (loaded) return;

    let alive = true;
    (async () => {
      try {
        await load();
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[account] load failed:", e);
      }

      if (!alive) return;

      // Migration: if the store has no name but the legacy key does, promote it.
      try {
        const storeNow = (useUserStore.getState().name ?? "").trim();
        if (!storeNow) {
          const legacy = (await AsyncStorage.getItem(LEGACY_NAME_KEY)) ?? "";
          const trimmed = legacy.trim();
          if (trimmed) {
            await setName(trimmed);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[account] migration failed:", e);
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      alive = false;
    };
  }, [loaded, load, setName]);

  useEffect(() => {
    ensureNotificationPermission().catch(() => {});
  }, []);

  const [editOpen, setEditOpen] = useState(false);
  const [editText, setEditText] = useState("");
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);

  // Derived display values â€” driven by the store, not local state.
  const trimmedName = name.trim();
  const hasName = trimmedName.length > 0;
  const isReady = loaded;
  const displayName = hasName
    ? trimmedName
    : isReady
      ? "Add your name"
      : "";
  const initial =
    (trimmedName.charAt(0) || "").toUpperCase() || (isReady ? "?" : "Â·");

  const openEdit = useCallback(() => {
    ensureNotificationPermission().catch(() => {});
    setEditText(trimmedName);
    setEditOpen(true);
  }, [trimmedName]);

  const closeEdit = useCallback(() => {
    if (saving) return;
    setEditOpen(false);
  }, [saving]);

  const trimmedEdit = editText.trim();
  const canContinue =
    !saving && trimmedEdit.length > 0 && trimmedEdit !== trimmedName;

  const handleContinue = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!canContinue) return;
    const t = trimmedEdit;
    setSaving(true);
    try {
      // Persist via the store (writes to @user_profile in AsyncStorage).
      await setName(t);

      // Mirror to the legacy key as a recovery backup â€” harmless on Android.
      await Promise.all([
        AsyncStorage.setItem(LEGACY_NAME_KEY, t).catch(() => {}),
        AsyncStorage.setItem(LEGACY_NAME_SET_KEY, "1").catch(() => {}),
      ]);

      // Close immediately â€” the store subscription updates the display.
      setEditOpen(false);
      setEditText("");

      // Success notification (best effort).
      const permitted = await ensureNotificationPermission();
      if (permitted) {
        await pushSystemNotification(
          `Welcome, ${t}!`,
          "Your username has been updated successfully.",
        );
      } else {
        showToast("Name saved");
      }
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not save name. Please try again.");
    } finally {
      setSaving(false);
    }
  }, [canContinue, trimmedEdit, setName]);

  const previewInitial = (editText.trim().charAt(0) || "?").toUpperCase();

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* Top bar */}
      <View style={[styles.topBar, { paddingTop: insets.top + 8 }]}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={() => nav.goBack()}
          hitSlop={10}
          activeOpacity={0.7}
        >
          <BackArrowIcon size={16} color={COLORS.primary} />
        </TouchableOpacity>
        <Text style={styles.topTitle}>Account</Text>
      </View>

      {/* Profile card */}
      <View style={styles.cardRow}>
        <View style={[styles.avatarCircle, { borderColor: COLORS.border }]}>
          <Text style={styles.avatarLetter}>{initial}</Text>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.nameBlock}>
          <Text
            numberOfLines={1}
            style={[
              styles.nameText,
              !hasName &&
                isReady && {
                  color: COLORS.textMuted,
                  fontStyle: "italic",
                },
            ]}
          >
            {displayName}
          </Text>
        </View>

        {isReady && (
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={openEdit}
            hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
            activeOpacity={0.7}
          >
            <Text style={styles.editName}>{hasName ? "Edit" : "Add"}</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        )}
      </View>

      {/* Disclaimer */}
      <View style={styles.disclaimerWrap}>
        <Text style={[styles.disclaimerText, { color: COLORS.textSecondary }]}>
          {DISCLAIMER}
        </Text>
      </View>

      {/* Divider */}
      <View style={[styles.hairline, { backgroundColor: COLORS.divider }]} />

      {/* Edit-name modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={editOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={closeEdit}
      >
        <KeyboardAvoidingView
          style={styles.modalKAV}
          behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : undefined}
        >
          <Pressable style={styles.modalBackdrop} onPress={closeEdit}>
            <Pressable
              style={styles.modalCard}
              onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <View style={styles.modalHeader}>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={closeEdit}
                  hitSlop={12}
                  disabled={saving}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.crossIcon}>âœ•</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>

              <View style={styles.modalRow}>
                <View style={styles.modalAvatarCircle}>
                  <Text style={styles.modalAvatarLetter}>
                    {previewInitial}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <TextInput
                  value={editText}
                  onChangeText={setEditText}
                  placeholder="Your name"
                  placeholderTextColor="rgba(255,255,255,0.35)"
                  style={styles.modalInput}
                  autoFocus
                  maxLength={40}
                  editable={!saving}
                  selectionColor={COLORS.primary}
                />
              </View>

              <View style={styles.modalFooter}>
                {saving ? (
                  <View style={styles.loadingRowFull}>
                    <ActivityIndicator color={COLORS.primary} size="small" />
                    <Text style={styles.loadingText}>Saving changes...</Text>
                  </View>
                ) : (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={handleContinue}
                    disabled={!canContinue}
                    activeOpacity={canContinue ? 0.8 : 1}
                    style={!canContinue && styles.continueBtnDisabled}
                  >
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.continueText,
                        !canContinue && styles.continueTextDisabled,
                      ]}
                    >
                      Continue
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                )}
              </View>
            </Pressable>
          </Pressable>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

========== src\screens\AlbumDetailsScreen.tsx ==========
// src/screens/AlbumDetailsScreen.tsx
import React, {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Image,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  FlatList,
  Animated,
  Share,
} from "react-native";
import {
  SafeAreaView,
  useSafeAreaInsets,
} from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useNavigation, useRoute } from "@react-navigation/native";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import Svg, { Path } from "react-native-svg";
import { getColors } from "react-native-image-colors";

import { GlassIconBtn } from "../components/LibraryPageHeaders";
import MiniPlayer from "../components/MiniPlayer";

import { usePlayerStore } from "../store/playerStore";

import { useArtworkCache } from "../hooks/useArtworkCache";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";
import {
  PlayIcon,
  ShuffleIcon,
  PinSongIcon,
  SkipNextIcon,
} from "../icons/Icons";

import {
  getCachedTags,
  getCachedArtwork,
  hasCachedTags,
  readAndCacheTags,
  setCachedArtwork,
} from "../services/audioTags/tagService";

const FONT = "sans-serif";
const MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG = require("../../assets/images/musicdefault.png");
const LOSSLESS_ICON = require("../../assets/icons/lossless-icon.png");
const MORE_ICON = require("../../assets/apple/moreicon.png");
const BACK_ICON = require("../../assets/icons/backicon.png");
const CREATE_PLAYLIST_ICON = require("../../assets/icons/createplaylisticona.png");

const DEFAULT_GENRE = "Bollywood";
const DEFAULT_YEAR = "2026";

const PINNED_FILE = (FileSystem.documentDirectory ?? "") + "pinned_songs.json";
const STORAGE_PLAYLISTS = "@library_playlists";

const UNKNOWN_ALBUM = "Unknown album";
const UNKNOWN_ARTIST = "Unknown artist";

const MIN_BOOT_LOADER_MS = 320;
const MAX_TAG_WAIT_MS = 3000;

const HEADER_SCROLL_DISTANCE = 180;
const HEADER_CONTENT_H = 44;

// Custom Share Icon
const ShareSvg = ({ color = "#fff", size = 18 }) => (
  <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">
    <Path
      d="M18 16.08c-.76 0-1.44.3-1.96.77L8.91 12.7c.05-.23.09-.46.09-.7s-.04-.47-.09-.7l7.05-4.11c.54.5 1.25.81 2.04.81 1.66 0 3-1.34 3-3s-1.34-3-3-3-3 1.34-3 3c0 .24.04.47.09.7L8.04 9.81C7.5 9.31 6.79 9 6 9c-1.66 0-3 1.34-3 3s1.34 3 3 3c.79 0 1.5-.31 2.04-.81l7.12 4.16c-.05.21-.08.43-.08.65 0 1.61 1.31 2.92 2.92 2.92 1.61 0 2.92-1.31 2.92-2.92s-1.31-2.92-2.92-2.92z"
      fill={color}

  </Svg>
