// â”€â”€ Color Utils â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const hexToRgb = (hex: string): [number, number, number] | null => {
  let h = (hex || "").trim().replace(/^#/, "");
  if (h.length === 3)
    h = h
      .split("")
      .map((c) => c + c)
      .join("");
  if (h.length !== 6) return null;
  const r = parseInt(h.slice(0, 2), 16);
  const g = parseInt(h.slice(2, 4), 16);
  const b = parseInt(h.slice(4, 6), 16);
  if ([r, g, b].some((n) => Number.isNaN(n))) return null;
  return [r, g, b];
};

const mixHex = (fg: string, bg: string, a: number): string => {
  const f = hexToRgb(fg);
  const b = hexToRgb(bg);
  if (!f || !b) return fg;
  const m = (i: number) =>
    Math.round(f[i] * a + b[i] * (1 - a))
      .toString(16)
      .padStart(2, "0");
  return `#${m(0)}${m(1)}${m(2)}`;
};

const cleanName = (raw?: string | null, fallback = ""): string => {
  if (!raw) return fallback;
  let s = String(raw)
    .replace(/[\(\[\{<][^\)\]\}>]*[\)\]\}>]/g, " ")
    .replace(/\bwww\.[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)+/gi, " ")
    .replace(
      /\b[a-z0-9-]+\.(?:com|net|org|in|co|se|pk|info|me|cc|xyz|live|fm|io|app|tv|club|site|online)\b/gi,
      " ",
    )
    .replace(/[\s_\-â€“â€”|:@~â€¢Â·]+/g, " ")
    .trim();
  return s || fallback;
};

const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(a.trim());
};

const syncArtwork = (
  song: any,
  artworkMap: Record<string, string>,
): string | undefined =>
  song?.artwork ??
  song?.artworkUri ??
  song?.cover ??
  getCachedArtwork(song.id) ??
  artworkMap[song.id];

const formatLongDate = (ts: number): string => {
  const d = new Date(ts);
  return d.toLocaleDateString("en-US", {
    month: "long",
    day: "numeric",
    year: "numeric",
  });
};

const formatDurationMinutes = (totalSec: number): string => {
  if (!totalSec || totalSec < 30) return "";
  const mins = Math.round(totalSec / 60);
  if (mins < 1) return "less than 1 minute";
  return `${mins} minute${mins === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
};

export default function AlbumDetailsScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const route = useRoute<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const theme = useTheme();

  const albumName: string = route.params?.albumName ?? UNKNOWN_ALBUM;
  const routeSongIds: string[] | undefined = route.params?.songIds;
  const routeArtwork: string | undefined = route.params?.artwork;

  const songs = useMediaStore((s) => s.songs ?? []);
  const favorites = useMediaStore((s) => s.favoritesSong);
  const { loadAndPlay } = useAudioPlayer();
  const { artworkMap } = useArtworkCache(songs);
  const currentTrack = usePlayerStore((s) => s.currentTrack);

  const [selectedSong, setSelectedSong] = useState<any>(null);
  const [addToMode, setAddToMode] = useState(false);
  const [playlists, setPlaylists] = useState<any[]>([]);
  const [pinnedIds, setPinnedIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const pinnedLoaded = useRef(false);
  const mounted = useRef(true);

  const [menuVisible, setMenuVisible] = useState(false);

  const [tagVersion, setTagVersion] = useState(0);
  const requestedRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());

  const [bootMinDone, setBootMinDone] = useState(false);
  const [tagsLoaded, setTagsLoaded] = useState(false);

  // âœ… Full Screen Background Color State
  const [bgColor, setBgColor] = useState(theme.background);

  // âœ… Sticky Header Animation
  const scrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const headerBgOpacity = scrollY.interpolate({
    inputRange: [HEADER_SCROLL_DISTANCE, HEADER_SCROLL_DISTANCE + 50],
    outputRange: [0, 1],
    extrapolate: "clamp",
  });
  const headerTitleOpacity = scrollY.interpolate({
    inputRange: [HEADER_SCROLL_DISTANCE, HEADER_SCROLL_DISTANCE + 50],
    outputRange: [0, 1],
    extrapolate: "clamp",
  });

  const onScroll = useMemo(
    () =>
      Animated.event([{ nativeEvent: { contentOffset: { y: scrollY } } }], {
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
    [scrollY],
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    if (MIN_BOOT_LOADER_MS <= 0) {
      setBootMinDone(true);
      return;
    }
    const t = setTimeout(() => setBootMinDone(true), MIN_BOOT_LOADER_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    Object.keys(artworkMap).forEach((id) => {
      const uri = artworkMap[id];
      if (uri) setCachedArtwork(id, uri);
    });
  }, [artworkMap]);

  const albumSongs = useMemo(() => {
    let list: any[];

    if (routeSongIds && routeSongIds.length > 0) {
      const idSet = new Set(routeSongIds);
      list = songs.filter((s: any) => idSet.has(s.id));
    } else {
      const target = cleanName(albumName, UNKNOWN_ALBUM).toLowerCase();
      list = songs.filter((s: any) => {
        const raw =
          getCachedTags(s.id)?.album ??
          (typeof s.album === "string" ? s.album : undefined);
        return cleanName(raw, UNKNOWN_ALBUM).toLowerCase() === target;
      });
    }

    const sorted = [...list].sort((a: any, b: any) =>
      (a.title ?? "").localeCompare(b.title ?? "", undefined, {
        sensitivity: "base",
      }),
    );

    // âœ… FIXED: Return ALL songs in the album instead of just the first one
    return sorted;
  }, [routeSongIds, songs, albumName, tagVersion]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (albumSongs.length === 0) {
      setTagsLoaded(true);
      return;
    }

    const missing = albumSongs.filter(
      (s: any) => !hasCachedTags(s.id) && !requestedRef.current.has(s.id),
    );
    if (missing.length === 0) {
      setTagsLoaded(true);
      return;
    }

    missing.forEach((s) => requestedRef.current.add(s.id));

    let cancelled = false;
    (async () => {
      for (const s of missing) {
        if (cancelled || !mounted.current) return;
        const uri = (s as any).uri ?? (s as any).path;
        if (uri) {
          try {
            await readAndCacheTags(s.id, uri);
          } catch {}
        }
      }
      if (!cancelled && mounted.current) {
        setTagVersion((v) => v + 1);
        setTagsLoaded(true);
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
  }, [albumSongs]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (tagsLoaded) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      if (mounted.current) setTagsLoaded(true);
    }, MAX_TAG_WAIT_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [tagsLoaded]);

  const displayTitle = (song: any): string => {
    const fromCache = getCachedTags(song.id)?.title;
    if (fromCache && String(fromCache).trim()) return cleanName(fromCache);

    const fromSong = (song as any).title;
    if (fromSong && String(fromSong).trim()) return cleanName(fromSong);

    const fromFile = (song as any).filename
      ? String((song as any).filename).replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, "")
      : "";
    return cleanName(fromFile) || "Unknown";
  };

  const displayArtist = (song: any): string => {
    const raw = getCachedTags(song.id)?.artist ?? (song as any).artist;
    const cleaned = cleanName(raw, "");
    return !isUnknownArtist(cleaned) ? cleaned : UNKNOWN_ARTIST;
  };

  const albumArtwork = useMemo(() => {
    if (routeArtwork) return routeArtwork;
    for (const s of albumSongs as any[]) {
      const a = syncArtwork(s, artworkMap);
      if (a) return a;
    }
    return undefined;
  }, [routeArtwork, albumSongs, artworkMap]);

  // âœ… Extract Album Color for Full Screen Background
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!albumArtwork) {
      setBgColor(theme.background);
      return;
    }
    let isMounted = true;
    getColors(albumArtwork, {
      fallback: theme.background,
      cache: false,
      key: albumArtwork,
    })
      .then((res: any) => {
        if (!isMounted) return;
        
        let picked = theme.background;
        
        if (res?.platform === "ios") {
          picked = res.background || res.primary || theme.background;
        } else {
          // ðŸ”¥ Use average to get the overall tint instead of pure black
          picked = res.average || res.vibrant || res.muted || res.dominant || theme.background;
        }

        // Final fallback check
        if (picked === "#000000" || picked === "#000") {
          picked = res.muted || res.vibrant || theme.background;
        }

        // ðŸ”¥ 0.25 ratio makes it a subtle dark tint (Image 1 style)
        setBgColor(mixHex(picked, theme.background, 0.25));
      })
      .catch(() => {
        if (isMounted) setBgColor(theme.background);
      });
    return () => {
      isMounted = false;
    };
  }, [albumArtwork, theme.background]);

  const withArt = useCallback(
    (song: any) => {
      const art = syncArtwork(song, artworkMap) ?? albumArtwork;
      return art ? { ...song, artwork: art } : song;
    },
    [artworkMap, albumArtwork],
  );

  const playableSongs = useMemo(
    () => albumSongs.map((s: any) => withArt(s)),
    [albumSongs, withArt],
  );

  // â”€â”€ Fixed theme-based colours â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";
  const textColor = theme.text;
  const subTextColor = theme.textSecondary;
  const accentColor = theme.primary;
  const lineColor = theme.border;
  const circleBg = isDark ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.15)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.08)";

  const headerColors = useMemo(
    () => ({
      background: bgColor,
      surface: theme.surface,
      border: theme.border,
      text: theme.text,
      textSecondary: theme.textSecondary,
      textMuted: (theme as any).textMuted || theme.textSecondary,
      primary: theme.primary,
    }),
    [theme, bgColor],
  );

  const albumGenre = useMemo(() => {
    for (const s of albumSongs as any[]) {
      const tags: any = getCachedTags(s.id);
      const raw = tags?.genre ?? s.genre;
      const g = Array.isArray(raw) ? raw[0] : raw;
      const c = cleanName(g, "");
      if (c) return c;
    }
    return DEFAULT_GENRE;
  }, [albumSongs, tagVersion]);

  const albumYear = useMemo(() => {
    for (const s of albumSongs as any[]) {
      const tags: any = getCachedTags(s.id);
      const raw = tags?.year ?? s.year;
      const m = raw ? String(raw).match(/\d{4}/) : null;
      if (m) return m[0];
    }
    return DEFAULT_YEAR;
  }, [albumSongs, tagVersion]);

  const albumArtist = useMemo(() => {
    for (const s of albumSongs as any[]) {
      const raw = getCachedTags(s.id)?.artist;
      if (raw) {
        const c = cleanName(raw, "");
        if (c && !isUnknownArtist(c)) return c;
      }
    }
    for (const s of albumSongs as any[]) {
      const c = cleanName((s as any).artist, "");
      if (c && !isUnknownArtist(c)) return c;
    }
    return UNKNOWN_ARTIST;
  }, [albumSongs, tagVersion]);

  const displayAlbumName = useMemo(() => {
    const passed = cleanName(albumName, "");
    if (passed && passed.toLowerCase() !== UNKNOWN_ALBUM.toLowerCase()) {
      return passed;
    }
    for (const s of albumSongs as any[]) {
      const raw = getCachedTags(s.id)?.album ?? (s as any).album;
      const c = cleanName(raw, "");
      if (c && c.toLowerCase() !== UNKNOWN_ALBUM.toLowerCase()) return c;
    }
    return passed || UNKNOWN_ALBUM;
  }, [albumSongs, albumName, tagVersion]);

  const albumDate = useMemo(() => {
    let newest = 0;
    for (const s of albumSongs as any[]) {
      const candidates = [
        s.dateAdded,
        s.createdAt,
        s.modificationTime,
        s.timestamp,
        s.addedAt,
      ];
      for (const c of candidates) {
        if (typeof c === "number" && Number.isFinite(c) && c > 0) {
          if (c > newest) newest = c;
        } else if (typeof c === "string") {
          const t = Date.parse(c);
          if (!isNaN(t) && t > newest) newest = t;
        }
      }
    }
    return newest || Date.now();
  }, [albumSongs]);

  const formattedDate = useMemo(() => formatLongDate(albumDate), [albumDate]);

  const totalDurationSec = useMemo(() => {
    let total = 0;
    for (const s of albumSongs as any[]) {
      const d = s.duration;
      if (typeof d === "number" && d > 0) {
        total += d > 36000 ? d / 1000 : d;
      }
    }
    return total;
  }, [albumSongs]);

  const songCountText = `${albumSongs.length} song${
    albumSongs.length === 1 ? "" : "s"
  }`;

  const footerCountLine = useMemo(() => {
    const dur = formatDurationMinutes(totalDurationSec);
    return dur ? `${songCountText}, ${dur}` : songCountText;
  }, [songCountText, totalDurationSec]);

  const playFrom = useCallback(
    async (index: number) => {
      if (playableSongs.length === 0) return;
      try {
        await loadAndPlay(playableSongs, index);
      } catch {
        showToast("Playback failed");
      }
    },
    [playableSongs, loadAndPlay],
  );

  const handlePlay = () => playFrom(0);

  const handleShuffle = () => {
    if (playableSongs.length === 0) return;
    const shuffled = [...playableSongs];
    for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
    }
    loadAndPlay(shuffled, 0).catch(() => showToast("Playback failed"));
  };

  const handleShareAlbum = async () => {
    try {
      await Share.share({
        message: `Check out "${displayAlbumName}" by ${albumArtist}`,
      });
    } catch {}
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(PINNED_FILE);
        if (info.exists) {
          const raw = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(PINNED_FILE);
          const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
          if (Array.isArray(arr)) {
            const map: Record<string, boolean> = {};
            arr.forEach((id: any) => {
              map[String(id)] = true;
            });
            setPinnedIds(map);
          }
        }
      } catch {
      } finally {
        pinnedLoaded.current = true;
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pinnedLoaded.current) return;
    FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(
      PINNED_FILE,
      JSON.stringify(Object.keys(pinnedIds)),
    ).catch(() => {});
  }, [pinnedIds]);

  const togglePin = useCallback((song: any) => {
    setPinnedIds((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      if (next[song.id]) {
        delete next[song.id];
        showToast("Unpinned");
      } else {
        next[song.id] = true;
        showToast("Pinned to top");
      }
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const openAddTo = async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const all = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      setPlaylists(Array.isArray(all) ? all : []);
      setAddToMode(true);
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not load playlists");
    }
  };

  const addToPlaylist = async (pl: any) => {
    if (!selectedSong) return;
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const all = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      const updated = all.map((p: any) =>
        p.id === pl.id
          ? {
              ...p,
              songs: [...(p.songs ?? []), selectedSong],
              artwork:
                p.artwork ??
                (selectedSong as any).artwork ??
                albumArtwork ??
                null,
              updatedAt: Date.now(),
            }
          : p,
      );
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS, JSON.stringify(updated));
      showToast(`Added to "${pl.name}"`);
      setSelectedSong(null);
      setAddToMode(false);
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not add to playlist");
    }
  };

  const playNext = () => {
    if (!selectedSong) return;
    const st: any = usePlayerStore.getState();

    if (!st.currentTrack) {
      const i = albumSongs.findIndex((s: any) => s.id === selectedSong.id);
      if (i >= 0) playFrom(i);
      setSelectedSong(null);
      return;
    }
    if (st.currentTrack.id === selectedSong.id) {
      showToast("Already playing");
      setSelectedSong(null);
      return;
    }
    const withCover = withArt(selectedSong);
    if (typeof st.playNext === "function") {
      st.playNext(withCover);
      showToast("Will play next");
      setSelectedSong(null);
      return;
    }
    if (Array.isArray(st.queue) && typeof st.setQueue === "function") {
      const without = st.queue.filter((q: any) => q.id !== selectedSong.id);
      const at = without.findIndex((q: any) => q.id === st.currentTrack.id);
      const next = [...without];
      next.splice(at + 1, 0, withCover);
      st.setQueue(next);
      showToast("Will play next");
    }
    setSelectedSong(null);
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedSong) setAddToMode(false);
  }, [selectedSong]);

  const renderSong = ({ item, index }: { item: any; index: number }) => {
    const isFav = !!favorites[item.id];
    const isCurrent = currentTrack?.id === item.id;

    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={styles.songRow}
        activeOpacity={0.7}
        onPress={() => playFrom(index)}
      >
        <View style={styles.starCol}>
          {isFav && (
            <Text style={[styles.star, { color: accentColor }]}>â˜…</Text>
          )}
        </View>

        <Text style={[styles.count, { color: subTextColor }]}>{index + 1}</Text>

        <View style={styles.songInfo}>
          <Text
            numberOfLines={1}
            style={[
              styles.songTitle,
              { color: isCurrent ? accentColor : textColor },
            ]}
          >
            {displayTitle(item)}
          </Text>
          <Text
            numberOfLines={1}
            style={[styles.songArtist, { color: subTextColor }]}
          >
            {displayArtist(item)}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={() => setSelectedSong(item)}
          hitSlop={10}
          style={styles.dotsHit}
        >
          <Image
            source={MORE_ICON}
            style={[styles.dots, { tintColor: subTextColor }]}
            resizeMode="contain"
            fadeDuration={0}
          />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  };

  const showLoading = !bootMinDone || !tagsLoaded;

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: bgColor }]}>
      {/* âœ… STICKY HEADER */}
      <View style={[styles.headerWrapper, { paddingTop: insets.top + 8 }]}>
        <Animated.View
          style={[
            StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
            { backgroundColor: bgColor, opacity: headerBgOpacity },
          ]}
        />
        <View style={styles.headerContent}>
          <GlassIconBtn
            colors={headerColors}
            icon={BACK_ICON}
            iconStyle={{ width: 16, height: 16 }}
            onPress={() => nav.goBack()}
          />

          <Animated.Text
            numberOfLines={1}
            style={[
              styles.headerTitle,
              { color: textColor, opacity: headerTitleOpacity },
            ]}
          >
            {displayAlbumName}
          </Animated.Text>

          <View style={styles.headerRight}>
            <GlassIconBtn
              colors={headerColors}
              icon={ShareSvg}
              iconStyle={{ width: 18, height: 18, tintColor: theme.text }}
              onPress={handleShareAlbum}
            />
            <GlassIconBtn
              colors={headerColors}
              icon={MORE_ICON}
              iconStyle={{ width: 18, height: 18, tintColor: theme.primary }}
              onPress={() => setMenuVisible(true)}
            />
          </View>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* âœ… SCROLLABLE CONTENT */}
      <Animated.FlatList
        data={albumSongs}
        keyExtractor={(it: any) => String(it.id)}
        renderItem={renderSong}
        style={{ opacity: showLoading ? 0 : 1 }}
        pointerEvents={showLoading ? "none" : "auto"}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          paddingBottom: 200,
          paddingTop: insets.top + HEADER_CONTENT_H + 20,
        }}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        onScroll={onScroll}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => (
          <View style={[styles.sep, { backgroundColor: lineColor }]} />
        )}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View>
            <View style={styles.heroWrap}>
              <Image
                source={
                  albumArtwork ? { uri: albumArtwork } : MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG
                }
                style={styles.hero}
                fadeDuration={0}
              />
            </View>

            <Text
              style={[styles.title, { color: textColor }]}
              numberOfLines={2}
            >
              {displayAlbumName}
            </Text>

            <Text
              style={[styles.artist, { color: subTextColor }]}
              numberOfLines={1}
            >
              {albumArtist}
            </Text>

            <View style={styles.metaRow}>
              <Text style={[styles.meta, { color: subTextColor }]}>
                {albumGenre} Â· {albumYear} Â·{" "}
              </Text>
              <Image
                source={LOSSLESS_ICON}
                style={[styles.losslessIcon, { tintColor: subTextColor }]}
                resizeMode="contain"
              />
              <Text style={[styles.meta, { color: subTextColor }]}>
                {" "}
                Hi-Res Lossless
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.controlsRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.circleBtn, { backgroundColor: circleBg }]}
                onPress={handleShuffle}
                disabled={albumSongs.length === 0}
              >
                <ShuffleIcon size={20} color={textColor} />
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.playBtn, { backgroundColor: textColor }]}
                onPress={handlePlay}
                disabled={albumSongs.length === 0}
              >
                <PlayIcon size={22} color={theme.background} />
                <Text style={[styles.playText, { color: theme.background }]}>
                  Play
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.circleBtn, { backgroundColor: circleBg }]}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
                onPress={() => setMenuVisible(true)}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.plusText, { color: textColor }]}>+</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <View style={[styles.divider, { backgroundColor: lineColor }]} />
          </View>
        }
        ListFooterComponent={
          <>
            <View style={[styles.divider, { backgroundColor: lineColor }]} />

            <View style={styles.footerMeta}>
              <Text style={[styles.footerDate, { color: textColor }]}>
                {formattedDate}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.footerCount, { color: subTextColor }]}>
                {footerCountLine}
              </Text>
            </View>
          </>
        }
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <View style={styles.empty}>
            <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: textColor }]}>
              No songs in this album
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: subTextColor }]}>
              Songs tagged with this album will appear here.
            </Text>
          </View>
        }
      />

      <ScreenLoader visible={showLoading} />

      <View style={styles.bottomArea} pointerEvents="box-none">
        <MiniPlayer />
      </View>

      {/* â”€â”€â”€ Song options modal â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
      <Modal
        visible={!!selectedSong}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        statusBarTranslucent
        onRequestClose={() => {
          setSelectedSong(null);
          setAddToMode(false);
        }}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <Pressable
            style={styles.backdrop}
            onPress={() => {
              setSelectedSong(null);
              setAddToMode(false);
            }}
          />
          <View
            style={[
              styles.sheet,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 12,
                borderTopColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={[styles.sheetHandle, { backgroundColor: theme.border }]}
            />

            <View style={styles.sheetHeader}>
              <Image
                source={
                  albumArtwork ? { uri: albumArtwork } : MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG
                }
                style={styles.sheetArt}
                fadeDuration={0}
              />
              <View style={styles.sheetHeaderText}>
                <Text
                  style={[styles.sheetTitle, { color: theme.text }]}
                  numberOfLines={1}
                >
                  {selectedSong ? displayTitle(selectedSong) : "Song options"}
                </Text>
                <Text
                  style={[styles.sheetSub, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
                  numberOfLines={1}
                >
                  {selectedSong ? displayArtist(selectedSong) : UNKNOWN_ARTIST}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[styles.sheetDivider, { backgroundColor: theme.border }]}
            />

            {!addToMode ? (
              <>
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.option}
                  onPress={() => {
                    const s = selectedSong;
                    setSelectedSong(null);
                    if (s) togglePin(s);
                  }}
                >
                  <View style={styles.optionRow}>
                    <PinSongIcon size={20} color={theme.primary} />
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.optionText,
                        { color: theme.text, marginLeft: 12 },
                      ]}
                    >
                      {selectedSong && pinnedIds[selectedSong.id]
                        ? "Unpin Song"
                        : "Pin Song"}
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity style={styles.option} onPress={openAddTo}>
                  <View style={styles.optionRow}>
                    <Image
                      source={CREATE_PLAYLIST_ICON}
                      style={[styles.optionIcon, { tintColor: theme.primary }]}
                      resizeMode="contain"
                      fadeDuration={0}
                    />
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.optionText,
                        { color: theme.text, marginLeft: 12 },
                      ]}
                    >
                      Add to playlist
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity style={styles.option} onPress={playNext}>
                  <View style={styles.optionRow}>
                    <SkipNextIcon size={20} color={theme.primary} />
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.optionText,
                        { color: theme.text, marginLeft: 12 },
                      ]}
                    >
                      Play next
                    </Text>
                  </View>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </>
            ) : (
              <>
                <Text
                  style={[styles.subHeader, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
                >
                  Choose a playlist
                </Text>

                <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 280 }}>
                  {playlists.length === 0 ? (
                    <Text
                      style={[
                        styles.noPlaylists,
                        { color: theme.textSecondary },
                      ]}
                    >
                      No playlists yet
                    </Text>
                  ) : (
                    playlists.map((pl) => (
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        key={pl.id}
                        style={styles.option}
                        onPress={() => addToPlaylist(pl)}
                      >
                        <Text
                          style={[styles.optionText, { color: theme.text }]}
                          numberOfLines={1}
                        >
                          â™ª {pl.name}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    ))
                  )}
                </ScrollView>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.option}
                  onPress={() => setAddToMode(false)}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.optionText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
                  >
                    â† Back
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </>
            )}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.cancel, { borderTopColor: theme.border }]}
              onPress={() => {
                setSelectedSong(null);
                setAddToMode(false);
              }}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.cancelText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
                Cancel
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* â”€â”€â”€ 3-dot menu (Settings / Account) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
      <Modal
        visible={menuVisible}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setMenuVisible(false)}
        statusBarTranslucent
      >
        <Pressable
          style={styles.menuBackdrop}
          onPress={() => setMenuVisible(false)}
        >
          <View
            style={[
              styles.menuCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                borderColor: theme.border,
                top: insets.top + 54,
                right: 10,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <Pressable onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.menuRow}
                onPress={() => {
                  setMenuVisible(false);
                  nav.navigate("Settings");
                }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.menuText, { color: theme.text }]}>
                  Settings
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.menuRow}
                onPress={() => {
                  setMenuVisible(false);
                  nav.navigate("Account");
                }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.menuText, { color: theme.text }]}>
                  Account
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1 },

  // â”€â”€ Sticky Header â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€

  },

  },

  // â”€â”€ Hero â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  heroWrap: {

  },
  metaRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",

  // â”€â”€ Controls â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  controlsRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    gap: 16,
    marginTop: 18,
    marginBottom: 18,
  },
  circleBtn: {
    width: 52,
    height: 52,
    borderRadius: 26,
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
  },
  playBtn: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    height: 48,
    minWidth: 120,
    paddingHorizontal: 28,
    borderRadius: 24,
    gap: 8,
  },
  playText: { fontSize: 16, fontWeight: "700", fontFamily: FONT },
  plusText: {
    fontSize: 28,
    fontWeight: "300",
    lineHeight: 30,
    fontFamily: FONT,
  },
  divider: {
    height: 1,

    marginBottom: 6,
  },

  // â”€â”€ Song List â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  songRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",

  starCol: {

    justifyContent: "center",
    marginRight: 2,

  star: { fontSize: 13 },
  count: {
    width: 34,
    minWidth: 34,
    textAlign: "right",
    paddingRight: 6,
    fontSize: 18,
    fontFamily: FONT,
    marginRight: 6,
    marginLeft: -35,
  },
  songInfo: { flex: 1, marginRight: 4, minWidth: 0 },
  songTitle: {
    fontSize: 16,
    fontWeight: "500",
    fontFamily: FONT,
    flexShrink: 1,
  },
  songArtist: {
    fontSize: 13,
    marginTop: 1,
    fontFamily: FONT,
    flexShrink: 1,
  },
  dotsHit: { paddingHorizontal: 10, paddingVertical: 4 },
  dots: { width: 17, height: 17 },
  sep: { height: 1, marginLeft: 42, marginRight: 16 },

  footerMeta: {
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingTop: 14,
    paddingBottom: 8,
  },
  footerDate: { fontSize: 13, fontWeight: "600", fontFamily: FONT },
  footerCount: { fontSize: 12, marginTop: 4, fontFamily: FONT },

  empty: {
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingTop: 40,
    paddingHorizontal: 32,
  },
  emptyTitle: { fontSize: 18, fontWeight: "600", fontFamily: FONT },
  emptyText: {
    marginTop: 6,
    fontSize: 13,
    textAlign: "center",
    fontFamily: FONT,
  },

  bottomArea: {
    position: "absolute",
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    backgroundColor: "transparent",
  },

  // â”€â”€ Modals / Sheets â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  modalRoot: { flex: 1, justifyContent: "flex-end" },
  backdrop: {
    position: "absolute",
    top: 0,
    left: 0,
    right: 0,
    bottom: 0,
    backgroundColor: "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)",
  },
  sheet: {
    borderTopLeftRadius: 24,
    borderTopRightRadius: 24,
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    paddingTop: 12,
    borderTopWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
  },
  sheetHandle: {
    width: 42,
    height: 5,
    borderRadius: 4,
    alignSelf: "center",
    marginBottom: 16,
  },
  sheetHeader: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    marginBottom: 12,
  },
  sheetArt: {
    width: 60,
    height: 60,
    borderRadius: 8,
    backgroundColor: "#1c1c1e",
  },
  sheetHeaderText: { flex: 1, marginLeft: 14, minWidth: 0 },
  sheetTitle: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: "600", fontFamily: FONT },
  sheetSub: { fontSize: 12.5, marginTop: 4, fontFamily: FONT },
  sheetDivider: { height: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth, marginBottom: 4 },

  option: {
    minHeight: 52,
    justifyContent: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 4,
  },
  optionRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
  },
  optionIcon: { width: 20, height: 20 },
  optionText: { fontSize: 16, fontFamily: FONT },

  subHeader: {
    fontSize: 12.5,
    fontFamily: FONT,
    paddingHorizontal: 4,
    paddingTop: 10,
    paddingBottom: 6,
  },
  noPlaylists: {
    fontSize: 14,
    textAlign: "center",
    paddingVertical: 24,
    fontFamily: FONT,
  },
  cancel: {
    marginTop: 8,
    paddingVertical: 14,
    alignItems: "center",
    borderTopWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
  },
  cancelText: { fontSize: 16, fontFamily: FONT },

  // â”€â”€ 3-dot menu â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  menuBackdrop: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "transparent" },
  menuCard: {
    position: "absolute",
    width: 200,
    borderRadius: 12,
    borderWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    shadowColor: "#000",
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 8 },
    shadowOpacity: 0.35,
    shadowRadius: 16,
    elevation: 18,
  },
  menuRow: {
    paddingHorizontal: 18,
    paddingVertical: 12,
  },
  menuText: {
    fontSize: 15,
    fontWeight: "500",
    fontFamily: FONT,
  },
});

========== src\screens\AlbumScreen.tsx ==========
// src/screens/AlbumScreen.tsx
import React, {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useRef,
  useState,
} from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  FlatList,
  StyleSheet,
  RefreshControl,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Platform,
  Image,
  useWindowDimensions,
} from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { useMediaStore } from "../store/mediaStore";
import { useMediaScanner } from "../hooks/useMediaScanner";
import { useAudioPlayer } from "../hooks/useAudioPlayer";
import { useArtworkCache } from "../hooks/useArtworkCache";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";
import { PlayIcon, ShuffleIcon } from "../icons/Icons";

// âœ… Shared header (top bar + title + search)
import LibraryPageHeaders from "../components/LibraryPageHeaders";

// âœ… Shared centered loader
import { ScreenLoader } from "../components/Loader";

import {
  AudioTags,
  getCachedTags,
  getCachedArtwork,
  hasCachedTags,
  readAndCacheTags,
  setCachedArtwork,
} from "../services/audioTags/tagService";

const FONT = "sans-serif";
const ACTION_RADIUS = 26;
const ACTION_HEIGHT = 52;
const SEARCH_FONT_SIZE = 18;
const ACTION_TOP_GAP = 22;

const MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG = require("../../assets/images/musicdefault.png");
const LIST_ICON = require("../../assets/images/list.png");

const UNKNOWN_ALBUM = "Unknown album";
const UNKNOWN_ARTIST = "Unknown artist";

const TAG_CONCURRENCY = 12;
const TAG_WAIT_MS = 600;
const MIN_BOOT_LOADER_MS = 320;

const cleanName = (raw?: string | null, fallback = ""): string => {
  if (!raw) return fallback;
  let s = String(raw)
    .replace(/[\(\[\{<][^\)\]\}>]*[\)\]\}>]/g, " ")
    .replace(/\bwww\.[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)+/gi, " ")
    .replace(
      /\b[a-z0-9-]+\.(?:com|net|org|in|co|se|pk|info|me|cc|xyz|live|fm|io|app|tv|club|site|online)\b/gi,
      " ",
    )
    .replace(/[\s_\-â€“â€”|:@~â€¢Â·]+/g, " ")
    .trim();
  return s || fallback;
};

const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(a.trim());
};

const GENERIC_FOLDERS =
  /^(music|audio|sdcard|emulated|0|1|download|downloads|songs|tracks|whatsapp|telegram|recordings|dcim|internal|external|storage)$/i;

const albumFromUri = (uri?: string | null): string | undefined => {
  if (!uri) return undefined;
  try {
    const clean = uri.split("?")[0].split("#")[0];
    const parts = clean.split("/").filter(Boolean);
    if (parts.length < 2) return undefined;
    const folder = decodeURIComponent(parts[parts.length - 2]).trim();
    if (!folder || GENERIC_FOLDERS.test(folder)) return undefined;
    return folder;
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
};

interface AlbumItem {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  artist: string;
  artwork?: string;
  songs: any[];
}

const shuffleArray = <T,>(arr: T[]): T[] => {
  const a = [...arr];
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
};

// â”€â”€ Theme-aware default album artwork with error fallback â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const DefaultAlbumArt = React.memo(function DefaultAlbumArt({
  size,
  radius = 10,
}: {
  size: number;
  radius?: number;
}) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = React.useState(false);
  const theme = useTheme() as any;

  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";
  const bgColor = isDark ? "#1c1c1e" : "#bcbcc4";
  const textColor = isDark ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.7)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.55)";

  return (
    <View
      style={{
        width: size,
        height: size,
        borderRadius: radius,
        backgroundColor: bgColor,
        alignItems: "center",
        justifyContent: "center",
        overflow: "hidden",
      }}
    >
      {!failed ? (
        <Image
          source={MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG}
          style={{ width: size, height: size }}
          resizeMode="cover"
          fadeDuration={0}
          onError={() => setFailed(true)}
        />
      ) : (
        <Text
          style={{
            color: textColor,
            fontSize: size * 0.4,
            fontWeight: "700",
          }}
        >
          â™ª
        </Text>
      )}
    </View>
  );
});

// â”€â”€ Safe album image â€” falls back to default when URI is broken â”€â”€â”€â”€
const SafeAlbumImage = React.memo(function SafeAlbumImage({
  uri,
  size,
  radius = 10,
}: {
  uri: string;
  size: number;
  radius?: number;
}) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = React.useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setFailed(false);
  }, [uri]);

  if (failed) {
    return <DefaultAlbumArt size={size} radius={radius} />;
  }

  return (
    <Image
      source={{ uri }}
      style={{ width: size, height: size, borderRadius: radius }}
      resizeMode="cover"
      fadeDuration={0}
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
});

export default function AlbumScreen() {
  const theme = useTheme();
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const BG_COLOR = theme.background;
  const SURFACE_COLOR = theme.surface;
  const BORDER_COLOR = theme.border;
  const ACCENT = theme.primary;

  const { width: winW } = useWindowDimensions();
  const H_PAD = 12;
  const GAP = 12;
  const CARD_W = (winW - H_PAD * 2 - GAP) / 2;

  const songs = useMediaStore((s) => s.songs ?? []);
  const { scanAll } = useMediaScanner();
  const { loadAndPlay } = useAudioPlayer();
  const { artworkMap } = useArtworkCache(songs);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(() => songs.length === 0);
  const refreshBusy = useRef(false);
  const initialScanStarted = useRef(false);
  const mounted = useRef(true);

  // âœ… Boot minimum â€” forced visible time on mount.
  const [bootMinDone, setBootMinDone] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (MIN_BOOT_LOADER_MS <= 0) {
      setBootMinDone(true);
      return;
    }
    const t = setTimeout(() => setBootMinDone(true), MIN_BOOT_LOADER_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  // Seed tagData + tagsReady synchronously from the cache
  const [tagData, setTagData] = useState<Record<string, AudioTags>>(() => {
    const init: Record<string, AudioTags> = {};
    songs.forEach((s: any) => {
      if (hasCachedTags(s.id)) init[s.id] = getCachedTags(s.id) ?? {};
    });
    return init;
  });

  const [tagsReady, setTagsReady] = useState(
    () => songs.length > 0 && songs.every((s: any) => hasCachedTags(s.id)),
  );

  const requestedRef = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  // Warm artwork cache so AlbumDetails reads it synchronously
  useEffect(() => {
    Object.keys(artworkMap).forEach((id) => {
      const uri = artworkMap[id];
      if (uri) setCachedArtwork(id, uri);
    });
  }, [artworkMap]);

  // â”€â”€ Initial scan â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialScanStarted.current) return;
    initialScanStarted.current = true;
    if (songs.length > 0) {
      setScanning(false);
      return;
    }
    setScanning(true);
    scanAll()
      .catch(console.error)
      .finally(() => {
        if (mounted.current) setScanning(false);
      });
  }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length > 0) setScanning(false);
  }, [songs.length]);

  // â”€â”€ Read ID3 tags in parallel â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length === 0) return;

    const initial: Record<string, AudioTags> = {};
    const missing: any[] = [];

    songs.forEach((s: any) => {
      if (hasCachedTags(s.id)) {
        initial[s.id] = getCachedTags(s.id) ?? {};
      } else if (!requestedRef.current.has(s.id)) {
        missing.push(s);
      }
    });

    if (Object.keys(initial).length > 0) {
      setTagData((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...initial }));
    }

    if (missing.length === 0) {
      setTagsReady(true);
      return;
    }

    missing.forEach((s) => requestedRef.current.add(s.id));

    (async () => {
      let batch: Record<string, AudioTags> = {};
      let count = 0;
      let cursor = 0;

      const flush = () => {
        if (Object.keys(batch).length > 0 && mounted.current) {
          const toSave = batch;
          setTagData((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...toSave }));
          batch = {};
        }
      };

      const worker = async () => {
        while (cursor < missing.length && mounted.current) {
          const s = missing[cursor++];
          const uri = (s as any).uri ?? (s as any).path;
          try {
            batch[s.id] = uri ? await readAndCacheTags(s.id, uri) : {};
          } catch {
            batch[s.id] = {};
          }
          count++;
          if (count % 10 === 0) flush();
        }
      };

      await Promise.all(
        Array.from(
          { length: Math.min(TAG_CONCURRENCY, missing.length) },
          () => worker(),
        ),
      );
      flush();

      if (mounted.current) setTagsReady(true);
    })();
  }, [songs]);

  // Safety timeout
  useEffect(() => {
    if (tagsReady) return;
    if (songs.length === 0) return;
    if (songs.every((s: any) => hasCachedTags(s.id))) {
      setTagsReady(true);
      return;
    }
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      if (mounted.current) setTagsReady(true);
    }, TAG_WAIT_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [songs, tagData, tagsReady]);

  // â”€â”€ Group songs â†’ albums â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const albums = useMemo<AlbumItem[]>(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, AlbumItem>();

    songs.forEach((song: any) => {
      const tagged = tagData[song.id];
      const cached = getCachedTags(song.id);
      const uri = song.uri ?? song.path;

      const rawAlbum =
        (tagged?.album && tagged.album.trim()) ||
        (cached?.album && cached.album.trim()) ||
        (typeof song.album === "string" && song.album.trim()) ||
        albumFromUri(uri);

      const rawArtist =
        (tagged?.artist && tagged.artist.trim()) ||
        (cached?.artist && cached.artist.trim()) ||
        (typeof song.artist === "string" && song.artist.trim()) ||
        albumFromUri(
          uri ? uri.split("/").slice(0, -1).join("/") : undefined,
        );

      const name = cleanName(rawAlbum, UNKNOWN_ALBUM);
      const key = name.toLowerCase();
      const art =
        song.artwork ?? artworkMap[song.id] ?? getCachedArtwork(song.id);
      const artist = cleanName(rawArtist, "");

      if (!map.has(key)) {
        map.set(key, {
          key,
          name,
          artist: !isUnknownArtist(artist) ? artist : UNKNOWN_ARTIST,
          artwork: art,
          songs: [],
        });
      }
      const entry = map.get(key)!;
      entry.songs.push(song);
      if (!entry.artwork && art) entry.artwork = art;
      if (entry.artist === UNKNOWN_ARTIST && !isUnknownArtist(artist)) {
        entry.artist = artist;
      }
    });

    return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) =>
      a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }),
    );
  }, [songs, artworkMap, tagData]);

  const filteredAlbums = useMemo(() => {
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return albums;
    return albums.filter((a) =>
      `${a.name} ${a.artist}`.toLowerCase().includes(q),
    );
  }, [albums, search]);

  const allSongsInView = useMemo(
    () => filteredAlbums.flatMap((a) => a.songs),
    [filteredAlbums],
  );

  const playAll = useCallback(
    async (shuffle: boolean) => {
      if (allSongsInView.length === 0) {
        showToast("No albums to play");
        return;
      }
      const queue = shuffle ? shuffleArray(allSongsInView) : allSongsInView;
      try {
        await loadAndPlay(queue, 0);
      } catch {
        showToast("Playback failed");
      }
    },
    [allSongsInView, loadAndPlay],
  );

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    if (refreshBusy.current) return;
    refreshBusy.current = true;
    setRefreshing(true);
    try {
      await scanAll();
      showToast("Library refreshed");
    } catch {
      showToast("Unable to scan");
    } finally {
      refreshBusy.current = false;
      if (mounted.current) setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [scanAll]);

  // â”€â”€ Row item â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const renderAlbum = useCallback(
    ({ item }: { item: AlbumItem }) => {
      const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";
      const artBorder = isDark
        ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.12)"
        : "rgba(0,0,0,0.10)";

      return (
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.albumCard, { width: CARD_W }]}
          activeOpacity={0.82}
          onPress={() =>
            nav.navigate("AlbumDetails", {
              albumName: item.name,
              songIds: item.songs.map((s: any) => s.id),
            })
          }
        >
          <View style={[styles.albumArtWrap, { width: CARD_W, height: CARD_W }]}>
            {item.artwork ? (
              <SafeAlbumImage uri={item.artwork} size={CARD_W} radius={10} />
            ) : (
              <DefaultAlbumArt size={CARD_W} radius={10} />
            )}

            <View
              pointerEvents="none"
              style={[
                StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
                {
                  borderRadius: 10,
                  borderWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
                  borderColor: artBorder,
                },
              ]}
            />
          </View>

          <Text
            numberOfLines={1}
            style={[styles.albumName, { color: theme.text }]}
          >
            {item.name}
          </Text>
          <Text
            numberOfLines={1}
            style={[styles.albumArtist, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
          >
            {item.artist}
          </Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      );
    },
    [CARD_W, theme, nav],
  );

  // âœ… Only the Play/Shuffle action row remains in the list header.
  //    Title + search now live in the shared LibraryPageHeaders.
  const listHeader = (
    <View style={styles.headerSection}>
      <View style={styles.actionRow}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.actionBtn, { backgroundColor: SURFACE_COLOR }]}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
          onPress={() => playAll(false)}
        >
          <PlayIcon size={20} color={ACCENT} />
          <Text style={[styles.actionLabel, { color: ACCENT }]}>Play</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={[styles.actionBtn, { backgroundColor: SURFACE_COLOR }]}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
          onPress={() => playAll(true)}
        >
          <ShuffleIcon size={20} color={ACCENT} />
          <Text style={[styles.actionLabel, { color: ACCENT }]}>Shuffle</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      <View style={[styles.listDivider, { backgroundColor: BORDER_COLOR }]} />
    </View>
  );

  // âœ… Loader visibility rule (unchanged)
  const showLoading =
    !bootMinDone || scanning || (songs.length > 0 && !tagsReady);

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: BG_COLOR }]}>
      {/* ---------- SHARED HEADER (top bar + title + search) ---------- */}
      <LibraryPageHeaders
        colors={theme}
        topInset={insets.top}
        title="Albums"
        onBack={() => nav.goBack()}
        searchValue={search}
        onSearchChange={setSearch}
        searchPlaceholder="Search albums, artists"
        actions={[
          {
            key: "settings",
            icon: LIST_ICON,
            iconStyle: { width: 23, height: 20 },
            onPress: () => nav.navigate("Settings"),
          },
        ]}
      />

      <View
        style={[styles.listWrap, { opacity: showLoading ? 0 : 1 }]}
        pointerEvents={showLoading ? "none" : "auto"}
      >
        <FlatList
          key="albums-grid"
          data={filteredAlbums}
          numColumns={2}
          keyExtractor={(it) => it.key}
          ListHeaderComponent={listHeader}
          renderItem={renderAlbum}
          columnWrapperStyle={{
            paddingHorizontal: H_PAD,
            justifyContent: "space-between",
          }}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 100, flexGrow: 1 }}
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={{ height: 18 }} />}
          initialNumToRender={8}
          maxToRenderPerBatch={10}
          windowSize={7}
          removeClippedSubviews={Platform.OS === "android"}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl
              refreshing={refreshing}
              onRefresh={onRefresh}
              tintColor={ACCENT}
              colors={[ACCENT]}
              progressBackgroundColor={SURFACE_COLOR}
              progressViewOffset={Platform.OS === "android" ? 8 : 0}
            />
          }
          ListEmptyComponent={
            <View style={styles.empty}>
              <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: theme.text }]}>
                {albums.length === 0 ? "No albums found" : "No matching albums"}
              </Text>
              <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
                {albums.length === 0
                  ? "Pull down to scan your device again."
                  : "Try another album or artist name."}
              </Text>
            </View>
          }
        />
      </View>

      <ScreenLoader visible={showLoading} />
    </View>
  );
}

// ============================================================

========== src\screens\ArtistDetailsScreen.tsx ==========
// src/screens/ArtistDetailsScreen.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Image,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  Dimensions,
} from "react-native";
import {
  SafeAreaView,
  useSafeAreaInsets,
} from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useNavigation, useRoute } from "@react-navigation/native";
import Svg, { Path } from "react-native-svg";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";

import LibraryPageHeaders from "../components/LibraryPageHeaders";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { useMediaStore } from "../store/mediaStore";
import { useAudioPlayer } from "../hooks/useAudioPlayer";
import { useArtworkCache } from "../hooks/useArtworkCache";
import { useArtistStore } from "../store/artistStore";
import { useId3Tags } from "../hooks/useId3Tags";
import { PlayIcon, ShuffleIcon } from "../icons/Icons";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";
import {
  resolveArtistName,
  cleanSiteName,
  UNKNOWN_ARTIST,
} from "../utils/cleanArtistName";

// âœ… Star icons
const STAR_ON = require("../../assets/images/favouritedsong.png");
const STAR_OFF = require("../../assets/images/favourite.png");

const MORE_ICON = require("../../assets/apple/moreicon.png");
const MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG = require("../../assets/images/musicdefault.png");
const FONT = "sans-serif";
const UNKNOWN_ALBUM = "Unknown album";

// â”€â”€â”€ Grid layout â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const { width: SCREEN_W } = Dimensions.get("window");
const GRID_COLS = 2;
const GRID_PAD = 12;
const GRID_GAP = 12;
const CARD_W =
  (SCREEN_W - GRID_PAD * 2 - GRID_GAP * (GRID_COLS - 1)) / GRID_COLS;

// â”€â”€â”€ Sort options â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
type SortMode = "title" | "recent" | "newest" | "oldest";
const SORT_OPTIONS: { id: SortMode; label: string }[] = [
  { id: "title", label: "Title" },
  { id: "recent", label: "Recently Added" },
  { id: "newest", label: "Newest First" },
  { id: "oldest", label: "Oldest First" },
];

// â”€â”€â”€ Module-level artwork cache â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const ART_CACHE_KEY = "@artist_details_art_cache_v1";
const artCache: Record<string, string> = {};
let artCacheLoaded = false;
let artCacheLoadPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
let artSaveTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;

const hydrateArtCache = (): Promise<void> => {
  if (artCacheLoaded) return Promise.resolve();
  if (artCacheLoadPromise) return artCacheLoadPromise;
  artCacheLoadPromise = (async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(ART_CACHE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
          Object.assign(artCache, parsed);
        }
      }
    } catch {}
    artCacheLoaded = true;
  })();
  return artCacheLoadPromise;
};

const persistArtCache = () => {
  if (artSaveTimer) return;
  artSaveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    artSaveTimer = null;
    AsyncStorage.setItem(ART_CACHE_KEY, JSON.stringify(artCache)).catch(
      () => {},
    );
  }, 1200);
};

// âœ… Music-note SVG placeholder
const ArtistPlaceholder = ({
  color = "#f92c47",
  size = 24,
}: {
  color?: string;
  size?: number;
}) => (
  <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="3.5 4 17 16" fill="none">
    <Path
      fill={color}
      d="M18.39 9.42c1.289-1.282 1.34-2.908.102-4.139-1.23-1.216-2.85-1.186-4.138.103zm-6.08 9.858a.66.66 0 0 0 .667-.667v-4.328l-.051-1.048 2.234-2.072c.842.11 1.728-.235 2.49-1.004l-4.03-4.035c-.776.761-1.098 1.633-.988 2.475l-6.805 7.332c-.278.3-.322.717.022 1.062L4.913 18.2a.31.31 0 0 0 .037.418l.212.22a.31.31 0 0 0 .425.029l1.208-.945c.33.344.755.3 1.048.022l3.8-3.516v4.182a.66.66 0 0 0 .667.667zm-5.053-2.073-.674-.674 6.453-6.84q.186.307.461.593.274.276.571.476z"
    />
  </Svg>
);

const getTimestamp = (song: any): number => {
  const candidates = [
    song?.dateAdded,
    song?.createdAt,
    song?.modificationTime,
    song?.creationTime,
    song?.timestamp,
    song?.addedAt,
    song?.date,
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (typeof c === "number" && Number.isFinite(c) && c > 0) return c;
    if (typeof c === "string") {
      const t = Date.parse(c);
      if (!isNaN(t)) return t;
    }
  }
  const id = String(song?.id ?? "");
  const m = id.match(/\d{10,}/);
  if (m) return parseInt(m[0], 10) || 0;
  return 0;
};

const shuffleArr = <T,>(arr: T[]): T[] => {
  const a = [...arr];
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
};

export default function ArtistDetailsScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const route = useRoute<any>();
  const theme = useTheme();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const artistName: string = route.params?.artistName ?? UNKNOWN_ARTIST;

  const songs = useMediaStore((s: any) => s.songs ?? []);
  const { loadAndPlay } = useAudioPlayer();
  const { artworkMap } = useArtworkCache(songs);
  const { tagData } = useId3Tags(songs);

  const favourites = useArtistStore((s) => s.favourites);
  const toggleFavourite = useArtistStore((s) => s.toggleFavourite);
  const hydrate = useArtistStore((s) => s.hydrate);
  useEffect(() => {
    hydrate();
  }, [hydrate]);

  const [sortMode, setSortMode] = useState<SortMode>("newest");
  const [menuVisible, setMenuVisible] = useState(false);

  const [cachedArt, setCachedArt] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => ({
    ...artCache,
  }));
  const mounted = useRef(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    hydrateArtCache().then(() => {
      if (mounted.current) setCachedArt({ ...artCache });
    });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    let changed = false;
    Object.keys(artworkMap).forEach((id) => {
      const uri = artworkMap[id];
      if (uri && artCache[id] !== uri) {
        artCache[id] = uri;
        changed = true;
      }
    });
    if (changed) {
      setCachedArt({ ...artCache });
      persistArtCache();
    }
  }, [artworkMap]);

  const artistSongs = useMemo(() => {
    const list = songs.filter(
      (s: any) =>
        resolveArtistName(s.artist, tagData[s.id]?.artist) === artistName,
    );
    const indexed = list.map((song: any, i: number) => ({
      song,
      i,
      ts: getTimestamp(song),
      title: (song.title || "").toString(),
    }));

    switch (sortMode) {
      case "title":
        indexed.sort((a, b) =>
          a.title.localeCompare(b.title, undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }),
        );
        break;
      case "newest":
        indexed.sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts || b.i - a.i);
        break;
      case "oldest":
        indexed.sort((a, b) => a.ts - b.ts || a.i - b.i);
        break;
      case "recent":
      default:
        indexed.sort((a, b) => b.i - a.i);
        break;
    }

    return indexed.map((x) => x.song);
  }, [songs, artistName, sortMode, tagData]);

  const isFav = !!favourites[artistName];

  const headerColors = useMemo(
    () => ({
      background: theme.background,
      surface: theme.surface,
      border: theme.border,
      text: theme.text,
      textSecondary: theme.textSecondary,
      textMuted: (theme as any).textMuted || theme.textSecondary,
      primary: theme.primary,
    }),
    [theme]
  );

  const buildPlayableQueue = (source: any[]) =>
    source.map((s: any) => {
      const id = s.id || s._id;
      const art =
        (s as any).artwork ??
        (s as any).artworkUri ??
        artworkMap[id] ??
        cachedArt[id];
      return {
        ...s,
        artwork: art,
        artworkUri: art,
        cover: art,
        artist: resolveArtistName(s.artist, tagData[id]?.artist),
        album:
          cleanSiteName(tagData[id]?.album || (s as any).album, false) ||
          UNKNOWN_ALBUM,
      };
    });

  const playAll = async (shuffle: boolean) => {
    if (!artistSongs.length) return;
    const baseQueue = shuffle ? shuffleArr(artistSongs) : artistSongs;
    const queue = buildPlayableQueue(baseQueue);
    try {
      await loadAndPlay(queue, 0);
    } catch {
      showToast("Playback failed");
    }
  };

  const playSong = async (song: any) => {
    const idx = artistSongs.findIndex((s: any) => s.id === song.id);
    if (idx < 0) return;
    const playable = buildPlayableQueue(artistSongs);
    try {
      await loadAndPlay(playable, idx);
    } catch {
      showToast("Playback failed");
    }
  };

  const renderSong = ({ item }: { item: any }) => {
    const id = item.id || item._id;
    const art =
      (item as any).artwork ??
      (item as any).artworkUri ??
      artworkMap[id] ??
      cachedArt[id];

    const albumName =
      cleanSiteName(tagData[id]?.album || (item as any).album, false) ||
      UNKNOWN_ALBUM;

    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={[styles.gridCard, { width: CARD_W }]}
        activeOpacity={0.75}
        onPress={() => playSong(item)}
      >
        <View
          style={[
            styles.gridArt,
            {
              width: CARD_W,
              height: CARD_W,
              backgroundColor: theme.surface,
            },
          ]}
        >
          <Image
            source={art ? { uri: art } : MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG}
            style={styles.gridArtImg}
            resizeMode="cover"
            fadeDuration={0}
          />
        </View>

        <Text
          numberOfLines={1}
          style={[styles.gridTitle, { color: theme.text }]}
        >
          {item.title || "Unknown"}
        </Text>

        <Text
          numberOfLines={1}
          style={[styles.gridSub, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
        >
          {albumName}
        </Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  };

  const songCountLine = `${artistSongs.length} ${
    artistSongs.length === 1 ? "song" : "songs"
  }`;

  return (
    <SafeAreaView
      style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: theme.background }]}
      edges={["top"]}
    >
      {/* âœ… Floating header â€” back (left) Â· â˜… Â· â‹® (right) */}
      <LibraryPageHeaders
        colors={headerColors}
        topInset={0}
        showBack
        onBack={() => nav.goBack()}
        title=""
        showSearch={false}
    
        actions={[
          {
            key: "fav",
            icon: isFav ? STAR_ON : STAR_OFF,
            iconStyle: { width: 20, height: 20 },
            onPress: () => toggleFavourite(artistName),
          },
          {
            key: "more",
            icon: MORE_ICON,
            iconStyle: {
              width: 18,
              height: 18,
              tintColor: theme.primary,
            },
            onPress: () => setMenuVisible(true),
          },
        ]}
      />

      <FlatList
        data={artistSongs}
        keyExtractor={(item: any) => item.id}
        renderItem={renderSong}
        extraData={cachedArt}
        numColumns={GRID_COLS}
        columnWrapperStyle={{
          paddingHorizontal: GRID_PAD,
          gap: GRID_GAP,
          marginBottom: 16,
        }}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingTop: 0, paddingBottom: 100 }}
        initialNumToRender={8}
        maxToRenderPerBatch={8}
        windowSize={7}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View>
            <View style={styles.hero}>
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.artistImgWrap,
                  { backgroundColor: theme.surface },
                ]}
              >
                <ArtistPlaceholder color={theme.primary} size={68} />
              </View>

              <Text
                style={[styles.artistName, { color: theme.text }]}
                numberOfLines={2}
              >
                {artistName}
              </Text>

              <Text
                style={[styles.heroMeta, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
              >
                {songCountLine}
              </Text>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.actionRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.actionBtn,
                  { backgroundColor: theme.surface },
                ]}
                activeOpacity={0.75}
                onPress={() => playAll(false)}
              >
                <PlayIcon size={20} color={theme.primary} />
                <Text
                  style={[styles.actionLabel, { color: theme.primary }]}
                >
                  Play
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.actionBtn,
                  { backgroundColor: theme.surface },
                ]}
                activeOpacity={0.75}
                onPress={() => playAll(true)}
              >
                <ShuffleIcon size={20} color={theme.primary} />
                <Text
                  style={[styles.actionLabel, { color: theme.primary }]}
                >
                  Shuffle
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        }
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <View style={styles.empty}>
            <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: theme.text }]}>
              No songs
            </Text>
            <Text
              style={[styles.emptyText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
            >
              This artist has no songs in your library.
            </Text>
          </View>
        }
      />

      {/* â”€â”€â”€ Sort By â€” bottom sheet (slides up from bottom) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€ */}
      <Modal
        visible={menuVisible}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setMenuVisible(false)}
        statusBarTranslucent
      >
        <View style={styles.sheetRoot}>
          <Pressable
            style={styles.sheetBackdrop}
            onPress={() => setMenuVisible(false)}
          />

          <View
            style={[
              styles.sortSheet,
              {
                backgroundColor: theme.surface,
                paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 16,
                borderTopColor: theme.border,
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View
              style={[styles.sheetHandle, { backgroundColor: theme.border }]}
            />

            <Text
              style={[
                styles.sheetTitle,
                { color: theme.textSecondary },
              ]}
            >
              Sort By
            </Text>

            {SORT_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
              const active = sortMode === opt.id;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.id}
                  style={styles.sortOption}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setSortMode(opt.id);
                    setMenuVisible(false);
                  }}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.sortOptionText,
                      { color: active ? theme.primary : theme.text },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {opt.label}
                  </Text>
                  {active && (
                    <Text
                      style={{
                        color: theme.primary,
                        fontSize: 18,
                        fontWeight: "700",
                      }}
                    >
                      âœ“
                    </Text>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}

             
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

// src/screens/ArtistsScreen.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  View, Text, StyleSheet, TouchableOpacity, Image,
  Modal, Pressable, FlatList,
} from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import Svg, { Path } from "react-native-svg";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { useMediaStore } from "../store/mediaStore";
import { useArtistStore } from "../store/artistStore";
import { useId3Tags } from "../hooks/useId3Tags";
import { resolveArtistName, UNKNOWN_ARTIST } from "../utils/cleanArtistName";

// âœ… shared header (top bar + title + search)
import LibraryPageHeaders from "../components/LibraryPageHeaders";

const LIST_ICON = require("../../assets/images/list.png");
const FONT = "sans-serif";

const StarFillIcon = ({ color = "#f92c47", size = 14 }: { color?: string; size?: number }) => (
  <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">
    <Path
      d="M12 2l3.09 6.26L22 9.27l-5 4.87 1.18 6.88L12 17.77l-6.18 3.25L7 14.14 2 9.27l6.91-1.01L12 2z"
      fill={color}
    />
  </Svg>
);

const ArtistPlaceholder = ({
  color = "#f92c47",
  size = 24,
}: {
  color?: string;
  size?: number;
}) => (
  <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="3.5 4 17 16" fill="none">
    <Path
      fill={color}
      d="M18.39 9.42c1.289-1.282 1.34-2.908.102-4.139-1.23-1.216-2.85-1.186-4.138.103zm-6.08 9.858a.66.66 0 0 0 .667-.667v-4.328l-.051-1.048 2.234-2.072c.842.11 1.728-.235 2.49-1.004l-4.03-4.035c-.776.761-1.098 1.633-.988 2.475l-6.805 7.332c-.278.3-.322.717.022 1.062L4.913 18.2a.31.31 0 0 0 .037.418l.212.22a.31.31 0 0 0 .425.029l1.208-.945c.33.344.755.3 1.048.022l3.8-3.516v4.182a.66.66 0 0 0 .667.667zm-5.053-2.073-.674-.674 6.453-6.84q.186.307.461.593.274.276.571.476z"
    />

type ArtistGroup = { name: string; songs: any[] };

export default function ArtistsScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const theme = useTheme();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const songs = useMediaStore((s: any) => s.songs ?? []);
  const { tagData } = useId3Tags(songs);

  const favourites = useArtistStore((s) => s.favourites);
  const hydrate = useArtistStore((s) => s.hydrate);
  useEffect(() => { hydrate(); }, [hydrate]);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [filterMode, setFilterMode] = useState<"all" | "favourites">("all");
  const [menuVisible, setMenuVisible] = useState(false);

  const artistGroups = useMemo<ArtistGroup[]>(() => {
    const map = new Map<string, ArtistGroup>();
    songs.forEach((s: any) => {
      const name = resolveArtistName(s.artist, tagData[s.id]?.artist);
      if (name === UNKNOWN_ARTIST) return;
      const key = name.toLowerCase();
      let g = map.get(key);
      if (!g) { g = { name, songs: [] }; map.set(key, g); }
      g.songs.push(s);
    });
    return Array.from(map.values()).sort((a, b) =>
      a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }),
    );
  }, [songs, tagData]);

  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    let list = artistGroups;
    if (filterMode === "favourites") list = list.filter((g) => !!favourites[g.name]);
    const q = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q) list = list.filter((g) => g.name.toLowerCase().includes(q));
    return list;
  }, [artistGroups, filterMode, search, favourites]);

  const renderArtist = ({ item }: { item: ArtistGroup }) => {
    const isFav = !!favourites[item.name];
    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={styles.row}
        activeOpacity={0.7}
        onPress={() => nav.navigate("ArtistDetails", { artistName: item.name })}
      >
        <View style={[styles.artWrap, { backgroundColor: theme.surface }]}>
          <ArtistPlaceholder color={theme.primary} size={30} />
        </View>
        <View style={styles.info}>
          <Text numberOfLines={1} style={[styles.name, { color: theme.text }]}>
            {item.name}
          </Text>
          <Text numberOfLines={1} style={[styles.count, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            {item.songs.length} {item.songs.length === 1 ? "song" : "songs"}
          </Text>
        </View>
        {isFav && <StarFillIcon color={theme.primary} size={14} />}
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: theme.background }]}>
      {/* ---------- SHARED HEADER (top bar + title + search) ---------- */}
      <LibraryPageHeaders
        colors={theme}
        topInset={insets.top}
        title="Artists"
        onBack={() => nav.goBack()}
        searchValue={search}
        onSearchChange={setSearch}
        searchPlaceholder="Search in Artists"
        actions={[
          {
            key: "menu",
            icon: LIST_ICON,
            iconStyle: { width: 23, height: 20 },
            onPress: () => setMenuVisible(true),
          },
        ]}
      />

      {/* ---------- LIST ---------- */}
      <FlatList
        data={filtered}
        keyExtractor={(item) => item.name}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 100 }}
        renderItem={renderArtist}
        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => (
          <View style={{ height: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth, backgroundColor: theme.border, marginLeft: 84 }} />
        )}
        ListEmptyComponent={
          <View style={styles.empty}>
            <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: theme.text }]}>
              {filterMode === "favourites" ? "No favourite artists" : "No artists found"}
            </Text>
            <Text style={[styles.emptyText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
              {filterMode === "favourites"
                ? "Tap the star on an artist to add them here."
                : "Scan your library to find artists."}
            </Text>
          </View>
        }
      />

      {/* ---------- MENU (unchanged popup card) ---------- */}
      <Modal
        visible={menuVisible}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setMenuVisible(false)}
        statusBarTranslucent
      >
        <Pressable style={styles.menuBackdrop} onPress={() => setMenuVisible(false)}>
          <View
            style={[styles.menuCard, { backgroundColor: theme.surface, borderColor: theme.border, top: insets.top + 44, right: 10 }]}
          >
            <Pressable onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.menuRow}
                onPress={() => { setMenuVisible(false); nav.navigate("Settings"); }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.menuText, { color: theme.text }]}>Settings</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.menuRow}
                onPress={() => { setMenuVisible(false); nav.navigate("Account"); }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.menuText, { color: theme.text }]}>Account</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <View style={[styles.menuDivider, { backgroundColor: theme.border }]} />

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.menuRow}
                onPress={() => { setMenuVisible(false); setFilterMode("all"); }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.menuText, { color: filterMode === "all" ? theme.primary : theme.text }]}>
                  All Artist
                </Text>
                {filterMode === "all" && <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 16 }}>âœ“</Text>}
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.menuRow}
                onPress={() => { setMenuVisible(false); setFilterMode("favourites"); }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.menuText, { color: filterMode === "favourites" ? theme.primary : theme.text }]}>
                  Favourites
                </Text>
                {filterMode === "favourites" && <Text style={{ color: theme.primary, fontSize: 16 }}>âœ“</Text>}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </Pressable>
          </View>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}
