const UNKNOWN_ARTIST = "Unknown artist";

const shuffleArr = <T,>(arr: T[]): T[] => {
  const a = [...arr];
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
};

const getArtwork = (s: any): string | undefined =>
  s?.artwork ?? s?.artworkUri ?? s?.cover ?? undefined;

const DefaultSongArt = React.memo(function DefaultSongArt({
  style,
}: {
  style?: any;
}) {
  return (
    <Image
      source={MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG}
      style={style}
      resizeMode="cover"
      fadeDuration={0}
    />
  );
});

const SafeImage = React.memo(function SafeImage({
  uri,
  style,
  reloadKey = 0,
  resizeMode = "cover",
}: {
  uri?: string | null;
  style: any;
  reloadKey?: number;
  resizeMode?: "cover" | "contain" | "stretch" | "center";
}) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setFailed(false);
  }, [uri, reloadKey]);

  let safeUri = uri;
  if (
    safeUri &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("http") &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("file://") &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("content://") &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("ph://") &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("data:")
  ) {
    safeUri = `file://${safeUri}`;
  }

  if (!safeUri || failed) {
    return <DefaultSongArt style={style} />;
  }

  return (
    <Image
      source={{ uri: safeUri }}
      style={style}
      resizeMode={resizeMode}
      fadeDuration={0}
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
});

// ===========================================================================
// ARTIST helpers
// ===========================================================================
const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  const t = a.trim();
  if (!t) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(t);
};

const getStoreArtist = (s: any): string => {
  const candidates = [
    s?.artist,
    s?.artists,
    s?.albumArtist,
    s?.album_artist,
    s?.author,
    s?.composer,
    s?.contributingArtist,
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (typeof c === "string" && !isUnknownArtist(c)) return c.trim();
    if (Array.isArray(c) && c.length > 0) {
      const joined = c.filter(Boolean).join(", ");
      if (joined && !isUnknownArtist(joined)) return joined;
    }
  }
  return "";
};

// â”€â”€ ID3 tag reader (fallback for uncached songs) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const B64_CHARS =
  "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
const B64_LOOKUP = (() => {
  const t = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < B64_CHARS.length; i++) t[B64_CHARS.charCodeAt(i)] = i;
  return t;
})();

const base64ToBytes = (b64: string): Uint8Array => {
  const clean = b64.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9+/]/g, "");
  const out = new Uint8Array(Math.floor((clean.length * 3) / 4));
  let p = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i += 4) {
    const a = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i)] || 0;
    const b = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 1)] || 0;
    const c = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 2)] || 0;
    const d = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 3)] || 0;
    out[p++] = (a << 2) | (b >> 4);
    out[p++] = ((b & 15) << 4) | (c >> 2);
    out[p++] = ((c & 3) << 6) | d;
  }
  return out;
};

const syncSafe = (b: Uint8Array, o: number) =>
  ((b[o] & 0x7f) << 21) |
  ((b[o + 1] & 0x7f) << 14) |
  ((b[o + 2] & 0x7f) << 7) |
  (b[o + 3] & 0x7f);

const decodeUtf16 = (b: Uint8Array, bigEndian: boolean): string => {
  let s = "";
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < b.length; i += 2) {
    const code = bigEndian ? (b[i] << 8) | b[i + 1] : (b[i + 1] << 8) | b[i];
    s += String.fromCharCode(code);
  }
  return s;
};

const decodeUtf8 = (b: Uint8Array): string => {
  try {
    let pct = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
      pct += "%" + (b[i] < 16 ? "0" : "") + b[i].toString(16);
    }
    return decodeURIComponent(pct);
  } catch {
    let s = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
    return s;
  }
};

const decodeId3Text = (b: Uint8Array, enc: number): string => {
  let s = "";
  if (enc === 1) {
    if (b[0] === 0xfe && b[1] === 0xff) s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), true);
    else if (b[0] === 0xff && b[1] === 0xfe)
      s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), false);
    else s = decodeUtf16(b, false);
  } else if (enc === 2) {
    s = decodeUtf16(b, true);
  } else if (enc === 3) {
    s = decodeUtf8(b);
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
  }
  return s
    .split("\u0000")
    .map((x) => x.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
};

const readId3Frame = (
  b: Uint8Array,
  frameIds: string[],
): string | undefined => {
  if (b.length < 10 || b[0] !== 0x49 || b[1] !== 0x44 || b[2] !== 0x33) {
    return undefined;
  }
  const major = b[3];
  const flags = b[5];
  const tagSize = syncSafe(b, 6);
  let pos = 10;

  if (major >= 3 && (flags & 0x40) !== 0) {
    const ext =
      major === 4
        ? syncSafe(b, pos)
        : (((b[pos] << 24) |
            (b[pos + 1] << 16) |
            (b[pos + 2] << 8) |
            b[pos + 3]) >>>
            0) +
          4;
    pos += ext;
  }

  const end = Math.min(b.length, 10 + tagSize);
  const idLen = major === 2 ? 3 : 4;
  const hdrLen = major === 2 ? 6 : 10;

  while (pos + hdrLen <= end) {
    let id = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < idLen; i++) id += String.fromCharCode(b[pos + i]);
    if (!/^[A-Z0-9]+$/.test(id)) break;

    let size: number;
    if (major === 2) {
      size = (b[pos + 3] << 16) | (b[pos + 4] << 8) | b[pos + 5];
    } else if (major === 4) {
      size = syncSafe(b, pos + 4);
    } else {
      size =
        ((b[pos + 4] << 24) |
          (b[pos + 5] << 16) |
          (b[pos + 6] << 8) |
          b[pos + 7]) >>>
        0;
    }

    const dataStart = pos + hdrLen;
    if (size <= 0 || dataStart + size > b.length) break;

    if (frameIds.includes(id)) {
      const text = decodeId3Text(
        b.subarray(dataStart + 1, dataStart + size),
        b[dataStart],
      );
      if (text && text.trim()) return text.trim();
    }

    pos = dataStart + size;
  }
  return undefined;
};

const parseId3Artist = (b: Uint8Array): string | undefined => {
  const primary = readId3Frame(b, ["TPE1", "TP1"]);
  if (primary && !isUnknownArtist(primary)) return primary;
  const albumArtist = readId3Frame(b, ["TPE2", "TP2"]);
  return !isUnknownArtist(albumArtist) ? albumArtist : undefined;
};

const readArtistFromFile = async (uri: string): Promise<string | undefined> => {
  try {
    const b64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(uri, {
      encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
      position: 0,
      length: 131072,
    });
    const artist = parseId3Artist(base64ToBytes(b64));
    return artist && !isUnknownArtist(artist) ? artist : undefined;
  } catch {
    return undefined;
  }
};

// ===========================================================================
const getDurationSec = (s: any): number => {
  const d =
    s?.duration ?? s?.durationSec ?? s?.durationSeconds ?? s?.length ?? 0;
  const num = typeof d === "number" ? d : parseFloat(d);
  if (!isFinite(num) || num <= 0) return 0;
  return num > 10000 ? num / 1000 : num;
};

const formatTotalDuration = (totalSec: number): string => {
  if (!totalSec || totalSec < 1) return "0 minutes";
  const totalMin = Math.round(totalSec / 60);
  if (totalMin < 1) {
    return `${Math.round(totalSec)} second${Math.round(totalSec) === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  }
  if (totalMin < 60) {
    return `${totalMin} minute${totalMin === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  }
  const hrs = Math.floor(totalMin / 60);
  const mins = totalMin % 60;
  if (mins === 0) return `${hrs} hr${hrs === 1 ? "" : "s"}`;
  return `${hrs} hr ${mins} min`;
};

// ===========================================================================
const POPUP_ART_SIZE = 72;

function PopupArtwork({ uri, bg }: { uri?: string; bg: string }) {
  return (
    <SafeImage
      uri={uri}
      style={{
        width: POPUP_ART_SIZE,
        height: POPUP_ART_SIZE,
        borderRadius: 10,
        backgroundColor: bg,
      }}
    />
  );
}

// ===========================================================================
// âœ… SongRow â€” memoized
// ===========================================================================
type SongRowProps = {
  item: Song & { artist?: string; artwork?: string };
  isPinned: boolean;
  colors: any;
  styles: any;
  onPress: (song: Song) => void;
  onTogglePin: (song: Song) => void;
  onMore: (song: Song) => void;
};

const SongRow = React.memo(function SongRow({
  item,
  isPinned,
  colors,
  styles,
  onPress,
  onTogglePin,
  onMore,
}: SongRowProps) {
  const artwork = getArtwork(item) ?? (item as any).artwork;
  const artist = (item as any).artist || UNKNOWN_ARTIST;

  return (
    <View style={styles.songRow}>
      <TouchableOpacity
        onPress={() => onTogglePin(item as any)}
        hitSlop={{ top: 8, bottom: 8, left: 6, right: 6 }}
        style={styles.starHit}
      >
        <Text style={[styles.star, isPinned && styles.starActive]}>â˜…</Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>

      <TouchableOpacity
        activeOpacity={0.8}
        onPress={() => onPress(item as any)}
        style={styles.artWrap}
      >
        <SafeImage uri={artwork} style={styles.art} />
      </TouchableOpacity>

      <TouchableOpacity
        style={styles.songInfo}
        activeOpacity={0.7}
        onPress={() => onPress(item as any)}
      >
        <Text style={styles.songTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
          {item.title}
        </Text>
        <Text style={styles.songArtist} numberOfLines={1}>
          {artist}
        </Text>
      </TouchableOpacity>

      <TouchableOpacity
        onPress={() => onMore(item as any)}
        hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 20, right: 20 }}
        style={styles.dotsHit}
      >
        <Image
          source={MORE_PNG}
          style={[styles.moreIcon, { tintColor: colors.textSecondary }]}
          resizeMode="contain"
        />
      </TouchableOpacity>
    </View>
  );
});

// ===========================================================================
export default function FavouriteSongsScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const themeRaw = useTheme() as any;
  const base =
    themeRaw && typeof themeRaw === "object" && themeRaw.background
      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : LIGHT_THEME;

  const COLORS = useMemo(() => {
    const isDark = (base.mode ?? "dark") === "dark";
    const fallback = isDark ? DARK_THEME : LIGHT_THEME;
    return {
      ...fallback,
      ...base,
      background: isDark ? SCREEN_BG_DARK : SCREEN_BG_LIGHT,
      field: isDark ? FIELD_BG_DARK : FIELD_BG_LIGHT,
    };
  }, [base]);

  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(COLORS), [COLORS]);

  const userName = useUserName();

  const songs = useMediaStore((s) => s.songs);
  const favorites = useMediaStore((s) => s.favoritesSong);
  const toggleFavorite = useMediaStore((s) => s.toggleSongFavorite);

  const { loadAndPlay } = useAudioPlayer();
  const { artworkMap } = useArtworkCache(songs);

  const [pinned, setPinned] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const [actionSong, setActionSong] = useState<Song | null>(null);
  const [playlists, setPlaylists] = useState<Playlist[]>([]);
  const [addToOpen, setAddToOpen] = useState(false);
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [searchVisible, setSearchVisible] = useState(false);
  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const [addSongsOpen, setAddSongsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSongIds, setSelectedSongIds] = useState<
    Record<string, boolean>
  >({});

  // âœ… Header title: slides up from the header bottom to the centre (smooth),
  //    and is removed instantly when you scroll back up.
  const titleAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const titleShown = useRef(false);
  const scrollY = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const searchRef = useRef<TextInput>(null);
  const listRef = useRef<any>(null);

  // hero bottom edge (scroll offset at which it has fully passed the header)
  const heroEnd = HERO_TOP_GAP + HERO_SIZE + (searchVisible ? SEARCH_BLOCK : 0);
  const titleThreshold = useRef(heroEnd + 4);
  titleThreshold.current = heroEnd + 4;

  // âœ… Seed artists synchronously from disk cache
  const [tagArtists, setTagArtists] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() => {
    const init: Record<string, string> = {};
    songs.forEach((s) => {
      try {
        const cached = getCachedTags(s.id);
        if (cached?.artist && !isUnknownArtist(cached.artist)) {
          init[s.id] = cached.artist;
        }
      } catch {
        /* cache miss is fine */
      }
    });
    return init;
  });

  const requestedTagIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const mounted = useRef(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length === 0) return;

    const missing = songs.filter(
      (s) =>
        !!favorites[s.id] &&
        !requestedTagIds.current.has(s.id) &&
        !tagArtists[s.id] &&
        !getStoreArtist(s),
    );
    if (missing.length === 0) return;

    missing.forEach((s) => requestedTagIds.current.add(s.id));

    (async () => {
      const results: Record<string, string> = {};
      let cursor = 0;

      const worker = async () => {
        while (cursor < missing.length && mounted.current) {
          const idx = cursor++;
          const s = missing[idx];
          const uri = (s as any).uri ?? (s as any).path;
          if (!uri) continue;
          const artist = await readArtistFromFile(uri);
          if (artist) results[s.id] = artist;
        }
      };

      await Promise.all(
        Array.from(
          { length: Math.min(TAG_CONCURRENCY, missing.length) },
          () => worker(),
        ),
      );

      if (mounted.current && Object.keys(results).length > 0) {
        setTagArtists((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...results }));
      }
    })();
  }, [songs, favorites]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  const onScroll = useCallback(
    (e: NativeSyntheticEvent<NativeScrollEvent>) => {
      const y = e.nativeEvent.contentOffset.y;
      const next = y > titleThreshold.current;
      if (next === titleShown.current) return;
      titleShown.current = next;

      if (next) {
        // smooth: bottom of header -> centre
        Animated.timing(titleAnim, {
          toValue: 1,
          duration: 260,
          easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }).start();
      } else {
        // instant remove
        titleAnim.stopAnimation();
        titleAnim.setValue(0);
      }
    },
    [titleAnim],
  );

  // â”€â”€ data â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const data = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();

    const list = songs
      .filter((s) => !!favorites[s.id])
      .map((s) => {
        let artist = getStoreArtist(s) || tagArtists[s.id];
        if (!artist) {
          try {
            const cached = getCachedTags(s.id);
            if (cached?.artist && !isUnknownArtist(cached.artist)) {
              artist = cached.artist;
            }
          } catch {
            /* ignore */
          }
        }
        return {
          ...s,
          artist: artist || UNKNOWN_ARTIST,
          artwork: getArtwork(s) ?? artworkMap[s.id],
        };
      })
      .filter((s) => {
        if (!q) return true;
        const title = (s.title ?? "").toLowerCase();
        const artist = (s.artist ?? "").toLowerCase();
        return title.includes(q) || artist.includes(q);
      });

    return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      const pa = pinned[a.id] ? 1 : 0;
      const pb = pinned[b.id] ? 1 : 0;
      if (pa !== pb) return pb - pa;
      return (a.title ?? "").localeCompare(b.title ?? "", undefined, {
        sensitivity: "base",
      });
    });
  }, [songs, favorites, artworkMap, pinned, query, tagArtists]);

  const indexMap = useMemo(() => {
    const map: Record<string, number> = {};
    for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) map[data[i].id] = i;
    return map;
  }, [data]);

  const nonFavouriteSongs = useMemo(() => {
    return songs
      .filter((s) => !favorites[s.id])
      .map((s) => ({
        ...s,
        artist: getStoreArtist(s) || tagArtists[s.id] || UNKNOWN_ARTIST,
        artwork: getArtwork(s) ?? artworkMap[s.id],
      }))
      .sort((a, b) =>
        (a.title ?? "").localeCompare(b.title ?? "", undefined, {
          sensitivity: "base",
        }),
      );
  }, [songs, favorites, artworkMap, tagArtists]);

  const totalSeconds = useMemo(
    () => data.reduce((sum, s) => sum + getDurationSec(s), 0),
    [data],
  );

  const loadPlaylists = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      setPlaylists(raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : []);
    } catch {
      setPlaylists([]);
    }
  }, []);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      loadPlaylists();
    }, [loadPlaylists]),
  );

  // â”€â”€ actions â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const playSong = useCallback(
    (song: Song) => {
      const index = indexMap[song.id];
      if (index === undefined) return;
      try {
        const p = loadAndPlay(data as any, index);
        if (p && typeof (p as any).catch === "function") {
          (p as Promise<any>).catch((e) => {
            console.error("[FavouriteSongs] play error:", e);
            Alert.alert("Playback failed", "Could not play this song.");
          });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[FavouriteSongs] play error:", e);
        Alert.alert("Playback failed", "Could not play this song.");
      }
    },
    [data, indexMap, loadAndPlay],
  );

  const playAll = useCallback(
    (shuffled = false) => {
      if (!data.length) return;
      const list = shuffled ? shuffleArr(data) : data;
      try {
        const p = loadAndPlay(list as any, 0);
        if (p && typeof (p as any).catch === "function") {
          (p as Promise<any>).catch((e) =>
            console.error("[FavouriteSongs] playAll error:", e),
          );
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[FavouriteSongs] playAll error:", e);
      }
    },
    [data, loadAndPlay],
  );

  const togglePin = useCallback((song: Song) => {
    setPinned((prev) => {
      const next = !prev[song.id];
      showToast(next ? "Pinned to top" : "Unpinned");
      return { ...prev, [song.id]: next };
    });
  }, []);

  const removeFavourite = useCallback(
    (song: Song) => {
      toggleFavorite(song.id);
      showToast("Removed from Favourites");
    },
    [toggleFavorite],
  );

  const openMore = useCallback((song: Song) => {
    setActionSong(song);
  }, []);

  const addToPlaylist = async (pl: Playlist, song: Song) => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const all: Playlist[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      const next = all.map((p) =>
        p.id === pl.id
          ? {
              ...p,
              songs: [...(p.songs ?? []), song],
              updatedAt: Date.now(),
              artwork: p.artwork ?? getArtwork(song) ?? null,
            }
          : p,
      );
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS, JSON.stringify(next));
      setPlaylists(next);
      setAddToOpen(false);
      showToast(`Added to "${pl.name}"`);
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not add to playlist");
    }
  };

  const toggleSelection = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedSongIds((prev) => ({
      ...prev,
      [id]: !prev[id],
    }));
  };

  const handleAddSelected = () => {
    const idsToAdd = Object.keys(selectedSongIds).filter(
      (id) => selectedSongIds[id],
    );
    if (idsToAdd.length === 0) {
      showToast("Select at least one song");
      return;
    }
    idsToAdd.forEach((id) => toggleFavorite(id));
    setAddSongsOpen(false);
    showToast(
      `Added ${idsToAdd.length} song${idsToAdd.length === 1 ? "" : "s"} to Favourites`,
    );
  };

  const selectAll = () => {
    const allSelected = nonFavouriteSongs.every((s) => selectedSongIds[s.id]);
    const newSelection: Record<string, boolean> = {};
    if (!allSelected) {
      nonFavouriteSongs.forEach((s) => {
        newSelection[s.id] = true;
      });
    }
    setSelectedSongIds(newSelection);
  };

  const playNext = (song: Song) => {
    showToast(`"${song.title}" will play next`);
  };

  const popupTarget: any = data.length > 0 ? data[0] : null;

  const renderItem = useCallback(
    ({ item }: ListRenderItemInfo<any>) => (
      <SongRow
        item={item}
        isPinned={!!pinned[item.id]}
        colors={COLORS}
        styles={styles}
        onPress={playSong}
        onTogglePin={togglePin}
        onMore={openMore}
      />
    ),
    [pinned, COLORS, styles, playSong, togglePin, openMore],
  );

  const actionArtwork = actionSong
    ? getArtwork(actionSong) ?? (actionSong as any).artwork
    : undefined;
  const actionArtist = actionSong
    ? (actionSong as any).artist || UNKNOWN_ARTIST
    : UNKNOWN_ARTIST;
  const isActionPinned = actionSong ? !!pinned[actionSong.id] : false;

  const topBarHeight = insets.top + HEADER_HEIGHT;
  // âœ… Header colour = screen background colour
  const headerBg = COLORS.background;

  const headerBgOpacity = scrollY.interpolate({
    inputRange: [heroEnd - 30, heroEnd + 4],
    outputRange: [0, 1],
    extrapolate: "clamp",
  });

  const handleScroll = useMemo(
    () =>
      Animated.event([{ nativeEvent: { contentOffset: { y: scrollY } } }], {
        useNativeDriver: false,
        listener: onScroll,
      }),
    [scrollY, onScroll],
  );

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: COLORS.background }]}>
      {/* ---------------- LIST ---------------- */}
      <AnimatedFlatList
        ref={listRef}
        style={styles.list}
        data={data}
        keyExtractor={(it) => it.id}
        renderItem={renderItem}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          paddingTop: topBarHeight,
          paddingBottom: 160,
        }}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
        onScroll={handleScroll}
        scrollEventThrottle={16}
        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={styles.songSep} />}
        initialNumToRender={15}
        maxToRenderPerBatch={10}
        windowSize={7}
        updateCellsBatchingPeriod={50}
        ListFooterComponent={
          data.length > 0 ? (
            <View>
              <View style={styles.songBottomDivider} />
              <View style={styles.footerInfoRow}>
                <Text style={styles.footerInfoText}>
                  {data.length} {data.length === 1 ? "song" : "songs"},{" "}
                  {formatTotalDuration(totalSeconds)}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>
          ) : null
        }
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View>
            {searchVisible && (
              <View style={styles.searchWrap}>
                <Image
                  source={SEARCH_ICON}
                  style={[
                    styles.searchIcon,
                    { tintColor: COLORS.textSecondary },
                  ]}
                />
                <TextInput
                  ref={searchRef}
                  value={query}
                  onChangeText={setQuery}
                  placeholder="Search"
                  placeholderTextColor={COLORS.textMuted}
                  style={styles.searchInput}
                  returnKeyType="search"
                  autoCorrect={false}
                  autoCapitalize="none"
                />
                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => {
                    setQuery("");
                    setSearchVisible(false);
                  }}
                  hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.clearIcon}>âœ•</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            )}

            <View style={styles.heroWrap}>
              <Image source={FAVOURITE_HERO} style={styles.hero} />
            </View>

            <View style={styles.titleBlock}>
              <View style={styles.titleRow}>
                <Text style={styles.title}>Favourite Songs</Text>
                <Text style={styles.titleStar}> â˜…</Text>
              </View>
              {userName ? (
                <Text style={styles.ownerName}>{userName}</Text>
              ) : null}
            </View>

            <View style={styles.controlsRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.circleBtn}
                onPress={() => playAll(true)}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
              >
                <ShuffleIcon size={18} color={COLORS.text} />
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.playBtn, { backgroundColor: COLORS.text }]}
                onPress={() => playAll(false)}
                activeOpacity={0.85}
              >
                <PlayIcon size={18} color={COLORS.background} />
                <Text style={[styles.playText, { color: COLORS.background }]}>
                  Play
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.circleBtn}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={() => {
                  setSelectedSongIds({});
                  setAddSongsOpen(true);
                }}
              >
                <Text style={styles.plusText}>+</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.divider} />

            {data.length === 0 && (
              <View style={styles.empty}>
                <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>
                  {query ? "No matching songs" : "No favourite song"}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}
          </View>
        }
      />

      {/* ---------------- TOP BAR â€” transparent, turns solid after the hero is hidden ---------------- */}
      <View
        pointerEvents="box-none"
        style={[
          styles.topBarWrap,
          { height: topBarHeight, paddingTop: insets.top },
        ]}
      >
        <Animated.View
          pointerEvents="none"
          style={[
            StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
            { backgroundColor: headerBg, opacity: headerBgOpacity },
          ]}
        />

        <View style={styles.topBar}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => nav.goBack()}
            hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
          >
            <BackArrowSvg size={24} color={COLORS.text} />
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <Animated.View
            style={[
              styles.headerCenter,
              {
                opacity: titleAnim,
                transform: [
                  {
                    translateY: titleAnim.interpolate({
                      inputRange: [0, 1],
                      outputRange: [HEADER_HEIGHT / 2, 0],
                    }),
                  },
                ],
              },
            ]}
            pointerEvents="none"
          >
            <Text style={styles.headerCenterTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
              Favourite Songs
            </Text>
          </Animated.View>

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setMenuOpen(true)}
            hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
          >
            <Image
              source={MORE_PNG}
              style={{ width: 16, height: 16, tintColor: COLORS.text }}
              resizeMode="contain"
            />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>
      </View>

      {/* ---------------- MINI PLAYER ---------------- */}
      <View style={styles.bottomArea} pointerEvents="box-none">
        <MiniPlayer />
      </View>

      {/* ---------------- 3-DOT BOTTOM-SHEET POPUP ---------------- */}
      <Modal
        visible={menuOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.popupSheet}>
            <View style={styles.popupHeader}>
              <View style={styles.popupArtWrap}>
                <Image
                  source={FAVOURITE_HERO}
                  style={styles.popupArt}
                  resizeMode="cover"
                />
                {/* play icon in the middle on a transparent white circle */}
                <View style={styles.popupPlayCircle} pointerEvents="none">
                  <Image
                    source={PLAY_PNG}
                    style={styles.popupPlayIcon}
                    resizeMode="contain"
                  />
                </View>
              </View>
              <View style={styles.popupHeaderText}>
                <Text style={styles.popupTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
                  Favourite Song
                </Text>
                {userName ? (
                  <Text style={styles.popupUsername} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {userName}
                  </Text>
                ) : null}
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.popupDivider} />

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.popupRow, styles.popupRowWithIcon]}
              onPress={() => {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                setSearchVisible(true);
                listRef.current?.scrollToOffset?.({ offset: 0, animated: true });
                setTimeout(() => searchRef.current?.focus(), 350);
              }}
            >
              <SearchSongIcon size={24} color={COLORS.primary} />
              <Text style={[styles.popupRowText, styles.popupRowTextIcon]}>
                Search Song
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.popupRow, styles.popupRowWithIcon]}
              onPress={() => {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                showToast("Sort options coming soon");
              }}
            >
              <SortIcon size={24} color={COLORS.primary} />
              <Text style={[styles.popupRowText, styles.popupRowTextIcon]}>
                Sort By
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.popupRow, styles.popupRowWithIcon]}
              onPress={() => {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                if (popupTarget) togglePin(popupTarget);
              }}
            >
              <PinPlaylistIcon size={24} color={COLORS.primary} />
              <Text style={[styles.popupRowText, styles.popupRowTextIcon]}>
                Pin Playlist
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.popupRow, styles.popupRowWithIcon]}
              onPress={() => {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                if (popupTarget) removeFavourite(popupTarget);
              }}
            >
              <FavStarIcon size={24} color={COLORS.primary} />
              <Text style={[styles.popupRowText, styles.popupRowTextIcon]}>
                Favourite
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.popupRow, styles.popupRowWithIcon]}
              onPress={() => {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                if (popupTarget) {
                  setActionSong(popupTarget);
                  setTimeout(() => setAddToOpen(true), 100);
                }
              }}
            >
              <AddPlaylistIcon size={24} color={COLORS.primary} />
              <Text style={[styles.popupRowText, styles.popupRowTextIcon]}>
                Add to Playlist
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={[styles.popupRow, styles.popupRowNoIcon]}
              onPress={() => {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                if (popupTarget) playNext(popupTarget);
              }}
            >
              <Text style={styles.popupRowText}>Play next</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* ---------------- SONG â‹® POPUP â€” bottom sheet ---------------- */}
      <Modal
        visible={!!actionSong && !addToOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setActionSong(null)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setActionSong(null)}
          />
          <View style={styles.popupSheet}>
            <View style={styles.popupHeader}>
              <SafeImage uri={actionArtwork} style={styles.popupArt} />
              <View style={styles.popupHeaderText}>
                <Text style={styles.popupTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {actionSong?.title ?? "Song options"}
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.popupUsername} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {actionArtist}
                </Text>
                <Text style={styles.popupUsername} numberOfLines={1}>
                  {isActionPinned ? "Pinned to top" : "In favourites"}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.popupDivider} />

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.popupRow}
              onPress={() => {
                const sg = actionSong;
                setActionSong(null);
                if (sg) playSong(sg);
              }}
            >
              <Text style={styles.popupRowText}>Play</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.popupRow}
              onPress={() => {
                if (actionSong) togglePin(actionSong);
                setActionSong(null);
              }}
            >
              <Text style={styles.popupRowText}>
                {isActionPinned ? "Unpin song" : "Pin song on this list"}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.popupRow}
              onPress={() => setAddToOpen(true)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.popupRowText}>Add to playlist</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.popupRow}
              onPress={() => {
                if (actionSong) playNext(actionSong);
                setActionSong(null);
              }}
            >
              <Text style={styles.popupRowText}>Play next</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.popupRow}
              onPress={() => {
                if (actionSong) removeFavourite(actionSong);
                setActionSong(null);
              }}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.popupRowText, { color: "#E53935" }]}>
                Remove from Favourites
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* ---------------- ADD ONE SONG TO PLAYLIST ---------------- */}
      <Modal
        visible={addToOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setAddToOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setAddToOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.bottomSheet}>
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Add to playlist</Text>

            {playlists.length === 0 ? (
              <Text style={styles.noPlaylists}>
                You don't have any playlists yet.
              </Text>
            ) : (
              <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 320 }}>
                {playlists.map((pl) => (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={pl.id}
                    style={styles.playlistPick}
                    onPress={() => {
                      const s = actionSong;
                      setActionSong(null);
                      if (s) addToPlaylist(pl, s);
                      else setAddToOpen(false);
                    }}
                  >
                    <View style={styles.plIcon}>
                      <Text style={styles.plIconText}>â™ª</Text>
                    </View>
                    <Text style={styles.optionText} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {pl.name}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                ))}
              </ScrollView>
            )}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={() => {
                setAddToOpen(false);
                setActionSong(null);
              }}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* ---------------- MULTI-SELECT ADD SONGS MODAL ---------------- */}
      <Modal
        visible={addSongsOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setAddSongsOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setAddSongsOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={[styles.bottomSheet, { height: "75%" }]}>
            <View style={styles.multiSelectHeader}>
              <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Add to Favourites</Text>
              <TouchableOpacity onPress={selectAll} style={{ padding: 4 }}>
                <Text
                  style={{
                    color: COLORS.primary,
                    fontSize: 15,
                    fontWeight: "600",
                  }}
                >
                  Select All
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            {nonFavouriteSongs.length === 0 ? (
              <Text style={styles.noPlaylists}>
                You have favourited all your songs!
              </Text>
            ) : (
              <FlatList
                data={nonFavouriteSongs}
                keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
                contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 20 }}
                initialNumToRender={12}
                maxToRenderPerBatch={10}
                windowSize={7}
                renderItem={({ item }) => {
                  const isSelected = !!selectedSongIds[item.id];
                  const artwork = getArtwork(item) ?? (item as any).artwork;

                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={styles.selectRow}
                      onPress={() => toggleSelection(item.id)}
                      activeOpacity={0.7}
                    >
                      <View style={styles.checkCircle}>
                        {isSelected && (
                          <View style={styles.checkCircleInner}>
                            <Text style={styles.checkText}>âœ“</Text>
                          </View>
                        )}
                      </View>

                      <SafeImage uri={artwork} style={styles.selectArt} />

                      <View style={styles.selectInfo}>
                        <Text style={styles.selectTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
                          {item.title}
                        </Text>
                        <Text style={styles.selectArtist} numberOfLines={1}>
                          {item.artist}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                }}
              />
            )}

            <View style={styles.multiSelectFooter}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.cancelMultiBtn}
                onPress={() => setAddSongsOpen(false)}
              >
                <Text style={styles.cancelText}>Cancel</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[
                  styles.addSelectedBtn,
                  {
                    opacity: Object.values(selectedSongIds).some(Boolean)
                      ? 1
                      : 0.5,
                  },
                ]}
                onPress={handleAddSelected}
                disabled={!Object.values(selectedSongIds).some(Boolean)}
              >
                <Text style={styles.addSelectedText}>
                  Add Selected (
                  {Object.values(selectedSongIds).filter(Boolean).length})
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

========== src\screens\MusicVideosScreen.tsx ==========
// src/screens/MusicVideosScreen.tsx
import React, {
  useCallback,
  useEffect,
  useMemo,
  useState,
} from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Image,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  useWindowDimensions,
} from "react-native";
import { useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";
import { PlayIcon, ShuffleIcon } from "../icons/Icons";
import { CheckIcon } from "../icons/VideoIcons";
import { MUSIC_VIDEOS, MusicVideo } from "../data/musicVideos";
import { useVideoStore } from "../store/videoStore";

// âœ… Shared header (top bar + title + search)
import LibraryPageHeaders from "../components/LibraryPageHeaders";

const FONT = "sans-serif";
const LIST_ICON = require("../../assets/images/list.png");

// â”€â”€ ACTION ROW styling (Play / Shuffle) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const ACTION_RADIUS = 26;
const ACTION_HEIGHT = 52;
const ACTION_TOP_GAP = 22;

const DEFAULT_COLORS = {
  background: "#000000",
  surface: "#1c1c1e",
  surfaceLight: "#2c2c2e",
  border: "#3a3a3c",
  text: "#ffffff",
  textSecondary: "#a1a1aa",
  textMuted: "#71717a",
  primary: "#f92c47",
};

type Filter = "all" | "fav";
type Sort = "artist" | "title" | "recent";

export default function MusicVideosScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { width: winW } = useWindowDimensions();

  // ðŸ‘‡ grid dimensions
  const GRID_GAP = 10;
  const PADDING_H = 12;
  const cardW = Math.floor((winW - PADDING_H * 2 - GRID_GAP) / 2);
  const cardH = Math.round((cardW * 9) / 16);

  // theme merge (same pattern as Playlists / Artists / Albums)
  const themeRaw = useTheme() as any;
  const base =
    themeRaw && typeof themeRaw === "object" && themeRaw.background
      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : themeRaw?.theme && typeof themeRaw.theme === "object"
          ? themeRaw.theme
          : {};
  const COLORS = { ...DEFAULT_COLORS, ...base };
  const styles = useMemo(
    () => createStyles(COLORS, PADDING_H),
    [themeRaw], // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  );

  const favourites = useVideoStore((s) => s.favourites);
  const loadFavs = useVideoStore((s) => s.loadFavs);
  const playQueue = useVideoStore((s) => s.playQueue);

  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<Filter>("all");
  const [sort, setSort] = useState<Sort>("recent");
  const [showMenu, setShowMenu] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadFavs();
  }, [loadFavs]);

  // â”€â”€ navigation helper (same approach as LibraryScreen) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const navigateTo = useCallback(
    (name: string): boolean => {
      let n: any = nav;
      while (n) {
        const names: string[] | undefined = n.getState?.()?.routeNames;
        if (names && names.includes(name)) {
          n.navigate(name);
          return true;
        }
        n = n.getParent?.();
      }
      showToast(`"${name}" screen is not registered in the navigator`);
      return false;
    },
    [nav],
  );

  // â”€â”€ list (filter â†’ search â†’ sort) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const list: MusicVideo[] = useMemo(() => {
    let l = MUSIC_VIDEOS.slice();
    if (filter === "fav") l = l.filter((v) => favourites[v.id]);

    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (q) {
      l = l.filter(
        (v) =>
          v.title.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
          v.artist.toLowerCase().includes(q),
      );
    }

    if (sort === "artist") {
      l.sort((a, b) => a.artist.localeCompare(b.artist));
    } else if (sort === "title") {
      l.sort((a, b) => a.title.localeCompare(b.title));
    } else {
      l.sort((a, b) => b.addedAt - a.addedAt);
    }
    return l;
  }, [filter, sort, query, favourites]);

  const playAll = () => {
    if (!list.length) return showToast("No music videos");
    playQueue(list, 0);
  };

  const shuffleAll = () => {
    if (!list.length) return showToast("No music videos");
    const shuffled = list.slice();
    for (let i = shuffled.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
      const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
      [shuffled[i], shuffled[j]] = [shuffled[j], shuffled[i]];
    }
    playQueue(shuffled, 0);
  };

  // â”€â”€ bottom-sheet menu row helper â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
  const SheetRow = ({
    label,
    selected,
    onPress,
    onPressOverride,
  }: {
    label: string;
    selected?: boolean;
    onPress?: () => void;
    onPressOverride?: () => void;
  }) => (
    <TouchableOpacity
      style={styles.sheetItem}
      activeOpacity={0.7}
      onPress={onPressOverride ?? onPress}
    >
      <Text
        style={[
          styles.sheetCheck,
          selected && { color: COLORS.text },
        ]}
      >
        {selected ? "âœ“" : ""}
      </Text>
      <Text
        style={[
          styles.sheetItemText,
          selected && { color: COLORS.text },
        ]}
      >
        {label}
      </Text>
    </TouchableOpacity>
  );

  const emptyText =
    filter === "fav" && !query.trim()
      ? "No favourite music videos yet."
      : "No music videos found.";

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* ---------- SHARED HEADER ---------- */}
      <LibraryPageHeaders
        colors={COLORS}
        topInset={insets.top}
        title="Music Videos"
        onBack={() => nav.goBack()}
        searchValue={query}
        onSearchChange={setQuery}
        searchPlaceholder="Search in Music Videos"
        actions={[
          {
            key: "menu",
            icon: LIST_ICON,
            iconStyle: { width: 23, height: 20 },
            onPress: () => setShowMenu(true),
          },
        ]}
      />

      <ScrollView
        contentContainerStyle={styles.content}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
      >
        {/* Play / Shuffle */}
        <View style={styles.actionRow}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.actionBtn}
            activeOpacity={0.75}
            onPress={playAll}
          >
            <PlayIcon size={20} color={COLORS.primary} />
            <Text style={styles.actionLabel}>Play</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>

          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.actionBtn}
            activeOpacity={0.75}
            onPress={shuffleAll}
          >
            <ShuffleIcon size={20} color={COLORS.primary} />
            <Text style={styles.actionLabel}>Shuffle</Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </View>

        <View style={styles.listDivider} />

        {/* Video grid */}
        {list.length === 0 ? (
          <Text style={styles.empty}>{emptyText}</Text>
        ) : (
          <View style={styles.gridWrap}>
            {list.map((v, i) => (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={v.id}
                style={{ width: cardW }}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                onPress={() => playQueue(list, i)}
              >
                <View
                  style={[
                    styles.gridThumbWrap,
                    { width: cardW, height: cardH },
                  ]}
                >
                  <Image
                    source={{ uri: v.thumbnail }}
                    style={styles.thumb}
                    resizeMode="cover"
                    fadeDuration={0}
                  />
                </View>

                <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.gridTitle}>
                  {v.title}
                </Text>
                <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.gridArtist}>
                  {v.artist}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ))}
          </View>
        )}

        <View style={{ height: 200 }} />
      </ScrollView>

      {/* ---------- MENU BOTTOM SHEET (matches Playlists/Artists) ---------- */}
      <Modal
        visible={showMenu}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setShowMenu(false)}
      >
        <Pressable
          style={styles.sheetBackdrop}
          onPress={() => setShowMenu(false)}
        >
          <View
            style={[styles.sheetContent, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 20 }]}
          >
            {/* Settings / Account */}
            <SheetRow
              label="Settings"
              onPressOverride={() => {
                setShowMenu(false);
                navigateTo("Settings");
              }}
            />
            <SheetRow
              label="Account"
              onPressOverride={() => {
                setShowMenu(false);
                navigateTo("Account");
              }}
            />

            <View style={styles.sheetDivider} />

            {/* Filter */}
            <SheetRow
              label="All Music Videos"
              selected={filter === "all"}
              onPress={() => {
                setFilter("all");
                setShowMenu(false);
              }}
            />
            <SheetRow
              label="Favourites"
              selected={filter === "fav"}
              onPress={() => {
                setFilter("fav");
                setShowMenu(false);
              }}
            />

            <View style={styles.sheetDivider} />

            {/* Sort */}
            <SheetRow
              label="Artist"
              selected={sort === "artist"}
              onPress={() => {
                setSort("artist");
                setShowMenu(false);
              }}
            />
            <SheetRow
              label="Title"
              selected={sort === "title"}
              onPress={() => {
                setSort("title");
                setShowMenu(false);
              }}
            />
            <SheetRow
              label="Recently Added"
              selected={sort === "recent"}
              onPress={() => {
                setSort("recent");
                setShowMenu(false);
              }}
            />
          </View>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}
