========== src\screens\PlaylistDetailsScreen.tsx ==========
// src/screens/PlaylistDetailsScreen.tsx
import React, { useCallback, useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  Image,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  ScrollView,
  TextInput,
  Alert,
  Share,
  Platform,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
} from "react-native";
import {
  useNavigation,
  useRoute,
  useFocusEffect,
} from "@react-navigation/native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";

import LibraryPageHeaders from "../components/LibraryPageHeaders";
import MiniPlayer from "../components/MiniPlayer";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { useUserName } from "../hooks/useUserName";
import { useMediaStore } from "../store/mediaStore";
import { usePlayerStore } from "../store/playerStore";
import { useAudioPlayer } from "../hooks/useAudioPlayer";
import { useArtworkCache } from "../hooks/useArtworkCache";
import {
  MUSIC_DEFAULT_IMAGE,
  PlayIcon,
  ShuffleIcon,
  UserPlaylistIcon,
} from "../icons/Icons";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";

const FONT = "sans-serif";
const STORAGE_PLAYLISTS = "@library_playlists";

const SEARCH_ICON = require("../../assets/images/search.png");
const MORE_ICON = require("../../assets/apple/moreicon.png");

const LIGHT_THEME = {
  mode: "light" as const,
  background: "#edeef0",
  surface: "#ffffff",
  surfaceLight: "#e2e3e5",
  border: "#c8c9cc",
  divider: "#b8b9bc",
  rowDivider: "#d1d2d4",
  text: "#000000",
  textSecondary: "#3c3c43",
  textMuted: "#8e8e93",
  primary: "#f92c47",
};

const DARK_THEME = {
  mode: "dark" as const,
  background: "#2d2e30",
  surface: "#3a3b3d",
  surfaceLight: "#45464a",
  border: "#525356",
  divider: "#5a5b5e",
  rowDivider: "#46474a",
  text: "#ffffff",
  textSecondary: "#a1a1aa",
  textMuted: "#71717a",
  primary: "#f92c47",
};

type Playlist = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  artwork?: string | null;
  songs?: any[];
  createdAt?: number;
  updatedAt?: number;
  lastPlayedAt?: number;
  isFeatured?: boolean;
};

type SortKey = "default" | "nameAsc" | "nameDesc" | "artistAsc" | "recent";

const SORT_OPTIONS: { key: SortKey; label: string }[] = [
  { key: "default", label: "Default" },
  { key: "nameAsc", label: "Name (A-Z)" },
  { key: "nameDesc", label: "Name (Z-A)" },
  { key: "artistAsc", label: "Artist (A-Z)" },
  { key: "recent", label: "Newest first" },
];

const getArtist = (s: any): string => {
  const candidates = [
    s?.artist,
    s?.artists,
    s?.albumArtist,
    s?.album_artist,
    s?.author,
    s?.composer,
  ];
  for (const c of candidates) {
    if (typeof c === "string" && c.trim()) return c.trim();
    if (Array.isArray(c) && c.length > 0) {
      const joined = c.filter(Boolean).join(", ");
      if (joined) return joined;
    }
  }
  return "Unknown Artist";
};

const getArtwork = (s: any): string | undefined =>
  s?.artwork ?? s?.artworkUri ?? s?.cover ?? undefined;

const persistArtwork = async (tempUri: string): Promise<string> => {
  try {
    const dir = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}playlist_artwork/`;
    const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(dir);
    if (!info.exists) {
      await FileSystem.makeDirectoryAsync(dir, { intermediates: true });
    }
    const ext = (tempUri.split(".").pop() || "jpg").split("?")[0];
    const dest = `${dir}pl_${Date.now()}.${ext}`;
    await FileSystem.copyAsync({ from: tempUri, to: dest });
    return dest;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[PlaylistDetails] copy artwork failed:", e);
    return tempUri;
  }
};

export default function PlaylistDetailsScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const route = useRoute<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const playlistId: string = route.params?.playlistId;

  const themeRaw = useTheme() as any;
  const base =
    themeRaw && typeof themeRaw === "object" && themeRaw.background
      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : LIGHT_THEME;

  const COLORS = useMemo(() => {
    const isDark = (base.mode ?? "dark") === "dark";
    const fallback = isDark ? DARK_THEME : LIGHT_THEME;
    return { ...fallback, ...base };
  }, [base]);

  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(COLORS), [COLORS]);

  const headerColors = useMemo(
    () => ({
      background: COLORS.background,
      surface: COLORS.surface,
      border: COLORS.border,
      text: COLORS.text,
      textSecondary: COLORS.textSecondary,
      textMuted: COLORS.textMuted,
      primary: COLORS.primary,
    }),
    [COLORS],
  );

  const userName = useUserName();

  const songs = useMediaStore((s) => s.songs);
  const favorites = useMediaStore((s) => s.favoritesSong);
  const toggleFavorite = useMediaStore((s) => s.toggleSongFavorite);
  const { loadAndPlay } = useAudioPlayer();
  const { artworkMap } = useArtworkCache(songs);

  const currentSongId = usePlayerStore(
    (s: any) => s.currentTrack?.id ?? s.currentSong?.id ?? null,
  );

  const [playlist, setPlaylist] = useState<Playlist | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey>("default");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

  const [menuOpen, setMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [songMenuOpen, setSongMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSong, setSelectedSong] = useState<any>(null);
  const [sortOpen, setSortOpen] = useState(false);
  const [renameOpen, setRenameOpen] = useState(false);
  const [renameText, setRenameText] = useState("");
  const [addToOpen, setAddToOpen] = useState(false);
  const [otherPlaylists, setOtherPlaylists] = useState<Playlist[]>([]);

  const [addMusicOpen, setAddMusicOpen] = useState(false);
  const [addMusicQuery, setAddMusicQuery] = useState("");

  const [imageMenuOpen, setImageMenuOpen] = useState(false);

  const loadPlaylist = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const all: Playlist[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      const found = all.find((p) => p.id === playlistId) ?? null;
      setPlaylist(found);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.log("[PlaylistDetails] load error:", e?.message);
      setPlaylist(null);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, [playlistId]);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      loadPlaylist();
    }, [loadPlaylist]),
  );

  useEffect(() => {
    loadPlaylist();
  }, [loadPlaylist]);

  const persist = useCallback(async (next: Playlist) => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const all: Playlist[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      const updated = all.map((p) => (p.id === next.id ? next : p));
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS, JSON.stringify(updated));
      setPlaylist(next);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.log("[PlaylistDetails] persist error:", e?.message);
      showToast("Could not save changes");
    }
  }, []);

  const songsWithArt = useMemo(() => {
    if (!playlist?.songs) return [];
    return playlist.songs.map((s: any) => ({
      ...s,
      artwork: getArtwork(s) ?? artworkMap[s.id],
    }));
  }, [playlist, artworkMap]);

  const visibleSongs = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    let list = q
      ? songsWithArt.filter((s) => {
          const t = (s.title ?? "").toLowerCase();
          const a = getArtist(s).toLowerCase();
          return t.includes(q) || a.includes(q);
        })
      : songsWithArt;

    const copy = [...list];

    switch (sortKey) {
      case "nameAsc":
        copy.sort((a, b) =>
          (a.title ?? "").localeCompare(b.title ?? "", undefined, {
            sensitivity: "base",
          }),
        );
        break;
      case "nameDesc":
        copy.sort((a, b) =>
          (b.title ?? "").localeCompare(a.title ?? "", undefined, {
            sensitivity: "base",
          }),
        );
        break;
      case "artistAsc":
        copy.sort((a, b) =>
          getArtist(a).localeCompare(getArtist(b), undefined, {
            sensitivity: "base",
          }),
        );
        break;
      case "recent":
        copy.reverse();
        break;
      default:
        break;
    }
    return copy;
  }, [songsWithArt, query, sortKey]);

  const totalDuration = useMemo(() => {
    let t = 0;
    for (const s of songsWithArt) {
      const d = (s as any).duration;
      if (typeof d === "number" && d > 0) t += d > 36000 ? d / 1000 : d;
    }
    return t;
  }, [songsWithArt]);

  const formatTotal = (sec: number) => {
    if (!sec) return "";
    const m = Math.floor(sec / 60);
    if (m < 60) return `${m} min`;
    const h = Math.floor(m / 60);
    const r = m % 60;
    return r === 0 ? `${h} hr` : `${h} hr ${r} min`;
  };

  const playFromIndex = useCallback(
    async (index: number) => {
      if (visibleSongs.length === 0) return;
      try {
        await loadAndPlay(visibleSongs as any, index);
        if (playlist) {
          persist({ ...playlist, lastPlayedAt: Date.now() });
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error("[PlaylistDetails] play error:", e);
        showToast("Playback failed");
      }
    },
    [visibleSongs, loadAndPlay, playlist, persist],
  );

  const handlePlayAll = () => playFromIndex(0);

  const handleShuffle = () => {
    if (visibleSongs.length === 0) return;
    const shuffled = [...visibleSongs].sort(() => Math.random() - 0.5);
    loadAndPlay(shuffled as any, 0).catch(() => {
      showToast("Playback failed");
    });
  };

  const handleSongPress = (song: any) => {
    const index = visibleSongs.findIndex((s) => s.id === song.id);
    if (index < 0) return;
    playFromIndex(index);
  };

  const handleSongOptions = (song: any) => {
    setSelectedSong(song);
    setSongMenuOpen(true);
  };

  const handleRemoveFromPlaylist = async () => {
    if (!playlist || !selectedSong) return;
    const next = {
      ...playlist,
      songs: (playlist.songs ?? []).filter(
        (s: any) => s.id !== selectedSong.id,
      ),
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
    };
    await persist(next);
    setSongMenuOpen(false);
    setSelectedSong(null);
    showToast("Removed from playlist");
  };

  const handlePlayNext = () => {
    if (!selectedSong) return;
    showToast(`"${selectedSong.title}" will play next`);
    setSongMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const handleShareSong = async () => {
    if (!selectedSong) return;
    try {
      await Share.share({
        title: selectedSong.title,
        message: `ðŸŽµ ${selectedSong.title}\nBy ${getArtist(selectedSong)}`,
      });
    } catch {}
    setSongMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const handleToggleFavourite = () => {
    if (!selectedSong) return;
    const isFav = !!favorites[selectedSong.id];
    toggleFavorite(selectedSong.id);
    showToast(isFav ? "Removed from Favourites" : "Added to Favourites");
    setSongMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const handleRename = () => {
    if (!playlist) return;
    setRenameText(playlist.name);
    setMenuOpen(false);
    setTimeout(() => setRenameOpen(true), 200);
  };

  const saveRename = async () => {
    if (!playlist) return;
    const trimmed = renameText.trim();
    if (!trimmed) {
      showToast("Please enter a name");
      return;
    }
    await persist({ ...playlist, name: trimmed, updatedAt: Date.now() });
    setRenameOpen(false);
    setRenameText("");
    showToast("Renamed");
  };

  const handleDeletePlaylist = () => {
    if (!playlist) return;
    Alert.alert(
      "Delete playlist",
      `Delete "${playlist.name}"? This cannot be undone.`,
      [
        { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
        {
          text: "Delete",
          style: "destructive",
          onPress: async () => {
            try {
              const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
              const all: Playlist[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
              const filtered = all.filter((p) => p.id !== playlist.id);
              await AsyncStorage.setItem(
                STORAGE_PLAYLISTS,
                JSON.stringify(filtered),
              );
              showToast("Playlist deleted");
              nav.goBack();
            } catch {
              showToast("Could not delete playlist");
            }
          },
        },
      ],
    );
    setMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const openAddToAnother = async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const all: Playlist[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      setOtherPlaylists(all.filter((p) => p.id !== playlist?.id));
      setSongMenuOpen(false);
      setTimeout(() => setAddToOpen(true), 200);
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not load playlists");
    }
  };

  const addToPlaylist = async (target: Playlist) => {
    if (!selectedSong) return;
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const all: Playlist[] = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      const updated = all.map((p) =>
        p.id === target.id
          ? {
              ...p,
              songs: [...(p.songs ?? []), selectedSong],
              updatedAt: Date.now(),
              artwork: p.artwork ?? getArtwork(selectedSong) ?? null,
            }
          : p,
      );
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS, JSON.stringify(updated));
      showToast(`Added to "${target.name}"`);
      setAddToOpen(false);
      setSelectedSong(null);
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not add to playlist");
    }
  };

  const handleSharePlaylist = async () => {
    if (!playlist) return;
    try {
      const songList = (playlist.songs ?? [])
        .slice(0, 5)
        .map((s: any) => `â€¢ ${s.title}`)
        .join("\n");
      await Share.share({
        title: playlist.name,
        message: `ðŸŽµ ${playlist.name}\n\n${songList}${
          (playlist.songs?.length ?? 0) > 5
            ? `\n... and ${(playlist.songs?.length ?? 0) - 5} more`
            : ""
        }`,
      });
    } catch {}
    setMenuOpen(false);
  };

  const playlistSongIds = useMemo(() => {
    if (!playlist?.songs) return new Set<string>();
    return new Set(playlist.songs.map((s: any) => s.id));
  }, [playlist]);

  const allSongsWithArt = useMemo(() => {
    return songs.map((s: any) => {
      const title =
        s.title ||
        (s.filename ? String(s.filename).replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, "") : "") ||
        "Unknown";
      return {
        ...s,
        artwork: getArtwork(s) ?? artworkMap[s.id],
        displayTitle: title,
        displayArtist: getArtist(s),
      };
    });
  }, [songs, artworkMap]);

  const filteredAllSongs = useMemo(() => {
    const q = addMusicQuery.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return allSongsWithArt;
    return allSongsWithArt.filter(
      (s) =>
        s.displayTitle.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        s.displayArtist.toLowerCase().includes(q),
    );
  }, [allSongsWithArt, addMusicQuery]);

  const handleAddSongToPlaylist = useCallback(
    async (song: any) => {
      if (!playlist) return;
      if (playlistSongIds.has(song.id)) {
        showToast("Already in playlist");
        return;
      }
      const next = {
        ...playlist,
        songs: [...(playlist.songs ?? []), song],
        artwork: playlist.artwork ?? getArtwork(song) ?? null,
        updatedAt: Date.now(),
      };
      await persist(next);
      showToast(`Added "${song.displayTitle || song.title || "song"}"`);
    },
    [playlist, playlistSongIds, persist],
  );

  const updateArtwork = async (tempUri: string) => {
    if (!playlist) return;
    const saved = await persistArtwork(tempUri);
    await persist({
      ...playlist,
      artwork: saved,
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
    });
    showToast("Playlist image updated");
  };

  const handleTakePhoto = async () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    try {
      const perm = await ImagePicker.requestCameraPermissionsAsync();
      if (!perm.granted) {
        Alert.alert(
          "Permission needed",
          "Camera access is required to take a photo.",
        );
        return;
      }
      const res = await ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 0.85,
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: [1, 1],
      });
      if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]?.uri) {
        await updateArtwork(res.assets[0].uri);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[PlaylistDetails] camera error:", e);
      showToast("Could not open camera");
    }
  };

  const handleChooseFromLibrary = async () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    try {
      const perm = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
      if (!perm.granted) {
        Alert.alert(
          "Permission needed",
          "Photo library access is required to pick an image.",
        );
        return;
      }
      const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 0.85,
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: [1, 1],
      });
      if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]?.uri) {
        await updateArtwork(res.assets[0].uri);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[PlaylistDetails] library error:", e);
      showToast("Could not open photos");
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveArtwork = async () => {
    if (!playlist) return;
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    await persist({
      ...playlist,
      artwork: null,
      updatedAt: Date.now(),
    });
    showToast("Image removed");
  };

  const renderItem = ({ item }: { item: any }) => {
    const isCurrent = currentSongId === item.id;
    const isFav = !!favorites[item.id];
    const art = getArtwork(item) ?? artworkMap[item.id];

    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={styles.songRow}
        activeOpacity={0.7}
        onPress={() => handleSongPress(item)}
      >
        <View style={styles.leftIcon}>
          {isCurrent ? (
            <Text style={[styles.playingDot, { color: COLORS.primary }]}>
              â™ª
            </Text>
          ) : isFav ? (
            <Text style={[styles.favStar, { color: COLORS.primary }]}>
              â˜…
            </Text>
          ) : null}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.artWrap}>
          {art ? (
            <Image source={{ uri: art }} style={styles.art} />
          ) : (
            <Image source={MUSIC_DEFAULT_IMAGE} style={styles.art} />
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.songInfo}>
          <Text
            numberOfLines={1}
            style={[
              styles.songTitle,
              { color: isCurrent ? COLORS.primary : COLORS.text },
            ]}
          >
            {item.title ?? "Unknown"}
          </Text>
          <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.songArtist}>
            {getArtist(item)}
          </Text>
        </View>

        <TouchableOpacity
          onPress={() => handleSongOptions(item)}
          hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
          style={styles.dotsHit}
        >
          <Text style={styles.rowDots}>â‹®</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  };

  if (loading) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.container, styles.center]}>
        <Text style={{ color: COLORS.textSecondary }}>Loadingâ€¦</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  if (!playlist) {
    return (
      <View style={[styles.container, styles.center]}>
        <Text style={{ color: COLORS.textSecondary }}>Playlist not found</Text>
      </View>
    );
  }

  const coverArt = playlist.artwork ?? null;

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* âœ… Transparent floating header â€” back (left) Â· â‹® (right) */}
      <LibraryPageHeaders
        colors={headerColors}
        topInset={insets.top}
        showBack
        onBack={() => nav.goBack()}
        title=""
        showSearch={false}
        transparent
        actions={[
          {
            key: "more",
            icon: MORE_ICON,
            iconStyle: {
              width: 18,
              height: 18,
              tintColor: COLORS.primary,
            },
            onPress: () => setMenuOpen(true),
          },
        ]}
      />

      {/* ---- List ---- */}
      <FlatList
        data={visibleSongs}
        keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
        renderItem={renderItem}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          paddingTop: insets.top + 50,
          paddingBottom: 220,
        }}
        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={styles.songSep} />}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          <View>
            <View style={styles.searchWrap}>
              <Image
                source={SEARCH_ICON}
                style={[
                  styles.searchIcon,
                  { tintColor: COLORS.textSecondary },
                ]}
              />
              <TextInput
                value={query}
                onChangeText={setQuery}
                placeholder="Search in playlist"
                placeholderTextColor={COLORS.textMuted}
                style={styles.searchInput}
                autoCorrect={false}
                autoCapitalize="none"
              />
              {query.length > 0 && (
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setQuery("")}>
                  <Text style={styles.clearIcon}>âœ•</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
            </View>

            {/* HERO â€” tap to change image */}
            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.heroWrap}
              onPress={() => setImageMenuOpen(true)}
              activeOpacity={0.85}
            >
              <View style={styles.heroInner}>
                {coverArt ? (
                  <Image
                    source={{ uri: coverArt }}
                    style={styles.hero}
                    resizeMode="cover"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <View style={styles.hero}>
                    <UserPlaylistIcon size={120} color={COLORS.primary} />
                  </View>
                )}
              </View>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <Text style={styles.title} numberOfLines={2}>
              {playlist.name}
            </Text>

            {!playlist.isFeatured ? (
              <Text style={styles.ownerName}>{userName || "You"}</Text>
            ) : null}

            {totalDuration > 0 && (
              <View style={styles.metaRow}>
                <Text style={styles.metaText}>
                  {formatTotal(totalDuration)}
                </Text>
              </View>
            )}

            <View style={styles.controlsRow}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.shuffleCircle}
                onPress={handleShuffle}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
                disabled={songsWithArt.length === 0}
              >
                <ShuffleIcon size={20} color={COLORS.text} />
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={[styles.playBtn, { backgroundColor: COLORS.text }]}
                onPress={handlePlayAll}
                activeOpacity={0.85}
                disabled={songsWithArt.length === 0}
              >
                <PlayIcon size={22} color={COLORS.background} />
                <Text
                  style={[styles.playText, { color: COLORS.background }]}
                >
                  Play
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.addCircle}
                onPress={() => setAddMusicOpen(true)}
                activeOpacity={0.8}
              >
                <Text style={styles.addCircleText}>+</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>

            <View style={styles.divider} />

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.addMusicRow}
              activeOpacity={0.75}
              onPress={() => setAddMusicOpen(true)}
            >
              <View style={styles.addMusicPlusWrap}>
                <Text style={styles.addMusicPlus}>+</Text>
              </View>
              <Text style={styles.addMusicLabel}>Add music</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        }
      />

      <View style={styles.bottomArea} pointerEvents="box-none">
        <MiniPlayer />
      </View>

      {/* Playlist image picker popup */}
      <Modal
        visible={imageMenuOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.bottomSheet}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Playlist image</Text>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.option} onPress={handleTakePhoto}>
              <Text style={styles.optionText}>Take Photo</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.option}
              onPress={handleChooseFromLibrary}
            >
              <Text style={styles.optionText}>Your Photos</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {playlist.artwork ? (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.option}
                onPress={handleRemoveArtwork}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.optionText, { color: "#E53935" }]}>
                  âŒ« Remove image
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ) : null}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Playlist options sheet */}
      <Modal
        visible={menuOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.bottomSheet}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
              {playlist.name}
            </Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.option}
              onPress={() => {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                setTimeout(() => setSortOpen(true), 200);
              }}
            >
              <Text style={styles.optionText}>ðŸ”€ Sort by</Text>
              <Text style={styles.optionArrow}>â€º</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.option} onPress={handleRename}>
              <Text style={styles.optionText}>âœŽ Rename playlist</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.option}
              onPress={() => {
                setMenuOpen(false);
                handleDeletePlaylist();
              }}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.optionText, { color: "#E53935" }]}>
                âŒ« Delete playlist
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={() => setMenuOpen(false)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Song options sheet */}
      <Modal
        visible={songMenuOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setSongMenuOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setSongMenuOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.bottomSheet}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle} numberOfLines={1}>
              {selectedSong?.title ?? "Song options"}
            </Text>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.option} onPress={handlePlayNext}>
              <Text style={styles.optionText}>â­ Play next</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.option}
              onPress={handleToggleFavourite}
            >
              <Text style={styles.optionText}>
                {selectedSong && favorites[selectedSong.id]
                  ? "â™¥  Remove from Favourites"
                  : "â™¥  Add to Favourites"}
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.option} onPress={openAddToAnother}>
              <Text style={styles.optionText}>
                ï¼‹ Add to another playlist
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity style={styles.option} onPress={handleShareSong}>
              <Text style={styles.optionText}>â†— Share song</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.option}
              onPress={handleRemoveFromPlaylist}
            >
              <Text style={[styles.optionText, { color: "#E53935" }]}>
                âŒ« Remove from this playlist
              </Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={() => setSongMenuOpen(false)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Sort modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={sortOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setSortOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setSortOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.bottomSheet}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Sort by</Text>

            {SORT_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
              const active = sortKey === opt.key;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={opt.key}
                  style={styles.option}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setSortKey(opt.key);
                    setSortOpen(false);
                  }}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.optionText,
                      active && { color: COLORS.primary, fontWeight: "700" },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {active ? "âœ“  " : "     "}
                    {opt.label}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={() => setSortOpen(false)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Rename modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={renameOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="fade"
        onRequestClose={() => setRenameOpen(false)}
      >
        <Pressable
          style={styles.modalCenterBackdrop}
          onPress={() => setRenameOpen(false)}
        >
          <Pressable
            style={styles.renameCard}
            onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Rename playlist</Text>
            <TextInput
              value={renameText}
              onChangeText={setRenameText}
              autoFocus
              selectTextOnFocus
              placeholder="Playlist name"
              placeholderTextColor={COLORS.textMuted}
              style={styles.renameInput}
            />
            <View style={styles.renameActions}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={() => setRenameOpen(false)}
                style={styles.actionButton}
              >
                <Text style={{ color: COLORS.textSecondary }}>Cancel</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <TouchableOpacity
                onPress={saveRename}
                style={[
                  styles.actionButton,
                  { backgroundColor: COLORS.primary },
                ]}
              >
                <Text style={styles.saveText}>Save</Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </View>
          </Pressable>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>

      {/* Add to playlist modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={addToOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setAddToOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.modalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setAddToOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.bottomSheet}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Add to playlist</Text>

            {otherPlaylists.length === 0 ? (
              <Text style={styles.noPlaylists}>
                You don't have any other playlists yet.
              </Text>
            ) : (
              <ScrollView style={{ maxHeight: 320 }}>
                {otherPlaylists.map((pl) => (
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    key={pl.id}
                    style={styles.playlistPick}
                    onPress={() => addToPlaylist(pl)}
                  >
                    <View style={styles.plIcon}>
                      <UserPlaylistIcon
                        size={22}
                        color={COLORS.textSecondary}
                      />
                    </View>
                    <Text style={styles.optionText} numberOfLines={1}>
                      {pl.name}
                    </Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                ))}
              </ScrollView>
            )}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={() => setAddToOpen(false)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>

      {/* Add music modal */}
      <Modal
        visible={addMusicOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => {
          setAddMusicOpen(false);
          setAddMusicQuery("");
        }}
      >
        <KeyboardAvoidingView
          style={styles.modalRoot}
          behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : undefined}
        >
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.backdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => {
              setAddMusicOpen(false);
              setAddMusicQuery("");
            }}
          />
          <View style={[styles.bottomSheet, { height: "80%" }]}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Add music</Text>

            <View style={[styles.searchWrap, { marginHorizontal: 0 }]}>
              <Image
                source={SEARCH_ICON}
                style={[
                  styles.searchIcon,
                  { tintColor: COLORS.textSecondary },
                ]}
              />
              <TextInput
                value={addMusicQuery}
                onChangeText={setAddMusicQuery}
                placeholder="Search songs"
                placeholderTextColor={COLORS.textMuted}
                style={styles.searchInput}
                autoCorrect={false}
                autoCapitalize="none"
              />
              {addMusicQuery.length > 0 && (
                <TouchableOpacity onPress={() => setAddMusicQuery("")}>
                  <Text style={styles.clearIcon}>âœ•</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              )}
            </View>

            {filteredAllSongs.length === 0 ? (
              <View style={styles.empty}>
                <Text style={styles.emptyStar}>â™ª</Text>
                <Text style={styles.emptyTitle}>No songs found</Text>
                <Text style={styles.emptySub}>
                  Scan your library first, then try again.
                </Text>
              </View>
            ) : (
              <FlatList
                data={filteredAllSongs}
                keyExtractor={(item: any) => String(item.id)}
                style={{ flex: 1 }}
                contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 12 }}
                ItemSeparatorComponent={() => (
                  <View style={[styles.songSep, { marginLeft: 72 }]} />
                )}
                renderItem={({ item }: { item: any }) => {
                  const added = playlistSongIds.has(item.id);
                  return (
                    <TouchableOpacity
                      style={styles.pickRow}
                      activeOpacity={added ? 1 : 0.7}
                      disabled={added}
                      onPress={() => handleAddSongToPlaylist(item)}
                    >
                      <Image
                        source={
                          item.artwork
                            ? { uri: item.artwork }
                            : MUSIC_DEFAULT_IMAGE
                        }
                        style={styles.pickArt}
                      />
                      <View style={styles.pickInfo}>
                        <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.pickTitle}>
                          {item.displayTitle}
                        </Text>
                        <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.pickArtist}>
                          {item.displayArtist}
                        </Text>
                      </View>
                      <Text
                        style={[
                          styles.pickAdd,
                          added && { color: COLORS.primary },
                        ]}
                      >
                        {added ? "âœ“" : "+"}
                      </Text>
                    </TouchableOpacity>
                  );
                }}
              />
            )}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.cancelButton}
              onPress={() => {
                setAddMusicOpen(false);
                setAddMusicQuery("");
              }}
            >
              <Text style={styles.cancelText}>Done</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

const createStyles = (C: any) =>
  StyleSheet.create({
    container: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: C.background },
    center: { alignItems: "center", justifyContent: "center" },

    searchWrap: {
      flexDirection: "row",
      alignItems: "center",
      marginHorizontal: 16,
      marginTop: 4,
      marginBottom: 16,
      backgroundColor: C.surface,
      borderRadius: 10,
      paddingHorizontal: 12,
      height: 44,
      borderWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
      borderColor: C.border,
    },
    searchIcon: {
      width: 18,
      height: 18,
      marginRight: 10,
      resizeMode: "contain",
    },
    searchInput: {
      flex: 1,
      color: C.text,
      fontSize: 15,
      paddingVertical: 0,
      fontFamily: FONT,
    },
    clearIcon: { color: C.textMuted, fontSize: 14, paddingLeft: 8 },

    heroWrap: {
      alignItems: "center",
      justifyContent: "center",
      marginBottom: 18,
    },
    heroInner: { position: "relative" },
    hero: {
      width: 220,
      height: 220,
      borderRadius: 10,
      backgroundColor: C.surface,
      shadowColor: "#000",
      shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 6 },
      shadowOpacity: 0.28,
      shadowRadius: 10,
      elevation: 10,
      alignItems: "center",
      justifyContent: "center",
    },

    title: {
      color: C.text,
      fontSize: 20,
      fontWeight: "600",
      letterSpacing: -0.5,
      textAlign: "center",
      fontFamily: FONT,
      marginTop: -5,
      paddingHorizontal: 24,
    },
    ownerName: {
      color: C.text,
      fontSize: 20,
      fontWeight: "500",
      fontFamily: FONT,
      textAlign: "center",
      marginTop: -5,
      marginBottom: 22,
      letterSpacing: 0.2,
    },
    metaRow: {
      flexDirection: "row",
      alignItems: "center",
      justifyContent: "center",
      gap: 6,
      marginTop: 6,
      marginBottom: 18,
    },
    metaText: { color: C.textSecondary, fontSize: 13, fontFamily: FONT },

    controlsRow: {
      flexDirection: "row",

    },

    modalRoot: { flex: 1, justifyContent: "flex-end" },

    optionText: {
      flex: 1,

========== src\screens\PlaylistsScreen.tsx ==========
// ============================================================
//  src/screens/PlaylistsScreen.tsx
// ============================================================
import React, { useCallback, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Image,
  Alert,
  Modal,
  KeyboardAvoidingView,
  Platform,
  Pressable,
  ActivityIndicator,
  ScrollView,
  TextInput as RNTextInput,
} from "react-native";
import { useNavigation, useFocusEffect } from "@react-navigation/native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";
import * as ImagePicker from "expo-image-picker";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { usePlayerStore } from "../store/playerStore";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";
import MiniPlayer from "../components/MiniPlayer";
import LibraryPageHeaders from "../components/LibraryPageHeaders";
import { UserPlaylistIcon } from "../icons/Icons";

// Assets (only assets this screen still needs directly)
const LIST_ICON = require("../../assets/images/list.png");
const FAVOURITE_HERO = require("../../assets/images/favouritepageimage.png");
const CREATE_PLAYLIST_ICON = require("../../assets/icons/createplaylisticona.png");
const MORE_ICON = require("../../assets/apple/moreicon.png");
const CAMERA_ICON = require("../../assets/icons/cameraicon.png");

const FONT = "sans-serif";
const STORAGE_PLAYLISTS = "@library_playlists";

// ============================================================
// Glass Settings
// ============================================================
const GLASS_SETTINGS = {
  distortionStrength: 40,
  frostBlur: 16,
  tintColor: 28,
  tintOpacity: 0.6,
  shadowSpread: 0,
  shadowBlur: 20,
};

const DEFAULT_COLORS = {
  background: "#1c1c1e",
  surface: "#434446",
  surfaceLight: "#45464a",
  border: "#3f4042",
  text: "#ffffff",
  textSecondary: "#a1a1aa",
  textMuted: "#71717a",
  primary: "#f92c47",
};

type Playlist = {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  artwork?: string | null;
  songs?: any[];
  createdAt?: number;
  updatedAt?: number;
  lastPlayedAt?: number;
  pinned?: boolean;
  favourite?: boolean;
};

type SortKey = "title" | "recent" | "played" | "type";
type FilterKey = "all" | "favourites";

const SORTS: { key: SortKey; label: string }[] = [
  { key: "title", label: "Title" },
  { key: "recent", label: "Recently Added" },
  { key: "played", label: "Recently Played" },
  { key: "type", label: "Playlist Type" },
];

const FILTERS: { key: FilterKey; label: string }[] = [
  { key: "all", label: "All playlists" },
  { key: "favourites", label: "Favourites" },
];

// Dummy playlists for visual testing
const DUMMY_PLAYLISTS: Playlist[] = [
  {
    id: "pl_dummy_1",
    name: "Dummy Playlist 1",
    artwork: null,
    songs: [],
    createdAt: Date.now(),
    updatedAt: Date.now(),
  },
  {
    id: "pl_dummy_2",
    name: "Chill Vibes (Dummy)",
    artwork: null,
    songs: [],
    createdAt: Date.now() - 100000,
    updatedAt: Date.now() - 100000,
  },
];

export default function PlaylistsScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const themeRaw = useTheme() as any;
  const base =
    themeRaw && typeof themeRaw === "object" && themeRaw.background
      ? themeRaw
      : themeRaw?.colors && typeof themeRaw.colors === "object"
        ? themeRaw.colors
        : DEFAULT_COLORS;

  const COLORS = useMemo(() => ({ ...DEFAULT_COLORS, ...base }), [base]);
  const styles = useMemo(() => createStyles(COLORS), [COLORS]);

  const [playlists, setPlaylists] = useState<Playlist[]>(DUMMY_PLAYLISTS);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [sortKey, setSortKey] = useState<SortKey>("title");
  const [filterKey, setFilterKey] = useState<FilterKey>("all");
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");

  // Modals
  const [createOpen, setCreateOpen] = useState(false);
  const [filterOpen, setFilterOpen] = useState(false);
  const [sortOpen, setSortOpen] = useState(false);

  // Long-press playlist action sheet
  const [actionSheetOpen, setActionSheetOpen] = useState(false);
  const [selectedPlaylist, setSelectedPlaylist] = useState<Playlist | null>(null);

  // Create Playlist States
  const [newName, setNewName] = useState("");
  const [coverImage, setCoverImage] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [imageMenuOpen, setImageMenuOpen] = useState(false);
  const [creating, setCreating] = useState(false);

  const currentSong = usePlayerStore(
    (s: any) => s.currentSong ?? s.currentTrack ?? null,
  );
  const isPlaying = !!currentSong;

  // ---------- load ----------
  const loadPlaylists = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS);
      const parsed = raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : [];
      setPlaylists(parsed.length > 0 ? parsed : DUMMY_PLAYLISTS);
    } catch (e: any) {
      console.log("Load playlists error:", e?.message);
      setPlaylists(DUMMY_PLAYLISTS);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }, []);

  useFocusEffect(
    useCallback(() => {
      loadPlaylists();
    }, [loadPlaylists]),
  );

  // ---------- save helper ----------
  const savePlaylists = useCallback(async (next: Playlist[]) => {
    try {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS, JSON.stringify(next));
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not save playlists");
    }
  }, []);

  // ---------- sorting / filtering ----------
  const visible = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    let filtered = q
      ? playlists.filter((p) => p.name.toLowerCase().includes(q))
      : playlists;

    if (filterKey === "favourites") {
      filtered = filtered.filter((p) => p.favourite);
    }

    const list = [...filtered];

    switch (sortKey) {
      case "title":
      case "type":
        list.sort((a, b) =>
          a.name.localeCompare(b.name, undefined, { sensitivity: "base" }),
        );
        break;
      case "recent":
        list.sort((a, b) => (b.createdAt ?? 0) - (a.createdAt ?? 0));
        break;
      case "played":
        list.sort((a, b) => (b.lastPlayedAt ?? 0) - (a.lastPlayedAt ?? 0));
        break;
    }

    // âœ… Pinned playlists always float to the top
    list.sort((a, b) => {
      const ap = a.pinned ? 1 : 0;
      const bp = b.pinned ? 1 : 0;
      return bp - ap;
    });

    return list;
  }, [playlists, query, sortKey, filterKey]);

  // ---------- Image Picker Logic ----------
  const persistArtwork = async (tempUri: string): Promise<string> => {
    try {
      const dir = `${FileSystem.documentDirectory}playlist_artwork/`;
      const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(dir);
      if (!info.exists) {
        await FileSystem.makeDirectoryAsync(dir, { intermediates: true });
      }
      const ext = (tempUri.split(".").pop() || "jpg").split("?")[0];
      const dest = `${dir}pl_${Date.now()}.${ext}`;
      await FileSystem.copyAsync({ from: tempUri, to: dest });
      return dest;
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] copy artwork failed:", e);
      return tempUri;
    }
  };

  const handleTakePhoto = async () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    try {
      const perm = await ImagePicker.requestCameraPermissionsAsync();
      if (!perm.granted) {
        Alert.alert("Permission needed", "Camera access is required.");
        return;
      }
      const res = await ImagePicker.launchCameraAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 0.85,
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: [1, 1],
      });
      if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]?.uri) {
        setCoverImage(res.assets[0].uri);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] camera error:", e);
      showToast("Could not open camera");
    }
  };

  const handleChooseFromLibrary = async () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    try {
      const perm = await ImagePicker.requestMediaLibraryPermissionsAsync();
      if (!perm.granted) {
        Alert.alert("Permission needed", "Photo library access is required.");
        return;
      }
      const res = await ImagePicker.launchImageLibraryAsync({
        mediaTypes: ImagePicker.MediaTypeOptions.Images,
        quality: 0.85,
        allowsEditing: true,
        aspect: [1, 1],
      });
      if (!res.canceled && res.assets?.[0]?.uri) {
        setCoverImage(res.assets[0].uri);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Library] library error:", e);
      showToast("Could not open photos");
    }
  };

  const handleRemoveCover = () => {
    setImageMenuOpen(false);
    setCoverImage(null);
  };

  // ---------- Actions ----------
  const deletePlaylist = (pl: Playlist) => {
    Alert.alert("Delete playlist", `Delete "${pl.name}"?`, [
      { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
      {
        text: "Delete",
        style: "destructive",
        onPress: async () => {
          const next = playlists.filter((p) => p.id !== pl.id);
          setPlaylists(next);
          try {
            await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS, JSON.stringify(next));
            showToast("Playlist deleted");
          } catch {
            showToast("Could not delete playlist");
            loadPlaylists();
          }
        },
      },
    ]);
  };

  const createPlaylist = async () => {
    const name = newName.trim();
    if (!name) return;
    setCreating(true);
    try {
      const savedArtwork = coverImage ? await persistArtwork(coverImage) : null;

      const pl: Playlist = {
        id: `pl_${Date.now()}`,
        name,
        artwork: savedArtwork,
        songs: [],
        createdAt: Date.now(),
        updatedAt: Date.now(),
      };
      const next = [pl, ...playlists];
      setPlaylists(next);

      setCreateOpen(false);
      setNewName("");
      setCoverImage(null);

      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_PLAYLISTS, JSON.stringify(next));
      showToast("Playlist created");
    } catch {
      showToast("Could not create playlist");
      loadPlaylists();
    } finally {
      setCreating(false);
    }
  };

  // ---------- Long press â†’ open action sheet ----------
  const openActionSheet = (pl: Playlist) => {
    setSelectedPlaylist(pl);
    setActionSheetOpen(true);
  };

  const closeActionSheet = () => {
    setActionSheetOpen(false);
    setTimeout(() => setSelectedPlaylist(null), 200);
  };

  // ---- Action handlers ----
  const handleTogglePin = async () => {
    if (!selectedPlaylist) return;
    const next = playlists.map((p) =>
      p.id === selectedPlaylist.id ? { ...p, pinned: !p.pinned } : p,
    );
    setPlaylists(next);
    await savePlaylists(next);
    showToast(
      selectedPlaylist.pinned
        ? "Unpinned from library"
        : "Pinned to library top",
    );
    closeActionSheet();
  };

  const handleDeleteFromLibrary = () => {
    const pl = selectedPlaylist;
    closeActionSheet();
    if (!pl) return;
    setTimeout(() => deletePlaylist(pl), 250);
  };

  const handleToggleFavourite = async () => {
    if (!selectedPlaylist) return;
    const next = playlists.map((p) =>
      p.id === selectedPlaylist.id ? { ...p, favourite: !p.favourite } : p,
    );
    setPlaylists(next);
    await savePlaylists(next);
    showToast(
      selectedPlaylist.favourite
        ? "Removed from Favourites"
        : "Added to Favourites",
    );
    closeActionSheet();
  };

  const handlePlayNext = async () => {
    const pl = selectedPlaylist;
    closeActionSheet();
    if (!pl) return;
    try {
      const songs = Array.isArray(pl.songs) ? pl.songs : [];
      if (songs.length === 0) {
        showToast("Playlist is empty");
        return;
      }
      const playerStore = usePlayerStore.getState() as any;
      if (typeof playerStore.addToQueue === "function") {
        playerStore.addToQueue(songs, "next");
        showToast("Added to play next");
      } else if (typeof playerStore.enqueueNext === "function") {
        playerStore.enqueueNext(songs);
        showToast("Added to play next");
      } else if (typeof playerStore.addToQueueNext === "function") {
        playerStore.addToQueueNext(songs);
        showToast("Added to play next");
      } else {
        showToast("Play next not available");
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[Playlists] play next failed:", e);
      showToast("Could not add to play next");
    }
  };

  // ---------- renderers ----------
  const renderPlaylist = ({ item }: { item: Playlist }) => {
    return (
      <TouchableOpacity
        style={styles.row}
        activeOpacity={0.7}
        onPress={() => nav.navigate("PlaylistDetails", { playlistId: item.id })}
        onLongPress={() => openActionSheet(item)}
        delayLongPress={250}
      >
        <View style={styles.plIcon}>
          {item.artwork ? (
            <Image
              source={{ uri: item.artwork }}
              style={styles.plIconImg}
              resizeMode="cover"
            />
          ) : (
            <UserPlaylistIcon size={55} color={COLORS.inactive} />
          )}
        </View>

        <View style={styles.info}>
          <Text style={styles.name} numberOfLines={1}>
            {item.name}
          </Text>
        </View>
      </TouchableOpacity>
    );
  };

  return (
    <View style={styles.container}>
      {/* ---------------- HEADERS (extracted component) ---------------- */}
      <LibraryPageHeaders
        colors={COLORS}
        topInset={insets.top}
        title="Playlists"
        onBack={() => nav.goBack()}
        searchValue={query}
        onSearchChange={setQuery}
        searchPlaceholder="Search in Playlists"
        actions={[
          {
            key: "create",
            icon: CREATE_PLAYLIST_ICON,
            onPress: () => setCreateOpen(true),
          },
          {
            key: "filter",
            icon: LIST_ICON,
            iconStyle: { width: 23, height: 20 },
            onPress: () => setFilterOpen(true),
          },
          {
            key: "sort",
            icon: MORE_ICON,
            iconStyle: { width: 18, height: 20 },
            onPress: () => setSortOpen(true),
          },
        ]}
      />

      {/* ---------------- LIST ---------------- */}
      <FlatList
        data={visible}
        keyExtractor={(item) => item.id}
        renderItem={renderPlaylist}
        ItemSeparatorComponent={() => <View style={styles.divider} />}
        contentContainerStyle={{
          paddingTop: 4,
          paddingBottom: isPlaying ? 260 : 220,
        }}
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
        ListHeaderComponent={
          filterKey === "all" ? (
            <View>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.favRow}
                activeOpacity={0.75}
                onPress={() => nav.navigate("FavouriteSongs")}
              >
                <Image source={FAVOURITE_HERO} style={styles.favArt} />
                <View style={styles.info}>
                  <Text style={styles.name}>Favourite Songs</Text>
                </View>
              </TouchableOpacity>
              <View style={styles.divider} />
            </View>
          ) : null
        }
      />

      {/* ---------------- MINI PLAYER ---------------- */}
      {isPlaying && (
        <View style={styles.bottomArea} pointerEvents="box-none">
          <MiniPlayer />
        </View>
      )}

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* ------------------ FILTER BOTTOM SHEET --------------------- */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <Modal
        visible={filterOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setFilterOpen(false)}
      >
        <Pressable
          style={styles.sheetBackdrop}
          onPress={() => setFilterOpen(false)}
        >
          <View
            style={[styles.sheetContent, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 20 }]}
          >
            <Text style={styles.sheetTitle}>Filter By</Text>
            {FILTERS.map((f) => {
              const active = f.key === filterKey;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={f.key}
                  style={styles.sheetItem}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setFilterKey(f.key);
                    setFilterOpen(false);
                  }}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.sheetCheck, active && { color: COLORS.text }]}
                  >
                    {active ? "âœ“" : ""}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.sheetItemText,
                      active && { color: COLORS.text },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {f.label}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* ------------------ SORT BOTTOM SHEET ----------------------- */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <Modal
        visible={sortOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setSortOpen(false)}
      >
        <Pressable
          style={styles.sheetBackdrop}
          onPress={() => setSortOpen(false)}
        >
          <View
            style={[styles.sheetContent, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 20 }]}
          >
            <Text style={[styles.sheetTitle, { textAlign: "center" }]}>
              Sort by
            </Text>
            {SORTS.map((s) => {
              const active = s.key === sortKey;
              return (
                <TouchableOpacity
                  key={s.key}
                  style={styles.sheetItem}
                  onPress={() => {
                    setSortKey(s.key);
                    setSortOpen(false);
                  }}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.sheetCheck, active && { color: COLORS.text }]}
                  >
                    {active ? "âœ“" : ""}
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={[
                      styles.sheetItemText,
                      active && { color: COLORS.text },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {s.label}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              );
            })}
          </View>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* ---------------- LONG-PRESS ACTION SHEET ------------------- */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <Modal
        visible={actionSheetOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={closeActionSheet}
      >
        <Pressable style={styles.actionBackdrop} onPress={closeActionSheet}>
          <Pressable
            style={[styles.actionSheet, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 16 }]}
            onPress={(e: any) => e.stopPropagation()}
          >
            {selectedPlaylist && (
              <>
                <View style={styles.actionHeader}>
                  <View style={styles.actionArtWrap}>
                    {selectedPlaylist.artwork ? (
                      <Image
                        source={{ uri: selectedPlaylist.artwork }}
                        style={styles.actionArt}
                        resizeMode="cover"
                      />
                    ) : (
                      <UserPlaylistIcon size={34} color={COLORS.primary} />
                    )}
                  </View>
                  <Text style={styles.actionName} numberOfLines={1}>
                    {selectedPlaylist.name}
                  </Text>
                </View>

                <View style={styles.actionDivider} />

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.actionItem}
                  onPress={handleTogglePin}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.actionItemText}>
                    {selectedPlaylist.pinned ? "Unpin playlist" : "Pin playlist"}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.actionItem}
                  onPress={handleDeleteFromLibrary}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.actionItemText, { color: "#E53935" }]}>
                    Delete from library
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.actionItem}
                  onPress={handleToggleFavourite}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.actionItemText}>
                    {selectedPlaylist.favourite
                      ? "Undo Favourite"
                      : "Favourite"}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.actionItem}
                  onPress={handlePlayNext}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.actionItemText}>Play next</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={styles.actionCancel}
                  onPress={closeActionSheet}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.actionCancelText}>Cancel</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </>
            )}
          </Pressable>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* ------------- CREATE PLAYLIST MODAL ------------------------ */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <Modal
        visible={createOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
      >
        <KeyboardAvoidingView
          style={{ flex: 1, justifyContent: "flex-end" }}
          behavior={Platform.OS === "ios" ? "padding" : undefined}
        >
          <Pressable
            style={styles.createBackdrop}
            onPress={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
          >
            <Pressable
              style={[styles.createSheet, { paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 20 }]}
              onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
            >
              <ScrollView
                bounces={false}
                showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
                keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
              >
                <View style={styles.createTopBar}>
                  <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={() => setCreateOpen(false)}
                    hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
                  >
                    <Text style={styles.crossIcon}>âœ•</Text>
                  </TouchableOpacity>

                  <TouchableOpacity
                    onPress={createPlaylist}
                    disabled={creating}
                    hitSlop={{ top: 10, bottom: 10, left: 10, right: 10 }}
                  >
                    {creating ? (
                      <ActivityIndicator color={COLORS.primary} size="small" />
                    ) : (
                      <Text style={styles.checkIcon}>âœ“</Text>
                    )}
                  </TouchableOpacity>
                </View>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => setImageMenuOpen(true)}
                  style={styles.coverUpload}
                  activeOpacity={0.8}
                >
                  {coverImage ? (
                    <Image
                      source={{ uri: coverImage }}
                      style={styles.coverImage}
                    />
                  ) : (
                    <View style={styles.coverPlaceholder}>
                      <View style={styles.cameraIconBg}>
                        <Image
                          source={CAMERA_ICON}
                          style={styles.cameraIconImage}
                          resizeMode="contain"
                        />
                      </View>
                    </View>
                  )}
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <RNTextInput
                  style={styles.input}
                  value={newName}
                  onChangeText={setNewName}
                  placeholder="Playlist Title"
                  placeholderTextColor={COLORS.textMuted}
                  maxLength={50}
                  cursorColor="#ffffff"
                  selectionColor="#ffffff"
                />
              </ScrollView>
            </Pressable>
          </Pressable>
        </KeyboardAvoidingView>
      </Modal>

      {/* ============================================================ */}
      {/* -------------- IMAGE PICKER BOTTOM SHEET ------------------- */}
      {/* ============================================================ */}
      <Modal
        visible={imageMenuOpen}
        transparent
        animationType="slide"
        onRequestClose={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
      >
        <View style={styles.imageModalRoot}>
          <TouchableOpacity
            style={styles.imageBackdrop}
            activeOpacity={1}
            onPress={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
          />
          <View style={styles.imageSheet}>
            <View style={styles.sheetHandle} />
            <Text style={styles.imageSheetTitle}>Playlist cover</Text>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.imageOption}
              onPress={handleTakePhoto}
            >
              <Text style={styles.imageOptionText}>Take Photo</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.imageOption}
              onPress={handleChooseFromLibrary}
            >
              <Text style={styles.imageOptionText}>Your Photos</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>

            {coverImage ? (
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.imageOption}
                onPress={handleRemoveCover}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.imageOptionText, { color: "#E53935" }]}>
                  âŒ« Remove image
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            ) : null}

            <TouchableOpacity
              style={styles.imageCancel}
              onPress={() => setImageMenuOpen(false)}
            >
              <Text style={styles.imageCancelText}>Cancel</Text>
            </TouchableOpacity>
          </View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}
