========== src\screens\QueueScreen.tsx ==========
import React from 'react';
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  FlatList,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Platform,
  TextStyle,
} from 'react-native';
import { SafeAreaView } from 'react-native-safe-area-context';
import { useNavigation } from '@react-navigation/native';
import { useTheme } from '../hooks/useTheme';
import { usePlayerStore } from '../store/playerStore';
import { useAudioPlayer } from '../hooks/useAudioPlayer';
import { BackIcon, TrashIcon } from '../icons/Icons';
import { safeGoBack } from "../utils/navigation";
import { formatDuration } from '../utils/formatDuration';

const SPACING = { sm: 8, md: 16, lg: 20, xl: 28 };
const FONT_SIZES = { xs: 11, sm: 12, md: 16, lg: 18, xl: 22 };

const ACCENT = '#f92c47';

// ---- SF font helper (same as MusicPlayer) ----
const SF = {
  regular: Platform.select({ ios: 'System', default: 'SFProDisplay-Regular' }),
  medium: Platform.select({ ios: 'System', default: 'SFProDisplay-Medium' }),
  semibold: Platform.select({
    ios: 'System',
    default: 'SFProDisplay-Semibold',
  }),
};
const iosWeight = (w: TextStyle['fontWeight']): TextStyle =>
  Platform.OS === 'ios' ? { fontWeight: w } : {};

export default function QueueScreen() {
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const theme = useTheme();
  const queue = usePlayerStore((s) => s.queue);
  const currentIndex = usePlayerStore((s) => s.currentIndex);
  const removeFromQueue = usePlayerStore((s) => s.removeFromQueue);
  const moveInQueue = usePlayerStore((s) => s.moveInQueue);
  const clearQueue = usePlayerStore((s) => s.clearQueue);
  const { loadAndPlay } = useAudioPlayer();

  return (
    <SafeAreaView style={styles.root}>
      {/* Header */}
      <View style={styles.header}>
       <TouchableOpacity
  onPress={() => safeGoBack(nav)}
  style={styles.iconBtn}
>
  <BackIcon color="#fff" />
</TouchableOpacity>
        <Text style={styles.title}>Queue</Text>
        <TouchableOpacity onPress={clearQueue} style={styles.iconBtn}>
          <TrashIcon color="#fff" />
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>

      {queue.length === 0 ? (
        <View style={styles.center}>
          <Text style={styles.emptyText}>Queue is empty</Text>
        </View>
      ) : (
        <FlatList
          data={queue}
          keyExtractor={(it, idx) => it.id + idx}
          contentContainerStyle={{ paddingBottom: 24 }}
          renderItem={({ item, index }) => {
            const isCurrent = index === currentIndex;
            return (
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.row,
                  isCurrent && styles.rowCurrent,
                ]}
              >
                <TouchableOpacity
                  style={{ flex: 1 }}
                  onPress={() => loadAndPlay(queue, index)}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text
                    numberOfLines={1}
                    style={[
                      styles.rowTitle,
                      { color: isCurrent ? ACCENT : '#fff' },
                    ]}
                  >
                    {item.title}
                  </Text>
                  <Text numberOfLines={1} style={styles.rowSubtitle}>
                    {item.artist} Â· {formatDuration(item.duration)}
                  </Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => moveInQueue(index, Math.max(0, index - 1))}
                  style={styles.mini}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.miniText}>â†‘</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() =>
                    moveInQueue(index, Math.min(queue.length - 1, index + 1))
                  }
                  style={styles.mini}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={styles.miniText}>â†“</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>

                <TouchableOpacity
                  onPress={() => removeFromQueue(index)}
                  style={styles.mini}
                  activeOpacity={0.7}
                >
                  <Text style={[styles.miniText, { color: ACCENT }]}>âœ•</Text>
                </TouchableOpacity>
              </View>
            );
          }}
        />
      )}
    </SafeAreaView>
  );
}

const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  root: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: '#000' },

  // Header
  header: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: 12,
    paddingVertical: 8,
    justifyContent: 'space-between',
  },
  iconBtn: {
    padding: 10,
    minWidth: 44,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  title: {
    flex: 1,
    color: '#fff',
    fontSize: FONT_SIZES.lg,
    fontFamily: SF.semibold,
    ...iosWeight('700'),
    letterSpacing: -0.2,
    textAlign: 'center',
  },

  center: {
    flex: 1,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
    padding: 24,
  },
  emptyText: {
    color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
    fontSize: FONT_SIZES.md,
    fontFamily: SF.medium,
    ...iosWeight('500'),
  },

  // Rows
  row: {
    flexDirection: 'row',
    alignItems: 'center',
    paddingHorizontal: SPACING.lg,
    paddingVertical: 12,
    borderBottomWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
    borderBottomColor: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.06)',
  },
  rowCurrent: {
    backgroundColor: 'rgba(249, 44, 71, 0.08)',
  },
  rowTitle: {
    fontSize: 14,
    fontFamily: SF.semibold,
    ...iosWeight('600'),
  },
  rowSubtitle: {
    color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)',
    fontSize: FONT_SIZES.sm,
    marginTop: 2,
    fontFamily: SF.medium,
    ...iosWeight('500'),
  },
  mini: {
    padding: 8,
    minWidth: 32,
    alignItems: 'center',
    justifyContent: 'center',
  },
  miniText: {
    color: 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)',
    fontSize: 16,
    fontFamily: SF.semibold,
    ...iosWeight('600'),
  },
});

========== src\screens\SettingsScreen.tsx ==========
// src/screens/SettingsScreen.tsx
import React, { useCallback, useRef, useState } from "react";
import {
  View,
  Text,
  StyleSheet,
  ScrollView,
  Switch,
  TouchableOpacity,
  Modal,
  Pressable,
  Linking,
  Platform,
} from "react-native";
import { useSafeAreaInsets } from "react-native-safe-area-context";
import { useNavigation } from "@react-navigation/native";
import Svg, { Path } from "react-native-svg";
import * as Application from "expo-application";
import Constants from "expo-constants";

import { useTheme } from "../hooks/useTheme";
import { useSettingsStore } from "../store/settingsStore";
import { useExtraSettings } from "../store/extraSettingsStore";
import { useMediaScanner } from "../hooks/useMediaScanner";
import { safeGoBack } from "../utils/navigation";
import { showToast } from "../utils/toast";
import type { ThemeMode } from "../types";

import TopStatusGlass from "../components/TopStatusGlass";

const SYSTEM_FONT = Platform.select({
  ios: "System",
  android: "sans-serif",
  default: "sans-serif",
});

const ACCENT = "#f92c47";
const DANGER = "#ff3b30";
const SWITCH_COLOR = "#007AFF";

const PORTFOLIO_FALLBACK = "https://rushicdhande.vercel.app/";
const CONTACT_EMAIL = "rushicdhande@gmail.com";

const THEME_OPTIONS: { key: ThemeMode; label: string }[] = [
  { key: "light", label: "Light" },
  { key: "dark", label: "Dark" },
  // { key: "system", label: "System Default" },
];

function getPortfolioUrl(): string {
  const fromConfig =
    (Constants.expoConfig?.extra as any)?.portfolioUrl ??
    (Constants as any).manifest?.extra?.portfolioUrl ??
    (Constants as any).manifest2?.extra?.expoClient?.extra?.portfolioUrl;

  return typeof fromConfig === "string" && fromConfig.length > 0
    ? fromConfig
    : PORTFOLIO_FALLBACK;
}

const BackArrowIcon = ({
  size = 22,
  color = "#000",
}: {
  size?: number;
  color?: string;
}) => (
  <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">
    <Path
      fill={color}
      d="M7.82843 10.9999H20V12.9999H7.82843L13.1924 18.3638L11.7782 19.778L4 11.9999L11.7782 4.22168L13.1924 5.63589L7.82843 10.9999Z"
    />
  </Svg>
);

const ChevronIcon = ({ size = 14, color = "#c4c4c6" }) => (
  <Svg width={size} height={size} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">
    <Path
      d="M9 6l6 6-6 6"
      stroke={color}
      strokeWidth={2.5}
      strokeLinecap="round"
      strokeLinejoin="round"
    />
  </Svg>
);

function SettingsSection({
  title,
  children,
  theme,
}: {
  title: string;
  children: React.ReactNode;
  theme: any;
}) {
  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";
  return (
    <View style={styles.section}>
      <Text style={[styles.sectionTitle, { color: isDark ? "#8e8e93" : "#6e6e73" }]}>
        {title.toUpperCase()}
      </Text>
      <View style={[styles.card, { backgroundColor: isDark ? "#1c1c1e" : "#ffffff" }]}>
        {children}
      </View>
    </View>
  );
}

type RowProps = {
  title: string;
  subtitle?: string;
  value?: string;
  accessory?: React.ReactNode;
  onPress?: () => void;
  isLast?: boolean;
  destructive?: boolean;
  theme: any;
};

function SettingsRow({
  title,
  subtitle,
  value,
  accessory,
  onPress,
  isLast,
  destructive,
  theme,
}: RowProps) {
  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";
  const textColor = destructive ? DANGER : theme.text;

  return (
    <>
      <TouchableOpacity
        onPress={onPress}
        disabled={!onPress}
        activeOpacity={0.6}
        style={styles.row}
      >
        <View style={styles.rowLeft}>
          <Text style={[styles.rowTitle, { color: textColor }]}>{title}</Text>
          {subtitle ? (
            <Text style={[styles.rowSubtitle, { color: isDark ? "#8e8e93" : "#6e6e73" }]}>
              {subtitle}
            </Text>
          ) : null}
        </View>

        {value ? (
          <Text style={[styles.rowValue, { color: isDark ? "#8e8e93" : "#6e6e73" }]}>
            {value}
          </Text>
        ) : null}

        {accessory ? (
          <View style={styles.rowAccessory}>{accessory}</View>
        ) : onPress ? (
          <View style={styles.rowAccessory}>
            <ChevronIcon color={isDark ? "#8e8e93" : "#c4c4c6"} />
          </View>
        ) : null}
      </TouchableOpacity>

      {!isLast && (
        <View style={[styles.divider, { backgroundColor: isDark ? "#38383a" : "#e5e5ea" }]} />
      )}
    </>
  );
}

function Toggle({
  value,
  onChange,
  color = SWITCH_COLOR,
}: {
  value: boolean;
  onChange: (v: boolean) => void;
  color?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <Switch
      value={value}
      onValueChange={onChange}
      trackColor={{ true: color, false: "#e5e5ea" }}
      thumbColor={"#ffffff"}
      ios_backgroundColor="#e5e5ea"
    />
  );
}

type ModalOption = {
  text: string;
  onPress?: () => void;
  destructive?: boolean;
};

type ModalState = {
  title: string;
  message?: string;
  options: ModalOption[];
} | null;

function SettingsModal({
  state,
  onClose,
  theme,
}: {
  state: ModalState;
  onClose: () => void;
  theme: any;
}) {
  const lastRef = useRef<ModalState>(null);
  if (state) lastRef.current = state;
  const view = state ?? lastRef.current;
  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";

  return (
    <Modal visible={!!state} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={onClose}>
      <Pressable style={styles.modalBackdrop} onPress={onClose}>
        <Pressable
          style={[
            styles.modalCard,
            { backgroundColor: isDark ? "#1c1c1e" : "#ffffff", borderColor: isDark ? "#38383a" : "#e5e5ea" },
          ]}
          onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        >
          {view?.title ? (
            <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: theme.text }]}>{view.title}</Text>
          ) : null}

          {view?.message ? (
            <Text style={[styles.modalMessage, { color: isDark ? "#8e8e93" : "#6e6e73" }]}>
              {view.message}
            </Text>
          ) : null}

          <View style={[styles.modalDivider, { backgroundColor: isDark ? "#38383a" : "#e5e5ea" }]} />

          {(view?.options ?? []).map((opt, idx, arr) => {
            const isLast = idx === arr.length - 1;
            const color = opt.destructive ? DANGER : theme.primary;

            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={`${opt.text}-${idx}`}
                activeOpacity={0.6}
                style={[
                  styles.modalOption,
                  !isLast && {
                    borderBottomWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
                    borderBottomColor: isDark ? "#38383a" : "#e5e5ea",
                  },
                ]}
                onPress={() => {
                  onClose();
                  if (opt.onPress) setTimeout(() => opt.onPress?.(), 150);
                }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.modalOptionText, { color, fontWeight: opt.destructive ? "600" : "400" }]}>
                  {opt.text}
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}
        </Pressable>
      </Pressable>
    </Modal>
  );
}

function ThemePopup({
  visible,
  onClose,
  current,
  onSelect,
  accent,
  isDark,
}: {
  visible: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  current: ThemeMode;
  onSelect: (key: ThemeMode, label: string) => void;
  accent: string;
  isDark: boolean;
}) {
  return (
    <Modal visible={visible} transparent animationType="fade" onRequestClose={onClose}>
      <Pressable style={styles.modalBackdrop} onPress={onClose}>
        <Pressable
          style={[
            styles.modalCard,
            { backgroundColor: isDark ? "#1c1c1e" : "#ffffff", borderColor: isDark ? "#38383a" : "#e5e5ea" },
          ]}
          onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}
        >
          <Text style={[styles.modalTitle, { color: isDark ? "#fff" : "#000" }]}>Theme</Text>

          {THEME_OPTIONS.map((opt) => {
            const selected = current === opt.key;
            return (
              <TouchableOpacity
                key={opt.key}
                activeOpacity={0.6}
                style={styles.themeRow}
                onPress={() => onSelect(opt.key, opt.label)}
              >
                <View style={styles.themeRowLeft}>
                  <Text style={[styles.themeLabel, { color: isDark ? "#fff" : "#000" }]}>
                    {opt.label}
                  </Text>
                </View>
                {selected && (
                  <Svg width={20} height={20} viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none">
                    <Path
                      d="M20 6L9 17l-5-5"
                      stroke={accent}
                      strokeWidth={2.5}
                      strokeLinecap="round"
                      strokeLinejoin="round"
                    />
                  </Svg>
                )}
              </TouchableOpacity>
            );
          })}

          <View style={[styles.modalDivider, { backgroundColor: isDark ? "#38383a" : "#e5e5ea" }]} />
          
          {/* âœ… FIXED: Added missing Cancel button text and styling */}
          <TouchableOpacity onPress={onClose} activeOpacity={0.6} style={styles.modalOption}>
            <Text style={[styles.modalOptionText, { color: accent, fontWeight: "600" }]}>
              Cancel
            </Text>
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </Pressable>
      </Pressable>
    </Modal>
  );
}

export default function SettingsScreen() {
  const theme = useTheme() as any;
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const s = useSettingsStore();
  const x = useExtraSettings();
  const { scanEverything } = useMediaScanner();
  
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();

  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";
  const accent = theme.primary ?? ACCENT;

  // âœ… Solid glass colors for the back button without shadows
  const glassBg = isDark ? "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.12)" : "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08)";
  const glassBorder = isDark ? "rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.25)" : "rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.15)";

  const [modal, setModal] = useState<ModalState>(null);
  const closeModal = useCallback(() => setModal(null), []);

  const [themeOpen, setThemeOpen] = useState(false);
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(false);

  const appVersion = Constants.expoConfig?.version ?? Application.nativeApplicationVersion ?? "1.0.0";
  const portfolioUrl = getPortfolioUrl();

  const currentThemeLabel = s.theme === "light" ? "Light" : s.theme === "system" ? "System Default" : "Dark";

  const openPortfolio = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const supported = await Linking.canOpenURL(portfolioUrl);
      if (!supported) return showToast("Cannot open link");
      await Linking.openURL(portfolioUrl);
    } catch (error) {
      showToast("Cannot open link");
    }
  }, [portfolioUrl]);

  const openEmail = useCallback(async () => {
    try {
      const url = `mailto:${CONTACT_EMAIL}`;
      const supported = await Linking.canOpenURL(url);
      if (!supported) return showToast("Cannot open email app");
      await Linking.openURL(url);
    } catch (error) {
      showToast("Cannot open email app");
    }
  }, []);

  const onRescan = useCallback(async () => {
    if (scanning) return;
    try {
      setScanning(true);
      showToast("Scanning media...");
      await scanEverything();
      showToast("Rescan complete");
    } catch (error) {
      showToast("Rescan failed");
    } finally {
      setScanning(false);
    }
  }, [scanEverything, scanning]);

  const handleSelectTheme = useCallback(
    (key: ThemeMode, label: string) => {
      s.setTheme(key);
      setThemeOpen(false);
      showToast(`Theme: ${label}`);
    },
    [s]
  );

  const openResetModal = useCallback(() => {
    setModal({
      title: "Reset All Settings",
      message: "This will restore every option to its default value. Your media and favourites will not be affected.",
      options: [
        {
          text: "Reset Everything",
          destructive: true,
          onPress: () => {
            try {
              s.setTheme("system");
              s.setDynamicBg(true);
              s.setAutoRescan(true);
              s.setNotifications(true);
              s.setDefaultSpeed(1);
              s.setShuffle(false);
              s.setRepeat("off");
              s.setContinuePlayback(true);
              s.setKeepAwake(false);
              x.reset();
              showToast("Settings reset");
            } catch {
              showToast("Reset failed");
            }
          },
        },
        { text: "Cancel" },
      ],
    });
  }, [s, x]);

  return (
    <View style={[styles.root, { backgroundColor: isDark ? "#000000" : "#f2f2f7" }]}>
      
      <ScrollView 
        contentContainerStyle={{ paddingTop: insets.top + 20, paddingBottom: 60 }} 
        showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
      >
        
        {/* Scrolling Title Header */}
        <View style={styles.scrollingHeader}>
          <Text style={[styles.headerTitle, { color: theme.text }]}>Settings</Text>
        </View>

        <SettingsSection title="Media" theme={theme}>
          <SettingsRow
            theme={theme}
            title="Rescan Now"
            subtitle={scanning ? "Scanning..." : "Refresh audio library"}
            onPress={onRescan}
            isLast
          />
        </SettingsSection>

        <SettingsSection title="Appearance" theme={theme}>
          <SettingsRow
            theme={theme}
            title="Theme"
            value={currentThemeLabel}
            onPress={() => setThemeOpen(true)}
          />
          <SettingsRow
            theme={theme}
            title="Dynamic Artwork Background"
            subtitle="Colour the player from the artwork"
            accessory={
              <Toggle
                value={!!s.dynamicBg}
                onChange={(v) => {
                  s.setDynamicBg(v);
                  showToast(v ? "Dynamic BG ON" : "Dynamic BG OFF");
                }}
              />
            }
            isLast
          />
        </SettingsSection>

        <SettingsSection title="About" theme={theme}>
          <SettingsRow theme={theme} title="Version" value={appVersion} />
          <SettingsRow
            theme={theme}
            title="Portfolio"
            subtitle="Open in browser"
            onPress={openPortfolio}
          />
          <SettingsRow
            theme={theme}
            title="Reset All Settings"
            destructive
            onPress={openResetModal}
            isLast
          />
        </SettingsSection>

        <View style={styles.footerSection}>
          <Text style={[styles.footerQuote, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            Kill Your Ideas <Text style={[styles.footerQuoteAccent, { color: accent }]}>Before</Text> You Build IT.
          </Text>

          <View style={[styles.footerDivider, { backgroundColor: theme.border }]} />

          <Text style={[styles.footerGreeting, { color: theme.text }]}>
            Hey WhatsApp guys ðŸ‘‹
          </Text>
          <Text style={[styles.footerContact, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}>
            I'm Rushikesh. Any concern, feel free to reach me at <Text style={[styles.footerEmail, { color: accent }]} onPress={openEmail} suppressHighlighting>{CONTACT_EMAIL}</Text>
          </Text>
        </View>
      </ScrollView>

      {/* Sticky Back Button - Clean Glass, No Shadow */}
      <TouchableOpacity
        onPress={() => safeGoBack(nav)}
        style={[
          styles.stickyBackBtn,
          {
            top: insets.top + 28, // âœ… FIXED: Added missing value for 'top'
            backgroundColor: glassBg,
            borderColor: glassBorder,
          },
        ]}
        hitSlop={10}
        activeOpacity={0.7}
      >
        <BackArrowIcon size={22} color={theme.text} />
      </TouchableOpacity>

      <TopStatusGlass />

      <ThemePopup
        visible={themeOpen}
        onClose={() => setThemeOpen(false)}
        current={s.theme}
        onSelect={handleSelectTheme}
        accent={accent}
        isDark={isDark}
      />

      <SettingsModal state={modal} onClose={closeModal} theme={theme} />
    </View>
  );
}

const shuffleArray = <T,>(arr: T[]): T[] => {
  const a = [...arr];
  for (let i = a.length - 1; i > 0; i--) {
    const j = Math.floor(Math.random() * (i + 1));
    [a[i], a[j]] = [a[j], a[i]];
  }
  return a;
};
const isDummy = (id?: string | null) => !!id && id.startsWith("dummy-");

// â”€â”€ site-name cleaner (kept identical) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const SITE_WORDS = [
  "pagalnew",
  "pagalworld",
  "pagalfree",
  "pagalmovies",
  "pagalsong",
  "pagal",
  "djmaza",
  "mrjatt",

  "sensongsmp3",
  "pendujatt",
  "jattmp3",
  "newmp3",
  "mp3 download",
  "free download",
];
const SITE_WORDS_RE = SITE_WORDS.map((w) =>
  w.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&").replace(/\s+/g, "\\s*"),
).join("|");
const TLDS =
  "com|net|org|in|co|se|pk|info|me|cc|ws|xyz|club|site|live|fm|mobi|io|app|top|online|tv|biz|us|uk";
const SEP = "[\\s_\\-â€“â€”|:@~â€¢Â·]";

const RE_BRACKET_SITE = new RegExp(
  `[\\(\\[\\{<][^\\)\\]\\}>]*(?:${SITE_WORDS_RE}|\\.(?:${TLDS})\\b)[^\\)\\]\\}>]*[\\)\\]\\}>]`,
  "gi",
);
const RE_SITE_WORD = new RegExp(
  `${SEP}*(?:${SITE_WORDS_RE})(?:\\.[a-z0-9]+)*`,
  "gi",
);
const RE_WWW = /\bwww\.[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)+/gi;
const RE_DOMAIN = new RegExp(
  `${SEP}*\\b[a-z0-9-]+\\.(?:${TLDS})(?:\\.[a-z]{2})?\\b`,
  "gi",
);
const RE_EDGE_SEP = new RegExp(`^${SEP}+|${SEP}+$`, "g");

const cleanSiteName = (raw?: string | null, keepOriginal = true): string => {
  if (!raw) return "";
  const original = String(raw).trim();
  if (!original) return "";

  let s = original
    .replace(RE_BRACKET_SITE, " ")
    .replace(RE_WWW, " ")
    .replace(RE_SITE_WORD, " ")
    .replace(RE_DOMAIN, " ")
    .replace(/_/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ")
    .replace(RE_EDGE_SEP, "")
    .trim();

  if (!s) return keepOriginal ? original : "";
  return s;
};

const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  const t = a.trim();
  if (!t) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(t);
};

// â”€â”€ ID3 reading (kept identical) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const B64_CHARS =
  "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
const B64_LOOKUP = (() => {
  const t = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < B64_CHARS.length; i++) t[B64_CHARS.charCodeAt(i)] = i;
  return t;
})();

const base64ToBytes = (b64: string): Uint8Array => {
  const clean = b64.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9+/]/g, "");
  const out = new Uint8Array(Math.floor((clean.length * 3) / 4));
  let p = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i += 4) {
    const a = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i)] || 0;
    const b = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 1)] || 0;
    const c = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 2)] || 0;
    const d = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 3)] || 0;
    out[p++] = (a << 2) | (b >> 4);
    out[p++] = ((b & 15) << 4) | (c >> 2);
    out[p++] = ((c & 3) << 6) | d;
  }
  return out;
};

const syncSafe = (b: Uint8Array, o: number) =>
  ((b[o] & 0x7f) << 21) |
  ((b[o + 1] & 0x7f) << 14) |
  ((b[o + 2] & 0x7f) << 7) |
  (b[o + 3] & 0x7f);

const decodeUtf16 = (b: Uint8Array, bigEndian: boolean): string => {
  let s = "";
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < b.length; i += 2) {
    const code = bigEndian ? (b[i] << 8) | b[i + 1] : (b[i + 1] << 8) | b[i];
    s += String.fromCharCode(code);
  }
  return s;
};

const decodeUtf8 = (b: Uint8Array): string => {
  try {
    let pct = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
      pct += "%" + (b[i] < 16 ? "0" : "") + b[i].toString(16);
    }
    return decodeURIComponent(pct);
  } catch {
    let s = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
    return s;
  }
};

const decodeId3Text = (b: Uint8Array, enc: number): string => {
  let s = "";
  if (enc === 1) {
    if (b[0] === 0xfe && b[1] === 0xff) s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), true);
    else if (b[0] === 0xff && b[1] === 0xfe)
      s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), false);
    else s = decodeUtf16(b, false);
  } else if (enc === 2) {
    s = decodeUtf16(b, true);
  } else if (enc === 3) {
    s = decodeUtf8(b);
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
  }
  return s
    .split("\u0000")
    .map((x) => x.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
};

const readId3Frame = (
  b: Uint8Array,
  frameIds: string[],
): string | undefined => {
  if (b.length < 10 || b[0] !== 0x49 || b[1] !== 0x44 || b[2] !== 0x33) {
    return undefined;
  }
  const major = b[3];
  const flags = b[5];
  const tagSize = syncSafe(b, 6);
  let pos = 10;

  if (major >= 3 && (flags & 0x40) !== 0) {
    const ext =
      major === 4
        ? syncSafe(b, pos)
        : (((b[pos] << 24) |
            (b[pos + 1] << 16) |
            (b[pos + 2] << 8) |
            b[pos + 3]) >>>
            0) +
          4;
    pos += ext;
  }

  const end = Math.min(b.length, 10 + tagSize);
  const idLen = major === 2 ? 3 : 4;
  const hdrLen = major === 2 ? 6 : 10;

  while (pos + hdrLen <= end) {
    let id = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < idLen; i++) id += String.fromCharCode(b[pos + i]);
    if (!/^[A-Z0-9]+$/.test(id)) break;

    let size: number;
    if (major === 2) {
      size = (b[pos + 3] << 16) | (b[pos + 4] << 8) | b[pos + 5];
    } else if (major === 4) {
      size = syncSafe(b, pos + 4);
    } else {
      size =
        ((b[pos + 4] << 24) |
          (b[pos + 5] << 16) |
          (b[pos + 6] << 8) |
          b[pos + 7]) >>>
        0;
    }

    const dataStart = pos + hdrLen;
    if (size <= 0 || dataStart + size > b.length) break;

    if (frameIds.includes(id)) {
      const text = decodeId3Text(
        b.subarray(dataStart + 1, dataStart + size),
        b[dataStart],
      );
      if (text && text.trim()) return text.trim();
    }

    pos = dataStart + size;
  }
  return undefined;
};

const parseId3Artist = (b: Uint8Array): string | undefined => {
  const primary = readId3Frame(b, ["TPE1", "TP1"]);
  if (primary && !isUnknownArtist(primary)) return primary;
  const albumArtist = readId3Frame(b, ["TPE2", "TP2"]);
  return !isUnknownArtist(albumArtist) ? albumArtist : undefined;
};

const parseId3Album = (b: Uint8Array): string | undefined =>
  readId3Frame(b, ["TALB", "TAL"]);

const parseId3Title = (b: Uint8Array): string | undefined =>
  readId3Frame(b, ["TIT2", "TT2"]);

const readTagsFromFile = async (
  uri: string,
): Promise<{ title?: string; artist?: string; album?: string }> => {
  try {
    const b64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(uri, {
      encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
      position: 0,
      length: 131072,
    });
    const bytes = base64ToBytes(b64);
    const title = parseId3Title(bytes);
    const artist = parseId3Artist(bytes);
    const album = parseId3Album(bytes);
    return {
      title: title && title.trim() ? title.trim() : undefined,
      artist: artist && !isUnknownArtist(artist) ? artist : undefined,
      album: album && album.trim() ? album.trim() : undefined,
    };
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
};

const DefaultSongArt = React.memo(function DefaultSongArt({
  style,
}: {
  style?: any;
}) {
  return (
    <Image
      source={MUSIC_DEFAULT_PNG}
      style={style}
      resizeMode="cover"
      fadeDuration={0}
    />
  );
});

const SafeImage = React.memo(function SafeImage({
  uri,
  style,
  reloadKey = 0,
  resizeMode = "cover",
}: {
  uri?: string | null;
  style: any;
  reloadKey?: number;
  resizeMode?: "cover" | "contain" | "stretch" | "center";
}) {
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    setFailed(false);
  }, [uri, reloadKey]);

  let safeUri = uri;
  if (
    safeUri &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("http") &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("file://") &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("content://") &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("ph://") &&
    !safeUri.startsWith("data:")
  ) {
    safeUri = `file://${safeUri}`;
  }

  if (!safeUri || failed) {
    return <DefaultSongArt style={style} />;
  }

  return (
    <Image
      source={{ uri: safeUri }}
      style={style}
      resizeMode={resizeMode}
      fadeDuration={0}
      onError={() => setFailed(true)}
    />
  );
});

function ArtworkBox({
  uri,
  size,
  radius = 6,
  fallbackBg,
  width,
  height,
  reloadKey = 0,
  borderWidth = 0,
  borderColor = "transparent",
}: {
  uri?: string;
  size: number;
  radius?: number;
  iconSize?: number;
  fallbackBg: string;
  iconColor: string;
  width?: number;
  height?: number;
  reloadKey?: number;
  borderWidth?: number;
  borderColor?: string;
}) {
  return (
    <SafeImage
      uri={uri}
      reloadKey={reloadKey}
      style={{
        width: width ?? size,
        height: height ?? size,
        borderRadius: radius,
        backgroundColor: fallbackBg,
        borderWidth,
        borderColor,
      }}
    />
  );
}

export default function SongsScreen() {
  const theme = useTheme();
  const nav = useNavigation<any>();
  const insets = useSafeAreaInsets();
  const { height: winH } = useWindowDimensions();

  const isDark = theme.mode === "dark";

  const BG_COLOR = theme.background;
  const SURFACE_COLOR = theme.surface;
  const BORDER_COLOR = theme.border;
  const ACCENT = theme.primary;

  const DIVIDER_COLOR = isDark ? "rgba(255,255,255,0.16)" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.14)";

  const songs = useMediaStore((s) => s.songs ?? []);
  const favorites = useMediaStore((s) => s.favoritesSong);
  const toggleFavorite = useMediaStore((s) => s.toggleSongFavorite);

  const currentTrack = usePlayerStore((s) => s.currentTrack);
  const isPlayerPlaying = usePlayerStore((s) => s.isPlaying);

  const { scanAll } = useMediaScanner();
    const audio = useAudioPlayer() as any;
  const { loadAndPlay } = audio;
  const { artworkMap, remove: removeArtwork } = useArtworkCache(songs);

  const artKeep = useRef<Record<string, string>>({});
  useEffect(() => {
    Object.keys(artworkMap).forEach((id) => {
      const uri = artworkMap[id];
      if (uri) artKeep.current[id] = uri;
    });
  }, [artworkMap]);

  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [refreshing, setRefreshing] = useState(false);
  const [scanning, setScanning] = useState(songs.length === 0);
  const [reloadKey, setReloadKey] = useState(0);

  const [menuVisible, setMenuVisible] = useState(false);
  const [selectedSong, setSelectedSong] = useState<Song | null>(null);
  const [actionBusy, setActionBusy] = useState(false);

  const [tagData, setTagData] = useState<
    Record<string, { title?: string; artist?: string; album?: string }>
  >({});
  const requestedTagIds = useRef<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const mounted = useRef(true);
  const refreshBusy = useRef(false);
  const initialScanStarted = useRef(false);

  const [tagsLoaded, setTagsLoaded] = useState(false);
  const [artLoaded, setArtLoaded] = useState(false);

  const [bootMinDone, setBootMinDone] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (MIN_BOOT_LOADER_MS <= 0) {
      setBootMinDone(true);
      return;
    }
    const t = setTimeout(() => setBootMinDone(true), MIN_BOOT_LOADER_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length === 0) return;
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      if (mounted.current) {
        setTagsLoaded(true);
        setArtLoaded(true);
      }
    }, MAX_LOADER_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [songs.length > 0]); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!tagsLoaded || artLoaded) return;

    const allHaveArt =
      songs.length > 0 &&
      songs.every(
        (s) => (s as any).artwork || artworkMap[s.id] || artKeep.current[s.id],
      );
    if (allHaveArt) {
      setArtLoaded(true);
      return;
    }

    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      if (mounted.current) setArtLoaded(true);
    }, ART_GRACE_MS);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  }, [tagsLoaded, artLoaded, songs, artworkMap]);

  const dropdownScale = useRef(new Animated.Value(0.9)).current;
  const dropdownOpacity = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const menuClosing = useRef(false);

  const [pinnedIds, setPinnedIds] = useState<Record<string, boolean>>({});
  const pinnedLoaded = useRef(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!menuVisible) return;

    menuClosing.current = false;
    dropdownScale.setValue(0.9);
    dropdownOpacity.setValue(0);

    Animated.parallel([
      Animated.timing(dropdownScale, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 220,
        easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
      Animated.timing(dropdownOpacity, {
        toValue: 1,
        duration: 180,
        easing: Easing.out(Easing.quad),
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
    ]).start();
  }, [menuVisible, dropdownScale, dropdownOpacity]);

  useEffect(() => {
    mounted.current = true;
    return () => {
      mounted.current = false;
    };
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    setupDeleteNotificationChannel().catch(() => {});
    registerDeleteNotificationEvents();
  }, []);

  const openMenu = useCallback(() => {
    setMenuVisible(true);
  }, []);

  const closeMenu = useCallback(() => {
    if (menuClosing.current) return;
    menuClosing.current = true;

    Animated.parallel([
      Animated.timing(dropdownScale, {
        toValue: 0.94,
        duration: 140,
        easing: Easing.in(Easing.quad),
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
      Animated.timing(dropdownOpacity, {
        toValue: 0,
        duration: 140,
        easing: Easing.in(Easing.quad),
        useNativeDriver: true,
      }),
    ]).start(() => {
      menuClosing.current = false;
      setMenuVisible(false);
    });
  }, [dropdownScale, dropdownOpacity]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (initialScanStarted.current) return;
    initialScanStarted.current = true;

    if (songs.length > 0) {
      setScanning(false);
      return;
    }

    setScanning(true);
    scanAll()
      .catch((error) => {
        console.error("[SongsScreen] Initial scan failed:", error);
      })
      .finally(() => {
        if (mounted.current) setScanning(false);
      });
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length > 0) setScanning(false);
  }, [songs.length]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (songs.length === 0) return;

    const missing = songs.filter((s) => !requestedTagIds.current.has(s.id));

    if (missing.length === 0) {
      if (mounted.current) setTagsLoaded(true);
      return;
    }

    missing.forEach((s) => requestedTagIds.current.add(s.id));

    (async () => {
      let batch: Record<
        string,
        { title?: string; artist?: string; album?: string }
      > = {};
      let count = 0;

      const flush = () => {
        if (Object.keys(batch).length > 0 && mounted.current) {
          const toSave = batch;
          setTagData((prev) => ({ ...prev, ...toSave }));
        }
        batch = {};
      };

      for (const s of missing) {
        if (!mounted.current) return;

        const uri = (s as any).uri ?? (s as any).path;
        if (uri) {
          const { title, artist, album } = await readTagsFromFile(uri);
          if (title || artist || album) {
            batch[s.id] = { title, artist, album };
          }
        }

        count++;
        if (count % 10 === 0) flush();
      }

      flush();
      if (mounted.current) setTagsLoaded(true);
    })();
  }, [songs]);

  const filteredSongs = useMemo(() => {
    const query = search.trim().toLowerCase();
    const allSongs = [...songs, ...(SHOW_DUMMY_SONGS ? DUMMY_SONGS : [])];

    let list = allSongs.map((song) => {
      const rawArtist = cleanSiteName((song as any).artist, false);
      const rawAlbum = cleanSiteName((song as any).album, false);
      const tagged = tagData[song.id];

      const taggedArtist = cleanSiteName(tagged?.artist, false);
      const artist = !isUnknownArtist(taggedArtist)
        ? taggedArtist
        : !isUnknownArtist(rawArtist)
          ? rawArtist
          : UNKNOWN_ARTIST;

      const album =
        cleanSiteName(tagged?.album, false) || rawAlbum || UNKNOWN_ALBUM;

      return {
        ...song,
        title:
          cleanSiteName(tagged?.title) ||
          cleanSiteName(song.title) ||
          ((song as any).filename
            ? cleanSiteName(
                String((song as any).filename).replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, ""),
              )
            : "") ||
          "Unknown song",
        artist,
        album,
        artwork:
          (song as any).artwork ??
          artworkMap[song.id] ??
          artKeep.current[song.id],
      };
    });

    if (query) {
      list = list.filter((song) =>
        `${song.title} ${song.artist} ${song.album}`
          .toLowerCase()
          .includes(query),
      );
    }

    return [...list].sort((a, b) => {
      const pa = pinnedIds[a.id] ? 0 : 1;
      const pb = pinnedIds[b.id] ? 0 : 1;
      if (pa !== pb) return pa - pb;
      return cmp(a.title, b.title);
    });
  }, [songs, search, artworkMap, tagData, pinnedIds]);

  const onRefresh = useCallback(async () => {
    if (refreshBusy.current) return;
    refreshBusy.current = true;
    setRefreshing(true);

    const started = Date.now();

    try {
      requestedTagIds.current.clear();
      await scanAll();

      const wait = MIN_REFRESH_MS - (Date.now() - started);
      if (wait > 0) await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, wait));

      if (mounted.current) {
        setReloadKey((k) => k + 1);
        showToast("Library refreshed");
      }
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("[SongsScreen] Refresh failed:", error);
      if (mounted.current) showToast("Unable to scan audio files");
    } finally {
      refreshBusy.current = false;
      if (mounted.current) setRefreshing(false);
    }
  }, [scanAll]);

  const playSong = useCallback(
    async (song: Song) => {
      if (isDummy(song.id)) {
        showToast("Dummy song â€” can't be played");
        return;
      }

      const playable = filteredSongs;
      const index = playable.findIndex((item) => item.id === song.id);

      if (index < 0) {
        showToast("Song unavailable â€” please scan again");
        return;
      }

      try {
        await loadAndPlay(playable, index);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("[SongsScreen] Playback error:", error);
        showToast("Playback failed. Check logs.");
      }
    },
    [filteredSongs, loadAndPlay],
  );

  const playAll = useCallback(
    async (shuffle: boolean) => {
      if (actionBusy) return;

      const playable = filteredSongs;
      if (playable.length === 0) {
        showToast("No songs to play");
        return;
      }

      const queue = shuffle ? shuffleArray(playable) : playable;

      try {
        await loadAndPlay(queue, 0);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("[SongsScreen] Playback error:", error);
        showToast("Playback failed. Check logs.");
      }
    },
    [filteredSongs, loadAndPlay, actionBusy],
  );

  const performDelete = useCallback(
    async (song: Song) => {
      setActionBusy(true);

      let cancelled = false;
      let deleteStarted = false;

      try {
        await dismissDeleteNotification();

        const uri = (song as any).uri ?? (song as any).path;
        let fileSizeBytes = 0;

        try {
          if (uri) {
            const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(uri);
            if (info.exists && typeof (info as any).size === "number") {
              fileSizeBytes = (info as any).size;
            }
          }
        } catch (error) {
          console.warn("[SongsScreen] File size lookup failed:", error);
        }

        if (fileSizeBytes <= 0 && typeof (song as any).fileSize === "number") {
          fileSizeBytes = (song as any).fileSize;
        }

        const fileSizeMB = fileSizeBytes / (1024 * 1024);

        const onCancel = (): boolean => {
          if (deleteStarted) {
            showToast("Deletion already in progress");
            return false;
          }
          cancelled = true;
          showToast("Delete cancelled");
          return true;
        };

        await showDeleteWaiting({
          songTitle: song.title,
          fileSizeMB,
          onCancel,
        });

        if (cancelled || isDeleteCancelled()) {
          await dismissDeleteNotification();
          return;
        }

        deleteStarted = true;

        try {
          const deleted = await MediaLibrary.deleteAssetsAsync([song.id]);

          if (deleted === false) {
            await dismissDeleteNotification();
            showToast("Delete cancelled");
            return;
          }
        } catch (error: any) {
          const msg = String(error?.message ?? error);
          const denied =
            /permission/i.test(msg) && /(grant|denied|allow|write)/i.test(msg);

          if (isDeleteCancelled() || denied) {
            await dismissDeleteNotification();
            showToast("Delete cancelled");
            return;
          }

          console.error("[SongsScreen] Delete failed:", error);
          await showDeleteFailed({
            songTitle: song.title,
            fileSizeMB,
            reason: msg,
          });
          showToast("Delete failed â€” try again");
          return;
        }

        await showDeleteSuccess({
          songTitle: song.title,
          fileSizeMB,
        });

        removeArtwork(song.id);
        delete artKeep.current[song.id];

        // ðŸ”¥ FIX 1: Instantly update MediaStore
        const store = useMediaStore.getState() as any;
        const nextSongs = (store.songs || []).filter((s: any) => String(s.id) !== String(song.id));
        const nextFav = { ...(store.favoritesSong || {}) };
        delete nextFav[song.id];
        useMediaStore.setState({ songs: nextSongs, favoritesSong: nextFav });

          // ðŸ”¥ FIX: Instantly update PlayerStore and stop audio
                // ðŸ”¥ FIX: Instantly update PlayerStore and stop native background audio
              // ðŸ”¥ FIX: Instantly update PlayerStore and stop native background audio
        try {
          const ps: any = usePlayerStore.getState();
          if (ps?.currentTrack && String(ps.currentTrack.id) === String(song.id)) {
            
            // Force native TrackPlayer to stop and clear the queue
            try {
              await TrackPlayer.reset(); 
            } catch (e) {
              console.log("TrackPlayer reset error:", e);
            }

            // Fallback to hook methods
            try { await audio?.stop?.(); } catch (e) {}
            try { await audio?.pause?.(); } catch (e) {}
            
            // Clear React Store
            if (typeof ps.setCurrentTrack === "function") ps.setCurrentTrack(null);
            if (typeof ps.setIsPlaying === "function") ps.setIsPlaying(false);
            if (typeof ps.clearQueue === "function") ps.clearQueue();
          } else if (Array.isArray(ps.queue)) {
            const newQueue = ps.queue.filter((s: any) => String(s.id) !== String(song.id));
            if (typeof ps.setQueue === "function") ps.setQueue(newQueue);
          }
        } catch (e) {
          console.warn("[SongsScreen] Failed to reset player:", e);
        }

        // ðŸ”¥ FIX 3: Run scan in background (don't await)
        scanAll().catch((scanError: any) => {
          console.error("[SongsScreen] Scan after delete failed:", scanError);
        });

        showToast("Deleted successfully");
        setSelectedSong(null);
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("[SongsScreen] Delete workflow error:", error);
        await dismissDeleteNotification();
        showToast("Something went wrong while deleting");
      } finally {
        setActionBusy(false);
      }
    },
    [scanAll, removeArtwork],
  );
  const deleteSong = useCallback(() => {
    const song = selectedSong;
    if (!song || actionBusy) return;
    if (isDummy(song.id)) {
      showToast("Dummy song â€” can't be deleted");
      return;
    }

    Alert.alert(
      "Delete song?",
      `Delete "${song.title}" from your device? Android may ask you to approve the deletion.`,
      [
        { text: "Cancel", style: "cancel" },
        {
          text: "Delete",
          style: "destructive",
          onPress: () => performDelete(song),
        },
      ],
    );
  }, [selectedSong, actionBusy, performDelete]);

  useEffect(() => {
    (async () => {
      try {
        const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(PINNED_FILE);
        if (info.exists) {
          const raw = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(PINNED_FILE);
          const arr = JSON.parse(raw);
          if (Array.isArray(arr) && mounted.current) {
            const map: Record<string, boolean> = {};
            arr.forEach((id: any) => {
              map[String(id)] = true;
            });
            setPinnedIds(map);
          }
        }
      } catch {
        // ignore
      } finally {
        pinnedLoaded.current = true;
      }
    })();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!pinnedLoaded.current) return;
    FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(
      PINNED_FILE,
      JSON.stringify(Object.keys(pinnedIds)),
    ).catch(() => {});
  }, [pinnedIds]);

  const togglePin = useCallback((song: Song) => {
    setPinnedIds((prev) => {
      const next = { ...prev };
      if (next[song.id]) {
        delete next[song.id];
        showToast("Unpinned");
      } else {
        next[song.id] = true;
        showToast("Pinned to top");
      }
      return next;
    });
  }, []);

  const playNext = useCallback(
    (song: Song) => {
      if (isDummy(song.id)) {
        showToast("Dummy song â€” can't be queued");
        return;
      }
      const st: any = usePlayerStore.getState();
      if (!st.currentTrack) {
        playSong(song);
        return;
      }
      if (st.currentTrack.id === song.id) {
        showToast("Already playing");
        return;
      }
      if (typeof st.playNext === "function") {
        st.playNext(song);
        showToast("Will play next");
        return;
      }
      if (Array.isArray(st.queue) && typeof st.setQueue === "function") {
        const without = st.queue.filter((q: any) => q.id !== song.id);
        const at = without.findIndex((q: any) => q.id === st.currentTrack.id);
        const next = [...without];
        next.splice(at + 1, 0, song);
        st.setQueue(next);
        showToast("Will play next");
        return;
      }
      showToast("Play Next is not supported yet");
    },
    [playSong],
  );

  const addToQueue = useCallback(
    (song: Song) => {
      if (isDummy(song.id)) {
        showToast("Dummy song â€” can't be queued");
        return;
      }
      const st: any = usePlayerStore.getState();
      if (!st.currentTrack) {
        playSong(song);
        return;
      }
      const fn = st.addToQueue ?? st.enqueue;
      if (typeof fn === "function") {
        fn(song);
        showToast("Added to queue");
        return;
      }
      if (Array.isArray(st.queue) && typeof st.setQueue === "function") {
        if (st.queue.some((q: any) => q.id === song.id)) {
          showToast("Already in queue");
          return;
        }
        st.setQueue([...st.queue, song]);
        showToast("Added to queue");
        return;
      }
      showToast("Add to Queue is not supported yet");
    },
    [playSong],
  );

  const sheetY = useRef(new Animated.Value(winH)).current;
  const backdropAnim = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const sheetDrag = useRef(new Animated.Value(0)).current;
  const sheetHRef = useRef(winH);
  const sheetShown = useRef(false);
  const sheetClosing = useRef(false);

  const resetSheet = useCallback(() => {
    sheetShown.current = false;
    sheetClosing.current = false;
    sheetY.setValue(winH);
    backdropAnim.setValue(0);
    sheetDrag.setValue(0);
  }, [winH, sheetY, backdropAnim, sheetDrag]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!selectedSong) resetSheet();
  }, [selectedSong, resetSheet]);

  const sheetPan = useMemo(
    () =>
      PanResponder.create({
        onMoveShouldSetPanResponderCapture: (_, g) =>
          g.dy > 8 && Math.abs(g.dy) > Math.abs(g.dx),
        onPanResponderMove: (_, g) => {
          sheetDrag.setValue(Math.max(0, g.dy));
        },
        onPanResponderRelease: (_, g) => {
          if (g.dy > 100 || g.vy > 0.8) {
            if (sheetClosing.current) return;
            sheetClosing.current = true;
            Animated.timing(sheetDrag, {
              toValue: winH,
              duration: 200,
              easing: Easing.in(Easing.quad),
              useNativeDriver: true,
            }).start(() => {
              resetSheet();
              setSelectedSong(null);
            });
          } else {
            Animated.timing(sheetDrag, {
              toValue: 0,
              duration: 180,
              easing: Easing.out(Easing.quad),
              useNativeDriver: true,
            }).start();
          }
        },
        onPanResponderTerminate: () => {
          Animated.timing(sheetDrag, {
            toValue: 0,
            duration: 180,
            useNativeDriver: true,
          }).start();
        },
      }),
    [winH, sheetDrag, resetSheet],
  );

  const onSheetLayout = useCallback(
    (e: any) => {
      const h = e.nativeEvent.layout.height;
      if (h <= 0) return;
      sheetHRef.current = h;
      if (!selectedSong || sheetShown.current) return;

      sheetShown.current = true;
      sheetClosing.current = false;
      sheetDrag.setValue(0);
      sheetY.setValue(h);
      backdropAnim.setValue(0);

      Animated.parallel([
        Animated.timing(sheetY, {
          toValue: 0,
          duration: 300,
          easing: Easing.out(Easing.cubic),
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
        Animated.timing(backdropAnim, {
          toValue: 1,
          duration: 250,
          easing: Easing.out(Easing.quad),
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
      ]).start();
    },
    [selectedSong, sheetY, backdropAnim, sheetDrag],
  );

  const closeSheet = useCallback(
    (after?: () => void) => {
      if (sheetClosing.current) return;
      sheetClosing.current = true;
      Animated.parallel([
        Animated.timing(sheetY, {
          toValue: sheetHRef.current + 24,
          duration: 220,
          easing: Easing.in(Easing.cubic),
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
        Animated.timing(backdropAnim, {
          toValue: 0,
          duration: 220,
          useNativeDriver: true,
        }),
      ]).start(() => {
        resetSheet();
        setSelectedSong(null);
        after?.();
      });
    },
    [sheetY, backdropAnim, resetSheet],
  );

  const renderSong = useCallback(
    ({ item }: { item: any }) => {
      const isFav = !!favorites[item.id];
      const isPinned = !!pinnedIds[item.id];

      const isPlaying = currentTrack?.id === item.id && isPlayerPlaying;

      const artwork = item.artwork;
      const artistName = item.artist || UNKNOWN_ARTIST;

      return (
        <TouchableOpacity
          activeOpacity={0.7}
          onPress={() => playSong(item)}
          onLongPress={() => setSelectedSong(item)}
          style={styles.listRow}
        >
          {isPlaying && (
            <View pointerEvents="none" style={styles.playingDotWrap}>
              <View
                style={[
                  styles.playingDot,
                  { backgroundColor: "rgba(255,255,255,0.4)" },
                ]}
              />
            </View>
          )}

          <ArtworkBox
            uri={artwork}
            size={52}
            radius={ART_RADIUS}
            borderWidth={ART_BORDER_WIDTH}
            borderColor={ART_BORDER_COLOR}
            reloadKey={reloadKey}
            fallbackBg={SURFACE_COLOR}
            iconColor={theme.textSecondary}
          />

          <View style={styles.listInfo}>
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={[styles.listTitle, { color: theme.text }]}
            >
              {item.title}
            </Text>
            <Text
              numberOfLines={1}
              style={[styles.listArtist, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
            >
              {artistName}
            </Text>
          </View>

          {isPinned && (
            <View style={styles.listPin}>
              <PinIcon size={13} color={theme.textSecondary} />
            </View>
          )}

          {isFav && (
            <Text style={[styles.listFavStar, { color: ACCENT }]}>â˜…</Text>
          )}

          <TouchableOpacity
            onPress={() => setSelectedSong(item)}
            style={styles.listMoreBtn}
            hitSlop={10}
          >
            <EllipsisVerticalIcon size={20} color={theme.textSecondary} />
          </TouchableOpacity>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      );
    },
    [
      favorites,
      pinnedIds,
      currentTrack,
      isPlayerPlaying,
      playSong,
      theme,
      SURFACE_COLOR,
      ACCENT,
      BORDER_COLOR,
      reloadKey,
    ],
  );

  const contentReady = tagsLoaded && artLoaded;
  const showLoading =
    !bootMinDone ||
    (scanning && songs.length === 0) ||
    (songs.length > 0 && !contentReady);

  const contentStyle =
    filteredSongs.length === 0 ? styles.emptyContainer : styles.listContent;

  // âœ… Only the Play/Shuffle action row remains in the list header.
  //    Title + search now live in the shared LibraryPageHeaders.
  const listHeader = (
    <View style={styles.headerSection}>
      <View style={styles.actionRow}>
        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.actionBtn}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
          onPress={() => playAll(false)}
        >
          <PlayIcon size={20} color={ACCENT} />
          <Text style={[styles.actionLabel, { color: ACCENT }]}>Play</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>

        <TouchableOpacity
          style={styles.actionBtn}
          activeOpacity={0.75}
          onPress={() => playAll(true)}
        >
          <ShuffleIcon size={20} color={ACCENT} />
          <Text style={[styles.actionLabel, { color: ACCENT }]}>Shuffle</Text>
        </TouchableOpacity>
      </View>
    </View>
  );

  return (
    <View style={[styles.container, { backgroundColor: BG_COLOR }]}>
      {/* ---------- SHARED HEADER (top bar + title + search) ---------- */}
      <LibraryPageHeaders
        colors={theme}
        topInset={insets.top}
        title="Songs"
        onBack={() => nav.goBack()}
        searchValue={search}
        onSearchChange={setSearch}
        searchPlaceholder="Search songs, artists, albums"
        actions={[
          {
            key: "menu",
            icon: LIST_ICON,
            iconStyle: { width: 23, height: 20 },
            onPress: openMenu,
          },
        ]}
      />

      <View
        style={[styles.listWrap, { opacity: showLoading ? 0 : 1 }]}
        pointerEvents={showLoading ? "none" : "auto"}
      >
        <FlatList
          key="songs-list"
          data={filteredSongs}
          ListHeaderComponent={listHeader}
          extraData={reloadKey}
          keyExtractor={(item: any) => item.id}
          keyboardShouldPersistTaps="handled"
          style={{ backgroundColor: BG_COLOR }}
          initialNumToRender={12}
          maxToRenderPerBatch={10}
          windowSize={7}
          alwaysBounceVertical
          showsVerticalScrollIndicator={false}
          bounces
          overScrollMode="always"
          nestedScrollEnabled
          renderItem={renderSong}
          ItemSeparatorComponent={() => (
            <View
              style={{
                height: 0.8,
                backgroundColor: DIVIDER_COLOR,
                marginLeft: 84,
                marginRight: 0,
              }}
            />
          )}
          refreshControl={
            <RefreshControl
              refreshing={refreshing}
              onRefresh={onRefresh}
              tintColor={ACCENT}
              colors={[ACCENT]}
              progressBackgroundColor={SURFACE_COLOR}
              progressViewOffset={Platform.OS === "android" ? 8 : 0}
            />
          }
          contentContainerStyle={contentStyle}
          ListEmptyComponent={
            <View style={styles.empty}>
              {songs.length === 0 ? (
                <>
                  <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: theme.text }]}>
                    No songs found
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.emptyText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
                  >
                    Pull down to scan your device again.
                  </Text>
                </>
              ) : (
                <>
                  <Text style={[styles.emptyTitle, { color: theme.text }]}>
                    No matching songs
                  </Text>
                  <Text
                    style={[styles.emptyText, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
                  >
                    Try another song or artist name.
                  </Text>
                </>
              )}
            </View>
          }
        />
      </View>

      <ScreenLoader visible={showLoading} />

      {/* ---- Header dropdown â€” Settings & Account only (unchanged) ---- */}
      <Modal
        visible={menuVisible}
        transparent
        animationType="none"
        statusBarTranslucent
        onRequestClose={closeMenu}
      >
        <Pressable style={styles.dropdownBackdrop} onPress={closeMenu}>
          <Animated.View
            style={[
              styles.dropdownCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: SURFACE_COLOR,
                borderColor: BORDER_COLOR,
                shadowColor: "#000",
                shadowOpacity: isDark ? 0.45 : 0.18,
                top: insets.top + 44,
                right: 10,
                opacity: dropdownOpacity,
                transform: [{ scale: dropdownScale }],
                transformOrigin: "top right",
              } as any,
            ]}
          >
            <Pressable onPress={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.dropdownRow}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
                onPress={() => {
                  closeMenu();
                  nav.navigate("Settings");
                }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.dropdownItemText, { color: theme.text }]}>
                  Settings
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>

              <View
                style={[styles.menuDivider, { backgroundColor: BORDER_COLOR }]}
              />

              <TouchableOpacity
                style={styles.dropdownRow}
                activeOpacity={0.7}
                onPress={() => {
                  closeMenu();
                  nav.navigate("Account");
                }}
              >
                <Text style={[styles.dropdownItemText, { color: theme.text }]}>
                  Account
                </Text>
              </TouchableOpacity>
            </Pressable>
          </Animated.View>
        </Pressable>
      </Modal>

      {/* ---- Song 3-dot bottom sheet (unchanged) ---- */}
      <Modal
        visible={!!selectedSong}
        transparent
        animationType="none"
        statusBarTranslucent
        onRequestClose={() => closeSheet()}
      >
        <View style={styles.sheetRoot}>
          <Pressable
            style={StyleSheet.absoluteFill}
            onPress={() => closeSheet()}
          >
            <Animated.View
              style={[
                StyleSheet.absoluteFill,
                {
                  backgroundColor: "rgba(0,0,0,0.45)",
                  opacity: Animated.multiply(
                    backdropAnim,
                    sheetDrag.interpolate({
                      inputRange: [0, winH],
                      outputRange: [1, 0],
                      extrapolate: "clamp",
                    }),
                  ),
                },
              ]}
            />
          </Pressable>

          <Animated.View
            {...sheetPan.panHandlers}
            onLayout={onSheetLayout}
            style={[
              styles.sheetCard,
              {
                backgroundColor: "#1c1c1e",
                borderColor: theme.border,
                paddingBottom: insets.bottom + 8,
                transform: [{ translateY: Animated.add(sheetY, sheetDrag) }],
              },
            ]}
          >
            <View style={styles.popupHeader}>
              <ArtworkBox
                uri={
                  selectedSong
                    ? ((selectedSong as any).artwork ??
                      artworkMap[selectedSong.id] ??
                      artKeep.current[selectedSong.id])
                    : undefined
                }
                size={76}
                radius={ART_RADIUS}
                borderWidth={ART_BORDER_WIDTH}
                borderColor={ART_BORDER_COLOR}
                reloadKey={reloadKey}
                fallbackBg={BG_COLOR}
                iconColor={theme.textSecondary}
              />
              <View style={styles.popupHeaderText}>
                <Text
                  style={[styles.popupTitle, { color: theme.text }]}
                  numberOfLines={1}
                >
                  {selectedSong ? selectedSong.title : "Song options"}
                </Text>
                {!!(selectedSong as any)?.album &&
                  (selectedSong as any).album !== UNKNOWN_ALBUM && (
                    <Text
                      style={[styles.popupAlbum, { color: theme.primary }]}
                      numberOfLines={1}
                    >
                      {(selectedSong as any).album}
                    </Text>
                  )}
                <Text
                  style={[styles.popupSubtitle, { color: theme.textSecondary }]}
                  numberOfLines={1}
                >
                  {selectedSong?.artist || UNKNOWN_ARTIST}
                </Text>
              </View>
            </View>

            <View
              style={[styles.popupDivider, { backgroundColor: theme.border }]}
            />

            <View style={styles.popupList}>
              {(() => {
                const song = selectedSong;
                if (!song) return null;
                const isFav = !!favorites[song.id];
                const isPinned = !!pinnedIds[song.id];

                const options: {
                  label: string;
                  icon?: string;
                  customIcon?: (color: string) => React.ReactNode;
                  destructive?: boolean;
                  onPress: () => void;
                }[] = [
                  {
                    label: isPinned ? "Unpin Song" : "Pin Song",
                    customIcon: (c) => <PinSongIcon size={26} color={c} />,
                    onPress: () => togglePin(song),
                  },
                  {
                    label: "Play Next",
                    icon: ICON_NEXT,
                    onPress: () => playNext(song),
                  },
                  {
                    label: "Add to Queue",
                    icon: ICON_QUEUE,
                    onPress: () => addToQueue(song),
                  },
                  {
                    label: isFav ? "Undo Favorite" : "Favorite",
                    customIcon: () => (
                      <Image
                        source={isFav ? ICON_HEART : ICON_HEART_OUTLINE}
                        style={{
                          width: isFav ? 22 : 28,
                          height: isFav ? 22 : 28,
                          resizeMode: "contain",
                        }}
                      />
                    ),
                    onPress: () => {
                      toggleFavorite(song.id);
                    },
                  },
                  {
                    label: "Delete Song",
                    icon: ICON_DELETE,
                    destructive: true,
                    onPress: deleteSong,
                  },
                ];

                return options.map((item) => {
                  const textColor = theme.text;
                  const iconColor = theme.primary;

                  return (
                    <View key={item.label}>
                      <TouchableOpacity
                        style={styles.popupItem}
                        activeOpacity={0.7}
                        disabled={actionBusy}
                        onPress={() => closeSheet(item.onPress)}
                      >
                        {item.customIcon ? (
                          item.customIcon(iconColor)
                        ) : item.label === "Play Next" ? (
                          <Image
                            source={ICON_NEXT}
                            style={{
                              width: 22,
                              height: 22,
                              resizeMode: "contain",
                            }}
                          />
                        ) : (
                          <SheetIcon
                            d={item.icon!}
                            size={22}
                            color={iconColor}
                          />
                        )}

                        <Text
                          style={[styles.popupItemText, { color: textColor }]}
                        >
                          {item.label}
                        </Text>
                      </TouchableOpacity>
                    </View>
                  );
                });
              })()}
            </View>
          </Animated.View>
        </View>
      </Modal>
    </View>
  );
}

// ============================================================
// Styles â€” header styles removed (now in LibraryPageHeaders)
// ============================================================
const styles = StyleSheet.create({
  container: { flex: 1 },

  headerSection: { paddingHorizontal: 12, paddingTop: 6, paddingBottom: 14 },

  actionRow: { flexDirection: "row", marginTop: ACTION_TOP_GAP },
  actionBtn: {
    flex: 1,
    height: ACTION_HEIGHT,
    borderRadius: ACTION_RADIUS,
    backgroundColor: ACTION_BG,
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",
    marginHorizontal: 6,
  },
  actionLabel: {
    marginLeft: 8,
    fontSize: 17,
    fontWeight: "600",
    fontFamily: FONT,
  },

  listWrap: { flex: 1 },

  listContent: { paddingBottom: 100 },
  listRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 4,
  },
  listInfo: { flex: 1, minWidth: 0, marginLeft: 14, marginRight: 8 },
  listTitle: { fontSize: 17, fontWeight: "600", fontFamily: SERIF },
  listArtist: { fontSize: 13.5, fontFamily: SERIF, marginTop: 0 },
  listPin: { marginLeft: 6 },
  listFavStar: { fontSize: 14, marginLeft: 4 },
  listMoreBtn: {
    width: 36,

    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "center",

  playingDotWrap: {

  emptyContainer: { flexGrow: 1 },
  empty: {
    flex: 1,
    minHeight: 300,

  },

    marginTop: 16,
    fontSize: 18,
