    fontFamily: SERIF,
    textAlign: "center",
  },
  emptyText: {
    marginTop: 8,
    fontSize: 13,
    fontFamily: SERIF,
    textAlign: "center",
  },

  // â”€â”€ Header Dropdown â”€â”€
  dropdownBackdrop: { flex: 1, backgroundColor: "transparent" },
  dropdownCard: {
    position: "absolute",
    width: 180,
    borderRadius: 12,
    borderWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
    paddingVertical: 6,
    overflow: "hidden",
    shadowRadius: 16,
    shadowOffset: { width: 0, height: 8 },
    elevation: 18,
  },
  dropdownRow: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    justifyContent: "space-between",
    paddingHorizontal: 16,
    paddingVertical: 12,
  },
  dropdownItemText: {
    fontSize: 15,
    fontFamily: SERIF,
    fontWeight: "500",
  },
  menuDivider: {
    height: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
    marginHorizontal: 8,
  },

  // â”€â”€ 3-Dot Bottom Sheet â”€â”€
  sheetRoot: { flex: 1, justifyContent: "flex-end" },
  sheetCard: {
    borderTopLeftRadius: 0,
    borderTopRightRadius: 0,
    borderWidth: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth,
    borderBottomWidth: 0,
    paddingTop: 20,
  },
  popupHeader: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
    marginBottom: 14,
  },
  popupHeaderText: { flex: 1, marginLeft: 16 },
  popupTitle: { fontSize: 15, fontWeight: "600", fontFamily: SERIF },
  popupAlbum: { fontSize: 15, marginTop: 3, fontFamily: SERIF },
  popupSubtitle: { fontSize: 15, marginTop: 4, fontFamily: SERIF },
  popupDivider: { height: StyleSheet.hairlineWidth },
  popupList: {},
  popupItem: {
    flexDirection: "row",
    alignItems: "center",
    paddingVertical: 15,
    paddingHorizontal: 20,
  },
  popupItemText: { marginLeft: 16, fontSize: 16, fontFamily: SERIF },
});

========== src\scripts\pad-icon.js ==========
// src/scripts/pad-icon.js
// Builds:
//   assets/images/applogo-adaptive.png  -> Android adaptive icon foreground
//   assets/images/applogo-icon.png      -> normal app icon (iOS + legacy Android)
// Source image: assets/images/appicon.png
// Splash image: assets/images/splashscreenicon.png (wired into app.json)
//
// Usage:  node src/scripts/pad-icon.js          (regenerate icons only)
//         node src/scripts/pad-icon.js --bump   (also bump android.versionCode)
//
// GLYPH_* = how much of the canvas the logo's longest side may fill.
//   Adaptive icons get masked (circle/squircle), keep it <= 0.55
//   Normal icon: 0.40 = small, 0.50 = medium, 0.62 = large
const sharp = require('sharp');
const path  = require('path');
const fs    = require('fs');

const ROOT     = path.resolve(__dirname, '..', '..');
const INPUT    = path.join(ROOT, 'assets', 'images', 'appicon.png');
const OUTPUT   = path.join(ROOT, 'assets', 'images', 'applogo-adaptive.png');
const NORMAL   = path.join(ROOT, 'assets', 'images', 'applogo-icon.png');
const SPLASH   = path.join(ROOT, 'assets', 'images', 'splashscreenicon.png');
const APP_JSON = path.join(ROOT, 'app.json');

const CANVAS         = 1024;
const GLYPH_ADAPTIVE = 0.36;
const GLYPH_NORMAL   = 0.36;
const TRIM_THRESHOLD = 100;   // ignore faint gray artifacts near the edges

// Set to e.g. { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 } to force a background colour.
// Leave null to auto-detect from the top-left corner of appicon.png.
const BG_OVERRIDE = null;

// Splash screen background colours (light mode / dark mode)
const SPLASH_BG_LIGHT = '#000000';
const SPLASH_BG_DARK  = '#000000';

const toHex = (r, g, b) =>
  '#' + [r, g, b].map((v) => v.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('').toUpperCase();

async function detectBackground() {
  if (BG_OVERRIDE) return BG_OVERRIDE;
  const { data, info } = await sharp(INPUT)
    .ensureAlpha()
    .raw()
    .toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true });
  const off = (4 * info.width + 4) * 4;       // pixel at (4,4)
  if (data[off + 3] < 128) return { r: 0, g: 0, b: 0 }; // transparent -> black
  return { r: data[off], g: data[off + 1], b: data[off + 2] };
}

async function buildIcon(trimmed, bg, glyphFraction, outFile) {
  const box = Math.round(CANVAS * glyphFraction);
  // fit 'inside' = whole logo visible, never cropped or stretched
  const glyph = await sharp(trimmed)
    .resize(box, box, { fit: 'inside' })
    .toBuffer();

  await sharp({
    create: { width: CANVAS, height: CANVAS, channels: 3, background: bg },
  })
    .composite([{ input: glyph, gravity: 'center' }])
    .png()
    .toFile(outFile);

  return box;
}

(async () => {
  if (!fs.existsSync(INPUT)) throw new Error('Input not found: ' + INPUT);
  if (!fs.existsSync(SPLASH)) throw new Error('Splash image not found: ' + SPLASH);

  if (fs.existsSync(OUTPUT)) fs.copyFileSync(OUTPUT, OUTPUT.replace('.png', '.bak.png'));
  if (fs.existsSync(NORMAL)) fs.copyFileSync(NORMAL, NORMAL.replace('.png', '.bak.png'));

  // 1. background colour
  const bg  = await detectBackground();
  const hex = toHex(bg.r, bg.g, bg.b);

  // 2. flatten onto the background, then trim empty space around the logo
  const flat = await sharp(INPUT)
    .flatten({ background: bg })
    .png()
    .toBuffer();

  let trimmed;
  try {
    trimmed = await sharp(flat)
      .trim({ background: bg, threshold: TRIM_THRESHOLD })
      .png()
      .toBuffer();
  } catch {
    trimmed = flat;
  }

  // 3. build both icons: logo centred on a flat background-colour canvas
  const innerA = await buildIcon(trimmed, bg, GLYPH_ADAPTIVE, OUTPUT);
  const innerN = await buildIcon(trimmed, bg, GLYPH_NORMAL, NORMAL);

  // 4. sync app.json
  const app = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(APP_JSON, 'utf8'));

  app.expo.icon = './assets/images/applogo-icon.png';
  app.expo.ios = app.expo.ios || {};
  app.expo.ios.icon = './assets/images/applogo-icon.png';

  app.expo.android = app.expo.android || {};
  app.expo.android.adaptiveIcon = app.expo.android.adaptiveIcon || {};
  app.expo.android.adaptiveIcon.foregroundImage = './assets/images/applogo-adaptive.png';
  app.expo.android.adaptiveIcon.backgroundColor = hex;

  // splash screen (expo-splash-screen plugin)
  app.expo.plugins = app.expo.plugins || [];
  const splashEntry = app.expo.plugins.find(
    (p) => Array.isArray(p) && p[0] === 'expo-splash-screen'
  );
  const splashCfg = {
    image: './assets/images/splashscreenicon.png',
    imageWidth: 150,
    resizeMode: 'contain',
    backgroundColor: SPLASH_BG_LIGHT,
    dark: { backgroundColor: SPLASH_BG_DARK },
  };
  if (splashEntry) {
    splashEntry[1] = { ...(splashEntry[1] || {}), ...splashCfg };
  } else {
    app.expo.plugins.push(['expo-splash-screen', splashCfg]);
  }

  if (process.argv.includes('--bump')) {
    app.expo.android.versionCode = (app.expo.android.versionCode || 1) + 1;
  }
  fs.writeFileSync(APP_JSON, JSON.stringify(app, null, 2) + '\n');

  console.log('OK  Wrote ' + OUTPUT);
  console.log('    adaptive: canvas ' + CANVAS + 'x' + CANVAS + ', logo max ' + innerA + 'px');
  console.log('OK  Wrote ' + NORMAL);
  console.log('    normal:   canvas ' + CANVAS + 'x' + CANVAS + ', logo max ' + innerN + 'px');
  console.log('    background -> ' + hex);
  console.log('    splash     -> ./assets/images/splashscreenicon.png');
  console.log('    splash bg  -> light ' + SPLASH_BG_LIGHT + ', dark ' + SPLASH_BG_DARK);
  console.log('    android.versionCode -> ' + app.expo.android.versionCode);
})().catch((e) => { console.error('ERROR: ' + e.message); process.exit(1); });

========== src\services\audio\mediaItem.ts ==========
import { Song } from '../../types';

export const toArtworkUri = (p?: string | null): string | undefined => {
  if (!p) return undefined;
  return /^(https?:|file:|content:|data:)/.test(p) ? p : `file://${p}`;
};

export function songToMediaItem(song: Song) {
  const art = (song as any).artwork ?? song.albumArt;
  return {
    mediaId: String(song.id),                 // v5 field (not "id")
    url: song.uri,
    title: song.title || 'Unknown Title',
    artist: song.artist || 'Unknown Artist',
    albumTitle: song.album || 'Unknown Album', // v5 field (not "album")
    artworkUrl: toArtworkUri(art),
    duration: song.duration > 0 ? song.duration : undefined, // seconds
  };
}

========== src\services\audio\trackPlayerSafe.ts ==========
type TrackPlayerApi = Record<string, any>;

let cachedPlayer: TrackPlayerApi | null = null;

function loadPlayerModule(): any {
  const mod = require("@rntp/player");
  return mod?.default ?? mod;
}

export function isTrackPlayerAvailable(): boolean {
  try {
    const player = loadPlayerModule();

    return (
      !!player &&
      typeof player.play === "function" &&
      typeof player.pause === "function"
    );
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("[TrackPlayerSafe] Player unavailable:", error);
    return false;
  }
}

export function getTrackPlayer(): TrackPlayerApi {
  if (cachedPlayer) {
    return cachedPlayer;
  }

  const player = loadPlayerModule();

  if (!player) {
    throw new Error("@rntp/player module is unavailable");
  }

  cachedPlayer = player as TrackPlayerApi;
  return cachedPlayer;
}

export function getTrackPlayerEnums(): {
  Event?: Record<string, any>;
  State?: Record<string, any>;
  RepeatMode?: Record<string, any>;
  Capability?: Record<string, any>;
  PlayerCommand?: Record<string, any>;
} {
  try {
    const mod = require("@rntp/player");

    return {
      Event: mod.Event,
      State: mod.State,
      RepeatMode: mod.RepeatMode,
      Capability: mod.Capability,
      PlayerCommand: mod.PlayerCommand,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn("[TrackPlayerSafe] Failed to load enums:", error);
    return {};
  }
}


========== src\services\audioTags\tagService.ts ==========
// src/services/audioTags/tagService.ts
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";

export interface AudioTags {
  title?: string;
  artist?: string;
  album?: string;
}

// â”€â”€ Module-level tag cache (survives across screens) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const tagCache = new Map<string, AudioTags>();

export const getCachedTags = (songId: string): AudioTags | undefined =>
  tagCache.get(songId);

export const hasCachedTags = (songId: string): boolean =>
  tagCache.has(songId);

export const setCachedTags = (songId: string, tags: AudioTags): void => {
  tagCache.set(songId, tags);
};

// â”€â”€ Module-level artwork cache (for instant cover display) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const artworkCache = new Map<string, string>();

export const setCachedArtwork = (songId: string, uri: string): void => {
  artworkCache.set(songId, uri);
};

export const getCachedArtwork = (songId: string): string | undefined =>
  artworkCache.get(songId);

// â”€â”€ Base64 â†’ bytes â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
const B64_CHARS =
  "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
const B64_LOOKUP = (() => {
  const t = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < B64_CHARS.length; i++) t[B64_CHARS.charCodeAt(i)] = i;
  return t;
})();

const base64ToBytes = (b64: string): Uint8Array => {
  const clean = b64.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9+/]/g, "");
  const out = new Uint8Array(Math.floor((clean.length * 3) / 4));
  let p = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i += 4) {
    const a = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i)] || 0;
    const b = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 1)] || 0;
    const c = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 2)] || 0;
    const d = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 3)] || 0;
    out[p++] = (a << 2) | (b >> 4);
    out[p++] = ((b & 15) << 4) | (c >> 2);
    out[p++] = ((c & 3) << 6) | d;
  }
  return out;
};

const syncSafe = (b: Uint8Array, o: number) =>
  ((b[o] & 0x7f) << 21) |
  ((b[o + 1] & 0x7f) << 14) |
  ((b[o + 2] & 0x7f) << 7) |
  (b[o + 3] & 0x7f);

const decodeUtf16 = (b: Uint8Array, bigEndian: boolean): string => {
  let s = "";
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < b.length; i += 2) {
    const code = bigEndian ? (b[i] << 8) | b[i + 1] : (b[i + 1] << 8) | b[i];
    s += String.fromCharCode(code);
  }
  return s;
};

const decodeUtf8 = (b: Uint8Array): string => {
  try {
    let pct = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
      pct += "%" + (b[i] < 16 ? "0" : "") + b[i].toString(16);
    }
    return decodeURIComponent(pct);
  } catch {
    let s = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
    return s;
  }
};

const decodeId3Text = (b: Uint8Array, enc: number): string => {
  let s = "";
  if (enc === 1) {
    if (b[0] === 0xfe && b[1] === 0xff) s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), true);
    else if (b[0] === 0xff && b[1] === 0xfe)
      s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), false);
    else s = decodeUtf16(b, false);
  } else if (enc === 2) {
    s = decodeUtf16(b, true);
  } else if (enc === 3) {
    s = decodeUtf8(b);
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
  }
  return s
    .split("\u0000")
    .map((x) => x.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
};

const readId3Frame = (b: Uint8Array, frameIds: string[]): string | undefined => {
  if (b.length < 10 || b[0] !== 0x49 || b[1] !== 0x44 || b[2] !== 0x33) {
    return undefined;
  }
  const major = b[3];
  const flags = b[5];
  const tagSize = syncSafe(b, 6);
  let pos = 10;

  if (major >= 3 && (flags & 0x40) !== 0) {
    const ext =
      major === 4
        ? syncSafe(b, pos)
        : (((b[pos] << 24) |
            (b[pos + 1] << 16) |
            (b[pos + 2] << 8) |
            b[pos + 3]) >>>
            0) +
          4;
    pos += ext;
  }

  const end = Math.min(b.length, 10 + tagSize);
  const idLen = major === 2 ? 3 : 4;
  const hdrLen = major === 2 ? 6 : 10;

  while (pos + hdrLen <= end) {
    let id = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < idLen; i++) id += String.fromCharCode(b[pos + i]);
    if (!/^[A-Z0-9]+$/.test(id)) break;

    let size: number;
    if (major === 2) {
      size = (b[pos + 3] << 16) | (b[pos + 4] << 8) | b[pos + 5];
    } else if (major === 4) {
      size = syncSafe(b, pos + 4);
    } else {
      size =
        ((b[pos + 4] << 24) |
          (b[pos + 5] << 16) |
          (b[pos + 6] << 8) |
          b[pos + 7]) >>>
        0;
    }

    const dataStart = pos + hdrLen;
    if (size <= 0 || dataStart + size > b.length) break;

    if (frameIds.includes(id)) {
      const text = decodeId3Text(
        b.subarray(dataStart + 1, dataStart + size),
        b[dataStart],
      );
      if (text && text.trim()) return text.trim();
    }

    pos = dataStart + size;
  }
  return undefined;
};

const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(a.trim());
};

// Filename fallback â€” "Artist - Movie.mp3"
const fromFilename = (
  uri: string,
  kind: "album" | "artist",
): string | undefined => {
  const base = uri.split("/").pop()?.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, "") ?? "";
  const [left, right] = base.split(/\s*-\s*/);
  if (!right) return undefined;
  return kind === "album" ? right.trim() : left.trim();
};

const readTagsFromFile = async (uri: string): Promise<AudioTags> => {
  try {
    const b64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(uri, {
      encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
      position: 0,
      length: 131072,
    });
    const bytes = base64ToBytes(b64);
    const title = readId3Frame(bytes, ["TIT2", "TT2"]);
    const artist = readId3Frame(bytes, ["TPE1", "TP1"]);
    const album = readId3Frame(bytes, ["TALB", "TAL"]);

    return {
      title: title && title.trim() ? title.trim() : undefined,
      artist:
        artist && !isUnknownArtist(artist)
          ? artist
          : fromFilename(uri, "artist"),
      album: album && album.trim() ? album.trim() : fromFilename(uri, "album"),
    };
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
};

/**
 * Read ID3 tags for a song. Cached result is returned instantly.
 * Always stores the result in the module cache.
 */
export const readAndCacheTags = async (
  songId: string,
  uri: string,
): Promise<AudioTags> => {
  const cached = tagCache.get(songId);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const tags = await readTagsFromFile(uri);
  tagCache.set(songId, tags);
  return tags;
};

========== src\services\mediaScanner\audioScanner.ts ==========
import * as MediaLibrary from 'expo-media-library/legacy';
import { Song } from '../../types';

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Duration normalization â€” MediaStore sometimes returns ms instead of seconds
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
const normalizeDuration = (d: unknown): number | undefined => {
  if (typeof d !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(d) || d <= 0) {
    return undefined;
  }

  // Anything above 10 hours in seconds is likely milliseconds
  return d > 36000 ? d / 1000 : d;
};

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Main audio scanner
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
export async function scanAudio(): Promise<Song[]> {
  try {
    // 1. Check audio permission
    let permission = await MediaLibrary.getPermissionsAsync(false, ['audio']);

    if (!permission.granted && permission.canAskAgain) {
      permission = await MediaLibrary.requestPermissionsAsync(false, [
        'audio',
      ]);
    }

    if (!permission.granted) {
      return [];
    }

    // 2. Paginate through audio assets
    const songs: Song[] = [];
    let after: string | undefined;

    while (true) {
      const page = await MediaLibrary.getAssetsAsync({
        mediaType: ['audio'],
        first: 500,
        after,
        sortBy: [['creationTime', false]],
      });

      for (const asset of page.assets as any[]) {
        // 2a. Get duration from the asset
        let duration = normalizeDuration(asset.duration);

        // 2b. Fallback to asset info
        if (!duration) {
          try {
            const info: any = await MediaLibrary.getAssetInfoAsync(asset.id);
            duration = normalizeDuration(info?.duration);
          } catch {
            // Continue gracefully if duration cannot be fetched
          }
        }

        songs.push({
          id: asset.id,
          uri: asset.uri,
          title: (asset.filename || 'Unknown Song').replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ''),
          artist: asset.artist || 'Unknown Artist',
          album: asset.album || 'Unknown Album',
          albumArt: null,
          duration: duration ?? 0,
          fileName: asset.filename || '',
          fileSize: asset.fileSize ?? 0,
          dateAdded: asset.creationTime ?? Date.now(),
          dateModified:
            asset.modificationTime ?? asset.creationTime ?? Date.now(),
          isFavorite: false,
        });
      }

      if (!page.hasNextPage || !page.endCursor) {
        break;
      }

      after = page.endCursor;
    }

    return songs;
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[scanAudio] Failed to scan audio:', error);
    return [];
  }
}

========== src\services\notifications\deleteNotificationService.ts ==========
// src/services/notifications/deleteNotificationService.ts
import * as Notifications from "expo-notifications";
import * as Device from "expo-device"; // âœ… à¤¡à¤¿à¤µà¥à¤¹à¤¾à¤‡à¤¸à¤šà¥‡ à¤¨à¤¾à¤µ à¤®à¤¿à¤³à¤µà¤£à¥à¤¯à¤¾à¤¸à¤¾à¤ à¥€ à¤‡à¤®à¥à¤ªà¥‹à¤°à¥à¤Ÿ à¤•à¥‡à¤²à¥‡
import { Platform } from "react-native";

const CHANNEL_ID = "song-delete-progress-v2";
const CANCEL_CATEGORY = "song-delete-actions";
const WAITING_NOTIF_ID = "song-delete-waiting";
const PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID = "song-delete-progress";

const SUCCESS_PREFIX = "song-delete-success-";
const FAILED_PREFIX = "song-delete-failed-";

const BAR_LENGTH = 29;

const MIN_PROGRESS_INTERVAL_MS = 200;
const MIN_PROGRESS_VISIBLE_MS = 100;
const FILL_STEP_PERCENT = 10;
const FILL_STEP_MIN_MS = 150;
const FILL_STEP_MAX_MS = 300;
const COMPLETE_HOLD_MS = 200;

type Phase = "idle" | "progress" | "success" | "failed" | "cancelled";

type ProgressParams = {
  songTitle: string;
  fileSizeMB: number;
  progressPercent: number;
};

let cancelHandler: (() => boolean | void) | null = null;
let userCancelled = false;
let eventsRegistered = false;
let currentPhase: Phase = "idle";
let hideTimers: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout>[] = [];

let progressFirstShownAt = 0;
let lastProgressAt = 0;
let lastPercent = 0;

let pendingProgress: ProgressParams | null = null;
let flushTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null;
let alertNextProgress = false;

let queue: Promise<void> = Promise.resolve();
const enqueue = (fn: () => Promise<void>): Promise<void> => {
  queue = queue.then(fn).catch((e) => {
    console.warn("[deleteNotif] queue error:", e);
  });
  return queue;
};

const sleep = (ms: number) => new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

Notifications.setNotificationHandler({
  handleNotification: async (notification) => {
    const isProgress = notification.request.identifier === PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID;
    let alert = true;
    if (isProgress) {
      alert = alertNextProgress;
      alertNextProgress = false;
    }
    return {
      shouldShowBanner: true,
      shouldShowList: true,
      shouldPlaySound: alert,
      shouldSetBadge: false,
    };
  },
});

const buildTextBar = (percent: number): string => {
  const p = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, percent));
  const filled = Math.round((p / 100) * BAR_LENGTH);
  return "â”".repeat(filled) + "â”€".repeat(Math.max(0, BAR_LENGTH - filled));
};

const formatSize = (mb: number) => {
  if (mb > 0) return `${mb.toFixed(2)}MB`;
  return "Unknown size";
};

const dismissById = async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
  try { await Notifications.dismissNotificationAsync(id); } catch {}
  try { await Notifications.cancelScheduledNotificationAsync(id); } catch {}
};

const clearHideTimers = (): void => {
  hideTimers.forEach((t) => clearTimeout(t));
  hideTimers = [];
};

const clearFlush = (): void => {
  if (flushTimer) clearTimeout(flushTimer);
  flushTimer = null;
  pendingProgress = null;
};

const clearProgressNow = async (): Promise<void> => {
  await dismissById(WAITING_NOTIF_ID);
  await dismissById(PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID);
  await sleep(150);
  await dismissById(PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID);
  progressFirstShownAt = 0;
};

const postProgressNow = async (params: ProgressParams): Promise<void> => {
  const percent = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, Math.round(params.progressPercent)));
  const sizeStr = formatSize(params.fileSizeMB);

  const content: any = {
    subtitle: `${percent}% of ${sizeStr}`,
    title: params.songTitle,
    body: buildTextBar(percent),
    autoDismiss: false,
    sticky: true,
    categoryIdentifier: CANCEL_CATEGORY,
    data: { kind: "delete-progress" },
  };

  if (Platform.OS === "android") {
    content.channelId = CHANNEL_ID;
  }

  if (!progressFirstShownAt) alertNextProgress = true;

  try {
    await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
      identifier: PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID,
      content,
      trigger: null,
    });
    if (!progressFirstShownAt) progressFirstShownAt = Date.now();
    lastProgressAt = Date.now();
    lastPercent = percent;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[deleteNotif] progress failed:", e);
  }
};

const flushPending = (): void => {
  flushTimer = null;
  const p = pendingProgress;
  pendingProgress = null;
  if (!p) return;
  enqueue(async () => {
    if (currentPhase !== "progress") return;
    await postProgressNow(p);
  });
};

export const setupDeleteNotificationChannel = async (): Promise<void> => {
  if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;
  try {
    await Notifications.setNotificationChannelAsync(CHANNEL_ID, {
      name: "Song Deletion",
      importance: Notifications.AndroidImportance.HIGH,
      sound: null,
      enableVibrate: true,
      vibrationPattern: [0, 120],
    });

    await Notifications.setNotificationCategoryAsync(CANCEL_CATEGORY, [
      {
        identifier: "cancel-delete",
        buttonTitle: "Cancel",
        options: { opensAppToForeground: false, isDestructive: false },
      },
    ]);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("[deleteNotif] channel setup failed:", e);
  }
};

export const registerDeleteNotificationEvents = (): void => {
  if (eventsRegistered) return;
  eventsRegistered = true;

  Notifications.addNotificationResponseReceivedListener(async (response) => {
    if (response.actionIdentifier !== "cancel-delete") return;

    const fn = cancelHandler;
    
    if (typeof fn === "function") {
      const actuallyCancelled = fn();
      if (actuallyCancelled === false) return;
    }

    cancelHandler = null;
    userCancelled = true;
    currentPhase = "cancelled";
    clearHideTimers();
    clearFlush();

    await enqueue(clearProgressNow);
  });
};

export const isDeleteCancelled = (): boolean => userCancelled;

export const showDeleteWaiting = async (params: {
  songTitle: string;
  fileSizeMB: number;
  onCancel: () => boolean | void;
}): Promise<void> => {
  if (currentPhase === "success" || currentPhase === "failed" || currentPhase === "cancelled") return;

  currentPhase = "progress";
  cancelHandler = params.onCancel;
  const sizeStr = formatSize(params.fileSizeMB);

  // âœ… à¤®à¥‹à¤¬à¤¾à¤ˆà¤²à¤šà¥‡ à¤¨à¤¾à¤µ (à¤¡à¤¿à¤µà¥à¤¹à¤¾à¤‡à¤¸ à¤¨à¥‡à¤®) à¤®à¤¿à¤³à¤µà¤£à¥‡
  const deviceName = Device.deviceName || Device.modelName || "device";

  const content: any = {
    subtitle: sizeStr,
    title: params.songTitle,
    body: `Waiting for ${deviceName} to accept...`,
    autoDismiss: false,
    sticky: true,
    categoryIdentifier: CANCEL_CATEGORY,
    data: { kind: "delete-waiting" },
  };
  
  if (Platform.OS === "android") content.channelId = CHANNEL_ID;

  try {
    await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
      identifier: WAITING_NOTIF_ID,
      content,
      trigger: null,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[deleteNotif] waiting failed:", e);
  }
};

export const showDeleteProgress = async (params: {
  songTitle: string;
  fileSizeMB: number;
  progressPercent: number;
  onCancel: () => boolean | void;
}): Promise<void> => {
  if (currentPhase === "success" || currentPhase === "failed" || currentPhase === "cancelled") return;

  currentPhase = "progress";
  cancelHandler = params.onCancel;

  const p: ProgressParams = {
    songTitle: params.songTitle,
    fileSizeMB: params.fileSizeMB,
    progressPercent: params.progressPercent,
  };

  if (!progressFirstShownAt) {
    clearFlush();
    await enqueue(async () => {
      if (currentPhase !== "progress") return;
      await dismissById(WAITING_NOTIF_ID);
      await postProgressNow(p);
    });
    return;
  }

  pendingProgress = p;
  if (flushTimer) return;

  const wait = Math.max(0, MIN_PROGRESS_INTERVAL_MS - (Date.now() - lastProgressAt));
  flushTimer = setTimeout(flushPending, wait);
};

export const showDeleteSuccess = async (params: {
  songTitle: string;
  fileSizeMB: number;
}): Promise<void> => {
  currentPhase = "success";
  cancelHandler = null;
  clearFlush();

  await enqueue(async () => {
    await dismissById(WAITING_NOTIF_ID);

    if (!progressFirstShownAt) {
      await postProgressNow({ ...params, progressPercent: 0 });
      await sleep(300);
    }

    let p = lastPercent;
    const remaining = 100 - p;
    if (remaining > 0) {
      const steps = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(remaining / FILL_STEP_PERCENT));
      const stepMs = Math.min(
        FILL_STEP_MAX_MS,
        Math.max(FILL_STEP_MIN_MS, Math.round(MIN_PROGRESS_VISIBLE_MS / steps)),
      );
      for (let i = 1; i <= steps; i++) {
        p = Math.min(100, lastPercent + FILL_STEP_PERCENT);
        if (i === steps) p = 100;
        await postProgressNow({ ...params, progressPercent: p });
        await sleep(stepMs);
      }
    } else {
      await postProgressNow({ ...params, progressPercent: 100 });
    }

    await sleep(COMPLETE_HOLD_MS);
    await clearProgressNow();

    const sizeStr = formatSize(params.fileSizeMB);

    // âœ… à¤¯à¥‡à¤¥à¥‡ à¤¡à¤¿à¤µà¥à¤¹à¤¾à¤‡à¤¸à¤šà¥‡ à¤¨à¤¾à¤µ à¤®à¤¿à¤³à¤µà¤²à¥‡ à¤†à¤¹à¥‡
    const deviceName = Device.deviceName || Device.modelName || "device";

    const content: any = {
      subtitle: sizeStr,
      title: params.songTitle,
      // âœ… "Delete song from [Device Name]" à¤…à¤¸à¥‡ à¤¦à¤¿à¤¸à¥‡à¤²
      body: `Delete song from ${deviceName}`,
      autoDismiss: false,
      sticky: true,
      data: { kind: "delete-success" },
    };

    if (Platform.OS === "android") content.channelId = CHANNEL_ID;

    const uniqueSuccessId = `${SUCCESS_PREFIX}${Date.now()}`;

    try {
      await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
        identifier: uniqueSuccessId,
        content,
        trigger: null,
      });
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn("[deleteNotif] success failed:", e);
    }
  });
};

export const showDeleteFailed = async (params: {
  songTitle: string;
  fileSizeMB: number;
  reason?: string;
}): Promise<void> => {
  clearFlush();

  if (userCancelled || currentPhase === "cancelled") {
    await enqueue(clearProgressNow);
    return;
  }

  currentPhase = "failed";
  cancelHandler = null;

  await enqueue(async () => {
    await clearProgressNow();

    const sizeStr = formatSize(params.fileSizeMB);
    const content: any = {
      subtitle: `Delete failed â€¢ ${sizeStr}`,
      title: params.songTitle,
      body: buildTextBar(100),
      autoDismiss: false,
      sticky: true,
      data: { kind: "delete-failed", reason: params.reason ?? "" },
    };
    if (Platform.OS === "android") content.channelId = CHANNEL_ID;

    const uniqueFailedId = `${FAILED_PREFIX}${Date.now()}`;

    try {
      await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
        identifier: uniqueFailedId,
        content,
        trigger: null,
      });
    } catch (e) {}
  });
};

export const dismissDeleteNotification = async (): Promise<void> => {
  clearHideTimers();
  clearFlush();
  currentPhase = "idle";
  cancelHandler = null;
  userCancelled = false;
  progressFirstShownAt = 0;
  lastProgressAt = 0;
  lastPercent = 0;
  alertNextProgress = false;

  await enqueue(async () => {
    await dismissById(WAITING_NOTIF_ID);
    await dismissById(PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID);
  });
};

========== src\services\notifications\deleteNotificationServicebackup.ts ==========
// src/services/notifications/deleteNotificationService.ts
import * as Notifications from "expo-notifications";
import { Platform } from "react-native";

const CHANNEL_ID = "song-delete-progress";
const CANCEL_CATEGORY = "song-delete-actions";
const PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID = "song-delete-progress";
const SUCCESS_NOTIF_ID = "song-delete-success";
const FAILED_NOTIF_ID = "song-delete-failed";

// Text bar length: longer = wider bar across the notification
const BAR_LENGTH = 29;

type Phase = "idle" | "progress" | "success" | "failed" | "cancelled";

let cancelHandler: (() => void) | null = null;
let userCancelled = false;
let eventsRegistered = false;
let currentPhase: Phase = "idle";
let lastSong: { songTitle: string; fileSizeMB: number } | null = null;

// Foreground presentation â€” silent updates for progress, sound for others.
Notifications.setNotificationHandler({
  handleNotification: async (notification) => {
    const isProgress = notification.request.identifier === PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID;
    return {
      shouldShowBanner: true,
      shouldShowList: true,
      shouldPlaySound: !isProgress,
      shouldSetBadge: false,
    };
  },
});

// Thin single-line text bar (low height). 100% => completely full.
// Only the text bar is used (no native progress bar) so height stays normal.
const buildTextBar = (percent: number): string => {
  const p = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, percent));
  const filled = Math.round((p / 100) * BAR_LENGTH);
  return "â”".repeat(filled) + "â”€".repeat(Math.max(0, BAR_LENGTH - filled));
};

const clearProgress = async (): Promise<void> => {
  try {
    await Notifications.dismissNotificationAsync(PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID);
  } catch {}
  try {
    await Notifications.cancelScheduledNotificationAsync(PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID);
  } catch {}
  // Some OEMs lag the first dismiss call
  setTimeout(() => {
    Notifications.dismissNotificationAsync(PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID).catch(() => {});
  }, 150);
};

export const setupDeleteNotificationChannel = async (): Promise<void> => {
  if (Platform.OS !== "android") return;
  try {
    await Notifications.setNotificationChannelAsync(CHANNEL_ID, {
      name: "Song Deletion",
      importance: Notifications.AndroidImportance.LOW,
      sound: null,
    });

    await Notifications.setNotificationCategoryAsync(CANCEL_CATEGORY, [
      {
        identifier: "cancel-delete",
        buttonTitle: "Cancel",
        options: {
          // Don't pull the app to the front just to cancel
          opensAppToForeground: false,
          isDestructive: false,
        },
      },
    ]);

    console.log("[deleteNotif] channel + category ready");
  } catch (e) {
    console.error("[deleteNotif] channel setup failed:", e);
  }
};

export const registerDeleteNotificationEvents = (): void => {
  if (eventsRegistered) return;
  eventsRegistered = true;

  Notifications.addNotificationResponseReceivedListener(async (response) => {
    if (response.actionIdentifier !== "cancel-delete") return;

    const fn = cancelHandler;
    cancelHandler = null;

    // Mark as user-cancelled BEFORE calling the handler so the delete
    // flow can check isDeleteCancelled() and skip error messages.
    userCancelled = true;
    currentPhase = "cancelled";

    if (typeof fn === "function") {
      try {
        fn();
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn("[deleteNotif] cancel handler error:", e);
      }
    }

    // Cancel pressed => just remove the notification. No message shown.
    await clearProgress();
  });
};

// Use this in the delete flow's catch block: if true, the failure was
// caused by the user pressing Cancel, so don't log or show any error.
export const isDeleteCancelled = (): boolean => userCancelled;

export const showDeleteProgress = async (params: {
  songTitle: string;
  fileSizeMB: number;
  progressPercent: number;
  onCancel: () => void;
}): Promise<void> => {
  // Ignore late progress updates after a final state was shown
  if (
    currentPhase === "success" ||
    currentPhase === "failed" ||
    currentPhase === "cancelled"
  )
    return;

  currentPhase = "progress";
  cancelHandler = params.onCancel;
  lastSong = { songTitle: params.songTitle, fileSizeMB: params.fileSizeMB };

  const percent = Math.max(
    0,
    Math.min(100, Math.round(params.progressPercent))
  );
  const sizeStr = `${params.fileSizeMB.toFixed(2)}MB`;

  const content: any = {
    // Header row on Android: [icon] Apple Music â€¢ 75% of 7.24MB â€¢ Just now
    subtitle: `${percent}% of ${sizeStr}`,
    // Expanded content
    title: params.songTitle,
    body: buildTextBar(percent),
    autoDismiss: false,
    categoryIdentifier: CANCEL_CATEGORY,
    data: { kind: "delete-progress" },
  };

  if (Platform.OS === "android") {
    content.channelId = CHANNEL_ID;
    // Native progress bar intentionally NOT set: it adds extra height.
  }

  try {
    await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
      identifier: PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID,
      content,
      trigger: null,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[deleteNotif] progress failed:", e);
  }
};

export const showDeleteSuccess = async (params: {
  songTitle: string;
  fileSizeMB: number;
}): Promise<void> => {
  currentPhase = "success";
  cancelHandler = null;

  await clearProgress();

  const sizeStr = `${params.fileSizeMB.toFixed(2)}MB`;

  const content: any = {
    subtitle: `100% of ${sizeStr}`,
    title: params.songTitle,
    body: "Delete successfully",
    autoDismiss: true,
    data: { kind: "delete-success" },
  };

  if (Platform.OS === "android") content.channelId = CHANNEL_ID;

  try {
    await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
      identifier: SUCCESS_NOTIF_ID,
      content,
      trigger: null,
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[deleteNotif] success failed:", e);
  }
};

// Shown when delete is cancelled or throws (e.g. no write permission).
// Bar is shown completely full on a single compact line.
export const showDeleteFailed = async (params: {
  songTitle: string;
  fileSizeMB: number;
  reason?: string;
}): Promise<void> => {
  // User pressed Cancel => never show a failed message
  if (userCancelled || currentPhase === "cancelled") {
    await clearProgress();
    return;
  }

  currentPhase = "failed";
  cancelHandler = null;

  await clearProgress();

  const sizeStr = `${params.fileSizeMB.toFixed(2)}MB`;

  const content: any = {
    subtitle: `Delete failed â€¢ ${sizeStr}`,
    title: params.songTitle,
    body: buildTextBar(100),
    autoDismiss: true,
    data: { kind: "delete-failed", reason: params.reason ?? "" },
  };

  if (Platform.OS === "android") content.channelId = CHANNEL_ID;

  try {
    await Notifications.scheduleNotificationAsync({
      identifier: FAILED_NOTIF_ID,
      content,
      trigger: null,
    });
    console.log("[deleteNotif] failed shown");
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn("[deleteNotif] failed-notification error:", e);
  }
};

// Call this at the START of every new deletion to reset state.
export const dismissDeleteNotification = async (): Promise<void> => {
  currentPhase = "idle";
  cancelHandler = null;
  userCancelled = false;
  lastSong = null;
  try {
    await Notifications.dismissNotificationAsync(PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID);
  } catch {}
  try {
    await Notifications.cancelScheduledNotificationAsync(PROGRESS_NOTIF_ID);
  } catch {}
  try {
    await Notifications.dismissNotificationAsync(FAILED_NOTIF_ID);
  } catch {}
};

========== src\services\storage\storageService.ts ==========
import AsyncStorage from '@react-native-async-storage/async-storage';

const NS = '@lmvp:';

export const storage = {
  async get<T>(key: string, def: T): Promise<T> {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(NS + key);
      if (raw == null) return def;
      return JSON.parse(raw) as T;
    } catch {
      return def;
    }
  },
  async set<T>(key: string, value: T): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(NS + key, JSON.stringify(value));
    } catch {}
  },
  async remove(key: string): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await AsyncStorage.removeItem(NS + key);
    } catch {}
  },
};

export const KEYS = {
  THEME: 'theme',
  AUTO_RESCAN: 'autoRescan',
  FAVORITES_SONG: 'favSongs',
  RECENT_SONGS: 'recentSongs',
  LAST_PLAYED: 'lastPlayed',
  POSITION: 'lastPosition',
  QUEUE: 'queue',
  NOTIFICATIONS: 'notifEnabled',
  DYNAMIC_BG: 'dynamicBg',
  DEFAULT_SPEED: 'defaultSpeed',
  SHUFFLE: 'shuffle',
  REPEAT: 'repeat',
  CONTINUE: 'continuePlayback',
  KEEP_AWAKE: 'keepAwake',
  SONG_IDS: 'songIds',
};

========== src\store\artistStore.ts ==========
// src/store/artistStore.ts
import { create } from "zustand";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";

const STORAGE_KEY = "@favourite_artists_v1";

type ArtistState = {
  favourites: Record<string, boolean>;
  hydrated: boolean;
  hydrate: () => Promise<void>;
  toggleFavourite: (name: string) => void;
  isFavourite: (name: string) => boolean;
};

export const useArtistStore = create<ArtistState>((set, get) => ({
  favourites: {},
  hydrated: false,

  hydrate: async () => {
    if (get().hydrated) return;
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        if (parsed && typeof parsed === "object") {
          set({ favourites: parsed, hydrated: true });
          return;
        }
      }
    } catch {}
    set({ hydrated: true });
  },

  toggleFavourite: (name: string) => {
    const key = (name ?? "").trim();
    if (!key) return;
    const next = { ...get().favourites };
    if (next[key]) delete next[key];
    else next[key] = true;
    set({ favourites: next });
    AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next)).catch(() => {});
  },

  isFavourite: (name: string) => !!get().favourites[(name ?? "").trim()],
}));

========== src\store\extraSettingsStore.ts ==========
// src/store/extraSettingsStore.ts
// Persisted store for the settings that previously lived only in local state.
// Requires: zustand, @react-native-async-storage/async-storage
import { create } from "zustand";
import { persist, createJSONStorage } from "zustand/middleware";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";

export type CellularKey =
  | "high_efficiency"
  | "high_quality"
  | "lossless"
  | "hi_res_lossless";

export type CrossfadeMode = "automatic" | "manual" | "off";

type Data = {
  addPlaylistSongs: boolean;
  addFavouriteSongs: boolean;
  losslessAudio: boolean;
  cellularStreaming: CellularKey;
  crossfadeMode: CrossfadeMode;
  crossfadeSeconds: number;
  soundCheck: boolean;
};

type Actions = {
  setAddPlaylistSongs: (v: boolean) => void;
  setAddFavouriteSongs: (v: boolean) => void;
  setLosslessAudio: (v: boolean) => void;
  setCellularStreaming: (v: CellularKey) => void;
  setCrossfadeMode: (v: CrossfadeMode) => void;
  setCrossfadeSeconds: (v: number) => void;
  setSoundCheck: (v: boolean) => void;
  reset: () => void;
};

export const EXTRA_DEFAULTS: Data = {
  addPlaylistSongs: true,
  addFavouriteSongs: true,
  losslessAudio: false,
  cellularStreaming: "high_quality",
  crossfadeMode: "automatic",
  crossfadeSeconds: 6,
  soundCheck: false,
};

export const useExtraSettings = create<Data & Actions>()(
  persist(
    (set) => ({
      ...EXTRA_DEFAULTS,
      setAddPlaylistSongs: (addPlaylistSongs) => set({ addPlaylistSongs }),
      setAddFavouriteSongs: (addFavouriteSongs) => set({ addFavouriteSongs }),
      setLosslessAudio: (losslessAudio) => set({ losslessAudio }),
      setCellularStreaming: (cellularStreaming) => set({ cellularStreaming }),
      setCrossfadeMode: (crossfadeMode) => set({ crossfadeMode }),
      setCrossfadeSeconds: (crossfadeSeconds) => set({ crossfadeSeconds }),
      setSoundCheck: (soundCheck) => set({ soundCheck }),
      reset: () => set({ ...EXTRA_DEFAULTS }),
    }),
    {
      name: "apple-extra-settings",
      storage: createJSONStorage(() => AsyncStorage),
    },
  ),
);

========== src\store\mediaStore.ts ==========
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { Song, SortKey } from '../types';
import { storage, KEYS } from '../services/storage/storageService';

interface MediaState {
  songs: Song[];
  sort: SortKey;
  favoritesSong: Record<string, boolean>;
  recentSongIds: string[];

  setSongs: (songs: Song[]) => void;
  setSort: (s: SortKey) => void;
  toggleSongFavorite: (id: string) => void;
  addRecentSong: (id: string) => void;
  hydrateFavorites: () => Promise<void>;
}

export const useMediaStore = create<MediaState>((set, get) => ({
  songs: [],
  sort: 'recent',
  favoritesSong: {},
  recentSongIds: [],

  setSongs: (songs) => set({ songs }),
  setSort: (sort) => set({ sort }),

  toggleSongFavorite: (id) => {
    const cur = get().favoritesSong;
    const next = { ...cur, [id]: !cur[id] };
    set({ favoritesSong: next });
    storage.set(KEYS.FAVORITES_SONG, next);
  },
  
  addRecentSong: (id) => {
    const cur = get().recentSongIds.filter((x) => x !== id);
    const next = [id, ...cur].slice(0, 100);
    set({ recentSongIds: next });
    storage.set(KEYS.RECENT_SONGS, next);
  },

  async hydrateFavorites() {
    const [fs, rec] = await Promise.all([
      storage.get<Record<string, boolean>>(KEYS.FAVORITES_SONG, {}),
      storage.get<string[]>(KEYS.RECENT_SONGS, []),
    ]);
    set({ favoritesSong: fs, recentSongIds: rec });
  },
}));

========== src\store\playerStore.ts ==========
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { Song, RepeatMode } from '../types';

interface PlayerState {
  queue: Song[];
  currentIndex: number;
  currentTrack: Song | null;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  position: number;
  duration: number;
  shuffle: boolean;
  repeat: RepeatMode;

  setQueue: (q: Song[], index?: number) => void;
  setCurrentIndex: (i: number) => void;
  setCurrentTrack: (track: Song | null) => void;
  setPlaying: (v: boolean) => void;
  setProgress: (pos: number, dur: number) => void;
  setShuffle: (v: boolean) => void;
  setRepeat: (r: RepeatMode) => void;
  addToQueue: (song: Song) => void;
  playNext: (song: Song) => void;
  removeFromQueue: (index: number) => void;
  clearQueue: () => void;
  moveInQueue: (from: number, to: number) => void;
}

export const usePlayerStore = create<PlayerState>((set, get) => ({
  queue: [],
  currentIndex: -1,
  currentTrack: null,
  isPlaying: false,
  position: 0,
  duration: 0,
  shuffle: false,
  repeat: 'off',

  setQueue: (queue, index = 0) => {
    const safeIndex =
      queue.length === 0
        ? -1
        : Math.max(0, Math.min(index, queue.length - 1));
    set({
      queue,
      currentIndex: safeIndex,
      currentTrack: queue[safeIndex] ?? null,
    });
  },

  setCurrentIndex: (i) => {
    const { queue, currentIndex } = get();
    if (i < 0 || i >= queue.length) return;
    // No-op when nothing changes: avoids extra renders / duplicate detects.
    if (i === currentIndex) return;
    set({
      currentIndex: i,
      currentTrack: queue[i] ?? null,
    });
  },

  setCurrentTrack: (track) => set({ currentTrack: track }),

  setPlaying: (isPlaying) => {
    if (get().isPlaying === isPlaying) return;
    set({ isPlaying });
  },

  setProgress: (position, duration) => {
    const s = get();
    if (s.position === position && s.duration === duration) return;
    set({ position, duration });
  },

  setShuffle: (shuffle) => set({ shuffle }),
  setRepeat: (repeat) => set({ repeat }),

  addToQueue: (song) => set((s) => ({ queue: [...s.queue, song] })),

  playNext: (song) =>
    set((s) => {
      const q = [...s.queue];
      const insertAt = Math.max(0, s.currentIndex + 1);
      q.splice(insertAt, 0, song);
      return { queue: q };
    }),

  removeFromQueue: (index) =>
    set((s) => {
      const q = s.queue.filter((_, i) => i !== index);
      let ci = s.currentIndex;
      if (index < ci) ci--;
      if (index === ci && q.length === 0) ci = -1;
      if (ci >= q.length) ci = q.length - 1;
      return {
        queue: q,
        currentIndex: ci,
        currentTrack: q[ci] ?? null,
      };
    }),

  clearQueue: () =>
    set({ queue: [], currentIndex: -1, currentTrack: null }),

  moveInQueue: (from, to) =>
    set((s) => {
      const q = [...s.queue];
      if (from < 0 || to < 0 || from >= q.length || to >= q.length)
        return {};
      const [item] = q.splice(from, 1);
      q.splice(to, 0, item);
      let ci = s.currentIndex;
      if (ci === from) ci = to;
      else if (from < ci && to >= ci) ci--;
      else if (from > ci && to <= ci) ci++;
      return {
        queue: q,
        currentIndex: ci,
        currentTrack: q[ci] ?? null,
      };
    }),
}));

========== src\store\playHistoryStore.ts ==========
import { create } from "zustand";
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";
import { usePlayerStore } from "./playerStore";

const FILE = (FileSystem.documentDirectory ?? "") + "play_history.json";
const MAX_RECENT = 50;
const COUNT_AFTER_MS = 5000; // a song counts as "played" after 5 seconds

// k: "s" = song, "v" = music video
export type RecentItem = { k: "s" | "v"; id: string; t: number };

type HistoryState = {
  counts: Record<string, number>; // songId â†’ play count
  recentItems: RecentItem[]; // songs + videos, most recent first (unique)
  hydrated: boolean;
  recordPlay: (id: string) => void; // song
  recordVideoPlay: (id: string) => void; // music video
};

const pushRecent = (list: RecentItem[], k: "s" | "v", id: string) =>
  [{ k, id, t: Date.now() }, ...list.filter((x) => !(x.k === k && x.id === id))].slice(
    0,
    MAX_RECENT,
  );

export const usePlayHistoryStore = create<HistoryState>((set) => ({
  counts: {},
  recentItems: [],
  hydrated: false,
  recordPlay: (id) =>
    set((s) => ({
      counts: { ...s.counts, [id]: (s.counts[id] ?? 0) + 1 },
      recentItems: pushRecent(s.recentItems, "s", id),
    })),
  recordVideoPlay: (id) =>
    set((s) => ({ recentItems: pushRecent(s.recentItems, "v", id) })),
}));

// â”€â”€ Load saved history once â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
(async () => {
  try {
    const info = await FileSystem.getInfoAsync(FILE);
    if (info.exists) {
      const saved = JSON.parse(await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(FILE));

      // new format
      let savedItems: RecentItem[] = Array.isArray(saved.recentItems)
        ? saved.recentItems
        : [];
      // old format (songs only): convert, keeping the order
      if (savedItems.length === 0 && Array.isArray(saved.recent)) {
        savedItems = (saved.recent as string[]).map((id, i) => ({
          k: "s" as const,
          id,
          t: -i,
        }));
      }

      usePlayHistoryStore.setState((cur) => {
        const counts: Record<string, number> = { ...(saved.counts ?? {}) };
        Object.entries(cur.counts).forEach(([id, n]) => {
          counts[id] = (counts[id] ?? 0) + n;
        });
        // merge, in case something was played before loading finished
        const seen = new Set(cur.recentItems.map((x) => x.k + ":" + x.id));
        const recentItems = [
          ...cur.recentItems,
          ...savedItems.filter((x) => !seen.has(x.k + ":" + x.id)),
        ]
          .sort((a, b) => b.t - a.t)
          .slice(0, MAX_RECENT);
        return { counts, recentItems };
      });
    }
  } catch {
    // start fresh
  } finally {
    usePlayHistoryStore.setState({ hydrated: true });
  }
})();

// â”€â”€ Save (debounced) â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
let saveTimer: any = null;
usePlayHistoryStore.subscribe((s) => {
  if (!s.hydrated) return;
  if (saveTimer) clearTimeout(saveTimer);
  saveTimer = setTimeout(() => {
    FileSystem.writeAsStringAsync(
      FILE,
      JSON.stringify({ counts: s.counts, recentItems: s.recentItems }),
    ).catch(() => {});
  }, 500);
});

// â”€â”€ Watch the player: count a song after 5s of real playback â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€â”€
let trackId: string | null = null;
let counted = false;
let playTimer: any = null;

const clearPlayTimer = () => {
  if (playTimer) {
    clearTimeout(playTimer);
    playTimer = null;
  }
};

usePlayerStore.subscribe((s: any) => {
  const id: string | null = s.currentTrack?.id ?? null;
  const playing = !!s.isPlaying;

  if (id !== trackId) {
    clearPlayTimer();
    trackId = id;
    counted = false;
  }

  if (!id || id.startsWith("dummy-") || counted) return;

  if (!playing) {
    clearPlayTimer();
    return;
  }

  if (!playTimer) {
    playTimer = setTimeout(() => {
      playTimer = null;
      const st: any = usePlayerStore.getState();
      if (st.currentTrack?.id === id && st.isPlaying && !counted) {
        counted = true;
        usePlayHistoryStore.getState().recordPlay(id);
      }
    }, COUNT_AFTER_MS);
  }
});

========== src\store\settingsStore.ts ==========
import { create } from 'zustand';
import { storage, KEYS } from '../services/storage/storageService';
import { ThemeMode, RepeatMode } from '../types';

interface SettingsState {
  hydrated: boolean;
  theme: ThemeMode;
  autoRescan: boolean;
  dynamicBg: boolean;
  notifications: boolean;
  defaultSpeed: number;
  shuffle: boolean;
  repeat: RepeatMode;
  continuePlayback: boolean;
  keepAwake: boolean;

  hydrate: () => Promise<void>;
  setTheme: (t: ThemeMode) => void;
  setAutoRescan: (v: boolean) => void;
  setDynamicBg: (v: boolean) => void;
  setNotifications: (v: boolean) => void;
  setDefaultSpeed: (v: number) => void;
  setShuffle: (v: boolean) => void;
  setRepeat: (v: RepeatMode) => void;
  setContinuePlayback: (v: boolean) => void;
  setKeepAwake: (v: boolean) => void;
}

export const useSettingsStore = create<SettingsState>((set) => ({
  hydrated: false,
  theme: 'dark',
  autoRescan: true,
  dynamicBg: true,
  notifications: true,
  defaultSpeed: 1,
  shuffle: false,
  repeat: 'off',
  continuePlayback: true,
  keepAwake: false,

  async hydrate() {
    const [
      theme, autoRescan, dynamicBg, notifications, defaultSpeed,
      shuffle, repeat, continuePlayback, keepAwake,
    ] = await Promise.all([
      storage.get<ThemeMode>(KEYS.THEME, 'dark'),
      storage.get<boolean>(KEYS.AUTO_RESCAN, true),
      storage.get<boolean>(KEYS.DYNAMIC_BG, true),
      storage.get<boolean>(KEYS.NOTIFICATIONS, true),
      storage.get<number>(KEYS.DEFAULT_SPEED, 1),
      storage.get<boolean>(KEYS.SHUFFLE, false),
      storage.get<RepeatMode>(KEYS.REPEAT, 'off'),
      storage.get<boolean>(KEYS.CONTINUE, true),
      storage.get<boolean>(KEYS.KEEP_AWAKE, false),
    ]);
    set({
      theme, autoRescan, dynamicBg, notifications, defaultSpeed,
      shuffle, repeat, continuePlayback, keepAwake,
      hydrated: true,
    });
  },

  setTheme: (t) => { set({ theme: t }); storage.set(KEYS.THEME, t); },
  setAutoRescan: (v) => { set({ autoRescan: v }); storage.set(KEYS.AUTO_RESCAN, v); },
  setDynamicBg: (v) => { set({ dynamicBg: v }); storage.set(KEYS.DYNAMIC_BG, v); },
  setNotifications: (v) => { set({ notifications: v }); storage.set(KEYS.NOTIFICATIONS, v); },
  setDefaultSpeed: (v) => { set({ defaultSpeed: v }); storage.set(KEYS.DEFAULT_SPEED, v); },
  setShuffle: (v) => { set({ shuffle: v }); storage.set(KEYS.SHUFFLE, v); },
  setRepeat: (v) => { set({ repeat: v }); storage.set(KEYS.REPEAT, v); },
  setContinuePlayback: (v) => { set({ continuePlayback: v }); storage.set(KEYS.CONTINUE, v); },
  setKeepAwake: (v) => { set({ keepAwake: v }); storage.set(KEYS.KEEP_AWAKE, v); },
}));

========== src\store\userStore.ts ==========
// src/store/userStore.ts
import { create } from "zustand";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";

const STORAGE_USER = "@user_profile";

type UserStore = {
  name: string;
  loaded: boolean;
  load: () => Promise<void>;
  setName: (name: string) => Promise<void>;
};

export const useUserStore = create<UserStore>((set) => ({
  name: "",
  loaded: false,
  load: async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(STORAGE_USER);
      if (raw) {
        const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
        set({
          name: typeof parsed?.name === "string" ? parsed.name : "",
          loaded: true,
        });
      } else {
        set({ loaded: true });
      }
    } catch {
      set({ loaded: true });
    }
  },
  setName: async (name: string) => {
    try {
      await AsyncStorage.setItem(STORAGE_USER, JSON.stringify({ name }));
      set({ name });
    } catch {}
  },
}));

========== src\store\videoPlayerBridge.ts ==========
// src/store/videoPlayerBridge.ts
import { create } from "zustand";

export type VideoBridgeState = {
  player: any | null;
  isPlaying: boolean;
  position: number;
  duration: number;

  muted: boolean;
  volume: number;
  _lastVolume: number;

  togglePlay: () => void;
  seekTo: (sec: number) => void;
  skip: (delta: number) => void;
  toggleMute: () => void;

  setVolume: (v: number) => void;
  setMuted: (m: boolean) => void;
  syncFromPlayer: (player: any) => void;

  _setPlayer: (p: any) => void;
  _setPlaying: (v: boolean) => void;
  _setPosition: (n: number) => void;
  _setDuration: (n: number) => void;
  _register: (c: {
    togglePlay?: () => void;
    seekTo?: (sec: number) => void;
    skip?: (d: number) => void;
    toggleMute?: () => void;
  }) => void;
};

export const useVideoBridge = create<VideoBridgeState>()((set, get) => ({
  player: null,
  isPlaying: false,
  position: 0,
  duration: 0,
  muted: false,
  volume: 1,
  _lastVolume: 1,

  togglePlay: () => {},
  seekTo: () => {},
  skip: () => {},

  toggleMute: () => {
    const { player, muted, volume, _lastVolume } = get();
    const nextMuted = !muted;
    const nextVolume = nextMuted ? 0 : _lastVolume > 0 ? _lastVolume : 1;

    // 1. Flip UI state FIRST Ã¢â‚¬â€ icon swaps on the next render.
    set({
      muted: nextMuted,
      volume: nextVolume,
      _lastVolume: nextMuted ? volume || _lastVolume || 1 : nextVolume,
    });

    // 2. Then apply to the native video player (best-effort).
    if (player) {
      try {
        player.muted = nextMuted;
        if (typeof player.volume === "number") {
          player.volume = nextVolume;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[videoBridge] player mute failed:", err);
      }
    }
  },

  setVolume: (v) => {
    const clamped = Math.max(0, Math.min(1, v));
    const { player } = get();

    if (player) {
      try {
        player.volume = clamped;
        player.muted = clamped === 0;
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[videoBridge] setVolume failed:", err);
      }
    }

    set((s) => ({
      volume: clamped,
      muted: clamped === 0,
      _lastVolume: clamped > 0 ? clamped : s._lastVolume,
    }));
  },

  setMuted: (m) => {
    const { player, volume, _lastVolume } = get();
    const nextVolume = m ? 0 : _lastVolume > 0 ? _lastVolume : 1;

    if (player) {
      try {
        player.muted = m;
        if (typeof player.volume === "number") player.volume = nextVolume;
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn("[videoBridge] setMuted failed:", err);
      }
    }

    set({
      muted: m,
      volume: nextVolume,
      _lastVolume: m ? volume || _lastVolume || 1 : nextVolume,
    });
  },

  syncFromPlayer: (player) => {
    if (!player) return;
    try {
      const muted = !!player.muted;
      const volume =
        typeof player.volume === "number" ? player.volume : muted ? 0 : 1;
      set((s) => ({
        muted,
        volume,
        _lastVolume: volume > 0 ? volume : s._lastVolume,
      }));
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn("[videoBridge] syncFromPlayer failed:", err);
    }
  },

  _setPlayer: (p) => {
    if (get().player === p) return;
    set({ player: p });
  },
  _setPlaying: (v) => {
    if (get().isPlaying === v) return;
    set({ isPlaying: v });
  },
  _setPosition: (n) => {
    if (get().position === n) return;
    set({ position: n });
  },
  _setDuration: (n) => {
    if (get().duration === n) return;
    set({ duration: n });
  },
  _register: (c) => {
    const cur = get();
    const next = {
      togglePlay: c.togglePlay ?? cur.togglePlay,
      seekTo: c.seekTo ?? cur.seekTo,
      skip: c.skip ?? cur.skip,
      toggleMute: c.toggleMute ?? cur.toggleMute,
    };
    if (
      next.togglePlay === cur.togglePlay &&
      next.seekTo === cur.seekTo &&
      next.skip === cur.skip &&
      next.toggleMute === cur.toggleMute
    )
      return;
    set(next);
  },
}));


========== src\store\videoStore.ts ==========
// src/store/videoStore.ts
import { create } from "zustand";
import AsyncStorage from "@react-native-async-storage/async-storage";
import type { MusicVideo } from "../data/musicVideos";

const FAV_KEY = "@music_video_favs";

type VideoState = {
  queue: MusicVideo[];
  index: number;
  current: MusicVideo | null;
  /** bumps on every (re)start so the host reloads even for the same video */
  playToken: number;
  expanded: boolean;
  favourites: Record<string, boolean>;

  playQueue: (list: MusicVideo[], startIndex?: number) => void;
  next: () => void;
  prev: () => void;
  setExpanded: (v: boolean) => void;
  close: () => void;
  toggleFav: (id: string) => void;
  loadFavs: () => Promise<void>;
};

export const useVideoStore = create<VideoState>((set, get) => ({
  queue: [],
  index: 0,
  current: null,
  playToken: 0,
  expanded: false,
  favourites: {},

  playQueue: (list, startIndex = 0) => {
    if (!list.length) return;
    const i = Math.max(0, Math.min(list.length - 1, startIndex));
    set((s) => ({
      queue: list,
      index: i,
      current: list[i],
      playToken: s.playToken + 1,
    }));
  },

  next: () => {
    const { queue, index } = get();
    if (!queue.length) return;
    const i = (index + 1) % queue.length;
    set((s) => ({
      index: i,
      current: queue[i],
      playToken: s.playToken + 1,
    }));
  },

  prev: () => {
    const { queue, index } = get();
    if (!queue.length) return;
    const i = (index - 1 + queue.length) % queue.length;
    set((s) => ({
      index: i,
      current: queue[i],
      playToken: s.playToken + 1,
    }));
  },

  setExpanded: (v) => set({ expanded: v }),

  close: () => set({ current: null, queue: [], index: 0, expanded: false }),

  toggleFav: (id) => {
    const favourites = { ...get().favourites };
    if (favourites[id]) delete favourites[id];
    else favourites[id] = true;
    set({ favourites });
    AsyncStorage.setItem(FAV_KEY, JSON.stringify(favourites)).catch(() => {});
  },

  loadFavs: async () => {
    try {
      const raw = await AsyncStorage.getItem(FAV_KEY);
      if (raw) set({ favourites: JSON.parse(raw) });
    } catch {}
  },
}));

========== src\utils\cleanArtistName.ts ==========
// src/utils/cleanArtistName.ts
const SITE_WORDS = [
  "pagalnew","pagalworld","pagalfree","pagalmovies","pagalsong","pagal",
  "djmaza","mrjatt","mr-jatt","songspk","wapking","webmusic","djpunjab",
  "djjohal","mp3mad","mp3song","mymp3song","freshmaza","musicmaza",
  "raagsong","bollywoodmp3","djyoungster","jiosaavn","hungama","gaana",
  "downloadming","songsmp3","sensongsmp3","pendujatt","jattmp3","newmp3",
  "mp3 download","free download",
];
const SITE_WORDS_RE = SITE_WORDS
  .map((w) => w.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&").replace(/\s+/g, "\\s*"))
  .join("|");
const TLDS =
  "com|net|org|in|co|se|pk|info|me|cc|ws|xyz|club|site|live|fm|mobi|io|app|top|online|tv|biz|us|uk";
const SEP = "[\\s_\\-â€“â€”|:@~â€¢Â·]";

const RE_BRACKET_SITE = new RegExp(
  `[\\(\\[\\{<][^\\)\\]\\}>]*(?:${SITE_WORDS_RE}|\\.(?:${TLDS})\\b)[^\\)\\]\\}>]*[\\)\\]\\}>]`,
  "gi"
);
const RE_SITE_WORD = new RegExp(`${SEP}*(?:${SITE_WORDS_RE})(?:\\.[a-z0-9]+)*`, "gi");
const RE_WWW = /\bwww\.[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)+/gi;
const RE_DOMAIN = new RegExp(`${SEP}*\\b[a-z0-9-]+\\.(?:${TLDS})(?:\\.[a-z]{2})?\\b`, "gi");
const RE_EDGE_SEP = new RegExp(`^${SEP}+|${SEP}+$`, "g");

export const UNKNOWN_ARTIST = "Unknown artist";

export const cleanSiteName = (raw?: string | null, keepOriginal = true): string => {
  if (!raw) return "";
  const original = String(raw).trim();
  if (!original) return "";
  const s = original
    .replace(RE_BRACKET_SITE, " ")
    .replace(RE_WWW, " ")
    .replace(RE_SITE_WORD, " ")
    .replace(RE_DOMAIN, " ")
    .replace(/_/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ")
    .replace(RE_EDGE_SEP, "")
    .trim();
  if (!s) return keepOriginal ? original : "";
  return s;
};

export const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  const t = a.trim();
  if (!t) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(t);
};

export const resolveArtistName = (
  raw?: string | null,
  tagged?: string | null,
): string => {
  const cleanedTagged = cleanSiteName(tagged, false);
  if (!isUnknownArtist(cleanedTagged)) return cleanedTagged;

  const cleanedRaw = cleanSiteName(raw, false);
  if (!isUnknownArtist(cleanedRaw)) return cleanedRaw;

  return UNKNOWN_ARTIST;
};

========== src\utils\cleanSiteName.ts ==========
// Shared helpers (same logic as SongsScreen)
export const UNKNOWN_ARTIST = "Unknown artist";

const SITE_WORDS = [
  "pagalnew","pagalworld","pagalfree","pagalmovies","pagalsong","pagal",
  "djmaza","mrjatt","mr-jatt","songspk","wapking","webmusic","djpunjab",
  "djjohal","mp3mad","mp3song","mymp3song","freshmaza","musicmaza",
  "raagsong","bollywoodmp3","djyoungster","jiosaavn","hungama","gaana",
  "downloadming","songsmp3","sensongsmp3","pendujatt","jattmp3","newmp3",
  "mp3 download","free download",
];
const SITE_WORDS_RE = SITE_WORDS.map((w) =>
  w.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&").replace(/\s+/g, "\\s*"),
).join("|");
const TLDS =
  "com|net|org|in|co|se|pk|info|me|cc|ws|xyz|club|site|live|fm|mobi|io|app|top|online|tv|biz|us|uk";
const SEP = "[\\s_\\-â€“â€”|:@~â€¢Â·]";

const RE_BRACKET_SITE = new RegExp(
  `[\\(\\[\\{<][^\\)\\]\\}>]*(?:${SITE_WORDS_RE}|\\.(?:${TLDS})\\b)[^\\)\\]\\}>]*[\\)\\]\\}>]`,
  "gi",
);
const RE_SITE_WORD = new RegExp(`${SEP}*(?:${SITE_WORDS_RE})(?:\\.[a-z0-9]+)*`, "gi");
const RE_WWW = /\bwww\.[a-z0-9-]+(?:\.[a-z0-9-]+)+/gi;
const RE_DOMAIN = new RegExp(`${SEP}*\\b[a-z0-9-]+\\.(?:${TLDS})(?:\\.[a-z]{2})?\\b`, "gi");
const RE_EDGE_SEP = new RegExp(`^${SEP}+|${SEP}+$`, "g");

export const cleanSiteName = (raw?: string | null, keepOriginal = true): string => {
  if (!raw) return "";
  const original = String(raw).trim();
  if (!original) return "";
  const s = original
    .replace(RE_BRACKET_SITE, " ")
    .replace(RE_WWW, " ")
    .replace(RE_SITE_WORD, " ")
    .replace(RE_DOMAIN, " ")
    .replace(/_/g, " ")
    .replace(/\s{2,}/g, " ")
    .replace(RE_EDGE_SEP, "")
    .trim();
  if (!s) return keepOriginal ? original : "";
  return s;
};

export const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  const t = a.trim();
  if (!t) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(t);
};

========== src\utils\formatDuration.ts ==========
export function formatDuration(seconds: number): string {
  if (!isFinite(seconds) || seconds < 0) return '0:00';
  const s = Math.floor(seconds % 60);
  const m = Math.floor((seconds / 60) % 60);
  const h = Math.floor(seconds / 3600);
  const ss = s.toString().padStart(2, '0');
  if (h > 0) return `${h}:${m.toString().padStart(2, '0')}:${ss}`;
  return `${m}:${ss}`;
}

export function formatFileSize(bytes?: number | null): string {
  if (!bytes || bytes <= 0) return '';
  const units = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
  let i = 0;
  let b = bytes;
  while (b >= 1024 && i < units.length - 1) {
    b /= 1024;
    i++;
  }
  return `${b.toFixed(b < 10 ? 1 : 0)} ${units[i]}`;
}

========== src\utils\globalFont.ts ==========
// src/utils/globalFont.ts
import React from 'react';
import { Text, TextInput } from 'react-native';
import * as Font from 'expo-font';

/** Family name you will use everywhere via `fontFamily`. */
export const APP_FONT_FAMILY = 'NotoSans_sub';
/** Symbol / emoji-ish fallback font. */
export const SYMBOL_FONT_FAMILY = 'NotoSansSymbols';

/** Assets registered with expo-font (keys = the family names used in styles). */
export const FONTS: Record<string, number> = {
  [APP_FONT_FAMILY]: require('../../assets/fonts/NotoSans_sub.ttf'),
  [SYMBOL_FONT_FAMILY]: require('../../assets/fonts/NotoSansSymbols.otf'),
};

const DEFAULT_TEXT_STYLE = { fontFamily: APP_FONT_FAMILY };

let applied = false;

/**
 * Registers the fonts and forces them onto every <Text> / <TextInput>.
 * Safe to call multiple times (idempotent).
 */
export function applyGlobalFont(): void {
  if (applied) return;
  applied = true;

  // 1) Register fonts natively.
  //    If you also add the expo-font config plugin (see app.json below),
  //    the fonts are embedded in the binary and this call just returns fast.
  Font.loadAsync(FONTS).catch((err) => {
    if (__DEV__) console.warn('[globalFont] Failed to load fonts:', err);
  });

  // 2) Inject the font family into Text / TextInput.
  patchComponent(Text as any, DEFAULT_TEXT_STYLE);
  patchComponent(TextInput as any, DEFAULT_TEXT_STYLE);
}

function patchComponent(Component: any, style: any): void {
  if (!Component || Component.__globalFontPatched) return;
  Component.__globalFontPatched = true;

  // ---- Preferred: patch render() (class components â€” RN Text/TextInput) ----
  const originalRender = Component.render;
  if (typeof originalRender === 'function') {
    Component.render = function (...args: any[]) {
      const element = originalRender.apply(this, args);
      if (!element || !element.props) return element;
      return React.cloneElement(element, {
        // [defaultFont, userStyle] â†’ user style always wins
        style: [style, element.props.style],
      });
    };
    return;
  }

  // ---- Fallback: defaultProps (forwardRef components / RN new-arch) ----
  Component.defaultProps = Component.defaultProps || {};
  Component.defaultProps.style = [
    style,
    Component.defaultProps.style,
  ].filter(Boolean);
}

========== src\utils\id3Tags.ts ==========
import * as FileSystem from "expo-file-system/legacy";

/**
 * Reads song info (title / artist / album = movie) from the ID3 tag at the
 * start of an MP3 file. Place this file at: src/utils/id3Tags.ts
 */
export type SongTags = {
  title?: string;
  artist?: string;
  album?: string;
};

export const isUnknownArtist = (a?: string | null): boolean => {
  if (!a) return true;
  const t = a.trim();
  if (!t) return true;
  return /^<?\s*unknown(\s+artist)?\s*>?$/i.test(t);
};

const B64_CHARS =
  "ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
const B64_LOOKUP = (() => {
  const t = new Uint8Array(256);
  for (let i = 0; i < B64_CHARS.length; i++) t[B64_CHARS.charCodeAt(i)] = i;
  return t;
})();

const base64ToBytes = (b64: string): Uint8Array => {
  const clean = b64.replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9+/]/g, "");
  const out = new Uint8Array(Math.floor((clean.length * 3) / 4));
  let p = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i += 4) {
    const a = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i)] || 0;
    const b = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 1)] || 0;
    const c = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 2)] || 0;
    const d = B64_LOOKUP[clean.charCodeAt(i + 3)] || 0;
    out[p++] = (a << 2) | (b >> 4);
    out[p++] = ((b & 15) << 4) | (c >> 2);
    out[p++] = ((c & 3) << 6) | d;
  }
  return out;
};

const syncSafe = (b: Uint8Array, o: number) =>
  ((b[o] & 0x7f) << 21) |
  ((b[o + 1] & 0x7f) << 14) |
  ((b[o + 2] & 0x7f) << 7) |
  (b[o + 3] & 0x7f);

const decodeUtf16 = (b: Uint8Array, bigEndian: boolean): string => {
  let s = "";
  for (let i = 0; i + 1 < b.length; i += 2) {
    const code = bigEndian ? (b[i] << 8) | b[i + 1] : (b[i + 1] << 8) | b[i];
    s += String.fromCharCode(code);
  }
  return s;
};

const decodeUtf8 = (b: Uint8Array): string => {
  try {
    let pct = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
      pct += "%" + (b[i] < 16 ? "0" : "") + b[i].toString(16);
    }
    return decodeURIComponent(pct);
  } catch {
    let s = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
    return s;
  }
};

const decodeId3Text = (b: Uint8Array, enc: number): string => {
  let s = "";
  if (enc === 1) {
    if (b[0] === 0xfe && b[1] === 0xff) s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), true);
    else if (b[0] === 0xff && b[1] === 0xfe)
      s = decodeUtf16(b.subarray(2), false);
    else s = decodeUtf16(b, false);
  } else if (enc === 2) {
    s = decodeUtf16(b, true);
  } else if (enc === 3) {
    s = decodeUtf8(b);
  } else {
    for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(b[i]);
  }
  return s
    .split("\u0000")
    .map((x) => x.trim())
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(", ");
};

const readId3Frame = (
  b: Uint8Array,
  frameIds: string[],
): string | undefined => {
  if (b.length < 10 || b[0] !== 0x49 || b[1] !== 0x44 || b[2] !== 0x33) {
    return undefined;
  }
  const major = b[3];
  const flags = b[5];
  const tagSize = syncSafe(b, 6);
  let pos = 10;

  if (major >= 3 && (flags & 0x40) !== 0) {
    const ext =
      major === 4
        ? syncSafe(b, pos)
        : (((b[pos] << 24) |
            (b[pos + 1] << 16) |
            (b[pos + 2] << 8) |
            b[pos + 3]) >>>
            0) +
          4;
    pos += ext;
  }

  const end = Math.min(b.length, 10 + tagSize);
  const idLen = major === 2 ? 3 : 4;
  const hdrLen = major === 2 ? 6 : 10;

  while (pos + hdrLen <= end) {
    let id = "";
    for (let i = 0; i < idLen; i++) id += String.fromCharCode(b[pos + i]);
    if (!/^[A-Z0-9]+$/.test(id)) break;

    let size: number;
    if (major === 2) {
      size = (b[pos + 3] << 16) | (b[pos + 4] << 8) | b[pos + 5];
    } else if (major === 4) {
      size = syncSafe(b, pos + 4);
    } else {
      size =
        ((b[pos + 4] << 24) |
          (b[pos + 5] << 16) |
          (b[pos + 6] << 8) |
          b[pos + 7]) >>>
        0;
    }

    const dataStart = pos + hdrLen;
    if (size <= 0 || dataStart + size > b.length) break;

    if (frameIds.includes(id)) {
      const text = decodeId3Text(
        b.subarray(dataStart + 1, dataStart + size),
        b[dataStart],
      );
      if (text && text.trim()) return text.trim();
    }

    pos = dataStart + size;
  }
  return undefined;
};

export const readId3Tags = async (uri: string): Promise<SongTags> => {
  try {
    const b64 = await FileSystem.readAsStringAsync(uri, {
      encoding: FileSystem.EncodingType.Base64,
      position: 0,
      length: 131072,
    });
    const bytes = base64ToBytes(b64);

    const title = readId3Frame(bytes, ["TIT2", "TT2"]);
    const album = readId3Frame(bytes, ["TALB", "TAL"]);

    let artist = readId3Frame(bytes, ["TPE1", "TP1"]);
    if (!artist || isUnknownArtist(artist)) {
      artist = readId3Frame(bytes, ["TPE2", "TP2"]);
    }

    return {
      title: title && title.trim() ? title.trim() : undefined,
      artist: artist && !isUnknownArtist(artist) ? artist : undefined,
      album: album && album.trim() ? album.trim() : undefined,
    };
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
};

========== src\utils\navigation.ts ==========
import type { NavigationProp } from "@react-navigation/native";

/**
 * Safe back navigation â€” only calls goBack() if there is actually a screen
 * to go back to. Prevents the "POP was not handled" dev warning when a
 * screen is the root of the stack.
 */
export const safeGoBack = (nav: NavigationProp<any> | any) => {
  try {
    if (nav?.canGoBack && nav.canGoBack()) {
      nav.goBack();
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.log("[safeGoBack] error:", e);
  }
};

========== src\utils\toast.ts ==========
import { Platform, ToastAndroid, Alert } from 'react-native';

/**
 * System toast (Android native) â€” bottom à¤²à¤¾ à¤¦à¤¿à¤¸à¤¤à¥‹
 * iOS à¤µà¤° toast à¤¨à¤¾à¤¹à¥€, à¤®à¥à¤¹à¤£à¥‚à¤¨ Alert à¤µà¤¾à¤ªà¤°à¤¤à¥‹ (fallback)
 */

export function showToast(message: string) {
  if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
    ToastAndroid.show(message, ToastAndroid.SHORT);
  } else {
    // iOS à¤µà¤° system toast à¤¨à¤¾à¤¹à¥€ â€” Alert fallback
    Alert.alert('', message);
  }
}

export function showLongToast(message: string) {
  if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
    ToastAndroid.show(message, ToastAndroid.LONG);
  } else {
    Alert.alert('', message);
  }
}

export function showToastWithGravity(
  message: string,
  gravity: 'top' | 'center' | 'bottom' = 'bottom'
) {
  if (Platform.OS === 'android') {
    const g =
      gravity === 'top'
        ? ToastAndroid.TOP
        : gravity === 'center'
        ? ToastAndroid.CENTER
        : ToastAndroid.BOTTOM;

    ToastAndroid.showWithGravity(message, ToastAndroid.SHORT, g);
  } else {
    Alert.alert('', message);
  }
}

/**
 * Shortcuts â€” success / error / info à¤¸à¤¾à¤ à¥€
 */
export const toast = {
  success: (msg: string) => showToast(msg),
  error: (msg: string) => showToast(msg),
  info: (msg: string) => showToast(msg),
  long: (msg: string) => showLongToast(msg),
  center: (msg: string) => showToastWithGravity(msg, 'center'),
  top: (msg: string) => showToastWithGravity(msg, 'top'),
};

+fun getTrack(index: Int, callback: Promise) = scope.launch {
+    if (verifyServiceBoundOrReject(callback) == true) return@launch
+    val service = musicService
+    if (service == null) {
+        callback.resolve(null)
+        return@launch
+    }
+    if (index >= 0 && index < service.tracks.size) {
+        val item = service.tracks[index].originalItem
+        if (item != null) {
+            callback.resolve(Arguments.fromBundle(item))
         } else {
             callback.resolve(null)
         }
+    } else {
+        callback.resolve(null)
     }

+fun getActiveTrack(callback: Promise) = scope.launch {
+    if (verifyServiceBoundOrReject(callback) == true) return@launch
+    val service = musicService
+    if (service == null) {
+        callback.resolve(null)
+        return@launch
     }
+    if (service.tracks.isEmpty()) {
+        callback.resolve(null)
+        return@launch
+    }
+    val index = service.getCurrentTrackIndex()
+    if (index < 0 || index >= service.tracks.size) {
+        callback.resolve(null)
+        return@launch
+    }
+    val item = service.tracks[index].originalItem
+    if (item != null) {
+        callback.resolve(Arguments.fromBundle(item))
+    } else {
+        callback.resolve(null)
+    }
+}
